用于代码优化的强化学习Reinforcement Learning for Code Optimization

皮埃尔·尚邦Pierre Chambon1,_2 , 郑坤浩Kunhao Zheng1,3 , 朱丽叶·德古吉斯Juliette Decugis1,_2 , 伯努瓦·萨戈特Benoit Sagot2 , 加布里埃尔·西尼夫Gabriel Synnaeve1

1Meta 展会、2Inria、3巴黎第九大学1FAIR at Meta, 2Inria, 3Université Paris Dauphine

现在已经建立了代码正确性的强化学习：让模型生成一个程序，针对隐藏的测试用例运行它，并奖励通过的解决方案。将其扩展到代码优化似乎很简单：只需将执行时间添加到奖励中即可。但在实践中，一旦时序驱动了奖励，测量噪声、奖励稀疏性或 GRPO 不稳定等小问题就会淹没信号并导致 RL 失败：生成的解决方案几乎没有更快，而且更多的解决方案可能会失败。我们通过三个阶段使执行时间变得可学习：（1）如何测试代码，通过构建具有大型优化测试和校准沙箱的 DMC-Optim； （2）如何将速度转化为奖励，通过在 RL 环境中组合正确性和速度，并使用离线模拟器来预测最有希望的配置； (3) 模型如何通过使 GRPO 和评估适应稀疏、噪声较大的定时执行设置来从奖励中学习。在 DMC-Optim 上，最强大的优化感知配置将 Qwen 2.5 7B 上严格的 top-50% pass@1 从 18.0% 提高到 31.3%，将 CWM 32B 从 30.7% 提高到 50.4%。这些收益在更严格的百分位数（例如前 30%）中进一步增加，CWM 32B 的相对改进为 125%，同时保留纯粹正确性分数。当时序沙箱降级时，鲁棒优化 RL 比标准 RLVR 提高了 100%–200%，具体取决于评估标准。在 LCB 上，与标准 RLVR 相比，CWM 32B 在中值样本速度比较中获胜高达 83%。相对于每个问题最快正确的人类提交速度，它达到了人类复杂性级别改进率的一半左右（14% 与 28%）。RL for code correctness is now established: have the model generate a program, run it against hidden test cases, and reward solutions that pass. Extending this to code optimization seems straightforward: just add execution time to the reward. But in practice, once timing drives the reward, small problems in measurement noise, reward sparsity, or GRPO instability overwhelm the signal and make RL fail: generated solutions are barely faster, and more of them can fail. We make execution time learnable through three stages: (1) how code is tested, by building DMC-Optim with large optimization tests and a calibrated sandbox; (2) how speed is turned into reward, by composing correctness and speed in the RL environment and using an offline simulator to predict the most promising configurations; and (3) how the model learns from that reward, by adapting GRPO and evaluation to the sparser, noisier timed-execution setting. On DMC-Optim, the strongest optimization-aware configurations improve strict top-50% pass@1 from 18.0% to 31.3% on Qwen 2.5 7B and from 30.7% to 50.4% on CWM 32B. These gains further increase at stricter percentiles such as top-30%, with 125% relative improvement for CWM 32B, while preserving pure-correctness scores. When the timing sandbox is degraded, robust optimization RL reaches up to 100%–200% improvement over standard RLVR, depending on the evaluation criterion. On LCB, CWM 32B wins up to 83% of median-sample speed comparisons against standard RLVR. Relative to the fastest correct human submissions per problem, it reaches about half the human rate of complexity-class improvements (14% vs. 28%).

通讯：Correspondence:皮埃尔·尚邦 (Pierre Chambon) pchambon@meta.comPierre Chambon at pchambon@meta.com

1 简介1 Introduction

正确性训练并没有使代码模型变得可靠、快速。在 SWE 效率方面（Ma et al., 2025），Claude 4.5 Sonnet 在 81% 的时间内生成正确的补丁，但仅捕获了 4.1% 的专家加速。存储库规模和昂贵的执行并不能完全解释这一点：这种差距也出现在竞争性编程中，廉价的测试和充足的人工提交提供了速度基线。在金星上，o4-mini 达到 89.1% pass@1，但在 Beyond-T 上得分仅为 56.9%，Beyond-T 是相对于人类参考的运行时效率百分位数（Du 等人，2025），而 Qwen3-32B 在 BigO(Bench) 上的最佳复杂性等级要求下，从 70.0% 的纯正确性 pass@1 下降到 43.5%（Chambon 等人， 2025）。Training for correctness has not made code models reliably fast. On SWE-fficiency (Ma et al., 2025), Claude 4.5 Sonnet produces correct patches 81% of the time but captures only 4.1% of expert speedup. Repository scale and expensive execution do not fully explain this: the gap also appears in competitive programming, where cheap tests and ample human submissions provide speed baselines. On Venus, o4-mini reaches 89.1% pass@1 but scores only 56.9% on Beyond-T, a runtime-efficiency percentile against human references (Du et al., 2025), and Qwen3-32B drops from 70.0% pure-correctness pass@1 to 43.5% under a best-complexity-class requirement on BigO(Bench) (Chambon et al., 2025).

竞争性编程的更受控制的设置让我们能够隔离优化强化学习失败的地方。在代码的标准强化学习和可验证奖励（RLVR）中，执行主要是一个二进制验证器：如果样本通过了正确性测试，就会获得奖励。在优化强化学习中，执行还必须测量正确程序在给定测试上的运行速度，因此计时噪声、弱测试和为快速但错误的代码提供速度信用的奖励可能会破坏训练信号。评估在正确性和速度之间提出了同样的耦合问题。为了评估，我们引入The more controlled setting of competitive-programming lets us isolate where optimization RL fails. In standard reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for code, execution is mostly a binary verifier: a sample is rewarded if it passes the correctness tests. In optimization RL, execution must also measure how fast a correct program runs on the given tests, so timing noise, weak tests, and rewards that give speed credit to fast but wrong code can corrupt the training signal. Evaluation raises the same coupling problem between correctness and speed. For evaluation, we introducepτ：只有当解决方案正确并且不慢于解决方案时才算数: a solution counts only if it is correct and no slower than theτ- 人类参考排行榜的第百分位，较小-th-percentile of the human reference leaderboard, with smallerτ更严格。该评估证实了朴素的优化训练目标是脆弱的：在二元正确性奖励之上奖励较低的平均运行时间，仅将 pass@1 改变 +0stricter. This evaluation confirms that the naive optimization training objective is brittle: rewarding lower average runtime on top of a binary correctness reward changes pass@1 by only +0.6分于6 points atp30，无论奖励范围映射是什么，并且通过30, whatever the reward-range mapping is, and by− 0 .3 至 +13 to +1.4分于4 points atp100，相当于纯粹的正确性。因此，问题是：是什么让执行时信号变得可学习？100, which is equivalent to pure correctness. The question is therefore: what makes an execution-time signal learnable?

优化强化学习通过一系列转换而失败。时序源必须分离解决方案（第 3 节）；环境和奖励必须选择测试、时间限制、参考比较，以及奖励如何在正确性与速度之间进行权衡，而不激励快速但错误的代码（第 4 节）；并且优化器必须在比通过/失败更稀疏、更嘈杂的奖励下保持稳定（第 5 节）。如果有的话Optimization RL fails through a chain of transformations. The timing source must separate solutions (Section 3); the environment and reward must choose tests, time limits, reference comparisons, and how rewards trade correctness against speed without incentivizing fast-but-wrong code (Section 4); and the optimizer must remain stable under rewards that are sparser and noisier than pass/fail (Section 5). If any

图 1 使 RL 的执行时间变得可学习。Figure 1 Making execution time learnable for RL.对 DMC-Optim 问题进行采样，LLM 生成候选解决方案。所有 RL 环境都共享相同的评估流程，但在注入优化压力的位置上有所不同。首先，每个候选解决方案都可以在标准正确性测试上进行评估，而且还可以生成大输入优化测试；预执行环境可以使用测试级别标准过滤进或出优化测试。然后，在本地沙箱（标准）或校准的远程执行服务（我们的设置）上运行这些测试的解决方案的执行；内部执行环境应用额外的每次测试时间限制。最后，沙箱返回是否所有正确性测试都通过（A DMC-Optim problem is sampled and the LLM generates candidate solutions. All RL environments share the same evaluation pipeline, but differ in where they inject optimization pressure. First, each candidate solution can be evaluated on standard correctness tests, but also generated large-input optimization tests; pre-execution environments may filter optimization tests in or out using test-level criteria. Execution of a solution on these tests is then run on a local sandbox (standard) or on a calibrated remote execution service (our setting); intra-execution environments apply additional per-test time limits. Finally, the sandbox returns whether all correctness tests pass (c），优化测试是否通过（), whether the optimization tests pass (g) 和执行持续时间；执行后环境将测量的持续时间与每次测试的校准人类参考分布进行比较，以产生聚合的标量质量分数), and the execution durations; post-execution environments compare measured durations against calibrated human reference distributions per test to produce an aggregated scalar quality scoreq（这实际上可以被门控并纳入(this can in practice be gated and incorporated intog）。三个信号，正确性门). The three signals, correctness gatec, 优化门, optimization gateg和质量得分, and quality scoreq，输入几种奖励组合之一。离线强化学习模拟器（底部）通过重放参考人类解决方案来评估候选环境。最有前途的环境奖励对用于在线 GRPO 训练，最佳优化 RL 设置在以下位置提出严格的 DMC-Optim pass@1, feed into one of several reward compositions. An offline RL simulator (bottom) evaluates candidate environments by replaying reference human solutions. The most promising environmentreward pairs are used in online GRPO training, and the best optimization-RL setting raises strict DMC-Optim pass@1 atp30 在 Qwen 2.5 7B 上从 7.7% 到 19.1%，在 CWM 32B 上从 13.7% 到 30.9%。30 from 7.7% to 19.1% on Qwen 2.5 7B and from 13.7% to 30.9% on CWM 32B.

链接失败，生成的程序速度不快，正确性也会降低。link fails, generated programs are not faster, and correctness can also degrade.

我们在图 1 中明确了这条链。根据 DeepMind 代码竞赛 (DMC) 语料库（Li 等人，2022），我们构建了包含 2,723 个已清理问题的 DMC-Optim，将正确性测试与更大的优化测试分开； 1,302 个问题具有足够的持续时间分布，可用于基于时间的奖励。我们扫描执行前过滤、执行内时间限制、针对人类参考的执行后排名，以及从仅优化/附加混合到多任务、折叠和硬门变体的奖励。离线模拟器会在在线 GRPO 之前筛选此空间。We make this chain explicit in Figure 1. From the DeepMind Code Contests (DMC) corpus (Li et al., 2022), we build DMC-Optim with 2,723 cleaned problems, separating correctness tests from larger optimization tests; 1,302 problems have enough duration spread for timing-based rewards. We sweep pre-execution filtering, intra-execution time constraints, post-execution ranking against human references, and rewards from optimization-only/additive blends to multitask, collapsed, and hard-gated variants. An offline simulator screens this space before online GRPO.

该堆栈在三个启动中提供了一致的域内增益：Qwen 2.5 7B/32B 基本检查点（Yang 等人，2025），我们在净化后的仅推理混合上进行 SFT，以及发布的 CWM 32B SFT 检查点（FAIR CodeGen 团队等人，2025）。在 DMC-Optim pass@1 上This stack gives consistent in-domain gains across three starts: Qwen 2.5 7B/32B base checkpoints (Yang et al., 2025), which we SFT on a decontaminated reasoning-only mix, and the released CWM 32B SFT checkpoint (FAIR CodeGen team et al., 2025). On DMC-Optim pass@1 atp50、Qwen 2.5 7B/32B和CWM 32B从18.0/21.1/30.7%上升至31.3/39.6/50.4%；在50, Qwen 2.5 7B/32B and CWM 32B rise from 18.0/21.1/30.7% to 31.3/39.6/50.4%; atp到 9 月 30 日，CWM 32B 从 13.7% 上升到 30.9%，相对增益为 125%，而纯粹正确性分数保持稳定。在 LCB 上，超时和百分位数分数不可靠，成对速度获胜率显示 CWM 32B 相对于标准 RLVR 达到高达 83.0% 的中值样本获胜率，同时保留 54.4% 的 pass@1。30, CWM 32B rises from 13.7% to 30.9%, a 125% relative gain, while pure-correctness scores stay stable. On LCB, where timeout and percentile scores are unreliable, pairwise speed win rates show CWM 32B reaching up to 83.0% median-sample wins against standard RLVR while retaining 54.4% pass@1.

我们的贡献有三方面：Our contributions are threefold:

  • 我们构建 DMC-Optim 及其计时堆栈：从 12,275 个原始 DMC 问题中清理出 2,723 个问题、1,302 个持续时间可过滤问题、430,215 个新的正确性测试和 352,740 个新的优化测试，We build DMC-Optim and its timing stack: 2,723 cleaned problems from 12,275 raw DMC problems, 1,302 duration-filterable problems, 430,215 new correctness tests, and 352,740 new optimization tests,

2

专门设计为需要更长的时间来执行。我们探索控制定时沙箱稳定性的方法，以便在其之上进行可靠的测量。specifically made to take longer to execute. We explore ways to control the stability of the timing sandbox to make reliable measurements on top of them.

  • 我们将先前工作中使用的优化约束以及新的公式组织为三个系列：执行前的测试过滤、执行期间的时间约束以及执行后的排名。然后我们测试哪些公式可以构成有用的强化学习环境。我们证明这取决于放置在环境之上的奖励，比较添加剂混合、多任务、折叠和门控奖励，使用离线模拟器来筛选有前途的配置，并使用适合稀疏计时奖励的 GRPO 配方运行在线强化学习。We organize optimization constraints used in prior work, along with new formulations, into three families: test filtering before execution, time constraints during execution, and ranking after execution. We then test which formulations make useful RL environments. We show that this depends on the reward placed on top of the environment, compare additive blends, multitask, collapsed, and gated rewards, use an offline simulator to screen promising configurations, and run online RL with a GRPO recipe adapted to sparse timing rewards.

  • 我们展示了评估中的优化收益：DMC-OptimWe show optimization gains across evaluations: DMC-OptimpQwen 2.5 7B/32B 和 CWM 32B 上的 50 pass@1 从 18.0/21.1/30.7% 提高到 31.3/39.6/50.4%；在50 pass@1 improves from 18.0/21.1/30.7% to 31.3/39.6/50.4% on Qwen 2.5 7B/32B and CWM 32B; atp30，CWM 32B 相对提高了 125%，同时保留了纯粹正确性分数； CWM 32B 相对于标准 RLVR 达到 83.0% LCB 中值样本速度获胜率；在退化的沙箱状态下，鲁棒优化 RL 比 RLVR 提高了大约 100%–200%，具体取决于评估标准。30, CWM 32B improves by 125% relative while preserving pure-correctness scores; CWM 32B reaches an 83.0% LCB median-sample speed win rate against standard RLVR; and under degraded sandbox state, robust optimization RL improves over RLVR by roughly 100%–200%, depending on the evaluation criterion.

2 相关工作2 Related Work

执行反馈和效率测量。Execution feedback and efficiency measurement.具有可验证执行反馈的强化学习主要以正确性为目标：CodeRL 引入了基于单元测试的训练，后来基于 GRPO 的工作表明二元奖励可以扩展到推理和代码生成（Le 等人，2022 年；Guo 等人，2025b）。效率改变了对象：奖励必须对已经正确的程序进行排名，因此测试构建、运行时噪声和聚合成为信号的一部分。效率基准通过相对于人类参考、压力测试、右审查超时、指令计数和增长曲线复杂性推断的百分位数得分使这一差距变得显而易见（Du et al., 2024; Huang et al., 2024; Qing et al., 2025; Liu et al., 2024; Qiu et al., 2025; Peng et al., 2025b; He et al., 2025b; He et al., 2024等人，2025；Ma 等人，2025；Chambon 等人，2025）。这些工具不会直接成为在线 RL 奖励：压力执行和曲线拟合可能过于昂贵，指令计数偏离挂钟时间，小型测试的原始计时噪音很大，以至于 PIE 报告相同代码的虚假加速为 1.91 倍（Shypula 等人，2024 年）。缺少的部分是与训练兼容的定时信号。RL with verifiable execution feedback has mostly targeted correctness: CodeRL introduced unit-test-based training, and later GRPO-based work showed binary rewards can scale to reasoning and code generation (Le et al., 2022; Guo et al., 2025b). Efficiency changes the object: rewards must rank already-correct programs, so test construction, runtime noise, and aggregation become part of the signal. Efficiency benchmarks make this gap visible with percentile scores against human references, stress tests, right-censored timeouts, instruction counts, and growth-curve complexity inference (Du et al., 2024; Huang et al., 2024; Qing et al., 2025; Liu et al., 2024; Qiu et al., 2025; Peng et al., 2025b; He et al., 2025; Ma et al., 2025; Chambon et al., 2025). These tools do not directly become online RL rewards: stress execution and curve fitting can be too expensive, instruction counts move away from wall-clock time, and raw timing on small tests is noisy enough that PIE reports 1.91x spurious speedups on identical code (Shypula et al., 2024). The missing piece is a training-compatible timing signal.

学习高效的代码。Learning efficient code.先前的效率训练工作包括性能反馈微调，包括针对较低级别优化任务的在线强化学习（Nichols et al., 2024；Wei et al., 2025a；Mikasa et al., 2026），以及对精选高效代码数据的效率感知监督微调（Huang et al., 2025）。 PIE 从离线慢-快编辑对中学习（Shypula 等人，2024），而其他系统在推理时搜索或迭代优化更快的程序，而不更新策略（Peng 等人，2025a；Gao 等人，2025；Ou 等人，2026；Romera-Paredes 等人，2023；Novikov 等人，2025）。这些方法是互补的，但更容易优化：系统从现有程序启动，或者在搜索过程中接收重复的执行反馈。在一次性生成中，模型必须在看到运行时反馈之前选择算法、数据结构和实现。Prior efficiency-training work includes performance-feedback fine-tuning, including online RL on lower-level optimization tasks (Nichols et al., 2024; Wei et al., 2025a; Mikasa et al., 2026), and efficiency-aware supervised fine-tuning on curated efficient-code data (Huang et al., 2025). PIE learns from offline slow-fast edit pairs (Shypula et al., 2024), while other systems search for or iteratively refine faster programs at inference time without updating the policy (Peng et al., 2025a; Gao et al., 2025; Ou et al., 2026; Romera-Paredes et al., 2023; Novikov et al., 2025). These approaches are complementary, but easier for optimization: the system starts from an existing program, or receives repeated execution feedback during search. In one-shot generation, the model must choose the algorithm, data structures, and implementation before seeing runtime feedback.

最接近的 RL 设置。Closest RL setting.最近最接近的比较是 Afterburner（Du et al., 2025）。它在迭代细化设置中使用 GRPO 进行训练，其中模型接收现有解决方案以及运行时指标，并附加地混合格式、正确性和效率奖励。该方法提高了效率，但也将比所有人类参考慢的解决方案比例从 0.33% 增加到 7.33%。我们研究了更难的一次性设置，其中法学硕士必须从头开始，在一次返回中且没有执行反馈的情况下生成一个正确的解决方案，该解决方案在参考竞争性程序员解决方案中排名尽可能高。我们仍然以 GRPO（Shao 等人，2024）以及最近对裁剪、基线和简并组的分析为基础（Yu 等人，2025；Hao 等人，2025；Liu 和 Chen，2026；Xu 和 Ding，2025；Liu 等人，2025），但重点关注全定时执行配方：可测量的信号、保持正确性的奖励，以及稀疏噪声回报下的稳定训练。The closest recent comparison is Afterburner (Du et al., 2025). It trains with GRPO in an iterative-refinement setting, where the model receives an existing solution plus runtime metrics and additively mixes format, correctness, and efficiency rewards. That recipe improves efficiency, but also increases the fraction of solutions slower than all human references from 0.33% to 7.33%. We study the harder one-shot setting, where the LLM must generate from scratch, in a single return and without execution feedback, a correct solution that ranks as high as possible among reference competitive-programmer solutions. We still build on GRPO (Shao et al., 2024) and recent analyses of clipping, baselines, and degenerate groups (Yu et al., 2025; Hao et al., 2025; Liu and Chen, 2026; Xu and Ding, 2025; Liu et al., 2025), but focus on the full timed-execution recipe: a measurable signal, a correctness-preserving reward, and stable training under sparse noisy returns.

我们对先前的工作如何定义优化约束（作为基准评估或培训目标）进行了广泛的文献回顾，并在 D.6 节中汇总了分类。We conduct an extensive literature review of how prior work defines optimization constraints, either as benchmark evaluations or as training objectives, and aggregate the classification in Section D.6.

图 2 DMC-Optim 施工流程。Figure 2 DMC-Optim construction pipeline.12 点起From 12,275 个原始 DMC 问题，我们重新执行人工解决方案，添加 430 个275 raw DMC problems, we re-execute human solutions, add 430,215 正确性测试和 352215 correctness tests and 352,740 次优化测试，并在测试、解决方案和问题级别进行筛选，以获得 2740 optimization tests, and filter at the test, solution, and problem levels to obtain a 2,723 问题清理语料库。最终的持续时间可过滤性门保留了 1723-problem cleaned corpus. A final duration-filterability gate retains the 1,302 个基于时序的 RL 问题，分为 1 个302 timing-based RL problems, split into 1, 000 /302 训练/测试。更具体地说，对于每个问题，我们用生成的正确性（corr.）和优化（opt.）测试来增强原始（orig.）测试，然后在多个级别进行过滤。第 1 步：在原始测试中重新执行人工解决方案，以确认标签并捕获误报 (FP)。第 2 步：为每个问题生成 10 个 LLM 编写的输入生成器，从每个生成器中抽取 15 个候选测试，并仅保留那些在人类解决方案中输出一致的测试。步骤 3：分三个级别进行过滤：超时或异常过多的测试、解决方案相互矛盾以及正确解决方案很少或 FP 率较高的问题。步骤 4：保留优化测试跨越较宽持续时间范围的问题，我们称之为可过滤持续时间。删除线标记已删除的测试（行）或重新标记的解决方案（列）；红色框是丢弃问题的示例。302 train/test. More specifically, for each problem, we augment the original (orig.) tests with generated correctness (corr.) and optimization (opt.) tests, then filter at multiple levels. Step 1: re-execute human solutions on original tests to confirm labels and catch false positives (FP). Step 2: generate 10 LLM-written input generators per problem, sample 15 candidate tests from each, and keep only those with output agreement across human solutions. Step 3: filter at three levels: tests with excessive timeouts or exceptions, contradicted solutions, and problems with few correct solutions or high FP rates. Step 4: retain problems whose optimization tests span a broad duration range, which we call duration filterable. Strikethroughs mark removed tests (rows) or relabeled solutions (columns); red boxes are examples of dropped problems.

3 如何获得可靠的计时测量3 How to get a reliable timing measure

基于时间的强化学习并不能通过简单地将运行时间添加到相同基础数据的奖励中来很好地发挥作用。当通过基本 DMC 问题奖励 RLVR 上更短的执行时间时，我们看到在优化约束评估下最多获得 5% 的收益（在Timing-based RL does not work well by simply adding runtime to the reward on the same base data. When rewarding shorter execution time on top of RLVR with base DMC problems, we see gains of at most 5% under optimization-constrained evaluation (atp50），纯粹正确性分数仅移动50), with pure-correctness scores moving by only−1% 至 +3%。伴随代码问题的测试必须得到改进：它们的执行必须拒绝错误代码，按持续时间分离正确的解决方案，并且即使在时序测量噪声下也能保留优化差异。原始 DMC 不满足这些要求：在最终的 RL 分割中，原始测试平均值为 01% to +3%. The tests accompanying code problems must be improved: their execution must reject wrong code, separate correct solutions by duration, and preserve optimization differences even under timing-measurement noise. Raw DMC does not fulfill these requirements: on the final RL split, original tests average 0.088秒，只达到0088 s and reach only 0. 145 / 0 .第463章463 s atp 95 /p99，因此数十毫秒的扰动可以主导决策（A 部分）。99, so tens-of-milliseconds perturbations can dominate decisions (Section A).

DMC-Optim 构建可测量的时序反馈。DMC-Optim builds measurable timing feedback.我们选择将正确性目标与用于评估解决方案的测试集的优化分开。从12,275个DMC问题中，我们重新执行人工解决方案，以验证正确和错误的提交作为对照，添加正确性测试，然后生成大输入优化测试；图 2 总结了语料库。裁决是不明确的：新的失败可能意味着无效的测试、错误标记的解决方案或不合适的问题。我们在测试、解决方案和问题级别进行过滤，用召回换取更清晰的标签和足够的 RL 规模。 B 节对此进行了详细说明。We choose to separate the correctness target from optimization in the set of tests used to evaluate the solutions. From 12,275 DMC problems, we re-execute human solutions, use verified correct and incorrect submissions as controls, add correctness tests, then generate large-input optimization tests; Figure 2 summarizes the corpus. Adjudication is ambiguous: a new failure can mean an invalid test, a mislabeled solution, or an unsuitable problem. We filter at test, solution, and problem levels, trading recall for cleaner labels and enough RL scale. This is detailed in Section B.

Original tests satisfy this for at most 3.8% of problems; optimization tests reach 48.2%. Ablations agree: non-filterable training loses strictp四种优化设置中的三种的性能为 10；剩余字符长度设置相对于仅增加 3.8%10 performance in three of four optimization settings; the remaining character-length setting gains only 3.8% relative atp10，但仍远低于排名10, but remains far below rankedp持续时间可过滤池上的 30。对于排名30 on the duration-filterable pool. 本地沙箱与隔离的远程执行。Local sandbox versus isolated remote execution.更好的测试在错误的后端下仍然会失败。本地执行包括在训练工作人员上执行代码，其中沙箱计时与推理和部署编排相竞争。另一种选择是在单独的专用 CPU 集群上运行 I/O 测试代码执行，其中每个执行都是隔离的，以最大限度地减少争用；在本文的其余部分，我们将这个远程沙箱服务称为 CES。在相同测试中独立重新运行未更改的代码时，本地计时使该代码相对于人类解决方案的固定参考排行榜的平均百分位排名平均移动了 41.2 个百分点。当尝试将本地持续时间映射到正确隔离执行的远程沙箱环境时，我们只能获得负交叉验证的拟合结果Better tests still fail under the wrong backend. Local execution consists in executing code on the training workers, where sandbox timing contends with inference and rollout orchestration. The other option is to run code executions on I/O tests on a separate dedicated CPU cluster, where each execution is isolated so as to minimize contention; in the rest of the paper, we call this remote sandbox service CES. In standalone reruns of unchanged code on the same tests, local timing moves the meanpercentile ranking of this code against a fixed reference leaderboard of human solutions by 41.2 percentage points on average. When trying to map local durations to our remote sandbox environment, which correctly isolates executions, we only obtain fits with negative cross-validatedR236,660 双。因此，我们使用 CES：它将时间与工人争用隔离开来；短期波动较小；仿射服务状态漂移校正将存储的 Spearman 与新鲜的 Spearman 从 0.54 提高到 0.96，从而提供第 4 节校准的正确性门控计时。 C 节进一步探讨了如何使执行环境足够稳定，以便 RL 循环可以使用定时测量。on 36,660 pairs. We therefore use CES: it isolates timing from worker contention; short-run variability is small; affine service-state drift correction raises stored-vs-fresh Spearman from 0.54 to 0.96, giving Section 4 calibrated, correctness-gated timings. Section C further explores how to make the execution environment stable enough for timing measurements to be usable by the RL loop.

4 如何将其转化为可学习的奖励4 How to turn it into a learnable reward

可靠的定时测量仍然不是可用的 RL 信号。第 3 部分为我们提供了一个经过改进的 I/O 测试的校准执行设置，可以拒绝错误代码并按持续时间准确比较正确的解决方案。但是，虽然下游优化 RL 需要这种设置，但它不足以获得显着收益：天真的奖励跨测试的直接聚合持续时间将优化约束分数提高 15%（在Reliable timing measurements are still not a usable RL signal. Section 3 gives us a calibrated execution setup with improved I/O tests that can reject wrong code and accurately compare correct solutions by duration. But while this setup is required for downstream optimization RL, it is not sufficient for obtaining significant gains: naively rewarding direct aggregate duration across tests raises optimization-constrained scores by 15% (atp50），同时将纯粹正确性 pass@1 降低 2-4%。因此，设计合适的 RL 环境和相应的奖励意味着决定优化约束如何进入 RL 循环，以便训练提高策略生成优化代码的能力，而不会奖励快速但错误的程序。我们将各种优化强化学习环境分为 3 个干预点：时序约束可以在执行之前、执行期间或执行之后进入。50), while dropping pure-correctness pass@1 by 2–4%. Designing a proper RL environment and corresponding reward therefore means deciding how the optimization constraint enters the RL loop, so that training improves the policy’s ability to generate optimized code without rewarding fast-but-wrong programs. We classify a broad variety of optimization-RL environments under 3 intervention points: timing constraints can enter before execution, during execution, or after execution.

时间进入环境的地方。Where timing enters the environment.我们通过查看先前的基准和训练目标放置优化约束的位置来定义优化强化学习环境空间，然后还在同一框架中添加新的公式。这给出了三个广泛的干预点：执行前过滤、执行内时间限制和执行后排名。 D.6 节展示了先前发表的作品如何符合该分类法。We define the optimization-RL environment space by looking at where prior benchmarks and training objectives place the optimization constraint, then also adding new formulations in the same framework. This gives three broad intervention points: pre-execution filtering, intra-execution time constraints, and post-execution ranking. Section D.6 shows how previously published work fits into this taxonomy.预执行Pre-execution系列在运行候选解决方案之前过滤优化测试，使用聚合绝对持续时间、问题内相对持续时间或输入输出长度作为工作负载代理；它们可以消除不稳定的尾部，并且较少依赖于参考解决方案，但可以在异构工作负载之间提供不太精细的优化压力。families filter optimization tests before the candidate solution is run, using aggregated absolute duration, within-problem relative duration, or input-output length as a workload proxy; they can remove unstable tails, and are less dependent on reference solutions, but can provide less refined optimization pressure across heterogeneous workloads.内部执行Intra-execution家庭在测试进行时施加时间限制，从全局时间限制到源自强大人类参考的限制；它们是字面上的效率限制，但如果调整不当，可能会切断整个学习信号。families impose time constraints while tests run, from global time limits to limits derived from strong human references; they are literal efficiency constraints, but can cut the entire learning signal if not properly tuned.执行后Post-execution家庭会保持执行完整无缺，并在之后汇总持续时间，通常是将候选人插入经过校准的人员库中，并在优化测试中对其进行排名。这在执行后保持了最大的灵活性，因为相同的测量解决方案可以在不同的排名规则或百分位阈值下重复使用，但它通常需要参考持续时间分布或其他额外数据。 D 部分定义了我们探索的确切环境和环境系列。families leave the execution intact and aggregate durations afterward, typically by inserting the candidate into the calibrated human pool and ranking it across optimization tests. This keeps the most flexibility after execution, since the same measured solution can be reused under different ranking rules or percentile thresholds, but it often requires reference duration distributions or other extra data. Section D defines the exact environments and families of environments that we explore.

面向奖励的界面。The reward-facing interface.执行后，所有环境都会减少到三个面向奖励的数量（After execution, all environments reduce to three reward-facing quantities (c、g、qc, g, q）。正确性门). The correctness gatec ∈ {c ∈{ 0 , 1 }检查固定的正确性测试；优化门checks the fixed correctness tests; the optimization gateg ∈ {g ∈{ 0 , 1 }检查优化分支是否满足超时容忍度和潜在的百分位排名阈值；可选分级信号checks whether the optimization branch satisfies the timeout tolerance and a potential percentile ranking threshold; the optional graded signalq ∈ [0 ,1] 是非门控的连续等价物。时限环境1] is a continuous equivalent that is not gated. 图 3 优化感知环境的离线模拟器筛选。Figure 3 Offline simulator screening of optimization-aware environments.每个点都是一种环境配置，由两个模拟器诊断定位：AUC、随着采样解决方案质量增加而模拟通过率曲线下的面积以及陡度Each point is one environment configuration, positioned by two simulator diagnostics: AUC, the area under the simulated pass-rate curve as sampled solution quality increases, and steepnessS = a/(1+(1 +|一个||a|)，该曲线的钳位线性拟合的有界斜率。 AUC 轴将稀疏设置与饱和设置分开，而陡度则显示从弱采样解到强采样解的过渡是平坦的还是相关的。形状标记干预点——执行前过滤、执行内时间限制或执行后排名——颜色标记子类；插图显示代表性曲线。在18轮Qwen 2.5 7B测试横扫中，严格), the bounded slope of a clamped-linear fit to that curve. The AUC axis separates sparse from saturated settings, while steepness shows whether the transition from weak to strong sampled solutions is flat or correlated. Shape marks the intervention point—pre-execution filtering, intra-execution time limits, or post-execution ranking—and color marks the subclass; insets show representative curves. 可以将超时率公开为can expose timeout rate asq。执行后排名环境可以通过将每个测量的持续时间与校准的人类参考进行比较、聚合每个测试的百分位数以及可选地将聚合转换为排行榜百分位数来返回标量百分位数排名。对于这个每次测试的聚合操作，我们在重复的噪声测量下筛选了 17 个排名指标，并保留基于平均值的百分位数系列，因为它是最稳定和最具辨别力的：它的重新运行噪声仍然很小，分数移动了 3. Post-execution ranking environments can return a scalar percentile ranking by comparing each measured duration to calibrated human references, aggregating per-test percentiles, and optionally converting the aggregate into a leaderboard percentile. For this per-test aggregation operation, we screened 17 ranking metrics under repeated noisy measurements and kept the mean-based percentile family because it was the most stable and discriminative: its rerun noise remains small, with scores moving by 3. 0 ± 1 .重复测量时百分位数为0，而动态范围仍然很大，强解和弱解平均相差47个点。排序函数的完整研究在 D.3 节中。0 percentile points under repeated measurements, while its dynamic range remains large, with strong and weak solutions separated by 47 points on average. The full study of ranking functions is in Section D.3.

当使用排行榜百分位执行后环境作为评估时，这直接定义了When using a leaderboard-percentile post-execution environment as an evaluation, this directly defines thepτ通行证家属@family of pass@k整篇论文中的许多表格和数字都使用了指标。让metrics used throughout the paper in many tables and figures. 这里Herec〜cor 是严格的正确性先决条件：它检查所有基础和生成的正确性测试是否成功，并且没有优化测试出现硬故障（但超时不会受到惩罚）。因此˜cor is the strict correctness prerequisite: it checks that all base and generated correctness tests succeed, and that no optimization test has a hard failure (but timeouts are not penalized). 平衡正确性和效率。Balancing correctness and efficiency.奖励构成决定如何在正确性和效率之间分配奖励范围，可能会以其中之一为条件。奖励的制定可能是Reward composition decides how to allocate the reward range between correctness and efficiency, potentially conditioning one on the other. A reward formulation could be to

6

保持正确性作为外门：keep correctness as an outer gate:r ( x、yx, y）=) =−1 当1 whenc ( x、yx, y) = 0，并且只有正确的解决方案才能获得正的效率分数，例如 1) = 0, and only correct solutions receive a positive efficiency score such as 1− q ( x、yx, y），可以保持完全连续、分桶或二值化。随着政策训练的进行，这种调节应该有助于平衡学习正确性和学习优化，同时避免奖励黑客场景。我们比较了各种奖励系列，从二元奖励到折叠奖励，包括多任务变体和附加混合。), which can be kept fully continuous, bucketed, or binarized. Such conditioning should help balance learning correctness and learning optimization as policy training proceeds, while avoiding reward-hacking scenarios. We compare various families of rewards, from binary to collapsed, including multitask variants and additive blends.

修剪候选强化学习环境的空间。Pruning the space of candidate RL environments.总而言之，多种形式的 RL 环境（包括它们使用的奖励）创造了很大的 RL 训练可能性空间，而每次在线运行都会使用 8-32 个 GPU 节点数小时甚至数天。因此，我们在在线 GRPO 之前使用离线模拟器进行筛选，并尝试选择最有前途的候选人。模拟器无法预测精确的学习曲线：它保持环境和奖励计算固定，用采样的人类解决方案替换模型生成，用预先计算的校准持续时间替换实时 CES 调用，并询问更好的采样解决方案是否会获得更好的奖励，而不会饱和或被噪音淹没。图3使用AUC来暴露过于稀疏或饱和的奖励信号，并使用陡度来暴露过于平坦的奖励曲线；下游 DMC-Optim 强化学习后测试性能与对角线偏差、原始单调性和陡度最相关，而曲线噪声在以下情况下无法预测：Taken together, the many forms of RL environments, including the reward they use, create a large space of RL training possibilities, while each online run uses 8–32 GPU nodes for hours, if not days. We therefore screen with an offline simulator before online GRPO, and try to select the most promising candidates. The simulator does not predict exact learning curves: it keeps the environment and reward computation fixed, replaces model generations by sampled human solutions, replaces live CES calls by precomputed calibrated durations, and asks whether better sampled solutions receive better rewards without saturating, or being overwhelmed by noise. Figure 3 uses AUC to expose reward signals that are too sparse or saturated, and steepness to expose reward curves that are too flat; downstream DMC-Optim post-RL test performance correlates the most with diagonal deviation, raw monotonicity, and steepness, while curve noise is not predictive atp 30 ( rs = − 0 . 002, p = 0 .994）。这会在第 5 节运行 GRPO 之前修剪环境/奖励空间，并提供与实时代码执行相关的稀疏、嘈杂的返回。 E 部分提供了有关离线 RL 模拟的更多详细信息。994). This prunes the environment/reward space before Section 5 runs GRPO with the sparse, noisy returns tied to live code execution. Section E provides more details about the offline RL simulations.

5 如何用它训练稳定的RL，并得到结果5 How to train stable RL with it, and getting the results

第 3 节介绍了 RL 数据和时序测量工具；然后，第 4 节定义了 RL 环境和在其之上进行训练的奖励。我们的目标是获得一个至少能够像标准 RLVR 一样学习正确性的 RL 设置，同时使生成正确代码的速度大大加快。由于我们有许多优化 RL 环境系列，并且每个环境中都有许多可能的参数化，因此第 4 节还介绍了一个离线模拟器，用于在启动在线 RL 之前修剪退化环境。这就是我们现在在本节中所做的：Section 3 introduced the RL data and timing-measurement tools; Section 4 then defined the RL environments and rewards to be trained on top of them. The goal is to obtain an RL setup that learns correctness at least as well as standard RLVR, while also making the generated correct code substantially faster. Because we have many optimization-RL environment families, and many possible parameterizations within each, Section 4 also introduced an offline simulator to prune degenerate environments before launching online RL. This is what we now do in this section:

  1. 实验设置。Experimental setup.我们使用两种尺寸的 Qwen 和 CWM 模型：Qwen 2.5 7B 来详细探索有前景的优化强化学习设置，Qwen 2.5 32B/CWM 32B 来验证更大规模的结果。We use Qwen and CWM models across two sizes: Qwen 2.5 7B to explore promising optimization-RL setups in detail, and Qwen 2.5 32B/CWM 32B to validate the results at larger scale.

  2. 重播评价。Replayed evaluation.由于我们详细讨论了 RL 训练依赖于定时测量时可能出现的噪声，因此我们还调整评估设置以产生更稳健的结果和比较。Since we discussed in detail the noise that can arise when RL training relies on timing measurements, we also adapt the evaluation setup to produce more robust results and comparisons.

  3. 首先，所有这些工作都是必要的吗？探索基线。Is all of this work necessary in the first place? Exploring baselines.标准 RLVR 和朴素的计时奖励无法解决任务，这就是我们探索其他 RL 设置的原因。Standard RLVR and naive timing rewards do not solve the task, which is the reason why we explore other RL setups.

  4. GRPO 对于稀疏、嘈杂的计时奖励是否足够稳定？Can GRPO be stable enough for sparse, noisy timing rewards?除了第 3 节和第 4 节中详细介绍的方法之外，GRPO 本身还需要处理更稀疏和更嘈杂的奖励；否则，在汇总损失并采取梯度步骤时，潜在的学习可能会被扼杀。On top of the methods detailed in Sections 3 and 4, GRPO itself needs to handle sparser and noisier rewards; otherwise, potential learning could be killed when aggregating the loss and taking gradient steps.

  5. 哪种优化强化学习环境最有效？What optimization RL environments work best?我们的框架试图将以前的工作对代码优化概念进行基准测试的方式与我们自己的公式相结合；在这里，我们测试哪些在转换为 RL 训练环境时效果最好，首先是在 Qwen 2.5 7B 规模上。Our framework tries to unify the ways previous work benchmarked the notion of code optimization, together with our own formulations; here we test which ones work best when converted into RL-training environments, first at Qwen 2.5 7B scale.

  6. 收益如何在不同的优化评估标准之间转移？How do the gains transfer across different optimization evaluation criteria?如果每个优化约束既可以是 RL 训练环境又可以是评估设置，我们会问是否存在一种通用的良好训练方法，以及是否有一种公式（可以相同或不同）最适合评估和区分这些训练运行。If each optimization constraint can be both an RL training environment and an evaluation setup, we ask whether there is a universally good way to train, and whether one formulation, which can be the same or another, is best at evaluating and discriminating these training runs.

  7. 什么奖励形式可以最大限度地提高正确性和优化的并发收益？What reward shape maximizes concurrent gains in correctness and optimization?我们已经讨论了 RL 环境的构建，但置于其之上的奖励也会改变下游结果。We have discussed the construction of the RL environment, but the reward placed on top of it also changes downstream results.

  8. 如果我们改变模型和/或扩大规模，它还能继续工作吗？如果根据 OOD 数据进行评估呢？If we change the model and/or scale it up, does it keep working? And if evaluated on OOD data?采用 Qwen 2.5 7B 发现的最佳 RL 环境和奖励，我们调查它们是否仍然可以在更大规模和分布外评估数据上发挥作用。Taking the best RL environments and rewards found with Qwen 2.5 7B, we investigate whether they still work at larger scale and on out-of-distribution evaluation data.

7

  1. 我们是否有公平的实验设置来做出这些主张，或者我们是否在作弊？Do we have a fair experiment setup to make these claims, or are we cheating?我们讨论了整体训练设置、比较的公平性以及如何进一步扩大收益。We discuss the overall training setup, the fairness of the comparisons, and how gains could be further scaled up.

  2. 如果计时环境减慢/加速会发生什么？What happens if the timing environment slows down/speeds up?作为一个潜在的限制，我们探讨了当评估设置（特别是底层计时工具）发生变化时，增益是否仍然有效。As a potential limitation, we explore whether the gains still hold when the evaluation setup, in particular the underlying timing tools, is altered.

  3. 这些收益只是噪音吗？Are these gains just noise?最后，我们使用变异性研究来检查结果的显着性。Finally, we use a variability study to check the significance of the results.

实验设置。Experimental setup.我们使用两种模型尺寸的两个模型系列：7B 和 32B 比例的 Qwen 2.5（Yang 等人，2025）以及 CWM 32B（FAIR CodeGen 团队等人，2025）。我们选择 Qwen 2.5，因为发布的检查点是预推理基础模型，这让我们可以在优化 RL 之前控制两种尺寸显示的推理轨迹：我们在由 OpenCodeReasoning-2 和 OpenMathReasoning 构建的仅公共推理混合上应用一个 epoch 的 SFT（NVIDIA，2025；Moshkov 等人，2025），并根据 DMC、LCB、和 BigO(Bench)（Li 等人，2022；Jain 等人，2024；Chambon 等人，2025）。这种净化包括我们构建的 DMC-Optim 测试集和用于 RL 的训练提示。因此，在 RL 过程中，Qwen 模型应该首次推理这些问题（从某种意义上说，它们在这些问题上不是 SFT，尽管示例仍然可能出现在更广泛的互联网预训练数据中），并仅从问题陈述中探索可能的优化，而不是在先前的监督暴露中进行搜索。We use two model families across two model sizes: Qwen 2.5 (Yang et al., 2025) at 7B and 32B scale, and CWM 32B (FAIR CodeGen team et al., 2025). We choose Qwen 2.5 because the released checkpoints are pre-reasoning base models, which lets us control the reasoning trajectories shown to both sizes before optimization RL: we apply one epoch of SFT on a public reasoning-only mix built from OpenCodeReasoning-2 and OpenMathReasoning (NVIDIA, 2025; Moshkov et al., 2025), and decontaminate the SFT data against DMC, LCB, and BigO(Bench) (Li et al., 2022; Jain et al., 2024; Chambon et al., 2025). This decontamination includes the DMC-Optim test set we build and the training prompts used for RL. Therefore, during RL, the Qwen models should be reasoning about these problems for the first time (in the sense that they were not SFT on these problems, though examples could still appear somewhere in their broader internet pretraining data) and exploring possible optimizations from the problem statement only, rather than searching inside a prior supervised exposure.

CWM 32B 的情况有所不同，它从已为推理 RL 准备的已发布的 SFT 检查点开始，因此我们不控制其 SFT 数据。 CWM 作者报告了针对 LCB 的去污，但我们不能对 DMC-Optim 测试或此处使用的 RL 训练提示做出相同的声明。由于 CWM SFT 使用 OpenCodeReasoning 的一次传递，因此任何重叠问题都可能出现在经过验证的 R1 代中，而不是定义我们的 DMC-Optim 时序排名的人类参考解决方案中。他们的中期训练数据还包括基于 DMC 问题构建的跟踪数据，使用 Llama 3.1 70B 代通过一些基本 DMC 测试进行跟踪。因此，CWM 32B 可能没有看到用于 DMC-Optim 评估和排名的真实人类解决方案（从某种意义上说，我们不希望这些人类参考成为其 SFT 或中期训练数据的一部分，尽管示例仍然可能出现在更广泛的互联网预训练数据中），并且没有收到优化反馈。这意味着使用 CWM 32B 的 RL 会询问模型是否可能已经知道一些问题陈述并暴露于采样生成的解决方案（尽管是无序且没有优化信号监督）是否可以学习使用优化标准搜索该空间，并可能发现暴露数据中不存在的新优化技巧。Things are different for CWM 32B, which starts from a released SFT checkpoint already prepared for reasoning RL, so we do not control its SFT data. The CWM authors report decontamination against LCB, but we cannot make the same claim for DMC-Optim test or for the RL training prompts used here. Since CWM SFT uses one pass over OpenCodeReasoning, any overlapping problems likely appear with verified R1 generations rather than with the human reference solutions that define our DMC-Optim timing rankings. Their midtraining data also includes tracing data built on top of DMC problems, using Llama 3.1 70B generations traced with some of the base DMC tests. CWM 32B therefore probably did not see the ground-truth human solutions used for DMC-Optim evaluation and ranking (in the sense that we do not expect those human references to be part of its SFT or midtraining data, though examples could still appear somewhere in broader internet pretraining data), and did not receive optimization feedback. This means that RL with CWM 32B instead asks whether a model that may already know some problem statements and was exposed to sampled generated solutions, though unordered and without optimization-signal supervision, can learn to search this space with an optimization criterion, and potentially discover new optimization tricks not present in the exposed data.

对于 Qwen SFT 阶段，所有 GPU 都是训练器：7B 运行使用 16 个 H100 节点（128 个 GPU，张量并行度 4），32B 运行使用 32 个 H100 节点（256 个 GPU，张量并行度 8），两种情况下数据并行度均为 32。两者都使用序列长度 32,768、每个数据并行秩批量大小 2、无梯度累积、学习率 8For the Qwen SFT stage, all GPUs are trainers: the 7B run uses 16 H100 nodes (128 GPUs, tensor parallelism 4) and the 32B run uses 32 H100 nodes (256 GPUs, tensor parallelism 8), with data parallelism 32 in both cases. Both use sequence length 32,768, per-data-parallel-rank batch size 2, no gradient accumulation, and learning rate 8. 6 × 10−_6经过 1k 步热身后，给出 32after a 1k-step warmup, giving 32× 2 × 32 , 768 = 2 , 097 ,_ 每个优化器步骤 152 个令牌。超过 24,829 个步骤，这是 52.1B 打包训练令牌，对应于仅推理组合中的 43.5B 原始未填充令牌。,_ 152 tokens per optimizer step. Over 24,829 steps, this is 52.1B packed training tokens, corresponding to 43.5B raw unpadded tokens from the reasoning-only mix.

主要 RL 运行使用 10,000 个优化器步骤，温度 1.0 采样，无 top-Main RL runs use 10,000 optimizer steps, temperature 1.0 sampling with no top-p截断，学习率 1truncation, learning rates 1× 10−_7对于 7B 和 1for 7B and 1. 4 × 10−_7对于 32B，部署节点和训练节点之间的比例为 50/50。 7B RL 运行使用 8 个 NVIDIA H100 节点大约一天，而 32B RL 运行使用 32 个 NVIDIA H100 节点大约一天半。尽管两个模型大小使用不同的并行化方案，但最终数据并行度均为 16，本地批次上限为 32,000 个令牌，并且没有跨批次的轨迹分割。每个提示有 16 个样本，每次部署平均有 10,000 个令牌，这为每个全局批次提供了大约 480,000 个令牌，或者每个优化器步骤的 16 个轨迹大约存在三个问题。由于 DMC-Optim 训练包含大约 1,000 个问题，因此 10,000 个训练步骤对应于整个训练集的大约 30 次传递。更多设置细节在 F 部分中分享。for 32B, and a 50/50 split between rollout and training nodes. A 7B RL run uses 8 NVIDIA H100 nodes for about one day, while a 32B RL run uses 32 NVIDIA H100 nodes for about a day and a half. Although the two model sizes use different parallelization schemes, both end up with data parallelism 16, local batches capped at 32,000 tokens, and no trajectory splitting across batches. With 16 samples per prompt and a typical average of 10,000 tokens per rollout, this gives about 480,000 tokens per global batch, or roughly three problems with their 16 trajectories per optimizer step. Since DMC-Optim train contains about 1,000 problems, 10,000 training steps correspond to roughly 30 passes over the full training set. More setup details are shared in Section F.

重播评价。Replayed evaluation.我们在两种机制中评估优化强化学习，并在讨论中将它们分开。 DMC-Optim 测试是域内基准测试，用于衡量模型是否生成正确的代码，并且还满足严格的人类速度百分位数阈值。 LiveCodeBench (LCB)（Jain 等人，2024）是分布外传输检查，我们根据标准 RLVR 基线报告 pass@1 和成对速度获胜率。正如 A.1 和 A.3 节所示，LCB 测试太小且太快，无法支持可靠的测试。We evaluate optimization RL in two regimes and keep them separate in the discussion. DMC-Optim test is the in-domain benchmark for measuring whether a model produces correct code that also meets strict human-speed percentile thresholds. LiveCodeBench (LCB) (Jain et al., 2024) is the out-of-distribution transfer check, where we report both pass@1 and pairwise speed win rates against the standard-RLVR baseline. As Sections A.1 and A.3 show, LCB tests are too small and fast to support reliable

8

每次测试超时扫描：更改超时阈值几乎不会移动信号，而获胜率仍然更加稳健，因为它会比较所有测试的累积持续时间。为了节省计算使用量，并尝试减少重新运行时的潜在噪音，对于每个评估步骤，我们首先运行 LLM 生成，并对每个问题采样 20 个解决方案。然后，我们在共享 CES 运行中运行计时：所有问题的测试和所有模型转储都会同时进行洗牌和执行，并且包含人工参考解决方案来校准评估运行的持续时间。只有在此计时通过之后，我们才会通过不同的百分位数阈值、时间预算、仿射校准和 LCB 获胜率比较来重放存储的每次测试结论和持续时间，而无需重新运行模型或计时沙箱。per-test timeout sweeps: changing timeout thresholds moves little signal, while win rate remains more robust because it compares accumulated durations across all tests. In order to spare compute usage, and try to mitigate potential noise across re-runs, for each evaluation step, we first run LLM generation and sample 20 solutions per problem. We then run timing in shared CES runs: tests from all problems and all model dumps are shuffled and executed concurrently, and human-reference solutions are included to calibrate durations across evaluation runs. Only after this timing pass do we replay the stored per-test verdicts and durations through different percentile thresholds, time budgets, affine calibrations, and LCB win-rate comparisons without re-running either the model or the timing sandbox.

首先，所有这些工作都是必要的吗？探索基线。Is all of this work necessary in the first place? Exploring baselines.在比较不同类型的优化 RL 环境之前，我们首先检查标准 RLVR 或直接计时奖励是否已经实现了良好的代码优化性能；如果是这样，那么改进数据集、沙箱和奖励的工作就没有必要了。如表 1 所示，第一个参考是 Qwen 2.5 7B 上的标准 RLVR：它仅使用基础测试，奖励正确性，并且在 10k RL 步骤后达到 43.5% pass@1 atBefore comparing different types of optimization RL environments, we first check whether standard RLVR or direct timing rewards already achieve good code-optimization performance; if so, the work on refining the dataset, sandbox, and rewards would be unnecessary. As seen in Table 1, the first reference is standard RLVR on Qwen 2.5 7B: it uses the base tests only, rewards correctness, and after 10k RL steps reaches 43.5% pass@1 atp100，对应于无限超时评估的正确性。然后性能急剧下降：性能下降近 60%100, which corresponds to correctness with an infinite-timeout evaluation. This then degrades sharply: performance drops by almost 60% atp50，该解决方案必须位于该问题的人类解决方案排行榜的上半部分。添加我们生成的正确性和优化测试，可以减少误报并提供更长的执行时间，将 pure pass@1 提高到 46.9%50, where the solution must rank in the top half of the human-solution leaderboard for the problem. Adding our generated correctness and optimization tests, which reduce false positives and provide longer executions, improves pure pass@1 to 46.9% atp100，但更严格的阈值性能没有大幅变化：100, but the stricter-threshold performance does not move substantially:p50 的相对收益仅为 14%50 sees only a 14% relative gain andp30 比标准 RLVR 相对增益 21%。30 a 21% relative gain over standard RLVR.

然后，我们探索了直接的时间奖励：在正确的解决方案中，执行速度越快，获得的奖励就越高。对于每个解决方案，我们都会测量测试的平均运行时间，并仅在解决方案正确时分配按比例的奖励。这并不能解决问题并导致令人失望的结果：在基础测试中，更严格的百分位数性能几乎没有提高，分别达到 18.9% 和 18.0% pass@1We then explored a direct timing reward: among correct solutions, faster executions receive higher rewards. For each solution, we measure the average runtime across tests and assign a proportional reward only when the solution is correct. This does not resolve the problem and leads to disappointing results: on base tests, stricter-percentile performance barely improves, reaching 18.9% and 18.0% pass@1 atp两种奖励塑造下为 50%，而标准 RLVR 为 18.0%。我们认为，除了奖励形状不完善之外，这主要是因为基础测试不太适合任何形式的优化反馈。50 under the two reward shapings, compared to 18.0% for standard RLVR. We believe that, beyond the poorly refined reward shape, this is mainly because the base tests are poorly suited to any form of optimization feedback.

使用优化测试给出了更令人满意的信号：相对于基础测试中的标准 RLVR，直接计时奖励将 pass@1 提高了 21%Using the optimization tests gives a more satisfying signal: relative to standard RLVR on base tests, direct timing rewards improve pass@1 by up to 21% atp50% 和 44%50 and 44% atp30. 如果我们通过与已经使用生成的正确性和优化测试的 RLVR 参考进行比较来隔离奖励变化，情况会更加微妙：在该比较中，最好的30. The picture is more nuanced if we isolate the reward change by comparing to the RLVR reference that already uses the generated correctness and optimization tests: in that comparison, the bestp50 的增益仅为 5%，并且在50 gain is only 5%, and it comes with an 8% relative degradation atp100. 因此，我们用一些纯粹正确性性能来换取更严格的百分位性能，并在翻译中损失了一些总的聚合性能。郑等人进一步探讨了这种权衡。 (2026)，他们表明代码强化学习方法暴露了正确性-效率边界，而不是单一的单调改进方向。增加的优化测试确实提供了优化信号，但需要做更多的工作才能使 RL 将正确性和效率结合在一起。此时，当数据未准备好用于优化反馈时，天真的原始持续时间奖励要么会失败，要么只能通过权衡正确性和效率来发挥作用。不幸的是，最强的基线仍然是标准 RLVR，并改进了基础数据。100. We therefore trade some pure-correctness performance for stricter-percentile performance, and lose some of the total aggregated performance in the translation. This trade-off is further explored by Zheng et al. (2026), who show that code RL methods expose correctness-efficiency frontiers rather than a single monotonic improvement direction. The added optimization tests do provide optimization signal, but more work is needed to make RL push correctness and efficiency together. At this point, the naive raw-duration reward either fails when the data is not prepared for optimization feedback, or works only by trading off correctness for efficiency. Unfortunately, the strongest baseline remains standard RLVR with improved underlying data.

GRPO 对于稀疏、嘈杂的计时奖励是否足够稳定？Can GRPO be stable enough for sparse, noisy timing rewards?基础测试的标准 RLVR 显示了经过训练的 LLM 的优化能力有限：正确性有所提高，但模型仍然损失了近 60%Standard RLVR on base tests shows the limited optimization capability of post-trained LLMs: correctness improves, but the model still loses almost 60% fromp100 至100 top50. 我们表明，改进底层 RL 数据已经有助于 RLVR 的优化能力。我们工作的目标是找到环境和奖励公式，使我们能够从改进的数据中获得更多收益，特别是当上面的直接计时奖励没有带来预期结果时。在启动 RL 运行并探索我们的候选环境之前，我们查看了 GRPO 本身，即最终将任何这些标量奖励转化为模型权重变化的更新规则：我们需要确保潜在的更好的优化 RL 环境不会被不良的损失聚合和固有的噪声优化器步骤所掩盖。50. We showed that improving the underlying RL data already helps optimization capabilities with RLVR. The goal of our work is to find the environment and reward formulation that allows us to squeeze more gains from that improved data, especially as the direct timing reward above did not bring the expected results. Before launching RL runs and exploring our candidate environments, we took a look at GRPO itself, the update rule that ultimately turns any of these scalar rewards into model-weight changes: we need to make sure that potentially better optimization RL environments do not get shadowed by bad loss aggregation and inherently noisy optimizer steps.

第 4 节中的离线模拟器表明，即使在沙箱和计时工具改进之后，我们的候选环境仍然与嘈杂的计时测量相关，并给出稀疏的奖励。与仅正确性 RLVR 相比，正确性加优化奖励具有更少的有用的 rollout 和更多的零优势组（当所有的 rollout 获得相同的奖励时，或者在 RL 问题太简单时全部为 1，或者在我们的情况下更频繁地为所有The offline simulator in Section 4 shows that even after the sandbox and timing tool improvements, our environment candidates remain tied to noisy timing measurements and give sparse rewards. Compared with correctness-only RLVR, correctness-plus-optimization rewards have fewer useful rollouts and more zero-advantage groups (when all rollouts get the same reward, either all 1 when the RL problem is too easy, or more frequently in our case all−1 当任何一次部署都无法解决该问题（至少是部分解决）时；例如，在 Qwen 2.5 7B 运行中，训练开始时的零优势上下文率可能约为1 when not a single rollout manages to solve it, at least partially); on a Qwen 2.5 7B run, for example, the zero-advantage-context rate at the beginning of training can be around

40-50% 用于二元优化奖励，如我们评估的奖励。沙箱状态在并发 CES 负载下也可能会发生变化，因此执行的计时测量的范围可能会发生变化，因此即使模型策略没有改变，在不同时间收集的两个奖励也不完全可比：我们希望避免为理论上比另一个优化程度较低的首次部署提供更多奖励，仅仅是因为沙箱状态更有利于第一次部署并使其运行得更快。40–50% for binary optimization rewards like the ones we evaluate. The sandbox state can also vary under concurrent CES load, and therefore the ranges of the timing measurements that are performed can shift, so two rewards collected at different times are not perfectly comparable even if the model policy has not changed: we want to avoid giving more reward to a rollout that is in theory less optimized than another one, simply because the sandbox state was more favorable for the first rollout and made it run faster.

由于这些原因，下面的所有优化 RL 运行都使用相同的稳定训练配方。我们保留标准的 async-GRPO 结构（我们在 F 节中详细介绍），但改进了以下几点：For these reasons, all optimization RL runs below use the same stabilized training recipe. We keep a standard async-GRPO structure (that we detail in Section F), but improve the following points:

  1. 我们首先增加工作端的相同提示推出的数量：这有助于解决零优势问题，并使运行更快地达到有用的更新，同时丢弃更少的数据批次；它还提高了优势计算中使用的蒙特卡罗估计的质量，这在个体奖励可能有噪音时很有帮助。这减少了每个全局训练批次出现的问题数量，但也减少了 RL 运行的挂钟持续时间。We first increase the number of same-prompt rollouts on the worker side: this helps with zero-advantage problems and makes runs reach useful updates faster while dropping fewer batches of data; it also improves the quality of the Monte Carlo estimates used in the advantage computation, which is helpful when individual rewards can be noisy. This reduces the number of problems seen per global trainer batch, but decreases the wall-clock duration of the RL runs.

  2. 在训练器方面，我们还增加了训练批量大小，在不同的优化 RL 环境中将 RL 运行的纯正确性性能提高了 10-25%，优化性能在On the trainer side, we also increase the training batch size, improving pure-correctness performance in RL runs by 10–25% across different optimization RL environments, optimization performance atp50 提升高达 35%，优化性能为50 by up to 35%, and optimization performance atp30 最高可达 60%。这与以下事实是一致的：太小的批次在时序噪声下容易产生不稳定的梯度估计，而增大这些批次有助于平滑它。30 by up to 60%. This is consistent with the fact that batches that are too small become prone to unstable gradient estimates under timing noise, and enlarging these batches helps smooth it out.

  3. 根据 GRPO 博士的分析，标准差归一化可能会引入难度偏差（Liu 等人，2025），我们将回报居中，但不除以组标准差。这对于基于时间的优化奖励来说更重要：我们不想在模型找不到优化技巧的情况下过度强调提示，或者在单个时间波动产生孤立的奖励差异的情况下的提示，相对于推出暴露各种优化技巧并因此携带更有用的教学信号的提示。Following the Dr. GRPO analysis that standard-deviation normalization can introduce a difficulty bias (Liu et al., 2025), we center returns but do not divide by the group standard deviation. This matters more for timing-based optimization rewards: we do not want to overweight prompts where the model does not find optimization tricks, or prompts where a single timing fluctuation creates an isolated reward difference, relative to prompts where rollouts expose a variety of optimization tricks and therefore carry a more useful teaching signal.

  4. 在问题层面，我们使用令牌加权提示平均值作为优势基线，并通过固定令牌范围对损失进行标准化At the problem level, we use a token-weighted prompt mean for the advantage baseline and normalize the loss by a fixed token horizonN= 32768，而不是按每次推出的长度。令牌加权平均值使全局批次梯度保持中性，因此总的正梯度和负梯度相互抵消。固定范围纠正了每次推出长度归一化的偏差，这不会对长的不正确轨迹进行足够的惩罚，并且会低估成功的深度推理轨迹。这在我们的环境中尤其重要，因为奖励可能非常稀疏，并且模型可能会迷失在不确定的错误推理中。通过这种设置，在训练结束时，模型往往会在简单问题上生成大约 9k 个标记，而困难问题平均会生成大约 16k 个标记。我们没有观察到 RLVR 训练和优化训练在推理长度方面存在特殊差异。= 32768, rather than by each rollout length. The token-weighted mean keeps the global batch gradient neutral, so total positive and negative gradients cancel. The fixed horizon corrects the bias of per-rollout length normalization, which would not penalize long incorrect trajectories enough and would under-reward successful deep-reasoning trajectories. This is especially important in our setting, where rewards can be very sparse and the model can get lost in indefinite incorrect reasoning. With this setting, by the end of training, models tend to generate around 9k tokens on easy problems, while hard problems average around 16k. We do not observe a particular difference between RLVR training and optimization training in reasoning length.

  5. 最后，为了限制过时的时间比较，我们首先调整工人/培训师的比率，以便及时消耗生成的批次：我们更喜欢温和的培训师端过时而不是工人端过时，尽管这Finally, to limit stale timing comparisons, we first tune the worker/trainer ratio so generated batches are consumed promptly: we prefer mild trainer-side staleness over worker-side staleness, although this

10

稍微损害 GPU 利用率，因为排队的工作端批次可能会变得过时，并与在不同沙箱状态下收集的批次混合。除此之外，我们对太旧的上下文进行额外的硬过滤，丢弃早于slightly hurts GPU utilization, because queued worker-side batches can become stale and be mixed with batches collected under different sandbox states. On top of this, we enforce an additional hard filtering of contexts getting too old, discarding contexts older thanSmax = 30 优化器步骤，可提高 5% 的分数。最后，我们避免使用重播缓冲区来重用旧沙箱状态的奖励。max = 30 optimizer steps, which gives a 5% score gain. Finally, we avoid a replay buffer that would reuse rewards from older sandbox states.

哪种优化强化学习环境最有效？What optimization RL environments work best?考虑到目前为止构建的完整管道，目标是找到一个优化 RL 设置，首先在 Qwen 2.5 7B 上，该设置比最强的标准 RLVR 数据基线有所改进：使用我们丰富的正确性和优化测试，该基线在以下位置达到 46.9 pass@1Taking the full pipeline built so far, the goal is to find an optimization RL setup, first on Qwen 2.5 7B, that improves over the strongest standard-RLVR data baseline: using our enriched correctness and optimization tests, this baseline reaches 46.9 pass@1 atp100 和 20.6 时100 and 20.6 atp50. 表 2 报告了我们尝试过的优化感知 RL 环境，按第 4 节和 D.2 节中介绍的环境系列排序。每个环境的详细信息和数学公式在 D.2 节中；总体而言，三个主要环境系列是：50. Table 2 reports the optimization-aware RL environments we tried, sorted by the environment families introduced in Section 4 and Section D.2. The details and mathematical formulations of each environment are in Section D.2; overall, the three main families of environments are:

  1. 预执行环境，通过选择评估生成的解决方案的优化测试来引入优化约束；例如，一种参数化通过人类参考解决方案的平均运行时间来过滤测试。Pre-execution environments, where the optimization constraint is introduced by selecting which optimization tests evaluate the generated solution; one parameterization, for example, filters tests by the mean runtime of human reference solutions.

  2. 内部执行环境，通过超时本身引入优化约束。Intra-execution environments, which introduce the optimization constraint through the timeout itself.

  3. 执行后环境使执行保持完整，然后通过根据人类参考对生成的解决方案进行排名来操纵记录的持续时间。Post-execution environments, which leave execution intact, then manipulate the recorded durations by ranking generated solutions against human references.

我们在几个百分位阈值下报告 pass@1 和 pass@10：We report pass@1 and pass@10 under several percentile thresholds:p100 对应于纯粹正确性评估，而100 corresponds to pure-correctness evaluation, whilep10 要求解决方案既正确又在同一问题的人类参考解决方案中排名前 10%。10 requires solutions to be both correct and in the top 10% of the human reference solutions for the same problem.

在这三个环境系列中，我们探索了三个执行前子系列、三个执行内子系列和两个执行后子系列，每个子系列都通过阈值或百分位数截止进行参数化。我们使用离线模拟器来删除过于退化的配置：太稀疏、太平坦，因为弱和强的解决方案获得相似的奖励，或者太嘈杂。我们还跳过产生几乎相同环境行为的参数化，而是运行一个代表。这给出了表 2 中探索的一组参数化 RL 环境。基于此扫描，什么最有效？Across these three families of environments, we explore three pre-execution subfamilies, three intra-execution subfamilies, and two post-execution subfamilies, each parameterized by thresholds or percentile cutoffs. We use the offline simulator to remove configurations that are too degenerate: too sparse, too flat because weak and strong solutions receive similar rewards, or too noisy. We also skip parameterizations that produce nearly identical environment behavior and run one representative instead. This gives the set of parameterized RL environments explored in Table 2. Based on this sweep, what works best?

By contrast, the strongest rows, in particular the top-30% per-test-percentile post-execution configuration, reach 31.3 atp50 和 19.150 and 19.1 atp30，这大约是 75% 的相对增益30, which is about a 75% relative gain atp50 和 150% 相对增益50 and a 150% relative gain atp超过标准 RLVR 30。30 over standard RLVR.

通过优化测试，相对于 RLVR 的收益仍然很大：同一行有所改进The gains remain substantial against RLVR with optimization tests as well: the same row improvesp50 大约 50% 以及50 by roughly 50% andp30 大约 100%，同时保持在相同的纯粹正确性范围内。温和的30 by roughly 100%, while staying in the same pure-correctness range. The mildp相对于基础测试 RLVR 的 100 改进主要应该被解读为改进的数据效果：当使用我们丰富的正确性和优化测试进行训练时，标准 RLVR 基线已经从 43.5 移动到 46.9。100 improvement over base-test RLVR should mostly be read as an improved-data effect: the standard-RLVR baseline already moves from 43.5 to 46.9 when trained with our enriched correctness and optimization tests.

严格的优化增益也不仅仅是 pass@10-to-pass@1 传递效应，其中 RL 将使第一个样本更接近 10 个样本中的最佳样本，同时保持采样解分布不变。 Pass@10 本身显着提高：基础测试的标准 RLVR 达到 41.1The strict optimization gains are also not only a pass@10-to-pass@1 transfer effect, where RL would make the first sample closer to the best sample among ten while leaving the sampled solution distribution unchanged. Pass@10 itself improves substantially: standard RLVR on base tests reaches 41.1 atp50 和 7.4 时50 and 7.4 atp10，而每次测试百分位数前 30% 的执行后配置达到 56.1 和 18.6。这表明该模型改变了采样解分布并学习新的优化行为，而不是仅仅将 pass@1 与标准 RLVR 已经引入的优化知识更好地对齐。10, while the top-30% per-test-percentile post-execution configuration reaches 56.1 and 18.6. This suggests that the model changes the sampled solution distribution and learns new optimization behavior, rather than only better aligning pass@1 with optimization knowledge already induced by standard RLVR.

在原本有希望的系列中，有一个明显失败了：相对超时执行内变体提高了更严格的百分位数，但它们的Among the families that could have been promising, one fails noticeably: relative-timeout intra-execution variants improve stricter percentiles, but theirp100 个纯粹正确性分数下降了大约 17-27%。因此，它们不会在不损害正确性的情况下提高优化能力。这是一种沿着正确性-效率帕累托边界移动而不是打破它的优化强化学习环境。其他环境落在边界的另一侧：100 pure-correctness scores drop by roughly 17–27%. They therefore do not improve optimization capability without hurting correctness. This is the kind of optimization RL environment that moves along the correctness-efficiency Pareto frontier rather than breaking it. 保留了正确性，但提供了与带有优化测试的标准 RLVR 几乎相同的严格百分位数配置文件。尽管环境被制定为暴露优化约束，但它并没有使该约束对于训练足够有用。preserves correctness, but gives almost the same strict-percentile profile as standard RLVR with optimization tests. Although the environment is formulated to expose an optimization constraint, it does not make that constraint useful enough for training.

表 2 中的大多数环境配置都使用二进制折叠奖励来包含正确性和优化约束。我们将在本节后面讨论奖励本身的形式，超越根环境的制定。Most environment configurations in Table 2 use the binary collapsed reward to include both the correctness and optimization constraints. We discuss the form of the rewards themselves later in this section, beyond the root environment formulation.

基于这次扫描，我们为后续实验保留了四种环境设置：2秒绝对预执行过滤器、排名最差的超时Based on this sweep, we retain four environment settings for the follow-up experiments: the 2 s absolute pre-execution filter, the ranked-worst timeout fromp80、以及80, and thep50 和50 andp每次测试百分位执行后奖励 30 个。在 Qwen 2.5 7B 上，这些设置在严格的百分位数上达到了大致相似的改进，但在纯粹正确性方面有不同的权衡。因此，我们接下来要问的是，它们在其他模型和尺寸上是否仍然保持良好的强化学习设置，以及在其他基准上评估时它们的收益是否会转移。30 per-test-percentile post-execution rewards. On Qwen 2.5 7B, these settings reach broadly similar improvements across strict percentiles, with different tradeoffs in pure correctness. We therefore next ask whether they remain good RL setups across other models and sizes, and whether their gains transfer when evaluated on other benchmarks.

收益如何在不同的优化评估标准之间转移？How do the gains transfer across different optimization evaluation criteria?在表2中，不同的训练环境以不同的方式插入优化约束，但在单个评估系列下进行评估：排行榜-百分位执行后排名，在In Table 2, the different training environments insert the optimization constraint in different ways, but are evaluated under a single evaluation family: leaderboard-percentile post-execution ranking, atp 100, p 50, p30、和30, andp10. 图 4 首先概括了所有十分位数的这一观点，比较了 10k RL 步骤后的绝对性能和从 1k 到 10k 的相对改进。我们使用 1k 而不是共享 SFT 模型作为参考点，因为前 1,000 个 RL 步骤主要用于学习推理格式，之后任务级探索和利用变得更加明显。标准 RLVR 在十分位数上显然表现不佳。10. Figure 4 first generalizes this view across all deciles, comparing both absolute performance after 10k RL steps and relative improvement from 1k to 10k. We use 1k rather than the shared SFT model as the reference point because the first 1,000 RL steps mostly account for learning the reasoning format, after which task-level exploration and exploitation become more visible. Standard RLVR is clearly under-performing across deciles.

相对增益面板将两个运行系列分开。一些环境在纯粹正确性方面产生了良好的收益，并且仅部分转移到更严格的十分位数，随着评估标准的收紧，转移幅度会减小；这些在右侧面板中显示为对角曲线。其他环境在纯粹正确性方面产生了良好的收益，并随着十分位数的减少而保持甚至改进这些收益，直到排名标准最终变得过于严格并且绝对性能下降。这些显示为更凹的曲线。其中，有些用纯粹的正确性来换取优化性能，可见较低The relative-gain panel separates two families of runs. Some environments produce good gains in pure correctness and only partial transfer to stricter deciles, with transfer magnitude decreasing as the evaluation criterion tightens; these appear as diagonal curves in the right panel. Other environments produce good gains in pure correctness and maintain, or even improve, these gains as the decile decreases, until the ranking criterion eventually becomes too strict and absolute performance falls. These appear as more concave curves. Among them, some trade pure correctness for optimization performance, visible as lowerpRW等100个起点100 starting points such as RWp20.其他，如QP20. Others, such as QPp 30/ p50（执行后百分位数排名）和 Abs 2s（执行前绝对过滤），与以正确性为导向的运行一样提高纯粹正确性，并在曲线开始减小之前通过大约十分位数值的前半部分保持该水平或改进它。的50 (per-test-percentile post-execution ranking) and Abs 2s (pre-execution absolute filtering), improve pure correctness as much as the correctness-oriented runs and keep that level, or improve it, through roughly the first half of decile values before the curve starts to diminish. Thep50 十分位看起来像是一个拐点，大多数跑步开始受到更严格的排名标准的影响，并且转移更少。我们相信这些曲线提供了学习平衡程度的有用视图：在理想情况下，随着标准收紧，绝对性能曲线将在顶部附近保持平坦。 Abs 0.5s 似乎处于过渡状态，介于主要学习正确性的运行和在正确性和优化之间进行更平衡学习的运行之间。50 decile looks like an inflection point where most runs begin to suffer from the harder ranking criterion and transfer less. We believe these curves provide a useful view of how balanced the learning is: in the ideal case, the absolute-performance curve would stay flat near the top as the criterion tightens. Abs 0.5s appears to be in a transition regime, between runs that mostly learn correctness and runs with more balanced learning across correctness and optimization.

环境，绝对超时从 10 秒到 0environments, absolute timeouts from 10 s to 0.1 s，以及多个预执行过滤器。因为这会产生大量分数，所以我们将整个网格总结为逐个评估的热图。在执行前、执行中和执行后评估标准中，稳定的顶级组保持在最佳分数附近：QP1 s, and multiple pre-execution filters. Because this produces a large number of scores, we summarize the full grid as a train-by-eval heatmap. Across pre-execution, intra-execution, and post-execution evaluation criteria, a stable top group remains near the best score: QPp50 位于 58/60 评估列中最佳行的 1 分以内，QP50 is within one pass@1 point of the best row in 58/60 evaluation columns, QPp52/60 中为 30，52/60 中 Abs 2 秒，54/60 中 TL 绝对 0.5 秒。相比之下，RW30 in 52/60, Abs 2s in 52/60, and TL abs 0.5s in 54/60. 评价标准的选择对于排行榜的中游最为重要。强大的配置几乎在任何地方都保持强大，而较弱的配置通常在接近家庭训练环境的标准下看起来更好。例如，RWThe choice of evaluation criterion matters most for the middle of the leaderboard. Strong configurations stay strong almost everywhere, while weaker configurations often look better under criteria close to their home training environment. For example, RWp20、相对过滤器20 and relative-filterp30 会交换许多排行榜位置，具体取决于我们是否在排名最差的执行内超时或相对执行前过滤下进行评估。现在看看评估标准，热图有助于识别表现相似的标准：排名最差和执行后排名在训练配置上产生几乎相同的排行榜（30 exchange many leaderboard positions depending on whether we evaluate under ranked-worst intra-execution timeouts or relative pre-execution filtering. Looking now at the evaluation criteria, the heatmap helps identify the ones that behave similarly: ranked-worst and post-execution ranking induce almost the same leaderboard over training configurations (ρ ≃ 0 .第995章），解释为什么RW995), explaining why RWp80 相当好地转移到表 2 中使用的执行后排名；字符长度和相对预执行过滤器的行为也类似（80 transfers reasonably well to the post-execution ranking used in Table 2; char-length and relative pre-execution filters behave similarly as well (ρ ≃ 0 . 975).

因此，这种交叉评估有助于确定什么是有用的优化评估器。我们想要一个评估器，它既能区分固定参数值下的训练运行，又能随着参数的变化跨越广泛的难度。通过这种诊断，排行榜百分位执行后排名是最干净的选择：它在训练行中的家庭平均分布是 13.5 pass@1 点，并且它的参数扫描跨度约为 40 点This cross-evaluation therefore helps identify what makes a useful optimization evaluator. We want an evaluator that both discriminates training runs at a fixed parameter value and spans a broad range of difficulty as the parameter changes. By this diagnostic, leaderboard-percentile post-execution ranking is the cleanest choice: its family-average spread across training rows is 13.5 pass@1 points, and its parameter sweep spans about 40 points fromp100 至100 top10，证明了我们在表 2 中使用这个评估标准的后验理由。绝对、字符长度和相对预执行过滤器区分运行良好，分布在 15.5-15.8 点左右，但它们的参数扫描更窄，大约 6-7 点。相对超时评估具有相反的失败模式：随着阈值收紧，它跨越 35.1 个点，但平均仅将训练行分开 2.3 个点，因此它主要改变全局难度，而没有明确对训练模型进行排名。仔细观察，情况会更糟，因为明显的难度分布主要来自于10, justifying a posteriori why we use this evaluation criterion in Table 2. Absolute, character-length, and relative pre-execution filters discriminate runs well, with spreads around 15.5–15.8 points, but their parameter sweeps are narrower, around 6–7 points. Relative-timeout evaluation has the opposite failure mode: it spans 35.1 points as the threshold tightens, but only separates training rows by 2.3 points on average, so it mostly changes global difficulty without clearly ranking trained models. Looking more closely, this is even worse, as the apparent difficulty spread mostly comes from ap100 个设置，消除了优化约束并仍然正确评估正确性；一旦相对超时约束处于活动状态，所有参数化的分数都会变得平坦且较低。同一家庭的培训环境较弱：其三项培训政策在每个家庭平均评估中均排名倒数五位，100 setting that removes the optimization constraint and still evaluates correctness correctly; once the relative-timeout constraint is active, scores become flat and low across all parameterizations. The same family is weak as a training environment: its three trained policies fall in the bottom five under every family-averaged evaluator,

For this reason, we mainly further explore the differences between the first three.

什么奖励形式可以最大化正确性和优化的并发收益？Whatrewardshapemaximizesconcurrentgainsincorrectnessandoptimization?表 3 采用最有前途的环境配置 - Abs 2s 预执行过滤和每次测试百分位数Table 3 takes the most promising environment configurations—Abs 2s pre-execution filtering and per-test-percentilep 30 /p50 执行后排名 - 并探索了如第 4 节和第 D.4 节中介绍的几种奖励形式，以平衡环境产生的三个共享信号：正确性、优化门和分级优化质量。如表结果所示，我们观察到仅设置优化目标会导致策略完全崩溃，而相应的标准 RLVR 在正确的测试中保持了良好的正确性，并且已经显示出优化指标有所改善的迹象。将这两个目标联合训练作为多任务强化学习运行效果并没有好多少，并且无法突破正确性优化的帕累托边界。加法混合是强化学习中用于其他任务的一种方法，但仍然令人失望：通过这种奖励形状，快速但错误的代码片段仍然可以在训练期间获得部分奖励，这会牺牲大量的纯正确性性能来换取体面但有限的优化收益。跟随郑等人。 （2026），有人可以说，在权重空间中，单独的正确性训练和单独的优化训练可以线性组合，以获得接近直接在组合任务上训练的策略。这反过来最终会导致正确性优化帕累托前沿，这就是我们在多任务和混合奖励实践中观察到的情况。混合奖励仍然在每个优势计算中包含两个目标，这有细微差别，我们相信这有助于解释为什么结果模型略高于多任务变体，但我们没有观察到它们中的任何一个出现重大帕累托断裂。50 post-execution ranking—and explores, as introduced in Section 4 and Section D.4, several reward forms to balance the three shared signals produced by the environments: correctness, the optimization gate, and the graded optimization quality. As shown in the table results, we observe that putting only an optimization objective leads to complete policy collapse, while the counterpart standard RLVR on the right tests keeps good correctness and already shows signs of improvement on optimization metrics. Training on these two objectives jointly as a multitask RL run does not work much better, and fails to break the Pareto frontier of correctness–optimization. Additive blending, an approach used in RL for other tasks, remains disappointing: with this reward shape, snippets of code that are fast but wrong can still receive partial reward during training, which trades a large amount of pure-correctness performance for decent but limited optimization gains. Following Zheng et al. (2026), one can argue that, in weight space, training for correctness alone and training for optimization alone can be linearly combined to obtain a policy close to one trained directly on the combined task. Which in turn ends up navigating a correctness–optimization Pareto front, which is what we observe in practice for multitask and blending rewards. With the nuance that the blended reward still includes both objectives in each advantage computation, which we believe helps explain why the resulting models sit slightly above the multitask variant, but we do not observe a major Pareto break for either of them.

我们的实验强调了两种可以打破这一局面的奖励方案：要么是门控目标，要么是单个折叠目标。对于双门奖励，除了二进制和分桶公式之外，我们还探索了一些连续等价物，其中二进制正确性信号分配强制Our experiments underline two reward formulations that can break this front: either a gating objective, or a single collapsed objective. For two-gate rewards, we explored, beyond the binary and bucketed formulations, some continuous equivalents, where the binary correctness signal assigns a forced−1 对不正确的代码进行奖励，当代码正确时，允许在 0-1 范围内连续优化信号。相对于分桶奖励，连续信号会损害优化性能，我们认为这是因为分桶在达到梯度步骤之前过滤掉了部分定时测量噪声。将基于持续优化的信用范围降低到更高的值也不会进一步提高正确性，反而会损害较低的百分位数分数。1 reward to incorrect code and, when the code is correct, allows a continuous optimization signal in the range 0–1. The continuous signal hurts optimization performance relative to bucketed rewards, which we believe is because bucketing filters out part of the timing-measurement noise before it reaches the gradient steps. Reducing the continuous optimization-based credit range to higher values does not further boost correctness either, and instead hurts lower-percentile scores.

最后，在分桶奖励和二元奖励之间、跨双门和折叠公式以及跨不同的优化 RL 环境，二元方法始终如一地提高了所有百分位数（例外情况是Finally, between bucketed and binary rewards, across two-gate and collapsed formulations and across the different optimization RL environments, the binary approach consistently improves all percentiles (exception to be made for thep100 个分桶预执行 Abs 2s 单元，但在更严格的百分位数下其性能要差得多）。这与经典的 DeepSeek-R1 观察相结合，即支持简单的二元奖励有助于避免隐藏奖励黑客攻击（Guo 等人，2025a）。它还证明了表 2 中做出的选择的合理性，即主要报告每个 RL 环境的二进制折叠奖励变体。我们将这些奖励保留为下面的主要变体，同时在分数保持接近二进制分数时跟踪双门和分桶变体。100 bucketed pre-execution Abs 2s cell, but it performs substantially worse at stricter percentiles). This joins the classic DeepSeek-R1 observation that favoring simple binary rewards can help avoid hidden reward hacking (Guo et al., 2025a). It also justifies a posteriori the choice made in Table 2 to mainly report binary collapsed reward variants of each RL environment. We keep these rewards as the main variants below, while tracking two-gate and bucketed variants when their scores remain close to the binary ones.

如果我们改变模型和/或扩大规模，它还能继续工作吗？如果根据 OOD 数据进行评估呢？If we change the model and/or scale it up, does it keep working? And if evaluated on OOD data?表 4 报告了使用执行后排行榜百分位评估环境的跨模型 DMC-Optim 传输，表 5 报告了 LiveCodeBench (LCB) 上的相同模型。正如上面介绍的以及 A.1 和 A.3 节中进一步探讨的，LCB 测试对于稳定的超时式优化评估来说太短，因此针对每个模型系列标准 RLVR 基线的获胜率更适合衡量优化增益。Table 4 reports cross-model DMC-Optim transfer, using the post-execution leaderboard-percentile evaluation environment, and Table 5 reports the same models on LiveCodeBench (LCB). As introduced above and further explored in Sections A.1 and A.3, LCB tests are too short for stable timeout-style optimization evaluation, so win rates against each model-family standard-RLVR baseline are better suited to measure optimization gains.

原来的 Qwen 2.5 7B 设置。在 DMC-Optim 上，较大的模型显示出与 Qwen 2.5 7B 相同的模式：Qwen 2.5 32B 在每次测试百分位排名中排名前 30%，在纯正确性方面有较小的改进，并且在更严格的阈值下有更大的增益，the original Qwen 2.5 7B setting. On DMC-Optim, the larger models show the same pattern as Qwen 2.5 7B: with top-30% per-test-percentile ranking, Qwen 2.5 32B has a small pure-correctness improvement and much larger gains at stricter thresholds, withp50 上升 88%，50 rising by 88%,p30 157%，以及30 by 157%, andp比标准 RLVR 提高 10 254%（尽管值得一提的是10 by 254% over standard RLVR (though it is worth mentioning that thep10 相对增益几乎是不公平的，因为基线 RLVR 根本无法解决此标准）。具有相同环境系列的 CWM 32B 也保持纯正确性稳定并提高10 relative gain is almost unfair as the baseline RLVR can simply not solve this criterion). CWM 32B with the same environment family also keeps pure-correctness stable and improvesp50 乘 64% 以及50 by 64% andp30 增 126%。环境系列的排名在不同规模上是一致的：我们没有观察到在 Qwen 2.5 7B 上运行并在更大规模下崩溃的配置，或者弱小规模配置突然成为最佳大规模运行的配置。我们还恢复了 Qwen 2.5 7B 数据改进结果：更好的数据质量和添加的测试提高了性能，但具有正确奖励的优化 RL 环境可以在顶部提取显着的额外严格百分位增益。30 by 126%. The ranking of environment families is coherent across scales: we do not observe a configuration that works at Qwen 2.5 7B and collapses at larger scale, or a weak small-scale configuration that suddenly becomes the best larger-scale run. We also recover the Qwen 2.5 7B data-improvement results: better data quality and the added tests improve performance, but an optimization RL environment with the right reward extracts significant additional strict-percentile gains on top.

表 5 给出了分布外 LCB 结果。在此基准上，每次测试百分位数的执行后训练，尤其是Table 5 gives the out-of-distribution LCB results. On this benchmark, per-test-percentile post-execution training, especiallyp30 和30 andp50，跨模型给出了较大的中值样本速度获胜率，Qwen 2.5 7B 的获胜率为 68.5%50, gives large median-sample speed win rates across models, from 68.5% for Qwen 2.5 7B withp30 训练 CWM 32B 高达 82.9%30 training up to 82.9% for CWM 32B withp30训练也是如此。在我们的WR下30 training as well. Under our WRm根据定义，这意味着，如果我们从标准 RLVR 模型中采样 20 个解决方案，从优化训练模型中采样 20 个解决方案，按速度保留每一侧的中值通过样本，并在同一问题上对它们进行比较，则优化训练的 CWM 32B 样本在 82.9% 的比较中更快。 WRdefinition, this means that if we sample 20 solutions from the standard-RLVR model and 20 from the optimization-trained model, keep the median passing sample by speed for each side, and compare them on the same problem, the optimization-trained CWM 32B sample is faster in 82.9% of comparisons. WRb相反，询问 20 个样本中最好的通过样本是否更快，这为标准 RLVR 基线在其自己的批次上提供了一个预言机，并捕获偶尔采样快速解决方案的情况（相同的预言机被赋予优化训练模型，但我们期望它对基本模型有更多好处，因为即使其各代的一致性较差，它也可以不时随机采样一个好的优化技巧）。即使在这种更严格的最佳样本比较下，CWM 32B 相对于标准 RLVR 仍达到 66.3–68.1%instead asks whether the best passing sample among 20 is faster, which gives the standard-RLVR baseline an oracle over its own batch and captures cases where it occasionally samples a fast solution (the same oracle is given to the optimization-trained model, but we expect it to benefit the base model more, since even with less consistency over its generations it can randomly sample a good optimization trick from time to time). Even under this stricter best-sample comparison, CWM 32B reaches 66.3–68.1% against standard RLVR for thep 30 /p50 执行后行。这表明标准 RLVR 模型上的推理时间搜索可以恢复一定的速度，但它并不能完全恢复优化 RL 所达到的更快的解决方案模式和新的优化技巧；经过如此训练的模型可以更直接地控制每一代的效率。50 post-execution rows. This suggests that inference-time search over a standard-RLVR model can recover some speed, but it does not fully recover the faster solution modes and new optimization tricks reached by optimization RL; and the so-trained models have more direct control over the efficiency of each of their generations.

关于 LCB 纯粹正确性分数，对于Concerning the LCB pure-correctness scores, for thep执行后第 30 行，pass@1 在 Qwen 2.5 7B 上下降了 3.6 个点，即约 8%，当我们将其扩展到 CWM 32B 时，下降幅度仅为 4%。我们认为这种降级部分是由于 LCB 难题造成的，这些问题比 DMC-Optim 困难得多，因此解决这些问题的任务可能与同时生成优化代码发生冲突。也体现了一个慷慨的评价30 post-execution row, pass@1 decreases by 3.6 points on Qwen 2.5 7B, or about 8%, which reduces to only a 4% decrease as we scale it up to CWM 32B. We believe this degradation is partly due to LCB hard problems, which are much harder than DMC-Optim, and so the task of solving them can conflict with producing optimized code at the same time. It also reflects a generous evaluation
