设置很多有利于优化不佳但正确的解决方案：对于 LCB 正确性数字，我们删除了数据集上的超时，该数据集的测试太短而无法测量优化差异，因此通过@setting much in favor of poorly optimized but correct solutions: for the LCB correctness numbers, we remove timeouts on a dataset whose tests are too short anyway to measure optimization differences, so pass@k只要解决方案保持正确，就不会奖励任何优化技巧。我们没有像 DMC-Optim 那样重建 LCB 测试，并且具有更接近在线编程竞赛的时间限制的版本将更有利于优化训练的模型。相应的 CWM 32B pass@10 分数也表明优化训练模型的纯正确性能力并未真正下降：标准 RLVR 达到 66.3，而执行后前 30% 和前 50% 行达到 65.6 和 66.3。因此，该模型的纯求解能力几乎相当于 pass@10 时的标准 RLVR，尽管其中一些尚未转换为 pass@1；进一步的强化学习训练可以通过将更多的能力转化回单样本的正确性来帮助缓解这种情况。does not reward any optimization trick whatsoever as long as the solution remains correct. We did not rebuild LCB tests as we did for DMC-Optim, and a version with time-limit constraints closer to online programming contests would be substantially more favorable to optimization-trained models. The corresponding CWM 32B pass@10 scores also support that pure-correctness capabilities of optimization-trained models are not really degraded: standard RLVR reaches 66.3, while the top-30% and top-50% post-execution rows reach 65.6 and 66.3. The model’s pure solving capacity is therefore pretty much equivalent to standard RLVR at pass@10, even though some of it is not converted to pass@1 yet; further RL training can help mitigate this by translating more of that capacity back into single-sample correctness.

作为旁注，本节开头的实验设置提到，CWM 32B 在 SFT 期间看到了 DMC 问题陈述，这与 Qwen 2.5 变体不同，Qwen 2.5 变体针对 DMC-Optim 训练和测试提示对其 SFT 进行了净化（尽管 CWM 32B 暴露是通过生成的解决方案而不是用于分配评估百分位数分数的人类参考）。标准 RLVR 行表明，该模型并未从这种可能的暴露中特别受益：如果没有正确定义的具有优化约束的 RL 环境，该模型似乎无法学习搜索从 SFT 继承​​的知识空间来寻找最优化的现有解决方案。最后，表 6 还报告了最成功环境中的 CWM 32B 奖励构成变体。它证实了 Qwen 2.5 7B 的发现，即折叠的二元奖励是最好的平衡选择，尽管 CWM 32B 差异较小，而且加性二元奖励在最严格的单元格中变得具有竞争力，同时仍然放弃更多的纯粹正确性分数。As a side note, the experimental setup at the beginning of this section mentioned that CWM 32B has seen DMC problem statements during SFT, unlike the Qwen 2.5 variants that were decontaminated for their SFT against the DMC-Optim train and test prompts (though the CWM 32B exposure was through generated solutions rather than the human references used to assign evaluation percentile scores). The standard-RLVR rows show that the model did not particularly benefit from this possible exposure: without a correctly defined RL environment with an optimization constraint, the model does not seem to learn to search its knowledge space inherited from SFT for the most optimized existing solution. Finally, Table 6 also reports CWM 32B reward-composition variants on the most successful environments. It confirms the Qwen 2.5 7B finding that collapsed binary rewards are the best balanced choice, although the CWM 32B differences are smaller and additive binary rewards become competitive in the strictest cells, while still giving up more pure-correctness score.

我们是否有公平的实验设置来做出这些主张，或者我们是否在作弊？Do we have a fair experiment setup to make these claims, or are we cheating?上述所有运行，无论是标准 RLVR 还是优化感知的 RL 环境之一，都在大约 1,000 个提示的相同底层 DMC-Optim 种子集上进行训练，并使用相同的 10,000 个优化器步骤预算。与较大的代码强化学习运行相比，这是一个很小的提示预算：CWM 32B 报告使用 81,000 个代码问题提示进行强化学习（FAIR CodeGen 团队等人，2025 年）。因此，我们将这些实验视为受控的小规模比较，而不是饱和的训练方案。考虑到此处观察到的效果大小，我们预计更大的提示池有助于进一步优化 RL：主要主张不是可能需要规模才能显现的 5% 增益，而是在这个有限设置中已经在更严格的百分位上实现大约 100% 的相对改进或更多。All runs above, whether standard RLVR or one of the optimization-aware RL environments, train on the same underlying DMC-Optim seed set of roughly 1,000 prompts and use the same 10,000 optimizer-step budget. This is a small prompt budget compared with larger code-RL runs: the CWM 32B report uses 81,000 code-problem prompts for RL (FAIR CodeGen team et al., 2025). We therefore read these experiments as a controlled small-scale comparison rather than as a saturated training recipe. Given the effect sizes observed here, we expect larger prompt pools to help optimization RL further: the main claims are not 5% gains that might need scale to become visible, but roughly 100% relative improvements, or more, at stricter percentiles already in this limited setup.

训练动力指向同一个方向。对于 Qwen 2.5 32B，标准 RLVR 基线在 1k 步后开始为 49.2%The training dynamics point in the same direction. For Qwen 2.5 32B, the standard-RLVR baseline starts after 1k steps at 49.2%pDMC-Optim 测试中的 100 pass@1 并以 54.8% 结束，而前 30% 的百分位数运行开始时较低，为 46.1%，但在 10k 步内追赶并达到 55.7%。在更严格的优化阈值下，标准 RLVR 根本不学习优化功能：100 pass@1 on DMC-Optim test and finishes at 54.8%, whereas the top-30% pertest-percentile run starts lower, at 46.1%, but catches up within 10k steps and reaches 55.7%. At stricter optimization thresholds, standard RLVR simply does not learn optimization capabilities:p50 pass@1 从 1k 步后的 22.2% 变为 10k 步后的 21.1%，并且50 pass@1 moves from 22.2% after 1k steps to 21.1% after 10k, andp30 从 10.7% 降至 9.4%。这表明纯粹的正确性强化学习甚至可能会忘记一些从更多样化的后 SFT 模型继承的偶尔有效的样本。相比之下，前 30% 的优化运行从 23.4% 上升到 39.6%30 from 10.7% to 9.4%. This suggests that pure correctness RL may even forget some occasional efficient samples inherited from the more diverse post-SFT model. By contrast, the top-30% optimization run moves from 23.4% to 39.6% atp50，在更严格的百分位数上具有相同的方向。我们在其他模型中观察到相同的结果。50, with the same direction at stricter percentiles. We observe the same findings with the other models.

18

等步比较控制训练器端 GPU 预算：运行在相似大小的批次上执行相同数量的优化器更新。工人方面的差异更大。标准 RLVR 基础测试不进行优化测试 CES 调用；带有生成的优化测试的 RLVR 增加了这些调用，使得获得正奖励变得更加困难； top-30% 优化环境使用相同的 CES 调用，添加少量 CPU 端奖励计算，并使奖励再次变得稀疏一些。在 Qwen 2.5 7B 上，标准 RLVR 跳过 25% 的批次，因为它们的优势为零；添加优化测试会使这一点提高 3 分，而前 30% 的环境又会增加 4 分。在 Qwen 2.5 32B 上，跳批率相对仅增加 9%，而在 CWM 32B 上则下降 10%。后者的发生是因为较大的模型首先性能更高，最终使许多基线提示全部成功；优化强化学习可以将一些过于简单的提示转回有用的训练信号，从而减少浪费的生成。The equal-step comparison controls the trainer-side GPU budget: runs perform the same number of optimizer updates on similarly sized batches. The worker side differs more. Standard RLVR on base tests makes no optimization-test CES calls; RLVR with the generated optimization tests adds those calls and makes it harder to get a positive reward; the top-30% optimization environment uses the same CES calls, adds a small amount of CPU-side reward computation, and makes the reward again a bit sparser. On Qwen 2.5 7B, standard RLVR skips 25% of batches because they have zero advantage; adding optimization tests raises this by 3 points, and the top-30% environment adds another 4 points. On Qwen 2.5 32B, the skipped-batch rate increases by only 9% relative, and on CWM 32B it decreases by 10%. The latter happens because larger models, which are more performant in the first place, end up making many baseline prompts all-success groups; optimization RL can turn some of those otherwise too-easy prompts back into useful training signal, reducing wasted generated rollouts.

在推出生成时间中，相对于基础测试中的标准 RLVR，添加的 CES 调用约占 15% 的开销，但在相同的优化测试中，前 30% 的优化环境与 RLVR 相当：剩余的优化 RL 特定操作是 CPU 端标量计算，在挂钟时间中可以忽略不计。In rollout generation time, the added CES calls account for about 15% overhead relative to standard RLVR on base tests, but the top-30% optimization environment is on par with RLVR on the same optimization tests: the remaining optimization RL-specific operations are CPU-side scalar computations that are negligible in wall-clock time.

将工作人员和培训师放在一起并查看完整的 RL 运行，从基础测试 RLVR 转移到优化测试 RLVR 平均花费约 25% 的挂钟训练时间，而前 30% 的优化 RL 和优化测试 RLVR 之间的差异保持在 5% 以内。如果我们通过提前停止优化 RL 来强制相等的挂钟，结论仍然是：以 7k 步停止 Qwen 2.5 7B top-30% 运行，并将其与 10k 步的标准 RLVR 进行比较Putting workers and trainers together and looking at the full RL runs, moving from base-test RLVR to optimization-test RLVR costs about 25% average wall-clock training time, while the difference between top-30% optimization RL and optimization-test RLVR stays within 5%. If we instead force equal wall-clock by stopping optimization RL earlier, the conclusion remains: stopping the Qwen 2.5 7B top-30% run at 7k steps and comparing it with standard RLVR at 10k keepsp100 次通过@1 与标准杆相同，并且仍在进步100 pass@1 on par, and still improvesp30 pass@1 从 7.7% 到 17.6%，相对增益约为 130%（对其他模型也进行了类似的观察）。30 pass@1 from 7.7% to 17.6%, about a 130% relative gain (and similar observations are made with the other models).

如果计时环境减慢/加速会发生什么？What happens if the timing environment slows down/speeds up?图 6 改变了应用于沙箱记录持续时间的仿射校准，模拟了执行时序在评估时间发生变化时发生的情况。对于插入了 LLM 候选解决方案的参考解决方案的固定排行榜，更改校准持续时间会改变输出分数。在本节的开头，我们介绍了一种评估设置，该设置使用人类参考解决方案来校准评估时记录的持续时间；在这里我们要问的是，如果我们放弃这种校准会发生什么，以及结论是否会改变。从图 6 中可以看出，运行顺序在整个扫描过程中基本保持不变：基础 RLVR 的性能始终比通过优化测试增强的 RLVR 差，而后者本身仍然低于上面选择的性能最佳的优化 RL 环境。Figure 6 varies the affine calibration applied to sandbox-recorded durations, simulating what happens when execution timing shifts at evaluation time. For a fixed leaderboard of reference solutions into which an LLM candidate solution is inserted, changing the calibrated durations changes the output score. At the beginning of this section, we introduced an evaluation setup that uses human reference solutions to calibrate recorded durations at evaluation time; here we ask what happens if we let go of that calibration, and whether the conclusions change. Looking at Figure 6, the ordering of runs is mostly preserved across the sweep: base RLVR consistently performs worse than RLVR augmented with optimization tests, which itself remains below the top-performing optimization RL environments selected above.

在所有子图中，x 轴左侧的值对应于较快的计时环境，右侧的值对应于较慢的环境。随着评估时间沙箱性能下降并测量更慢的持续时间，优化 RL 相对于基础 RLVR 的相对优势增长得比线性更快，而优化测试 RLVR 仅以较慢、大致线性的速度提高。相反，当沙箱变得更快时，即使没有明确的优化技巧，来自基础 RLVR 的许多解决方案也会被归类为参考排行榜上的顶级提交。即便如此，曲线也达到了渐近状态，其中前 30% 的训练模型与优化测试 RLVR 相比保持了约 7% 的改进，与基础 RLVR 相比保持了 15% 的改进。On all subplots, values on the left side of the x-axis correspond to faster timing environments, and values on the right correspond to slower environments. As the evaluation-time sandbox degrades and measures slower durations, the relative advantage of optimization RL over base RLVR grows faster than linearly, whereas optimization-test RLVR improves only at a slower, roughly linear pace. Conversely, when the sandbox is made much faster, many solutions from base RLVR are classified as top submissions on the reference leaderboards even without a clear optimization trick. Even then, the curves reach an asymptotic regime where the top-30% trained model maintains about 7% improvement over optimization-test RLVR and 15% over base RLVR atp50，分别约为 30-35% 和 50-60%50, and roughly 30–35% and 50–60% respectively atp10. 这证实了即使我们通过在不更新排行榜的情况下大幅提高执行速度来欺骗沙箱，优化强化学习仍能保持附加值。在上面报告的表格中，我们的评估设置尝试估计“真实”时序值，因此报告的数字反映了通过将生成的解决方案提交到在线代码竞赛平台等方式平均可以获得的收益。10. This confirms that even when we cheat the sandbox by making execution substantially faster without updating the leaderboard, optimization RL keeps added value. In the tables reported above, our evaluation setup tries to estimate the “true” timing values, so the reported numbers reflect the gains one could expect on average by submitting the generated solutions to an online code competition platform for instance.

这些只是噪音吗？Arethesegainsjustnoise?之前的结果显示，最佳优化 RL 运行相对于标准 RLVR 有着明显的增益。然而，即使有了我们上面描述的数据、沙箱、强化学习环境、GRPO 和评估贡献，这些训练和评估任务仍然特别容易受到噪音的影响。因此，我们尝试估计在训练和评估期间可能出现多少变异性。表 7 报告了训练时间和评估时间的 95% 置信区间半宽度，给出了人们可以在报告的分数周围预期的噪声的数量级。The previous results show clear gains for the best optimization RL runs over standard RLVR. Nevertheless, even with our data, sandbox, RL-environment, GRPO, and evaluation contributions we described above, these trainings and evaluation tasks remain particularly prone to noise. We therefore try to estimate how much variability can appear during training and during evaluation. Table 7 reports training-time and evaluation-time 95% confidence-interval half-widths, to give an order of magnitude of the noise one can expect around the reported scores.

表 7：DMC-Optim 评估的训练时间和测试时间不确定性。Table 7: Training-time and test-time uncertainty for DMC-Optim evaluation.值是单次训练运行得分的 pass@1 点中的 95% 置信区间半宽度。训练时间行通过汇集重复启动的偏差并根据所有训练的偏差方差构建共享的稳健 CI，来估计单次运行的分数可能变化的程度。测试时行使用 302 DMC-Optim 问题的经典引导方法。对于同一测试集上两个单次运行配置之间的成对比较，要了解它们的差异是否具有 95% 置信度的统计意义，请将差异与相应的训练时间 CI 半宽乘以Values are 95% confidence-interval half-widths in pass@1 points for a single training run’s score. Training-time rows estimate how much a single run’s score may vary, by pooling deviations across repeated launches and building a shared robust CI from the variance of deviations across all trainings. Test-time rows use a classic bootstrapping approach of the 302 DMC-Optim problems. For pairwise comparisons between two single-run configurations on the same test set, to know whether their difference is statistically meaningful with 95% confidence, compare the difference with the corresponding training-time CI half-width multiplied by√2（尽管在实践中配对差异往往会消除2 (though in practice paired differences tend to remove the√2 处罚依据Wang (2026))；当评估集不固定时，测试时 CI 会带来额外的不确定性。2 penalty according to Wang (2026)); test-time CIs give the additional uncertainty when the evaluation set is not fixed.

无偏通过@unbiased pass@kChen 等人的估计。 （2021），更重要的是来自用于分配优化分数的测量持续时间以及有限 DMC-Optim 测试集本身。以前的大多数表格报告了多次训练运行和评估（如果有）的平均分数；在这里，我们报告单次训练运行报告分数的 CI，因此平均分数的方差比这些 CI 更小，但这给出了单次运行的最坏情况估计。estimator of Chen et al. (2021), and more importantly from the measured durations used to assign optimization scores and from the finite DMC-Optim test set itself. Most previous tables report averaged scores over multiple training runs and evaluations when available; here we report CIs for single training-run reported scores, so averaged scores would have less variance than these CIs, but this gives a good worst-case estimate of having a single run.

表 7 中的训练时间行汇集了重复启动的配方内偏差，将所有训练配方中的偏差组合起来，以计算单个、更稳健的方差估计和共享 CI。测试时行使用 302 DMC-Optim 问题的经典引导方法：我们通过替换重新采样问题并重新计算聚合分数。根据我们尝试进行的比较，这些数量的使用方式可能有所不同。如果我们比较固定测试集和固定评估轮次上的两次运行，则表 7 中的训练时间绝对半宽乘以Training-time rows in Table 7 pool the within-recipe deviations of repeated launches, combining them across all training recipes to compute a single, more robust variance estimate and shared CI. Test-time rows use a classic bootstrapping approach of the 302 DMC-Optim problems: we resample problems with replacement and recompute the aggregated scores. These quantities can be used differently depending on the comparison we are trying to make. If we compare two runs on a fixed test set and fixed evaluation round, the training-time absolute half-width from Table 7 multiplied by√2给出差值CI的半宽；因此，如果两个模型的得分差异超过该值，则显着性达到 95%。如果检查点是固定的，并且我们想问它们在类似 DMC 问题的一般分布上如何比较，为了能够说“在一般类似 DMC 问题上检查点 A 比检查点 B 更好”，评估时间间隔就是相关尺度。如果我们想知道一种训练方法是否比另一种训练方法更好，一种保守的方法是将两种 CI 结合起来。我们的目标仍然是简单地给出变化的一个数量级，同时保持悲观：我们不计算配对引导间隔，也不进行片面测试来提前假设哪一次运行应该更好。2 gives the half-width of the difference CI; therefore, if the two models’ score difference exceeds that value, it is significant at 95%. If the checkpoints are fixed and we want to ask how they compare on a general distribution of DMC-like problems, to be able to say “checkpoint A is better than checkpoint B on general DMC-like problems”, the evaluation-time interval is the relevant scale. If we want to know whether one training method is better than another in general, a conservative approach is to combine both CIs. Our goal remains to simply give an order of magnitude of the variations while staying pessimistic: we do not compute paired bootstrap intervals, nor one-sided tests that would assume in advance which run should be better.

在此读数下，主要优化 RL 增益仍远高于估计的变异性。在Under this reading, the main optimization RL gains remain well above the estimated variability. Atp50 中，前 30% 的执行后训练模型比标准 RLVR 提高了 19.7 点，比优化测试 RLVR 提高了 14.8 点，即 950, the top-30% post-execution trained model improves over standard RLVR by 19.7 points and over optimization-test RLVR by 14.8 points, which is 9. 9 ×和 7and 7. 4 ×分别是差异的训练时间半宽。即使在训练时间和测试时间 CI 的保守组合下（通过简单地添加方差），半宽也为 4.7 个点the training-time half-width of differences, respectively. Even under a conservative combination of training-time and test-time CIs (by simply adding the variances), the half-width is 4.7 points atp50，所以这些差距仍然是 450, so these gaps remain 4. 2 ×和 3and 3. 1 ×在它上面。在above it. Atp30 点，相应的差距为 17.2 点和 13.8 点，这再次远远超过显着性水平。与标准 RLVR 相比，优化测试 RLVR 增益较小，在测试集不确定性下更接近边界，但如果我们修复测试集，增益仍高于训练时间变异性。30, the corresponding gaps are 17.2 and 13.8 points, which is again well over significance levels. The smaller optimization-test RLVR gain over standard RLVR is more borderline under test-set uncertainty, but if we fix the test set, gains remain above training-time variability.

我们还包括仅 5k RL 步骤后的不确定性。它们大于最终的 10k 间隔，特别是对于严格阈值下的 CWM 32B，这表明 RL 重新运行的性能在中间训练步骤中变化更为显着，尽管随着训练的进行，它们会收敛到更一致的性能水平。我们还注意到测试时间方差We also include uncertainties after only 5k RL steps. They are larger than the final 10k intervals, especially for CWM 32B at strict thresholds, suggesting that performance of RL reruns varies more significantly at intermediate training steps, though they converge toward a more consistent performance level as training proceeds. We also note the test-time variance atp100：由于 LCB 和 DMC-Optim 具有相似的测试集大小，因此该方差在 LCB 上的顺序应该相似，并且这也减轻了在某些优化 RL 配置中观察到的 4% 纯正确性下降的实际意义。100: since LCB and DMC-Optim have similar test-set sizes, this variance should be of similar order on LCB, and this also mitigates the actual significance of the 4% pure-correctness decrease observed for some optimization RL configurations.

During training, optimization RL brings gains across all percentiles, and the amplitude of the gains is greater as the evaluation constraint gets stricter.

6 模型学到什么？6 What Does the Model Learn?

上一节讨论的结果表明，当 LLM 以正确结合正确性和优化约束的目标进行 RL 训练时，优化任务会获得巨大收益。但更高的性能可能来自简单的实现技巧，例如更快的输入解析，或更有趣的算法发现，并且可能隐藏不同难度级别的差异。The results discussed in the previous section show large gains on optimization tasks when LLMs are RLtrained with an objective that correctly combines correctness and optimization constraints. But higher performance could come from simple implementation hacks, such as faster input parsing, or from more interesting algorithmic discoveries, and could hide discrepancies across difficulty levels.

保持keepsp100 pass@1 在所有三个分段上均略高于标准 RLVR，从 Easy 上的 74.6 升至 76.4，Medium 上的 72.5 升至 74.9，Hard 上从 49.5 升至 50.6。更严格的阈值变动幅度更大：100 pass@1 slightly above standard RLVR on all three splits, moving from 74.6 to 76.4 on Easy, 72.5 to 74.9 on Medium, and 49.5 to 50.6 on Hard. The stricter thresholds move much more: atp50、pass@1 在 Easy 上从 31.9 上升到 51.3，在 Medium 上从 32.4 上升到 52.8，在 Hard 上从 21.9 上升到 30.6；在50, pass@1 rises from 31.9 to 51.3 on Easy, 32.4 to 52.8 on Medium, and 21.9 to 30.6 on Hard; atp30，简单/中级的增益约为 125%，困难级的增益约为 70%。因此，优化 RL 可以在所有难度级别上带来收益，而不会用一种难度级别换取另一种难度级别，特别是不会牺牲困难问题的纯粹正确性（至少在 DMC-Optim 上）来提高较低难度的优化性能。我们还观察到，随着评估百分位数变得更加严格，各个级别的收益都会增长。然而，在困难问题上它们比在简单/中等问题上要小，这自然是因为这些问题一开始就更难解决，并且在考虑任何优化之前需要调动更多的模型容量。30, gains are in the order of 125% on Easy/Medium and 70% on Hard. Optimization RL therefore brings gains across all levels of difficulty, without trading off one difficulty level for another, and in particular without sacrificing hard-problem pure correctness (at least on DMC-Optim) to improve lower-difficulty optimization performance. We also observe that gains grow across all levels as evaluation percentiles get stricter. They are nevertheless smaller on Hard problems than on Easy/Medium, which naturally follows from the fact that these problems are harder to solve in the first place and mobilize more model capacity before any optimization can be considered.

表 8 给出了 LCB 上同一问题的发行版外版本。 Easy split 在纯正确性 pass@1 中基本上已经饱和。这与我们在上一节中观察到的情况相符：对于 CWM 32B，标准 RLVR 可能会丢弃许多批次，因为相应的提示太简单并产生零优势的全成功组，而优化 RL 可以将部分无用的样本转化为有用的训练信号。因此，简单问题的胜率不如其他难度级别的胜率高。由于问题很简单，标准 RLVR 已经有更多机会采样好的算法；对于前 30% 的执行后运行，最佳中值样本获胜率为 70.2%，而大多数其他优化环境接近 60%。Table 8 gives the out-of-distribution version of the same question on LCB. The Easy split is essentially saturated in pure-correctness pass@1. This matches what we observed in the previous section: for CWM 32B, standard RLVR probably throws away many batches because the corresponding prompts are too easy and produce zero-advantage all-success groups, whereas optimization RL can turn part of those otherwise useless samples into useful training signal. The win rates on Easy problems are therefore not as large as on other difficulty levels. Since the problems are easy, standard RLVR has more chances to sample a good algorithm already; the best median-sample win rate is 70.2% for the top-30% post-execution run, while most other optimization environments are closer to 60%.

在 Easy 和 Medium 上，我们不会诊断出较大的正确性差异。前 50% 的执行后运行具有与标准 RLVR 相同的 Easy pass@1，并且在 Medium 上相差不到 1 个点。只有在困难问题上，我们才能观察到真正的解决率差异：前 50% 和前 30% 的执行后训练将 pass@1 从 29.8% 减少到约 25.7–25.8%，相对下降约 13%。尽管如此，优化水平仍然非常大，在困难问题上达到 86.0% WRmed。我们已经讨论了为什么 LCB 对于这个特定的设置可以显示较低的纯正确性数字：基准是分布外的，硬分割比 DMC-Optim 更困难，并且这个特定数据集上的 pass@1 仅使用无限超时的小测试，这根本不给任何形式的优化带来竞争优势。相反，在 10k RL 步骤后查看硬问题 pass@10，前 50% 的运行分数与标准 RLVR 保持相同的 44.7，而前 30% 的运行分数下降On Easy and Medium, we do not diagnose large correctness differences. The top-50% post-execution run has the same Easy pass@1 as standard RLVR, and is within one point on Medium. Only on Hard problems do we observe a real solve-rate difference: top-50% and top-30% post-execution training reduce pass@1 from 29.8% to about 25.7–25.8%, a roughly 13% relative drop. The optimization levels are nevertheless very large, reaching 86.0% WRmed on Hard problems. We already discussed why LCB can show lower pure-correctness numbers for this specific setting: the benchmark is out-of-distribution, the hard split is more difficult than DMC-Optim, and the pass@1 on this specific dataset only uses small tests with infinite timeout, which simply gives no competitive advantage to any form of optimization. Looking instead at hard-problem pass@10 after 10k RL steps, the top-50% run keeps the same 44.7 score as standard RLVR, while the top-30% run decreases

23

仅约3%。也许这太乐观了，但我们认为，这表明进一步的 RL 训练应该有助于将更多保留的 pass@10 容量转化回 pass@1，而且底层多样本求解能力与标准 RLVR 下一样多的事实是一个很好的信号（推理时间搜索方法也可以利用这一点）。by only about 3%. Maybe this is too optimistic, but we believe that this shows that further RL training should help translate more of this preserved pass@10 capacity back into pass@1, and the fact that the underlying multi-sample solving capacity is moving as much as it does under standard RLVR is a good signal (something that inference-time search methods can also exploit).

优化 RL 训练模型学会生成哪些代码优化技巧？What are the code optimization tricks that optimization RL trained models learned to generate?

我们比较了 302 个 DMC-Optim 测试问题的三个来源的解决方案：执行后优化训练的 CWM 32B、CWM 32B RLVR 基线以及每个问题可用的最快正确的人类 Codeforces 解决方案。对于模型解决方案，我们根据优化测试持续时间为每个问题选择 20 个中最好的通过解决方案；对于人类来源，我们使用最快的正确人类解决方案。然后，我们丢弃相对速度差异低于 1% 的代码对，并将剩余的盲码对发送到 GPTOSS 120B（OpenAI，2025）。每对接受 10 次随机评审，其中两个解决方案随机分配给“解决方案 1”和“解决方案 2”。法官必须首先确定哪个解决方案更快，这是我们从优化测试的执行中得知的，作为过滤步骤来捕获接下来的判断是否有机会正确。然后，它对确定为最快的解决方案和确定为最慢的解决方案之间的差异进行结构化分类：实现级别与算法、改进的子类别（I/O 优化、数学快捷方式、数据结构更改、算法更改等；参见图 8）以及渐近复杂性是否似乎有所改进。We compare solutions from three sources on the 302 DMC-Optim test problems: a post-execution optimization-trained CWM 32B, the CWM 32B RLVR baseline, and the fastest correct human Codeforces solution available for each problem. For the model solutions, we select the best-of-20 passing solution per problem by optimization-test duration; for the human source, we use the fastest correct human solution. We then discard pairs where the relative speed difference is below 1%, and send the remaining blinded code pairs to GPTOSS 120B (OpenAI, 2025). Each pair receives 10 randomized judge rollouts with the two solutions randomly assigned to “Solution 1” and “Solution 2”. The judge must first identify which solution is faster, which we know from execution on optimization tests, as a filtering step to capture whether the judgment that follows has any chance to be correct. It then emits a structured classification of the differences between the solution identified as fastest and the one identified as slowest: implementation-level versus algorithmic, the sub-category of improvement (among I/O optimization, math shortcut, data-structure change, algorithm change, etc.; see Figure 8), and whether the asymptotic complexity appears to improve.

对于经过训练的优化与 RLVR 模型的比较，302 个任务中的 78 个在判断或分类之前被过滤：39 个是因为两个系统都无法生成通过样本，12 个是因为只有一个系统可以生成，27 个是因为最佳通过样本是速度平局。剩下 224 对，其中两个模型中的一个以速度取胜，For the trained optimization-vs-RLVR model comparison, 78 of the 302 tasks are filtered before judging or classification: 39 because both systems fail to produce a passing sample, 12 because only one system does, and 27 because the best passing samples are speed ties. This leaves 224 pairs where one of the two models win by speed, with

图 8：在可判断分类的解决方案对上，按代码改进类别进行 DMC-Optim 测试的速度胜利细目。Figure 8: Breakdown of speed-wins on DMC-Optim test per category of code improvement, on judge-classifiable pairs of solutions.数字是给定的可分类对总数的比例，其中一对由针对特定问题的主题最佳解决方案和对手最佳解决方案组成。Numbers are proportions of the given total number of pairs that were classifiable, where a pair is made of a subject best solution and adversary best solution on a unique problem.

其中 200 个训练模型的速度更快。经过 2,240 次法官推出后，少于 3 个速度正确推出的配对（这意味着法官识别真正最快解决方案的推出）被标记为不可分类，留下 174 个分类条目（我们还注意到，GPT-OSS 在寻找最快样本的任务上具有 68% 的准确率）。图 8 总结了分类结果。在优化 RL 与 RLVR 比较的可分类对中，由于更好的 I/O 优化，47% 的人支持优化 RL 模型的速度优势。更有趣的情况是，6% 的配对发现了算法改进，因此击败了 RLVR，6% 的配对使用了数学捷径，2% 的配对使用了更好的数据结构，1% 的配对进行了实际的完整算法更改。我们还注意到 34% 的情况对应于常量因子调整，这仍然是在代码竞争中产生影响的不可忽视的变化：避免中间分配和动态构建事物、收紧循环边界或识别提前终止情况。the trained model faster in 200 of them. After 2,240 judge rollouts, pairs with fewer than three speed-correct rollouts, meaning rollouts where the judge identifies the true fastest solution, are marked unclassifiable, leaving 174 classified entries (we also note that GPT-OSS on the task of finding the fastest sample has 68% accuracy). Figure 8 summarizes the resulting classification results. On the classifiable pairs of the optimization-RL versus RLVR comparison, 47% are speed wins in favor of the optimization-RL model due to better I/O optimization. More interesting cases are the 6% of pairs where it finds an algorithmic improvement and therefore beats RLVR, 6% with a math shortcut, 2% with a better data structure, and 1% with an actual full algorithm change. We also notice the 34% of cases that correspond to constant-factor tweaks, which can still be non-negligible changes that make a difference in code competitions: avoiding intermediate allocations and building things on the fly, tightening loop bounds, or identifying early-termination cases.

我们离人类最佳解决方案还有多远？How close are we to the best human solutions?优化 RL 主导了标准 RLVR，但人类仍然在 227 个速度获胜对中的 153 个中击败了经过训练的模型 (67%)。值得注意的是，optimizationRL 在 33% 的情况下击败了最优秀的人类。 I/O 优化是模型已经学到的东西：Optimization-RL dominates standard RLVR, yet humans still beat the trained model in 153 of 227 speed-win pairs (67%). It is also useful to notice that optimizationRL beats the best humans in 33% of cases. I/O optimization is something the model has learned well:

24

优化强化学习在这种速度获胜方面优于人类，并且凭借这些技巧赢得了 12% 的比赛。我们还注意到，由于数学捷径，该模型比人类获得了 4% 的速度优势。然而，总的来说，人类在发现此类改进方面仍然更强：与基线相比，由于数学捷径，人类赢得了 16% 的案例，而优化 RL 仅在 6% 的案例中以这种方式击败了 RLVR，算法改进也是如此，分别为 11% 和 6%。最后，查看图 8 的底行，我们记录到，根据 GPT-OSS，在 13% 的分类对中，优化 RL 凭借复杂性的改进战胜了 RLVR。人类仍然发现了更多这样的改进，其中 22% 的案例反对 RLVR，但优化 RL 发现复杂性改进超过 RLVR 的一半以上。直接将人类与优化强化学习进行比较仍然对人类有利，人类在 16% 的情况下获胜，复杂性有所提高，但相反的情况也发生在 7% 的分类对中。optimization-RL dominates humans on this type of speed win, and wins 12% of matches thanks to such tricks. We also note that 4% of speed wins are obtained by the model over humans thanks to a math shortcut. In general, however, humans remain stronger at finding such improvements: compared to the baseline, humans win 16% of cases thanks to math shortcuts, while optimization-RL only beats RLVR this way in 6% of cases, and the same holds for algorithmic refinements, with 11% versus 6%. Finally, looking at the bottom row of Figure 8, we record that in 13% of classified pairs, according to GPT-OSS, optimization-RL wins over RLVR with a complexity improvement. Humans still find more such improvements, with 22% of cases against RLVR, but optimization-RL finds more than half as many complexity improvements over RLVR. Directly comparing humans to optimization-RL still favors humans, which win in 16% of cases with a complexity improvement, but the converse also happens in 7% of the classified pairs.

从每个难度的细分来看，人类和优化强化学习在直接速度获胜方面在简单问题上大致相当，尽管人类发现了更多的复杂性改进：10% 的案例是人类在复杂性改进的情况下获胜，而优化强化学习为 7.7%。我们还注意到，在简单问题上，与 RLVR 相比，优化 RL 实际上比人类获得了更多的速度优势。随着难度的增加，人类的优势变得更加明显：在困难问题上，人类在 70% 的情况下击败了优化强化学习，而 23% 的情况则由复杂性提高的人类赢得。然而，与 RLVR 相比，优化 RL 仍然在难题上表现出色，在 25% 的情况下发现复杂性比 RLVR 有所改善。总而言之，这些结果表明人类仍然克服了我们最好的优化强化学习配置，并且这种强化学习训练可以受益于某些类型的改进，也许更注重学习真正的算法改进或 I/O 优化的变化。但即使在目前的表述中，优化强化学习也学习了几种优化技巧，这些技巧在某些问题上击败了最好的人类解决方案，并进行了非幼稚的代码改进。Looking at the per-difficulty breakdown, humans and optimization-RL are roughly on par on easy problems in terms of direct speed wins, even though humans find a few more complexity improvements: 10% of cases are human wins with a complexity improvement, versus 7.7% for optimization-RL. We also note that on easy problems, compared to RLVR, optimization-RL actually gets more speed wins than humans. As difficulty increases, the human advantage becomes clearer: on hard problems, humans beat optimization-RL in 70% of cases, and 23% are won by humans with a complexity improvement. Compared to RLVR, however, optimization-RL still shines on hard problems, finding a complexity improvement over RLVR in 25% of cases. Taken together, these results show that humans still overcome our best optimization-RL configuration, and that this RL training could benefit from favoring some types of improvements, maybe with a stronger focus on learning real algorithmic refinements or changes over I/O optimizations. But even in its current formulation, optimization-RL learns several optimization tricks that beat the best human solutions on some problems, with non-naive code improvements.

这些发现仍然取决于 GPT-OSS 分配的标签的有效性，尽管我们试图通过地面实况速度任务和多重采样来控制这一点。特别是，仅对其中一个模型或另一个模型以速度取胜的问题的子集进行分类。判断速度识别准确度为 68%，在人类与优化 RL 对上下降至 59%，其中记录的持续时间差异更小。为了支持上述分析，我们手动审查了优化 RL-与 RLVR 标签，发现被认为可分类的条目上的标签是准确的：复杂性分类，即更快的解决方案是否是复杂性改进，在 94.8% 的情况下被 GPT-OSS 正确解决。这项判断研究的主要局限性在于，优化 RL 和 RLVR 之间的速度优势有 22% 无法分类，因此未在图 8 的数字中报告。这些案例可能很有趣，因为 GPT-OSS 无法准确判断它们；我们认为 I/O 优化技巧更容易被发现，因此跳过的优化技巧更有可能包含其他形式的改进。法官还被迫将每一对分类为一个类别，这也同样可以隐藏更多有趣的并发改进。These findings remain conditioned on the validity of the labels assigned by GPT-OSS, although we tried to control for this with a ground-truth speed task and multi-sampling. In particular, only a subset of the problems where one model or the other wins by speed are classified. The judge speed-identification accuracy is 68%, and drops to 59% on human-vs-optimization-RL pairs where the recorded-duration differences are tighter. To ground the analysis above, we manually reviewed the optimization-RL-vs-RLVR labels and found the labels on entries deemed classifiable to be accurate: complexity categorization, meaning whether the faster solution is a complexity improvement, was correctly solved by GPT-OSS in 94.8% of cases. The main limitations of this judge study are that 22% of the speed wins between optimization-RL and RLVR were not classifiable and are therefore not reported in the numbers of Figure 8. Among these are probably interesting cases, since GPT-OSS could not accurately judge them; we believe that an I/O optimization trick is easier to spot, so the skipped optimization tricks are more likely to contain other forms of improvements. The judge was also forced to classify each pair into a single category, which can also similarly hide more interesting concurrent improvements.

局限性。Limitations.DMC-Optim 仍然是软件优化的一个狭窄设置。这些任务是单文件 Python 竞争性编程问题，一些奖励与固定的人类参考计时池进行比较，并且计时信号取决于嘈杂的沙箱执行。这使得该基准对于查找和比较不同形式的奖励非常有用，但它不涵盖存储库规模分析、内存目标、多语言系统代码或长期编辑循环。完整的管道也很昂贵：它需要更强大的测试、许多沙箱执行和大量计算资源，才能通过 RL 学习此类计时奖励。模拟器有助于降低在线 RL 之前的成本，但端到端的成本仍然不可忽略。最近的工作通过存储库工作负载指向这些设置（He 等人，2025；Ma 等人，2025）。这些方向需要的奖励与静态人力池不太相关。对于执行后排名奖励，一种途径是用生成或学习的分布替换固定参考分布，甚至以对抗性方式对其进行细化（Romera-Paredes 等人，2023；Novikov 等人，2025）。当前的预执行过滤 RL 系列也可能是一个有用的候选者，尽管概括如何过滤测试池并选择相应的超时将需要额外的工作。由于完整的 SWE 任务无疑更加困难，我们可能还需要重新审视如何平衡正确性和优化目标。DMC-Optim is still a narrow setting for software optimization. The tasks are single-file Python competitive-programming problems, some rewards compare against a fixed pool of human reference timings, and the timed signal depends on noisy sandbox executions. This makes the benchmark useful for finding and comparing different shape of rewards, but it does not cover repository-scale profiling, memory objectives, multi-language systems code, or long-horizon edit loops. The full pipeline is also expensive: it requires stronger tests, many sandbox executions, and large compute resources for such timing rewards to be learned through RL. The simulator helps reduce this cost before online RL, but end-to-end it remains non-negligible. Recent work points toward these settings through repository workloads (He et al., 2025; Ma et al., 2025). Those directions need rewards that are not too tied to static human pools. For post-execution ranking rewards, one route is to replace the fixed reference distribution with generated or learned distributions, or even to refine it adversarially (Romera-Paredes et al., 2023; Novikov et al., 2025). The current pre-execution filtering RL family could also be a useful candidate, although generalizing how to filter the test pool and choose the corresponding timeout would require additional work. As full SWE tasks are definitively harder, we would also probably need to revisit how to balance correctness and optimization objectives.

LCB 基准测试还暴露了优化 RL 训练模型的意外出现的行为。它们倾向于消除对基准测试有用但运行时速度较慢的开销，例如类包装器、方法分派或未使用的接口结构。这不一定是一般情况下不可避免的The LCB benchmarking also exposed an unexpected emerging behavior of optimization RL-trained models. They tend to remove overhead that is useful for a benchmark harness but slower at runtime, such as class wrappers, method dispatch, or unused interface structure. This is not necessarily a general unavoidable

25

我们的强化学习方法的局限性；这是奖励 DMC-Optim 列车上的快速可执行代码的结果，该列车接受精简的 I/O 版本，以及删除 SFT 的指令跟随分割（至少对于 Qwen 2.5 7B 和 32B 而言）。因此，我们使用 I/O 评估对 LCB 进行评估，而不是依赖基于调用的包装器，但针对下游使用进行训练的模型可能需要指令遵循演练或接口保留约束。limitation of our RL methods; it is a consequence of rewarding fast executable code on DMC-Optim train, which accepts a stripped I/O version, as well as of removing the instruction-following split of the SFT, at least for Qwen 2.5 7B and 32B. We therefore evaluate on LCB with I/O evaluation rather than relying on call-based wrappers, but a model trained for downstream usage would likely need instruction-following rehearsal or interface-preserving constraints.

最后，法官分析提出了改善奖励的潜在角度，也许可以通过价值模型。奖励观察到解决方案更快，但没有说明收益是否来自 I/O 处理、数据结构、数学、算法更改或复杂性改进。因此，学习的很大一部分花费在 I/O 和常数因子改进上，但人类比较表明，缩小剩余差距，以及使模型为算法发现产生更有趣的输出，可能需要算法感知的反馈。Finally, the judge analysis suggests a potential angle for improving the rewards, maybe with value models. The reward observes that a solution is faster, but it does not say whether the gain came from I/O handling, data structures, math, algorithm change, or complexity improvements. A significant part of the learning is therefore spent on I/O and constant-factor improvements, but the human comparison suggests that closing the remaining gap, as well as making the model produce more interesting outputs for algorithm discovery, may require feedback that is algorithm-aware.

7 结论7 Conclusion

当底层数据、测量工具、RL 环境和奖励以及 GRPO 组合在一起以传播清晰的定时反馈信号时，用于代码优化的 RL 就变得可训练：更大的优化测试、校准执行、执行前/执行内/执行后优化约束、折叠的二进制奖励和稳定的 GRPO 更新。在 DMC-Optim 上，这改进了跨模型和困难的严格优化，同时保留RL for code optimization becomes trainable when the underlying data, measurement tools, RL environments and rewards, and GRPO are composed together to propagate a clear timing-feedback signal: larger optimization tests, calibrated execution, pre-/intra-/post-execution optimization constraints, collapsed binary rewards, and stable GRPO updates. On DMC-Optim, this improves strict optimization across models and difficulties while preservingp100 纯正确性：top-50% pass@1 在 Qwen 2.5 7B 上从 18.0% 上升到 31.3%，在 CWM 32B 上从 30.7% 上升到 50.4%，相对增益分别为 150% 和 125%100 pure-correctness: top-50% pass@1 rises from 18.0% to 31.3% on Qwen 2.5 7B and from 30.7% to 50.4% on CWM 32B, with respective 150% and 125% relative gains atp30. 在 LCB 上，我们观察到类似的优化收益，其中大部分保留了纯粹正确性，并且仅在出现时包含减少。该模型学习 I/O 优化和常数因子改进，但也进行了一些算法改进：在一组比较问题上，优化 RL 在 89% 的速度获胜对中击败了标准 RLVR，并在 13% 的情况下以复杂性改进获胜，而最好的人类提交则在 22% 的情况下以复杂性改进击败了相同的基线。 Optimization-RL 还击败了人类提交本身，在 7% 的情况下提高了复杂性。我们将此视为算法发现的 RL 训练的一条途径：算法感知反馈（可能通过价值模型）应该会进一步增强这些能力。30. On LCB, we observe similar optimization gains with pure-correctness mostly preserved, and only contained decreases when they appear. The model learns I/O optimization and constant-factor improvements, but also some algorithmic refinements: on a comparison set of problems, optimization-RL beats standard RLVR in 89% of speed-win pairs and wins with complexity improvements in 13% of cases, while the best human submissions beat the same baseline with complexity improvements in 22% of cases. Optimization-RL also beats human submissions themselves with a complexity improvement in 7% of cases. We view this as a path toward RL training for algorithm discovery: algorithm-aware feedback, potentially through value models, should further boost these capabilities.

我们最初在竞争性编程中测量的正确性与效率差距相同——标准 RLVR 从 43.5% 下降The same correctness-efficiency gap we originally measured on competitive programming—where standard RLVR drops from 43.5%p100 次通过@1 至 7.7%100 pass@1 to 7.7% atp在 Qwen 2.5 7B 上从 30% 上升，在 CWM 32B 上从 69.8% 上升到 13.7%——也出现在现实世界的软件工程任务中，如果有的话，它甚至更为重要。在 SWE 效率方面，在发布时，Claude 4.5 Sonnet 在 81% 的时间内生成了正确的补丁，但仅捕获了 4.1% 的专家加速（Ma et al., 2025）。因此，编写正确的代码和编写高效的代码之间的差距也是一个从竞争性编程转化为 SWE 任务的问题。30 on Qwen 2.5 7B, and from 69.8% to 13.7% on CWM 32B—also appears on real-world software-engineering tasks, where it is, if anything, even more significant. On SWE-fficiency, at the time it was released, Claude 4.5 Sonnet produced correct patches 81% of the time but captured only 4.1% of expert speedup (Ma et al., 2025). The gap between writing correct code and writing efficient code is therefore a problem that translates from competitive programming to SWE tasks as well.

提高代码生成正确性的强化学习方法已经从“游乐场”设置转移到现实世界的软件工程中。使 SWE-RL（Wei 等人，2025b）能够将 RL 应用到现实世界软件工程的方法首先是在计算密集度较低的环境中开发的：通过 DeepSeekMath（Shao 等人，2024）在数学问题上引入 GRPO，以及基于规则的可验证奖励和RL methods to improve code generation correctness have already transferred from “playground” settings to real-world software engineering. The methods that enabled SWE-RL (Wei et al., 2025b) to apply RL to real-world software engineering were developed first in less compute-intensive settings: GRPO was introduced on math problems through DeepSeekMath (Shao et al., 2024), and rule-based verifiable rewards and the推理模板通过 DeepSeek-R1 在数学和竞争性编码上进行了验证（Guo 等人，2025b）。 SWE-RL 针对现实世界的软件工程领域调整了奖励和支架，将基于执行的奖励替换为文本相似性，因为在真实存储库中运行代码过于昂贵。reasoning template were validated on math and competitive coding through DeepSeek-R1 (Guo et al., 2025b). SWE-RL adapted the reward and scaffolding for the real-world software-engineering domain, swapping execution-based rewards for text similarity because running code in real repositories was too expensive.

软件工程任务的效率尚未得到正确掌握。不仅软件工程任务的执行难度更大、成本更高，从而减慢了强化学习的迭代速度，而且导致竞争性编程效率难以提高的挑战（测量可靠性、奖励噪声和训练不稳定）在现实世界的软件工程任务中都会加剧。我们认为这项工作是朝着类似的效率转移迈出的一步：它在竞争性编程“游乐场”中开发了一些方法，其中反馈循环更容易处理，并且可能为以后适应现实世界的软件优化提供基础。Efficiency has not been correctly mastered on software-engineering tasks yet. Not only are software-engineering tasks harder and far more expensive to execute, slowing down RL iterations, but the challenges that make competitive-programming efficiency hard—measurement reliability, reward noise, and training instability—are all exacerbated on real-world software-engineering tasks. We see this work as a step toward a similar transfer for efficiency: it develops some methods in the competitive-programming “playground”, where the feedback loop is more easily handled, and may provide a basis for later adaptation to real-world software optimization.

附录Appendix

测量噪声下代码优化成功指标的仿真研究A Simulation study of code optimization success metrics under measurement noise

本附录隔离了第 3 节背后的测量问题：如果两个正确的解决方案在运行时不同，那么嘈杂的执行系统何时可以足够可靠地对它们进行排名以支持 RL 奖励或评估指标？我们特意删除了 RL 循环并研究了一个更小的问题，即每次执行的时序扰动如何与测试套件以及用于聚合持续时间的指标相互作用。这里考虑的噪声是围绕固定执行机制的随机变化：由于调度延迟、调度抖动或其他本地时序影响，同一解决方案在同一测试中的两次执行可能会有所不同。我们的远程沙箱执行服务 CES 中的更多系统性转变，例如更改负载模式、沙箱升级或将沙箱服务重新部署到不同的基础设施，会在多次执行中以更一致的方式影响测量，并在第 C.7 和 C.8 节中与存储持续时间校准一起单独处理。创建此处研究的时序测量的测试生成步骤，以及更广泛的数据集清理和持续时间可过滤性管道，在 B 节中进行了处理。因此，本附录的目的不是证明特定的奖励训练，而是解释为什么本文在比较奖励设计之前首先更改测试机制，以及为什么代码优化成功指标应取决于所使用的测试集的时序机制。This appendix isolates the measurement question behind Section 3: if two correct solutions differ in runtime, when can a noisy execution system rank them reliably enough to support either an RL reward or an evaluation metric? We deliberately remove the RL loop and study a smaller problem, namely how per-execution timing perturbations interact with the test suite and the metric used to aggregate durations. The noise considered here is random variation around a fixed execution regime: two executions of the same solution on the same test can differ because of dispatch latency, scheduling jitter, or other local timing effects. More systematic shifts in CES, our remote sandbox execution service, such as changing load patterns, sandbox upgrades, or redeploying the sandbox service to different infrastructure, affect measurements in a more consistent way across many executions and are treated separately in Sections C.7 and C.8, together with stored-duration calibration. The test generation step that creates the timing measurements studied here, and more broadly the dataset cleaning and duration-filterability pipeline, are treated in Section B. The point of this appendix is therefore not to prove that a particular reward trains, but to explain why the paper first changes the test regime before comparing reward designs, and why the code-optimization success metric should depend on the timing regime of the test set being used.

在这种简化的设置中，有两种设计选择很重要。首先，测试套件必须使算法差异相对于固定调度和调度开销较大；否则，数十毫秒的扰动可能占信号的很大一部分。其次，当相对比较可以聚合许多每次测试持续时间的比较时，该指标不应依赖于一个脆弱的边界。我们通过比较 LiveCodeBench (LCB) 的测试套件属性来研究这些选择，LiveCodeBench 是一个具有较短基础测试和每个问题较少测试的数据集，与 DMC-Optim 是一个具有较大优化测试、较长运行时间和每个问题较多测试的数据集；然后，我们根据无阈值获胜率指标模拟有阈值的超时指标。我们仅以散文形式讨论原始DMC测试：如第3节所示，它们与LCB保持相同的快速测试制度，因此我们在定量比较中使用LCB作为短期测试制度的代表性基准。Two design choices matter in this simplified setting. First, the test suite must make algorithmic differences large relative to fixed dispatch and scheduling overheads; otherwise a tens-of-milliseconds perturbation can be a large fraction of the signal. Second, the metric should not depend on one fragile boundary when a relative comparison can aggregate many per-test duration comparisons. We study these choices by contrasting the test-suite properties of LiveCodeBench (LCB), a dataset with short base tests and fewer tests per problem, against DMC-Optim, a dataset with larger optimization tests, longer runtimes, and more tests per problem; we then simulate a thresholded timeout metric against a threshold-free win-rate metric. We discuss the original DMC tests in prose only: as shown in Section 3, they remain in the same fast-test regime as LCB, so we use LCB as the representative benchmark for short-test regimes in the quantitative comparison.

A.1 经验计时制度A.1 Empirical timing regimes

快速测试和慢速测试制度。Fast-test and slow-test regimes.在任何模拟之前，两种制度之间的经验差异是显而易见的，但证据来自两个互补的来源。第3节报告了DMC内部施工效果的人工参考统计：原始DMC测试仅达到0The empirical difference between the two regimes is visible before any simulation, but the evidence comes from two complementary sources. Section 3 reports human-reference statistics showing the construction effect inside DMC: original DMC tests reach only 0.145 秒145 s atp95 和 095 and 0.第463章463 s atp99，而生成的优化测试达到 199, whereas generated optimization tests reach 1.296 秒和 3296 s and 3.710秒。710 s.1下面的训练分割分位数和保留的比较支持相同的定性点：原始 DMC 和 LCB 是快速测试方案，而 DMC-Optim 将更多质量转移到可以测量时序差异的持续时间中。图 9 显示，与 LCB 相比，DMC-Optim 使用更大的优化测试和每个问题更多的测试。 LCB 在 287 个问题上汇总了 10,125 个公共/私人测试，或平均每个问题 35.3 个测试，而 DMC-Optim 在 302 个问题上贡献了 36,814 个优化测试，或每个问题 121.9 个测试。典型的 LCB 测试保持在数十个字符，而典型的 DMC-Optim 测试约为 10 个字符The training-split quantiles and the held-out comparisons below support the same qualitative point: original DMC and LCB are fast-test regimes, while DMC-Optim moves much more mass into durations where timing differences can be measured. Figure 9 shows that DMC-Optim uses both larger optimization tests and more tests per problem than LCB. LCB aggregates 10,125 public/private tests over 287 problems, or 35.3 tests per problem on average, while DMC-Optim contributes 36,814 optimization tests over 302 problems, or 121.9 per problem. The typical LCB test remains in the tens of characters, whereas the typical DMC-Optim test is around 103字符，具有更重的上尾。characters, with a much heavier upper tail.

同样的模式出现在运行时。 LCB 不是原始的 DMC 基准，但它采用相同的快速测试机制：图 10 显示了非常快速的执行和 2 到 10 秒之间生存曲线的宽阔平台，因此改变该范围内的超时几乎不会产生什么信号。相反，DMC-Optim 显示出更稳定的衰减，这意味着阈值的相同变化仍然可以使测试的重要部分跨越边界。The same pattern appears in runtime. LCB is not the original DMC benchmark, but it occupies the same fast-test regime: Figure 10 shows a sharp concentration on very fast executions and a broad plateau in the survival curve between 2 and 10 s, so changing the timeout across that range moves little signal. DMC-Optim instead shows a steadier decay, which means the same change in threshold can still move a non-trivial fraction of tests across the boundary.

A.2 紧凑噪声假设A.2 A compact noise hypothesis

加法和乘法项。Additive and multiplicative terms.让Letd真实true S,i是解决方案的执行时间be the execution time of solutionS测试中on testi在固定的执行环境中。我们将观察到的持续时间建模为in a fixed execution environment. We model the observed duration as

乘法项捕获随工作负载扩展的成比例的减速。附加项捕获固定开销，例如调度抖动、调度延迟或上下文切换成本。这些效应不能单独观察，并且很难机械地建模，因此我们将它们的残余贡献视为随机加性噪声。在快速测试中更重要的数量是相对附加负担The multiplicative term captures proportional slowdowns that scale with the workload. The additive term captures fixed overheads such as scheduling jitter, dispatch latency, or context-switch costs. These effects are not observed separately and are hard to model mechanistically, so we treat their residual contribution as random additive noise. The quantity that matters more on fast tests is the relative additive burden

As d真实true S,i变小，相同的绝对扰动占据测量的较大部分。becomessmall,thesameabsoluteperturbationoccupiesalargerfractionofthemeasurement.

这种分解不仅是合成的。 C.7 节中的存储到新鲜的 CES 校准符合截距为 53 ms 的仿射图，这与执行堆栈中不可忽略的固定成本项一致。由于该截距对应于沙箱状态之间的系统转变，因此我们使用校准模型将其删除，而不是将其视为随机的每次调用噪声。尽管如此，它还是为此处研究的添加剂成分提供了正确的比例。作为对用于基准比较的测试执行的确证检查，对于 75.9% 的 LCB 执行和 52.4% 的 DMC-Optim 执行，假设的 53 毫秒扰动将至少达到观察到的持续时间的一半；在 100 毫秒时，它将分别超过整个观察到的持续时间 68.8% 和 46.3%。这些辅助数字不是我们主要的持续时间统计数据，但它们与第 3 节和图 10 指向相同的方向：固定的数十毫秒项在快速测试机制中更难吸收。This decomposition is not only synthetic. The stored-to-fresh CES calibration in Section C.7 fits an affine map with a 53 ms intercept, which is consistent with a non-negligible fixed-cost term in the execution stack. Because this intercept corresponds to a systematic shift between sandbox states, we remove it with a calibration model rather than treating it as random per-call noise. It nevertheless gives the right scale for the additive component studied here. As a corroborative check on the test executions used for the benchmark comparison, a hypothetical 53 ms perturbation would amount to at least half of the observed duration for 75.9% of LCB executions and 52.4% of DMC-Optim executions; at 100 ms, it would exceed the full observed duration for 68.8% and 46.3%, respectively. These auxiliary numbers are not our main duration statistics, but they point in the same direction as Section 3 and Figure 10: a fixed tens-of-milliseconds term is much harder to absorb in the fast-test regime.

A.3 模拟研究：超时与胜率A.3 Simulation study: timeout versus win-rate

为什么要比较这两个指标。Why compare these two metrics.本节中的每个指标都是根据相同的噪声每次测试持续时间构建的，但聚合决定了噪声可以在何处翻转决策。因此，我们比较两个最小和自然的系列：一个有阈值的绝对度量，以超时表示，以及一个无阈值的相对度量，以胜率表示。超时是询问解决方案是否满足固定延迟预算的最简单方法，而获胜率是询问一个正确解决方案是否比另一个更快且无需选择绝对阈值的最简单方法。这些并不意味着指定最终的奖励设计。每个都可以代替Every metric in this section is built from the same noisy per-test durations, but the aggregation determines where noise can flip the decision. We therefore compare two minimal and natural families: a thresholded absolute metric, represented by timeout, and a threshold-free relative metric, represented by win-rate. Timeout is the simplest way to ask whether a solution satisfies a fixed latency budget, while win-rate is the simplest way to ask whether one correct solution is faster than another without choosing an absolute threshold. These are not meant to specify the final reward design. Each can instead be

可以理解为一个简单的代码优化成功指标：在强化学习期间，它可以转化为奖励，推动生成更快的正确解决方案；在评估时，它可以用作在测试集上比较模型的分数。模拟仅保留阈值与相对的区别，这是无需完整 RL 环境即可研究的部分。read as a simple code-optimization success metric: during RL, it can be converted into a reward that pushes generation toward faster correct solutions; at evaluation time, it can be used as a score for comparing models on a test set. The simulation keeps only the thresholded-versus-relative distinction, which is the part that can be studied without the full RL environment.

两个简单的度量系列。Two simple metric families.对于固定超时For a fixed timeoutτ，解决方案的超时分数, the timeout score of solutionS is

Noise can then make the same threshold comparison less stable, because each per-test measurement is another chance for an execution near the boundary to cross it.

对于胜率，我们逐个比较观察到的两个持续时间：For win-rate, we compare the two observed durations test by test:

胜率指标表明The win-rate metric declaresB获胜者如果the winner ifw ( B ; A ) > w ( A ; B）。它仍然有噪声，尽管它不需要调整外部阈值，并且它对所有测试而不是单个边界事件的比较进行平均，从而更容易消除每个测试持续时间的噪声。). It is still noisy, though it does not require tuning an external threshold and it averages the comparison over all tests rather than over a single boundary event, making it easier for per-test duration noise to cancel out.

综合问题族。Synthetic problem families.该模拟使用经验锚定的合成 LCB 类和 DMC-Optim 类问题来隔离定性机制差异；下一段给出了确切的参数化。这些参数固定于图 9 和图 10 中的经验测试计数和持续时间范围：每个问题有 35 次测试和特征亚秒持续时间的类似 LCB 的系列，以及类似 DMC-Optim 的系列The simulation uses empirically anchored synthetic LCB-like and DMC-Optim-like problems that isolate the qualitative regime difference; the next paragraph gives the exact parameterization. 每个问题有 122 个测试，而且测试速度要慢得多。在每次试验中，解决方案with 122 tests per problem and substantially slower tests. In each trial, solutionB才是真正的赢家和解决方案is the true winner and solutionA平均慢 20%，并且每个测试都有额外的可变性，因此某些单独的测试仍然可以颠倒顺序。我们注入乘性噪声或加性噪声，并测量每个指标识别正确获胜者的频率。除了图 11 中的显式阈值扫描之外，超时结果使用事后选择的预言机阈值，因此报告的超时曲线应被视为乐观的。下面的三张图回答了互补的问题：图 11 显示了阈值调整是否可以挽救超时，图 12 显示了更多的测试对每个指标系列有多少帮助，图 13 显示了随着噪声的增长，两个数据集体系如何分离。is 20% slower on average, with additional per-test variability so that some individual tests can still reverse the ordering. We inject either multiplicative noise or additive noise and measure how often each metric identifies the correct winner. Except for the explicit threshold sweep in Figure 11, timeout results use an oracle threshold chosen after the fact, so the reported timeout curves should be read as optimistic. The three figures below answer complementary questions: Figure 11 shows whether threshold tuning can rescue timeout at all, Figure 12 shows how much more tests help each metric family, and Figure 13 shows how the two dataset regimes separate as noise grows.

合成游乐场规格。Synthetic playground specification.对于政权For regimer ∈ {r ∈{类LCBLCB-like,类DMC-OptimDMC-Optim-like}，我们首先绘制基本测试持续时间, we first draw base test durations双~bi ∼对数正态分布（LogNormal(µr, σr), 与 (), with (µr, σr ) = ( − 2 . 3 , 0 .5) 对于类似 LCB 的问题以及 (15) for LCB-like problems and (1. 0 , 0 .5) 对于类似 DMCOptim 的问题。我们使用5) for DMCOptim-like problems. We useNr= 35 和= 35 andNr= 122 次测试，分别匹配经验测试计数差距。然后我们生成两个竞争解决方案的真实持续时间：= 122 tests, respectively, to match the empirical test-count gap. We then generate the true durations of the two competing solutions as

平均间隙 Δ= 0with mean gap ∆= 0.2、每次测试的变异性2 and per-test variabilityηA,i, ηB,i ∼N (0 , 0 . 152），然后应用方程（3）中的观测模型。这些值是经验锚点，旨在保留对论证重要的主要成分：正的和强烈右偏的持续时间、快速和慢速测试之间的数量级分离，以及每个问题测试的经验差异。每个蒙特卡洛试验都会对基本持续时间、测试特定间隙因子和测量噪声进行重新采样，因此报告的曲线是执行噪声和问题间变异性的平均值，而不是单个冻结合成实例的平均值。), before applying the observation model from Equation (3). These values are empirical anchors chosen to preserve the main ingredients that matter for the argument: positive and strongly right-skewed durations, the order-of-magnitude separation between fast and slow tests, and the empirical difference in tests per problem. Each Monte Carlo trial resamples the base durations, the test-specific gap factors, and the measurement noise, so the reported curves average over both execution noise and problem-to-problem variability rather than over a single frozen synthetic instance.

脆弱性阈值。Threshold fragility.图 11 显示了两个度量标准系列之间的第一个差异。胜率持平，因为它没有调整的门槛。超时仅在两个解决方案极端值之间的窄带内提供信息，因此即使在乘性噪声下，其准确性也会随着所选阈值的变化而急剧变化。在 LCB 类问题的加性噪声​​下，有用的频带很大程度上消失了，并且超时在整个扫描过程中仍然接近随机猜测，而获胜率明显高于随机猜测。Figure 11 shows the first difference between the two metric families. Win-rate is flat because it has no threshold to tune. Timeout is only informative in a narrow band between the two solutions’ extremes, so its accuracy varies sharply with the chosen threshold even under multiplicative noise. Under additive noise on LCB-like problems, that useful band largely disappears and timeout remains close to random guessing across the full sweep, whereas win-rate stays clearly above random guessing.

更多的测试会有所帮助，但效果并不相同。More tests help, but not equally.图 12 显示增加测试数量可以改善这两个指标系列，但增益并不对称。这很重要，因为 DMC-Optim 为每个问题提供的测试比 LCB 多大约 3.5 倍。在加性噪声下类似 LCB 的问题上，随着更多测试的增加，获胜率不断提高，而超时的提高速度要慢得多。在类似 DMC-Optim 的问题上，特别是在加性噪声下，额外测试的收益对于这两个指标来说都更强，这正是我们希望从更慢、更密集的测试套件中获得的行为。Figure 12 shows that increasing the number of tests improves both metric families, but the gain is not symmetric. This matters because DMC-Optim provides about 3.5 times more tests per problem than LCB. On LCB-like problems under additive noise, win-rate keeps improving as more tests are added, whereas timeout improves at a much slower rate. On DMC-Optim-like problems, especially under additive noise, the gains from additional tests are much stronger for both metrics, which is exactly the behavior we want from a slower and denser test suite.

政权分裂出现在加性噪声下。The regime split appears under additive noise.图 13 通过直接扫描噪声幅度总结了比较。在乘性噪声下，两个数据集都逐渐退化，并且超时和获胜率之间的差距不大。定性分裂出现在加性噪声下。在类似 LCB 的问题上，超时接近随机猜测，而胜率仍然明显高于随机猜测。在类似 DMC-Optim 的问题上，这两个指标在更广泛的范围内仍然可用，尽管胜率始终保持较高。Figure 13 summarizes the comparison by sweeping the noise magnitude directly. Under multiplicative noise, both datasets degrade gradually and the gap between timeout and win-rate is modest. The qualitative split appears under additive noise. On LCB-like problems, timeout approaches random guessing while win-rate remains meaningfully above random guessing. On DMC-Optim-like problems, both metrics remain usable over a much wider range, although win-rate stays higher throughout.

对实验设计的实际意义。Practical implication for experiment design.这些数字表明了简单的操作顺序。首先改变数据机制：更大的输入、更长的运行时间以及每个问题更多的测试可以同时改善两个指标系列。只有这样，才值得决定是否应将硬超时保留为优化目标的一部分。如果套件停留在快速测试状态，更严格的阈值调整并不能解决根本问题；它仅在测量设置中搜索狭窄的操作窗口，其中阈值决策本质上是脆弱的。对于训练和 DMC-Optim 评估，这就是为什么我们的实验首先从原始的快速测试机制转向 DMC-Optim，然后才研究不同效率指标在该数据集上的表现。表 2 显示，一旦测试制度发生变化，严格的 DMC-Optim 阈值可以分离 Qwen 2.5 7B 训练配置。对于 LCB，相同的论点导致不同的评估选择：因为 LCB 仍处于快速测试制度中，所以我们在表 5 中使用获胜率式的速度分数，而不是绝对超时扫描。表9给出了相应的实证检验。在 Qwen 2.5 7B 检查点中，LCB 超时 pass@1 主要作为阈值的共享函数移动，并且提供的很少These figures suggest a simple order of operations. First change the data regime: larger inputs, longer runtimes, and more tests per problem improve both metric families at once. Only then does it become worthwhile to decide whether a hard timeout should remain part of the optimization objective. If the suite stays in the fast-test regime, tighter threshold tuning does not fix the underlying problem; it only searches for a narrow operating window in a measurement setting where thresholded decisions are intrinsically brittle. For training and DMC-Optim evaluation, this is why our experiments first move from the original fast-test regime to DMC-Optim, and only then study how different efficiency metrics behave on top of that dataset. Table 2 shows that strict DMC-Optim thresholds can separate Qwen 2.5 7B training configurations once the test regime has been changed. For LCB, the same argument leads to a different evaluation choice: because LCB remains in the fast-test regime, we use win-rate-style speed scores in Table 5 rather than absolute timeout sweeps. Table 9 gives the corresponding empirical check. Across Qwen 2.5 7B checkpoints, LCB timeout pass@1 moves mostly as a shared function of the threshold and provides little

A.4 要点A.4 Takeaways

本附录支持本文中使用的操作顺序。首先，具有附加项的混合噪声模型是快速测试套件的正确抽象：当持续时间接近调度和调度开销时，相同的绝对扰动可以主导测量。其次，更大的输入、更长的运行时间和每个问题的更多测试通过使时序信号更大并对特定于测试的反转进行平均来帮助阈值和相对指标。最初的 DMC 和 LCB 仍然处于这一鸿沟的脆弱一侧，而 DMC-Optim 将足够的质量转移到较慢的区域，以使基于时间的决策提供更多信息。第三，在快速测试机制中，相对聚合比硬超时的生存时间更长，因为它比较多个测试的持续时间，而不是依赖于一个边界。下游结果是 LCB 传输是通过获胜率式的速度分数而不是超时扫描来评估的：LCB 是一个短期测试基准，上面的模拟与表 9 一起表明，阈值比较This appendix supports the order of operations used in the paper. First, a mixed noise model with an additive term is the right abstraction for fast test suites: when durations are close to dispatch and scheduling overheads, the same absolute perturbation can dominate the measurement. Second, larger inputs, longer runtimes, and more tests per problem help both thresholded and relative metrics by making the timing signal larger and by averaging over test-specific reversals. Original DMC and LCB remain on the fragile side of this divide, while DMC-Optim moves enough mass into the slower regime to make timing-based decisions more informative. Third, relative aggregation survives longer than a hard timeout in the fast-test regime because it compares durations across many tests instead of hinging on one boundary. A downstream consequence is that LCB transfer is evaluated with win-rate-style speed scores rather than timeout sweeps: LCB is a short-test benchmark, and the simulations above together with Table 9 show that thresholded comparisons in that

图 12 每个问题的测试数量的影响。Figure 12 Effect of the number of tests per problem.根据乘性噪声（顶部）和加性噪声（底部）下类似 LCB 问题（左）和类似 DMC-Optim 问题（右）的测试数量绘制了准确度。在具有附加噪声的快速问题上，获胜率继续从额外的测试中获得，因为它对整个套件中的成对比较进行平均，而超时的改善速度要慢得多，因为每个决策仍然取决于单个阈值。对于较慢的问题，这两个指标都可以从更多的测试中获益更多，这是 DMC-Optim 比 LCB 更容易可靠测量的原因之一。Accuracy is plotted against the number of tests for LCB-like problems (left) and DMC-Optim-like problems (right), under multiplicative noise (top) and additive noise (bottom). On fast problems with additive noise, win-rate continues to gain from additional tests because it averages pairwise comparisons across the suite, while timeout improves at a much slower rate because every decision still hinges on a single threshold. On slower problems, both metrics benefit more from having more tests, which is one reason DMC-Optim is easier to measure reliably than LCB.

制度不支持稳定的模型比较。regime do not support stable model comparisons.

这些结论并没有消除对测量堆栈其余部分的需要。 DMC-Optim 仍然需要持续时间过滤性来选择问题，其中正确的解决方案可以按时间排序（B 部分），并且仍然需要 CES 校准，以便测量的持续时间在不同服务状态下保持可比性（C 部分）。模拟解释了第一步：在选择奖励之前，基准必须离开固定 10-100 毫秒扰动与持续时间信号相当的状态。一旦测试套件提供了更大的输入、更长的持续时间以及每个问题的更多试验，第 4 部分就可以询问如何将这些测量结果转化为正确性门控的 RL 奖励。These conclusions do not remove the need for the rest of the measurement stack. DMC-Optim still needs duration filterability to select problems where correct solutions can be ranked by time (Section B), and CES calibration is still needed so that measured durations remain comparable across service states (Section C). The simulation explains the first step: before choosing a reward, the benchmark has to leave the regime where fixed 10–100 ms perturbations are comparable to the duration signal. Once the test suite provides larger inputs, longer durations, and more trials per problem, Section 4 can ask how to turn those measurements into a correctness-gated RL reward.

B Building DMC-Optim：更强的正确性信号和新的优化反馈B Building DMC-Optim: stronger correctness signal and new optimization feedback

本附录扩展了第 3 节的一半数据。主要论文以压缩形式陈述了因果链：原始 DMC 作为正确性门和时序源都太弱； DMC-Optim 重建测试体系；然后，持续时间过滤性会选择时间可以对正确解决方案进行排序的子集。这里我们详细阐述DMC-Optim的构建细节。主管道如图2；本附录记录了支持它的阈值、计数、侧分支和诊断数据。This appendix expands the data half of Section 3. The main paper states the causal chain in compressed form: raw DMC is too weak as both a correctness gate and a timing source; DMC-Optim rebuilds the test regime; duration filterability then selects the subset where timing can rank correct solutions. Here we spell out the construction details of DMC-Optim. The main pipeline is Figure 2; this appendix records the thresholds, counts, side branches, and diagnostic figures that support it.

B.1 数据集必须建立什么B.1 What the dataset must establish

并非每个编码数据集都可以作为优化 RL 的种子数据。即使只有正确性的目标也需要仔细的数据管理，而增加执行时间会使要求更加严格。数据必须同时满足三个条件。首先，问题必须存在于可学习的机制中：如果问题太简单，强化学习就会很快饱和；如果问题太简单，强化学习就会很快饱和；如果问题太简单，强化学习就会很快饱和。如果太难，几乎每次部署都会失败，优化目标就没有机会出现。其次，测试必须跨越足够的计算负载，使执行时间反映代码行为，而不仅仅是调度程序噪音、解释器开销或固定沙箱成本。第三，如果生成这样的测试，生成过程需要可信的正向和负向控制。经过验证的正确人类解决方案既可以用来验证候选测试，也可以在以后作为计时参考。已验证的错误解决方案用作阴性对照，用于检查增强套件是否仍然允许错误代码通过。Not every coding dataset can serve as seed data for optimization RL. Even a correctness-only objective demands careful data curation, and adding execution time makes the requirements stricter. The data must satisfy three conditions at once. First, the problems must lie in a learnable regime: if they are too easy, RL saturates quickly; if they are too hard, nearly every rollout fails and the optimization objective has no chance to appear. Second, the tests must span enough computational load that execution time reflects code behavior rather than only scheduler noise, interpreter overhead, or a fixed sandbox cost. Third, if such tests are generated, the generation process needs trusted positive and negative controls. Verified correct human solutions are used both to validate candidate tests and, later, as timing references. Verified incorrect solutions are used as negative controls for checking whether an augmented suite still lets wrong code pass.

这就是 DMC-Optim 将两个角色分开的原因，而这两个角色在编程基准测试中经常被合并。正确性测试收紧了通过/失败门：它们应该减少误报而不引入误报。优化测试会产生持续时间分布：在强制执行正确性之后，他们应该按运行时间分离正确的解决方案。因此，最终数据集不仅存储测试输入和预期输出，还存储经过验证的人类解决方案的测量参考持续时间，因为几个下游奖励和评估变体将模型推出与存储的人类时间分布进行比较。This is why DMC-Optim separates two roles that are often collapsed in programming benchmarks. Correctness tests tighten the pass/fail gate: they should reduce false positives without introducing false negatives. Optimization tests create duration spread: after correctness has already been enforced, they should separate correct solutions by runtime. The final dataset therefore stores not only test inputs and expected outputs, but also measured reference durations for the verified human solutions, because several downstream reward and evaluation variants compare model rollouts against this stored human timing distribution.

B.2 源数据、限制和净化B.2 Source data, limitations, and decontamination

DeepMind 代码竞赛。DeepMind Code Contests.基础数据集是 DeepMind 代码竞赛 (DMC) 数据集（Li et al., 2022），大约有 12 个数据集The base dataset is the DeepMind Code Contests (DMC) dataset (Li et al., 2022), a collection of approximately 12,275 个竞争性编程问题源自 Codeforces 等平台。每个问题都包含自然语言描述、输入/输出规范、约束、示例输入/输出对以及三个原始测试类别：比赛期间可见的公共测试、用于最终评分的私人测试以及由 DMC 作者使用输入生成器和强力验证器生成的生成测试。人类提交的解决方案以多种编程语言提供，并标记为正确或错误。275 competitive-programming problems sourced from platforms such as Codeforces. Each problem contains a natural-language description, input/output specifications, constraints, example input/output pairs, and three original test categories: public tests visible during competition, private tests used for final grading, and generated tests produced by the DMC authors with input generators and brute-force verifiers. Human-submitted solutions are provided in multiple programming languages and labeled as correct or incorrect.

我们保留Python解决方案。 C++ 是竞争性编程中的主导语言，但它也是低级速度最受控制的设置：编译器优化、内存布局和手动调整的实现细节可以主导该方法。 Python 的噪音更大，开销也更大，这使得它对于必须在 RL 循环内运行的计时方法的设置要求更高。如果优化强化学习能够在这种嘈杂的环境中学习有用的算法变化，我们期望同样的想法We retain Python solutions. C++ is the dominant language in competitive programming, but it is also the most controlled setting for low-level speed: compiler optimizations, memory layout, and hand-tuned implementation details can dominate the methodology. Python is noisier and more overhead-heavy, which makes it the more demanding setting for a timing methodology that must operate inside an RL loop. If optimization RL can learn useful algorithmic changes in this noisier setting, we expect the same ideas to

38

转移到开销较低的语言，例如 C++，尽管仍然需要特定于语言的校准。transfer to lower-overhead languages such as C++, although language-specific calibration would still be needed.

原始测试规模太小，无法计时。Original tests are too small for timing.最初的 DMC 测试是为了功能正确性而设计的，而不是为了测量效率。即使在清理之后，原始类别仍然非常小：在最终的 RL 训练分割中，对于公共、私有和 DMC 生成的测试，输入加输出大小的中位数为 18、24 和 31 个字符。在原始类别中，正确人类解决方案的汇总平均执行持续时间仅为 0The original DMC tests were designed for functional correctness, not for measuring efficiency. Even after cleaning, the original categories remain extremely small: on the final RL training split, median input-plus-output sizes are 18, 24, and 31 characters for public, private, and DMC-generated tests. Across the original categories, the pooled mean execution duration over correct human solutions is only 0.088 秒，与088 s, withp 95 = 0 .145秒和145 s andp 99 = 0 .463秒（表21）。在该规模下，10-100 毫秒的加性扰动可以匹配或超过信号。 A 节中的模拟研究表明了为什么这种机制很脆弱：在 100 毫秒左右的附加噪声下，对如此快速的测试进行基于超时的比较的准确度可能会下降到大约 55%，几乎是偶然的。相对运行时比较更稳定，因为它们聚合了许多每次测试的比较，而不是依赖于一个超时边界，但当测试如此短时，它们仍然很弱；只有当更大的输入、更长的运行时间和更多的测试使定时信号大于执行本底噪声时，它们才变得可靠。463 s (Table 21). At that scale, additive perturbations of 10–100 ms can match or exceed the signal. The simulation study in Section A shows why this regime is fragile: with additive noise around 100 ms, timeout-based comparisons on such fast tests can fall to roughly 55% accuracy, close to chance. Relative runtime comparisons are more stable because they aggregate many per-test comparisons instead of depending on one timeout boundary, but they remain weak while the tests are this short; they become reliable only when larger inputs, longer runtimes, and more tests make the timing signal larger than the execution-noise floor.

原始测试作为正确性门也太弱了。Original tests are also too weak as a correctness gate.原始的 DMC 测试本身对于评估正确性也很弱。这很重要，因为本文比较了优化强化学习配方，这些配方可以在生成更快的程序和保持正确性之间进行权衡。如果种子训练数据已经让不正确的解决方案通过，那么模型通过率的后续变化就很难解释：它们可能来自不受控制的标签噪声，而不是来自正在测试的奖励或环境。因此，在询问 RL 配方是否会提高速度、降低正确性或同时改变两者之前，我们需要一个更清晰的正确性门。 CodeContests+（Wang 等人，2025）报告称，只有 67The original DMC tests are also weak for evaluating correctness itself. This matters because the paper compares optimization-RL recipes that can trade off generating faster programs against preserving correctness. If the seed training data already lets incorrect solutions pass, later changes in model pass rate are hard to interpret: they could come from uncontrolled label noise rather than from the reward or environment being tested. We therefore need a cleaner correctness gate before asking whether an RL recipe improves speed, degrades correctness, or changes both. CodeContests+ (Wang et al., 2025) reports that only 67.原始数据集中 1% 的突变生成测试通过了输入验证，并且超过 4 个1% of the mutation-generated tests in the original dataset pass input validation, and that more than 4,000 个问题将几乎所有正确的提交错误分类为不正确。我们自己的增强分析发现了相同的故障模式：在来自 3 个项目的随机 300 个问题样本中的 257 个问题中，000 problems misclassify nearly all correct submissions as incorrect. Our own augmentation analysis found the same failure mode: among the 257 problems in a random 300-problem sample from the 3,928 个执行验证池可生成正确性测试，其中 81 个仅在原始测试中误报率就已超过 5%。因此，原始 DMC 作为定时信号源和可靠的正确性门都受到限制。928 execution-validated pool for which generated correctness tests were available, 81 already had false-positive rates above 5% on the original tests alone. Thus raw DMC is limited both as a source of timing signal and as a reliable correctness gate.

重复数据删除和质量过滤。Deduplication and quality filtering.为了减少训练和评估之间的泄漏，我们根据 LiveCodeBench（Jain 等人，2024）等评估基准在问题级别进行重复数据删除。根据问题描述进行匹配。我们还删除了由指令调整模型识别出的格式错误、缺失或不明确的问题陈述。重复数据删除阶段和质量过滤阶段将语料库从 12 个减少到To reduce leakage between training and evaluation, we deduplicate at the problem level against evaluation benchmarks such as LiveCodeBench (Jain et al., 2024). Matching is performed on problem descriptions. We also remove malformed, missing, or ambiguous problem statements identified by an instruction-tuned model. The deduplication stage and quality filtering stage reduce the corpus from 12,275 至 11275 to 11,468 问题。468 problems.

溶液式过滤。Solution-type filtering.我们要求每个保留的问题都有正确和错误的Python解决方案。只有正确解决方案的问题没有阴性对照，因此无法检查测试套件是否有误报。仅包含错误解决方案的问题没有积极的控制，因此无法通过执行已知正确的代码来验证生成的测试。没有解决办法的问题不能用于建设或培训。此步骤删除 2We require every retained problem to have both correct and incorrect Python solutions. Problems with only correct solutions have no negative controls, so the test suite cannot be checked for false positives. Problems with only incorrect solutions have no positive controls, so generated tests cannot be validated by executing known-correct code. Problems with no solutions cannot be used for construction or training. This step removes 2,210 道仅正确题，1210 correct-only problems, 1,045 个仅不正确的问题，以及 1045 incorrect-only problems, and 1,507 个问题没有可用的解决方案，剩下 6 个507 problems with no usable solutions, leaving 6,706 问题。706 problems.

这种持续时间过滤性选择将数据集清洁度与优化准备情况分开。两者都是 1This duration-filterability selection separates dataset cleanliness from optimization readiness. Both the 1,302 持续时间可过滤的问题和 1302 duration-filterable problems and the 1,第421章 不可持续时间可过滤的补集来自同一个2421 non-duration-filterable complement come from the same 2,723问题清理语料库；区别不在于是否存在测试，而在于优化测试是否创建足够的持续时间范围来支持时间奖励。723-problem cleaned corpus; the difference is not whether tests exist, but whether the optimization tests create enough duration spread to support a timing reward.

B.4 测试生成B.4 Test generation

为什么要生成测试呢？Why generate tests at all.清理后的语料库仍然继承了主要的 DMC 限制：原始测试规模很小，并且经常无法暴露错误的提交。因此，测试生成不是可选的放大步骤；这种机制将经过验证的人类解决方案转变为更强大的通过/失败门，并单独转变为更大的计时工作负载。该构造故意要求模型仅提供输入生成器，而不是预期输出，以便可以根据可信的人类代码而不是另一个模型完成来检查生成的输入。The cleaned corpus still inherits the main DMC limitation: original tests are small and often fail to expose incorrect submissions. Test generation is therefore not an optional scale-up step; it is the mechanism that turns verified human solutions into a stronger pass/fail gate and, separately, into larger workloads for timing. The construction deliberately asks the model only for input generators, not for expected outputs, so that generated inputs can be checked against trusted human code rather than against another model completion.

40

生成设置。Generation setup.我们使用监督微调的 CWM 32B 检查点生成额外的测试。对于每个问题，我们采样 10 个独立的输入生成器候选者。提示包含完整的问题陈述和一个随机选择的经过验证的人类解决方案。不要求模型直接产生预期输出。它发出一个 Python InputGenerator 类，环境执行该类来生成候选输入。这一设计很重要：一个模型生成的 InputGenerator 类可以扩展到许多候选输入，而输出端仍然以可信参考代码为基础。受监督的检查点也是多样性的保守选择。生成器的工作不是一次性地持续解决任务；它是在重复采样下产生多样化的、尊重约束的输入程序。使用 SFT 检查点可以降低生成器已经崩溃到任务中狭窄的奖励驱动模式的风险，这会削弱对多个候选测试生成器进行采样的目的。We generate additional tests with a supervised fine-tuned CWM 32B checkpoint. For each problem, we sample 10 independent input-generator candidates. The prompt contains the full problem statement and one randomly selected verified human solution. The model is not asked to produce expected outputs directly. It emits a Python InputGenerator class, which the environment executes to produce candidate inputs. This design matters: one model-generated InputGenerator class can expand into many candidate inputs, while the output side remains grounded in trusted reference code. The supervised checkpoint is also a conservative choice for diversity. The generator’s job is not to consistently solve the task in a one-shot fashion; it is to produce varied, constraint-respecting input programs under repeated sampling. Using the SFT checkpoint reduces the risk that the generator has already collapsed toward a narrow reward-driven pattern on the task, which would undercut the purpose of sampling multiple candidate test generators.

这种设计在精神上与CodeContests+（Wang et al., 2025）很接近，后者也使用LLM编写的生成器来增强DMC风格的测试套件，但目标不同。对于我们的奖励设计来说，二元超时门是不够的：优化强化学习需要创建一系列持续时间的测试，以便正确的解决方案可以按效率进行排名，而不仅仅是分类为通过或超时。因此，我们存储可重复的输入/输出对，然后消除不明确的问题，而不是依赖自定义检查器。This design is close in spirit to CodeContests+ (Wang et al., 2025), which also uses LLM-written generators to strengthen DMC-style test suites, but the target is different. A binary time-limit-exceeded gate is not enough for our reward design: optimization RL needs tests that create a range of durations so correct solutions can be ranked by efficiency, not only classified as passing or timing out. We therefore store reproducible input/output pairs and later remove ambiguous problems rather than rely on custom checkers.

输入生成器任务环境。InputGenerator task environment.该环境实施两种活动变体。正确性活动重复对边缘、小和样本案例进行采样。优化活动以小换大。每次尝试总共执行 15 个方法调用，因此一个问题可以在 10 个采样生成器中生成最多 150 个候选测试。表 11 总结了数据集级过滤之前的接受循环。The environment implements two campaign variants. The correctness campaign repeatedly samples edge, small, and sample cases. The optimization campaign swaps small for large. Each attempt executes 15 method calls in total, so one problem can yield up to 150 candidate tests across the 10 sampled generators. Table 11 summarizes the acceptance loop before dataset-level filtering.

在此阶段，管道已生成更多测试，但尚未形成最终数据集。接受的生成器尝试仍然可能包含对于正确性检查来说太慢的输入，违反隐藏的约束，或者揭示所谓正确的解决方案被错误标记。因此，下一小节是裁决步骤，决定哪些测试、解决方案和问题能够幸存。At this stage the pipeline has produced more tests, not yet a final dataset. Accepted generator attempts can still contain inputs that are too slow for correctness checking, violate hidden constraints, or reveal that a supposedly correct solution was mislabeled. The next subsection is therefore the adjudication step that decides which tests, solutions, and problems survive.

生成提示。Generation prompt.图 14 显示了一个代表性的提示框架。真正的提示包含完整的问题陈述和一个随机选择的经过验证的人类解决方案，然后要求模型仅发出导入和 InputGenerator 类。每个方法调用都会返回一个随机输入字符串，因此重复调用同一生成的类会产生一个小型测试活动，而不是一个静态示例。Figure 14 shows a representative prompt skeleton. The real prompt contains the full problem statement and one randomly selected verified human solution, then asks the model to emit only imports and the InputGenerator class. Each method call returns one randomized input string, so repeatedly calling the same generated class yields a small test campaign rather than one static example.

测试生成的高级布局已在图 2 中显示，而表 10 给出了相应的问题数减少。因此，特定于生成的详细信息保留在提示框架和表 11 和表 12 中：模型提出输入生成器，人工解决方案定义输出，两个活动分别针对正确性覆盖范围和优化工作负载。The high-level placement of test generation is already shown in Figure 2, while Table 10 gives the corresponding problem-count reductions. The details that are specific to generation are therefore kept in the prompt skeleton and in Tables 11 and 12: the model proposes input generators, human solutions define the outputs, and the two campaigns separately target correctness coverage and optimization workloads.

B.5 过滤生成的测试B.5 Filtering generated tests

生成器生成候选测试，而不是最终评估套件。当针对已验证的正确解决方案执行新生成的测试并且其中一些解决方案失败时，失败可能意味着三种不同的情况。测试可能无效，因为它违反了约束或触发了不相关的运行时错误。测试可能是有效的，而失败的解决方案可能是新发现的误报。或者问题可能会承认同一输入有多个有效输出，从而在没有自定义检查器的情况下使得严格的输入/输出比较不合适。管道必须在测试、解决方案和整个问题中驾驭这种模糊性。The generators produce candidate tests, not final evaluation suites. When a newly generated test is executed against verified correct solutions and some of them fail, the failure can mean three different things. The test may be invalid because it violates constraints or triggers unrelated runtime errors. The test may be valid and the failing solution may be a newly discovered false positive. Or the problem may admit multiple valid outputs for the same input, making strict input/output comparison unsuitable without a custom checker. The pipeline has to navigate this ambiguity across tests, solutions, and whole problems.

这是 DMC-Optim 中的核心数据质量压力。积极的过滤会产生更清晰的标签，但可能会将池缩小到 RL 所需的规模以下。它还可能使保留池偏向于更简单的问题：如果生成的测试暴露出许多误报或许多阳性对照失败的每个问题都被删除，那么剩余的数据集可能主要包含原始测试已经足够的问题，很少有解决方案位于正确性边界附近的边缘情况，从而为强化学习留下很少的有用信号。相反，弱过滤可以保留规模，但会使奖励暴露于标签错误的解决方案或不明确的测试中。因此，我们在三个级别上进行过滤，并通过对小问题子集的手动案例研究来调整阈值，同时为最终的 RL 分割保留足够的持续时间可过滤问题。This is the central data-quality tension in DMC-Optim. Aggressive filtering produces cleaner labels but can shrink the pool below the scale needed for RL. It can also bias the retained pool toward easier problems: if every problem where generated tests expose many false positives or many positive-control failures is removed, the remaining dataset may mostly contain problems whose original tests were already adequate, with few edge cases where solutions sit near the correctness boundary, leaving little useful signal for RL. Conversely, weak filtering preserves scale but leaves the reward exposed to mislabeled solutions or ambiguous tests. We therefore filter at three levels and tune thresholds through manual case studies on small problem subsets, while preserving enough duration-filterable problems for the final RL split.

后生成过滤路径对应于图 2 中的测试增强和质量过滤阶段以及表 10 中的阶段 4-6。对于正确性测试分支，分布式重新执行输出通过问题标识符连接回增强的问题记录，然后管道删除病态生成的测试，删除标签与过滤套件相矛盾的解决方案，最后删除剩余标签仍然太弱的整个问题。同时，原始通过率诊断和手动案例指导阈值选择。这些诊断对于理解生成的测试所暴露的内容很有用，但最终的训练集使用过滤后的测试和过滤后的解决方案标签。The post-generation filtering path corresponds to the test-augmentation and quality-filtering stage in Figure 2 and to stages 4–6 in Table 10. For the correctness-test branch, distributed re-execution outputs are joined back to the augmented problem records by problem identifier, then the pipeline removes pathological generated tests, removes solutions whose labels are contradicted by the filtered suite, and finally removes whole problems whose remaining labels are still too weak. In parallel, raw pass-rate diagnostics and manual cases guide threshold selection. Those diagnostics are useful for understanding what the generated tests expose, but the final training set uses the filtered tests and filtered solution labels.

过滤参数和阈值。Filtering parameters and thresholds.表 13 列出了阈值作为简洁参考。以下段落在使用上下文中定义了每个速率和上限：首先是测试级别过滤器，然后是解决方案级别分类，最后是问题级别过滤。正确性清理回合更加严格，因为它负责重新标记解决方案、消除不明确的问题并减少误报。后面的优化轮次放宽了超时处理，因为大输入超时是效率信号的一部分，而不是无效测试的必然证据。Table 13 lists the thresholds as a compact reference. The following paragraphs define each rate and cap in the context where it is used: first the test-level filters, then solutionlevel classification, and finally problem-level filtering. The correctness-cleaning round is stricter because it is responsible for relabeling solutions, removing ambiguous problems, and reducing false positives. The later optimization round relaxes timeout handling because large-input timeouts are part of the efficiency signal rather than necessarily evidence of an invalid test.

42

表 13 后处理过滤参数。Table 13 Post-processing filtering parameters.该表是阈值的紧凑索引；接下来的段落定义了分母、上限和上下文中的决策规则。正确性回合是主要的正确性清理阶段。优化轮次重复使用相同的异常过滤和正确解决方案下限，但放宽了超时处理，因为大输入超时是效率信号的一部分，而不一定是错误。The table is a compact index of the thresholds; the paragraphs that follow define the denominators, caps, and decision rules in context. The correctness round is the main correctness-cleaning stage. The optimization round reuses the same exception filtering and correct-solution drop cap, but relaxes timeout handling because large-input timeouts are part of the efficiency signal rather than necessarily an error.
