实施不变量。Implementation invariants.过滤器永远不会删除原始的公共、私人或 DMC 生成的测试；他们只从新生成的测试套件中删除列。当生成的测试数量超出了上限所允许的删除阈值时，测试将按违规分数进行排序，最差的测试（这意味着那些产生最多阳性对照失败的测试）将首先被删除。异常/失败过滤器排除语法错误结果，因此它的目标是生成的输入，这些输入使经过验证的正确解决方案在运行时失败或产生错误的输出，而不是解决方案解析器失败。在每个测试删除阶段中，过滤通过标准是单调的：删除生成的测试只能保留或增加通过过滤套件的解决方案集。然后，问题级门决定是否可以接受任何剩余的误报或正确解决方案的损耗。The filters never remove original public, private, or DMC-generated tests; they only remove columns from the newly generated test suite. When more generated tests exceed a removal threshold than the cap allows, the tests are sorted by the offending fraction and the worst tests, meaning those that create the most positive-control failures, are removed first. The exception/failure filter excludes syntax-error outcomes, so it targets generated inputs that make verified-correct solutions fail at runtime or produce wrong outputs rather than solution-parser failures. Within each test-removal stage, the filtered pass criterion is monotone: removing generated tests can only keep or increase the set of solutions that pass the filtered suite. The problem-level gates then decide whether any remaining false-positive or correct-solution attrition is acceptable.

测试级过滤器。Test-level filters.对于正确性测试，超时证明生成的输入过于激进，无法用作可靠的通过/失败检查。我们删除了超过 20% 的正确解决方案超出时间限制的测试，以及超过 40% 引发运行时异常或错误输出失败的测试。对于优化测试，大量输入是有意的。超时可以准确地表示测试探测有效输入空间的较慢部分的信号。因此，我们在优化测试中容忍高达 90% 的超时率。仅当几乎所有正确的解决方案都超时时，测试才变得无法提供信息，因为这样它就不再区分较快的实现和较慢的实现。超时过滤器可以删除最多 25% 的问题生成的测试，而后面的异常/失败过滤器可以删除最多 30% 的剩余生成的测试。这些上限可以防止少量不良样本删除原本有用的套件，同时仍然删除最有可能无效的测试。For correctness tests, a timeout is evidence that the generated input is too aggressive to serve as a reliable pass/fail check. We remove tests on which more than 20% of correct solutions exceed the time limit, and tests on which more than 40% raise runtime exceptions or wrong-output failures. For optimization tests, large inputs are intentional. A timeout can be exactly the signal that a test probes a slower part of the valid input space. We therefore tolerate timeout rates up to 90% on an optimization test. The test becomes uninformative only when almost all correct solutions time out, because then it no longer separates faster from slower implementations. The timeout filter can remove at most 25% of a problem’s generated tests, while the later exception/failure filter can remove at most 30% of the remaining generated tests. These caps prevent a small number of bad samples from erasing an otherwise useful suite, while still removing the tests most likely to be invalid.

解决方案级别分类。Solution-level classification.经过测试级过滤后，过滤后的增强套件用于更新正负解池。经验证正确的解决方案仅在通过过滤套件时才保留为阳性对照；否则，它将从保留的正确解决方案池中删除。仅当不正确的解决方案仍然未能通过过滤套件时，它才仍然是阴性对照；如果它通过了更强的套件，它就不再是可靠的负例，并从保留的错误解决方案池中删除。这是将生成的测试证据转化为 B.6 节中报告的解决方案计数变化的步骤。如果生成没有针对某个问题产生可用的测试，则此步骤无法修复原始测试套件；该类别中的高误报问题只能稍后通过问题级别门删除。After test-level filtering, the filtered augmented suite is used to update the positive and negative solution pools. A verified-correct solution remains a positive control only if it passes the filtered suite; otherwise it is removed from the retained correct-solution pool. An incorrect solution remains a negative control only if it still fails the filtered suite; if it passes the stronger suite, it is no longer a reliable negative example and is removed from the retained incorrect-solution pool. This is the step that turns generated-test evidence into the solution-count changes reported in Section B.6. If generation produces no usable tests for a problem, this step cannot repair the original test suite; high-false-positive problems in that category can only be removed later by the problem-level gate.

问题级过滤器。Problem-level filters.在正确性清理回合中，保留问题必须保留至少 5 个经过验证的正确解，最多丢失原始正确解的 25%，并且增强后误报率低于 5%。假阳性率是在原始错误解决方案池上计算的：如果超过 5% 的阴性对照通过了增强套件，则问题将被消除，而不是通过删除大部分难阴性示例来使问题看起来很干净。这消除了不明确的多输出问题、错误标记的正确解决方案以及薄弱的原始套件。导致太多正确解决方案在许多生成的测试中失败的问题可能不适合严格的输入/输出评估。仍然让太多错误解决方案通过的问题无法提供可靠的奖励信号。在后面的优化轮次中，问题已经通过了更严格的正确性清理阶段，因此不以优化测试为主In the correctness-cleaning round, a retained problem must keep at least 5 verified correct solutions, lose at most 25% of its original correct solutions, and have false-positive rate below 5% after augmentation. The false-positive rate is computed over the original incorrect-solution pool: if more than 5% of those negative controls pass the augmented suite, the problem is removed rather than made to look clean by deleting most of its hard negative examples. This removes ambiguous multi-output problems, mislabeled correct solutions, and weak original suites. Problems that make too many correct solutions fail across many generated tests are likely unsuitable for strict input/output evaluation. Problems that still let too many incorrect solutions pass cannot provide reliable reward signal. In the later optimization round, the problem has already passed the stricter correctness-cleaning stage, so optimization tests are not used as the primary

43

误报过滤器。我们仍然将正确解决方案的损耗上限限制在 25%，并要求至少有一个幸存的正确解决方案，但我们让超时作为持续时间结果，而不是作为立即的正确性判决。false-positive filter. We still cap correct-solution attrition at 25% and require at least one surviving correct solution, but we let timeouts function as duration outcomes rather than as immediate correctness verdicts.

300 个问题样本：假阳性减少。300-problem sample: false-positive reduction.图 15 至 17 显示了生成的正确性测试对来自 3 个项目的随机 300 个问题样本的影响。Figures 15 to 17 show the effect of generated correctness tests on a random 300-problem sample from the 3,第928章 执行验证池。生成的测试可用于这 300 个问题中的 257 个。在这 257 个问题中，平均假阳性率从 8.27% 下降到 2.11%，中位假阳性率从 0.60% 变化到 0.00%，49 个问题从非零假阳性率变为没有观察到假阳性。高于 5% 假阳性阈值的问题数量从 81 个下降到 27 个； 10%和20%以上的问题数量从60个减少到17个，从43个减少到8个。假阳性率分别减少或保持不变，有120个和137个问题，没有增加。代价是更严格的积极控制：平均正确解决方案通过率从 99.99% 下降到 89.98%。这些数字在上面的超时和异常/失败过滤器之前使用原始生成的测试通过/失败结果，因此它们测量生成的测试暴露的内容。最终的训练集是经过测试级、解决方案级和问题级过滤后更保守的工件。928 execution-validated pool. Generated tests were available for 257 of these 300 problems. On those 257 problems, mean false-positive rate drops from 8.27% to 2.11%, the median false-positive rate moves from 0.60% to 0.00%, and 49 problems go from a nonzero false-positive rate to no observed false positives. The number of problems above the 5% false-positive threshold drops from 81 to 27; the counts above 10% and 20% drop from 60 to 17 and from 43 to 8. False-positive rate is reduced or unchanged on 120 and 137 problems respectively, with no increases. The cost is stricter positive control: mean correct-solution pass rate drops from 99.99% to 89.98%. These figures use raw generated-test pass/fail outcomes, before the timeout and exception/failure filters above, so they measure what the generated tests expose. The final training set is the more conservative artifact after test-level, solution-level, and problem-level filtering.

300 个问题样本：正确解决方案通过率成本。300-problem sample: correct-solution pass-rate cost.假阳性的减少并不是免费的。对于相同的 257 个生成测试问题，由于与超时相关的重新执行随机性，正确解决方案通过率在 134 个问题上下降，在 121 个问题上保持不变，在 2 个问题上增加。下降问题中，平均下降19.21个百分点，中位下降6.40个百分点，最大下降89.77个百分点。尾部激发了 25% 问题级正确解决方案下降上限：在问题级过滤之前，53 个问题下降超过 10 分，34 个问题下降超过 25 分，20 个问题下降超过 50 分。The false-positive reduction is not free. On the same 257 generated-test problems, correct-solution pass rate drops on 134 problems, is unchanged on 121, and increases on 2 because of timeout-related re-execution stochasticity. Among the dropped problems, the mean drop is 19.21 percentage points, the median drop is 6.40 points, and the largest drop is 89.77 points. The tail motivates the 25% problem-level correct-solution drop cap: 53 problems drop by more than 10 points, 34 by more than 25 points, and 20 by more than 50 points before problem-level filtering.

50 个问题诊断：为什么需要过滤以及示例。50-problem diagnostics: why filtering is needed and examples.图 15 至图 17 以及表 14 和表 15 中显示的 300 个问题给出了标题统计数据；图 18 至 20 和表 16 至 18 中的 50 个问题切片解释了过滤器背后的机制。在 50 个问题中，40 个问题接受了生成的正确性测试，10 个问题没有。在生成的测试子集中，平均假阳性率从 6.67% 下降到 1.64%，中位假阳性率从 0.43% 下降到 0.00%，并且没有问题在增强后具有更高的假阳性率。与此同时，平均正确解通过率从 100.00% 下降到 87.07%。因此，有用的问题不在于生成的测试只是“好”还是“坏”，而在于正确解决方案通过率的下降是陈旧标签的证据，这是由无效或过大的生成测试引起的，以及哪些问题仍然太模糊而无法保留。当正确解通过率仅下降The 300-problem displays in Figures 15 to 17 and Tables 14 and 15 give the headline statistics; the 50-problem slice in Figures 18 to 20 and Tables 16 to 18 explains the mechanisms behind the filters. Out of 50 problems, 40 received generated correctness tests and 10 did not. On the generated-test subset, the mean false-positive rate falls from 6.67% to 1.64%, the median false-positive rate falls from 0.43% to 0.00%, and no problem has a higher false-positive rate after augmentation. At the same time, mean correct-solution pass rate falls from 100.00% to 87.07%. The useful question is therefore not whether generated tests are simply “good” or “bad”, but which drops in correct-solution pass rate are evidence of stale labels, which are caused by invalid or over-large generated tests, and which problems remain too ambiguous to keep. When the correct-solution pass rate drops by only∼1%，新测试是否发现了一小部分有问题的解决方案，或者只是测量噪声？当下降幅度达到 50% 或更多时，测试是否发现了许多过时的标签，或者是否引入了超时或约束工件？当误报消失而不损害阳性对照时，问题能否保持干净利落？当没有生成可用的生成测试时，是否应该删除而不是修复高误报问题？1%, did the new tests find a small family of buggy solutions, or only measurement noise? When the drop is 50% or more, did the tests discover many stale labels, or did they introduce timeout or constraint artifacts? When false positives disappear without hurting positive controls, can the problem be kept as a clean win? When no usable generated tests are produced, should a high-false-positive problem be removed rather than repaired?

阅读图 18 至图 20 时应牢记这一区别。在许多问题上都可以看到所需的模式：假阳性曲线向下移动，而正确解曲线保持在 1 附近。在这些情况下，生成的测试会在不干扰阳性对照的情况下增加覆盖范围。这种不对称是机械性的：只有添加测试才能减少误报，但由于多种原因，正确解决方案的通过率可能会下降。因此，正确解图的左尾部是不能跳过过滤的原因，也是出现歧义情况的地方。原始生成的套件可能会使许多经过验证正确的解决方案失败，因为测试发现了真正的边缘情况，因为它对于正确性套件来说太慢，因为它违反了Figures 18 to 20 should be read with that distinction in mind. The desired pattern is visible on many problems: the false-positive curve moves down while the correct-solution curve stays near one. Those are the cases where generated tests add coverage without disturbing positive controls. The asymmetry is mechanical: false positives can only decrease when tests are added, but correct-solution pass rate can decrease for several reasons. The left tail of the correct-solution plot is therefore the reason filtering cannot be skipped, and it is also where ambiguous cases surface. A raw generated suite can make many verified-correct solutions fail because a test found a genuine edge case, because it is too slow for a correctness suite, because it violates a

隐藏约束，因此不公平地惩罚尊重问题边界的解决方案，或者因为它暴露了严格输入/输出语义不明确的问题。hidden constraint and therefore unfairly penalizes solutions that respect the problem boundaries, or because it exposes a problem whose strict input/output semantics are ambiguous.

表 16 中的手动案例使这种歧义变得具体。 Train/103 有一个小下降，手动检查确认为标签修复：生成的测试消除了 26 个误报，而只有三个先前验证正确的解决方案失败。相比之下，train/67 和 train/88 的下降幅度太大，无法视为无害的证据。因此，管道将正确解决方案失败视为需要解释的证据，而不是作为每个失败解决方案都有独立错误的证据。跨解决方案的模式很重要：一些特殊的解决方案未能满足有效的边缘情况，这与一大系列类似的解决方案一起失败不同，这可能会揭示一个共享的错误，但也可能意味着生成的测试已经改变了问题边界。生成的测试的模式也很重要：拒绝同一解决方案系列的多个不同测试表明这些解决方案中缺少属性，而拒绝许多阳性对照的一个单独生成的测试更有可能太慢、不符合规范或不明确。过滤规则使用这些模式来决定是否The manual cases in Table 16 make this ambiguity concrete. Train/103 has a small drop that manual inspection confirms as label repair: generated tests remove 26 false positives while only three previously verified-correct solutions fail. By contrast, train/67 and train/88 have drops that are too large to treat as harmless evidence. The pipeline therefore treats correct-solution failures as evidence to explain, not as proof that every failing solution has an independent bug. The pattern across solutions matters: a few idiosyncratic solutions failing a valid edge case differs from a large family of similar solutions failing together, which may reveal a shared bug but may also mean that the generated test has shifted the problem boundary. The pattern across generated tests matters as well: several diverse tests rejecting the same solution family suggests a missing property in those solutions, while one isolated generated test rejecting many positive controls is more likely to be too slow, out-of-spec, or ambiguous. The filtering rules use these patterns to decide whether to

删除测试、更新解决方案池或消除问题。remove tests, update the solution pools, or remove the problem.

图 19 和 20 中的执行状态图解释了为什么相同的通过率下降需要不同的响应。经过验证正确的解决方案的超时模式意味着生成的输入对于正确性检查来说过于昂贵，即使它捕获了不正确的解决方案。失败或异常较多的模式可以The execution-status plots in Figures 19 and 20 explain why the same pass-rate drop requires different responses. A timeout-heavy pattern on verified-correct solutions means the generated input is too expensive for a correctness check, even if it catches an incorrect solution. A failure- or exception-heavy pattern can

表 15 来自执行验证池的随机 300 个问题样本的最大假阳性减少。Table 15 Largest false-positive reductions in a random 300-problem sample from the execution-validated pool.这些例子表明，总体假阳性的减少不仅仅是由小解决方案数量的案例驱动的。有些问题得到改善，但正确解决方案的通过率没有损失，而另一些问题后来被删除，因为生成的测试对于阳性对照来说过于严格。These examples show that the aggregate false-positive reduction is not driven only by small-solution-count cases. Some problems improve with no loss of correct-solution pass rate, while others are later removed because the generated tests are too strict for positive controls.

指示不符合规格的输入、缺少检查器或多输出歧义。在这些情况下，首先删除最差的生成测试并不会削弱基准测试；相反，它可以防止无效或过大的测试丢弃其他有用的问题。拆卸盖定义了何时该修复仍然可以接受。如果可以通过仅删除一小部分生成的导致许多阳性对照失败的测试来恢复问题，那么它仍然有价值。如果需要删除太多生成的套件，则证据是不明确的：要么阳性对照仍然是事实来源，并且许多生成的测试是错误的，要么生成的测试揭示了标记为正确的一大系列解决方案中的共享错误模式。我们消除此类问题，而不是用粗略的阈值来决定；更精细的裁决程序需要联合分析解决方案集群和生成的测试集群。indicate an out-of-spec input, a missing checker, or a multi-output ambiguity. In those cases, removing the worst generated tests first is not weakening the benchmark; it prevents one invalid or over-large test from discarding an otherwise useful problem. The removal caps define when this repair is still acceptable. If a problem can be recovered by removing only a small fraction of generated tests that fail many positive controls, it remains valuable. If it requires removing too much of the generated suite, the evidence is ambiguous: either the positive controls remain the source of truth and many generated tests are wrong, or the generated tests have revealed a shared bug pattern in a large family of solutions labeled correct. We remove such problems rather than decide this with a coarse threshold; a finer adjudication procedure would need to analyze clusters of solutions and clusters of generated tests jointly.

表 16 至表 18 中的示例表解释了为什么在聚合曲线中看起来相似的问题可能需要不同的决策。一些生成的套件是干净的胜利，消除了误报，同时保留了阳性对照。有些是干净的无操作，原始测试已经足够了。有些解决方案暴露了以前标记为正确的解决方案中的真正错误，因此适度的正确解决方案下降是可取的。其他失败的原因是生成的测试太慢、不符合规范或即使在移除上限后也太激进。这就是为什么在性能图之前说明过滤规则的原因：目的不是不惜一切代价最大限度地减少误报，而是在明确的阳性控制预算下减少误报，同时保留仍然可以教导 RL 系统的边界问题。The example tables in Tables 16 to 18 explain why problems that look similar in the aggregate curves can require different decisions. Some generated suites are clean wins, removing false positives while preserving positive controls. Some are clean no-ops, where the original tests were already sufficient. Some expose real bugs in solutions previously labeled correct, so a modest correct-solution drop is desirable. Others fail because generation produced tests that are too slow, out-of-spec, or too aggressive even after capped removal. This is why the filtering rules are stated before the performance plots: the purpose is not to maximize false-positive reduction at any cost, but to reduce false positives under an explicit positive-control budget while keeping the borderline problems that can still teach the RL system.

表 16 中的八个示例故意是异构的。 Train/103 是理想的情况：生成的测试消除了一个大的假阳性口袋，几乎没有阳性对照损失。 Train/64 也很有用，即使没有任何变化，因为它表明当原始套件已经足够时，允许保守的增强。 Train/20 的情况阻止了对正确解决方案损耗的简单解释：7.33pp 的下降不会被视为噪声，因为手动检查显示生成的测试是有效的，并揭示了 DMC 标记为正确的解决方案中的真正错误。 Train/77 说明了为什么问题级别上限不应太严格。它的假阳性率大幅下降，从 20.28% 下降到 3.00%，正确解率下降，从 100.00% 下降到 80.52%，这是不平凡但可接受的；手动检查表明这确实是一个难题，其中许多微妙之处并未由原始测试套件控制。删除此类情况会使数据集偏离硬边界区域，而在硬边界区域中更强的测试很重要。The eight examples in Table 16 are deliberately heterogeneous. Train/103 is the ideal case: the generated tests remove a large false-positive pocket with almost no positive-control loss. Train/64 is also useful, even though nothing changes, because it shows that augmentation is allowed to be conservative when the original suite is already adequate. Train/20 is the case that prevents a simplistic interpretation of correct-solution attrition: a 7.33pp drop is not treated as noise because manual inspection shows the generated test is valid and reveals genuine bugs in solutions that DMC had labeled correct. Train/77 shows why the problem-level cap should not be too strict. It has a large false-positive reduction, from 20.28% to 3.00%, and a nontrivial but acceptable correct-solution drop, from 100.00% to 80.52%; manual inspection suggests this is indeed a hard problem where many subtleties were not controlled by the original test suite. Removing such cases would bias the dataset away from the hard boundary region where stronger tests matter.

表 16 中被拒绝的案例解释了阈值的另一面。 Train/67 捕获误报，但它是通过超时生成的输入来实现的，这使得大多数正确的解决方案失败。 Train/97 和 train/86 显示了违反约束的模式，其中生成的测试暴露了不正确的解决方案，但也要求经过验证的正确代码来处理超出预期规范的输入。 Train/88 显示了上限过滤后的残余故障模式：生成的套件始终拒绝大多数阳性对照，并且生成的测试没有足够大的子集与足够大的阳性对照子集一致。因此，该问题被删除而不是强行放入数据集中。The rejected cases in Table 16 explain the other side of the threshold. Train/67 catches a false positive, but it does so through timeout-heavy generated inputs that make most correct solutions fail. Train/97 and train/86 show constraint-violation patterns, where generated tests expose incorrect solutions but also ask verified-correct code to handle inputs outside the intended specification. Train/88 shows the residual failure mode after capped filtering: the generated suite consistently rejects most positive controls, and no sufficiently large subset of generated tests agrees with a sufficiently large subset of positive controls. The problem is therefore removed rather than forced into the dataset.

表 17 中的未生成测试用例给出了不同的教训。他们不仅缺少额外保障的来源，而且还缺少额外的保障。它们可能正是需要额外保险的情况。在 50 个问题的切片中，train/24、train/26、train/131 和 train/135 在增强之前的假阳性率均高于 5%，但是The no-generated-test cases in Table 17 teach a different lesson. They are not merely missing a source of extra coverage; they may be exactly the cases where extra coverage was needed. In the 50-problem slice, train/24, train/26, train/131, and train/135 all have false-positive rates above 5% before augmentation, but

一代人没有为他们提供可用的测试。解决方案级步骤没有新的证据来重新标记这些问题，因此唯一保守的选择是通过问题级误报门去除。这是最终池不应被理解为到处添加测试的结果的另一个原因。它是生成、过滤和原始控制共同留下可用正确性信号的子集。一个有用的后续工作是研究为什么输入生成器综合在这些问题上失败，即使模型同时看到问题陈述和正确的解决方案：它们可能需要从示例解决方案进行更困难的程序推理，具有不寻常的输入结构，或者依赖于未声明的、隐藏在问题文本中的约束，甚至从解决方案代码也很难推断。generation produced no usable tests for them. The solution-level step has no new evidence to relabel these problems, so the only conservative option is removal by the problem-level false-positive gate. This is another reason the final pool should not be understood as the result of adding tests everywhere. It is the subset where generation, filtering, and the original controls jointly leave a usable correctness signal. A useful follow-up would be to study why input-generator synthesis fails on these problems even when the model sees both the problem statement and a correct solution: they may require harder program reasoning from the example solution, have unusual input structure, or depend on constraints that are unstated, hidden in the problem text, and hard to infer even from solution code.

表 18 中列出了最大跌幅表，以避免误导成功案例。即使误报率降至零，大量正确解的下降也不会自动带来好处。 Train/67 和 train/88 表明，明显的假阳性改善可能来自压倒阳性对照的测试。 train/13 和 train/49 显示了不同的警告信号：正确解决方案大幅下降，但没有假阳性益处，这表明添加的测试将通过/失败边界转向阳性对照，而不是向原始套件错过的假阳性转移。相比之下，train/77 仍然低于 25% 的正确丢弃上限，同时减少了The largest-drop table in Table 18 is included to avoid misleading success stories. Large correct-solution drops are not automatically beneficial, even when false positives fall to zero. Train/13 and train/49 show a different warning sign: large correct-solution drops with no false-positive benefit, suggesting that the added tests shift the pass/fail boundary against positive controls rather than toward the false positives missed by the original suite. 误报率低于 5%，因此它代表了管道应保留的那种困难但可用的情况。该上限消除了生成的测试拒绝太多阳性对照的问题，同时仍然保留了生成的测试暴露原始套件遗漏的微妙之处的难题。false positives below 5%, so it represents the kind of hard-but-usable case the pipeline should retain. The cap removes problems where generated tests reject too many positive controls, while still keeping hard problems where generated tests expose subtleties missed by the original suite.

第二生成阶段在正确性测试之后添加了优化测试，为该逻辑又增加了一层。对于正确性测试，许多阳性对照的超时通常是一个警告，表明生成的输入对于通过/失败判断而言太大。对于优化测试，我们故意推动更大的输入，因此一些正确的人类解决方案超时根本不是缺陷：它可以帮助创建区分更快和更慢正确实现的斜率。有用的信号不仅是每个测试的硬度，还包括整个优化测试池的多样性。我们希望通过不同的测试来强调解决方案的不同压力点，从而揭示哪些实现因输入大小、值范围、图形形状、组合结构或其他工作负载属性而失败。如果所有生成的优化测试都使同一解决方案子集超时，则该套件会限制下游使用：它的行为就像一个重复的超时门，而不是排名信号。这个池级别的要求也限制了每个单独的测试：两个极端都是没有用的。如果几乎每个正确的解决方案都超时，那么测试就只是一个超时门，不再对速度进行排名。如果没有强调正确的解决方案，则测试几乎不会增加优化信号。有用的机制是中间，其中优化测试在不同的解决方案和输入模式中创建持续时间分布和部分超时，同时仍然留下足够的成功执行来比较正确的解决方案。The second generation phase, which adds optimization tests after the correctness tests, adds one more layer to this logic. For correctness tests, a timeout on many positive controls is usually a warning that the generated input is too large for pass/fail adjudication. For optimization tests, we deliberately push toward larger inputs, so some correct human solutions timing out is not at all a defect: it can help create the slope that separates faster and slower correct implementations. The useful signal is not only per-test hardness, but diversity across the optimization-test pool. We want different tests to stress different pressure points of the solutions, exposing which implementations fail because of input size, value ranges, graph shape, combinatorial structure, or other workload properties. If all generated optimization tests time out the same subset of solutions, the suite has limited downstream use: it behaves like one repeated timeout gate rather than a ranking signal. This pool-level requirement also constrains each individual test: neither extreme is useful. If nearly every correct solution times out, the test becomes only a timeout gate and no longer ranks speed. If no correct solution is stressed, the test adds little optimization signal. The useful regime is the middle, where optimization tests create duration spread and partial timeouts across diverse solution and input patterns while still leaving enough successful executions to compare correct solutions.

总而言之，图 15 至 17 以及表 14 和 15 中研究的 300 个问题池以及图 18 至 20 和表 16 至 18 中对 50 个问题池的细粒度人工审查都支持相同的保守解释。生成的正确性测试暴露了原始 DMC 遗漏的许多误报，但原始增强套件不被视为基本事实，因为原始增强混合了标签修复、有效的边缘情况发现、过大的工作负载、约束违规、生成失败以及严格的输入/输出语义对于该管道来说太脆弱的问题。保留的语料库仅保留通过测试级别、解决方案级别和问题级别检查的子集，同时避免使用会删除每个困难边界情况的过滤器。Taken together, the 300-problem pool studied in Figures 15 to 17 and Tables 14 and 15 and the fine-grained manual review of the 50-problem pool in Figures 18 to 20 and Tables 16 to 18 support the same conservative interpretation. Generated correctness tests expose many false positives missed by original DMC, but the raw augmented suite is not treated as ground truth because raw augmentation mixes label repair, valid edge-case discovery, over-large workloads, constraint violations, generation failures, and problems whose strict input/output semantics are too brittle for this pipeline. The retained corpus keeps only the subset that survives test-level, solution-level, and problem-level checks, while avoiding a filter that would remove every difficult boundary case.

B.6 选择持续时间之前还剩下什么B.6 What remains before duration selection

问题和解决方案很重要。Problem and solution counts.表 19 报告了 B.5 节中诊断分析的生产规模对应部分：生成测试过滤之后和应用持续时间过滤性之前的计数。此时，语料库已经清理了标签，并生成了正确性并生成了优化测试，但尚未仅限于具有足够时间分布的问题。在构建过程中需要不正确的解决方案，因为它们会测量误报，但它们不会用作培训中的部署Table 19 reports the production-scale counterpart of the diagnostic analysis in Section B.5: the counts after generated-test filtering and before applying duration filterability. This is the point where the corpus has cleaned labels plus both generated correctness and generated optimization tests, but it has not yet been restricted to problems with enough timing spread. Incorrect solutions are needed during construction because they measure false positives, but they are not used as training rollouts in the

最大的生产损耗发生在测试增强过滤阶段：执行验证池从 3 下降The largest production attrition happens at the test-augmentation filtering stage: the execution-validated pool drops from 3,928 比 3928 to 3,061 个问题，删除 867 个问题，占总数的 22.1%。在解决方案级别，这保留了 85.1% 的已验证正确解决方案和 69.1% 的错误解决方案池。这种不对称是预料之中的：生成的正确性测试有意从不正确的池中删除误报和不可靠的阴性对照，而正确解决方案的下降则受到限制，以避免保留生成的测试显得噪音太大或未指定的问题。061 problems, removing 867 problems, or 22.1% of the pool. At the solution level, this retains 85.1% of the verified-correct solutions and 69.1% of the incorrect-solution pool. That asymmetry is expected: generated correctness tests intentionally remove false positives and unreliable negative controls from the incorrect pool, while the correct-solution drop is capped to avoid keeping problems where generated tests appear too noisy or underspecified.

这样就完成了构造正确性的一半。剩下的问题是不同的：在清理标签以及生成的正确性和优化测试的问题中，哪些具有持续时间变化足以定义有用的计时目标的优化测试？This completes the correctness half of the construction. The remaining question is different: among problems with cleaned labels and both generated correctness and optimization tests, which ones have optimization tests whose durations vary enough to define a useful timing objective?

我们的实验中使用的语料库变体。Corpus variants used in our experiments.在这个阶段，我们维护两个语料库变体。完整变体大约为 117 GB，包含所有正确和错误的解决方案、所有测试类别以及每个测试执行持续时间的元数据。轻量级版本大约为 35 GB，为每个问题保留一个正确的解决方案和一个错误的解决方案，并且不包括优化测试以加快开发迭代速度。两种变体共享相同的分割。At this stage we maintain two corpus variants. The full variant is approximately 117 GB and contains all correct and incorrect solutions, all test categories, and per-test execution-duration metadata. The lightweight variant is approximately 35 GB, keeps one correct and one incorrect solution per problem, and excludes optimization tests for faster development iteration. Both variants share the same split.

B.7 持续时间过滤性B.7 Duration filterability

持续时间过滤性是测量门，而不是另一个标签清洁过滤器。基于持续时间的奖励需要问题内的时间分布：如果所有正确的解决方案在所有测试中几乎同时运行，则奖励只能放大噪声或常数因子伪影。对于每个问题的每个测试类别，我们通过经过验证的正确人类解决方案汇总运行时间，以获得每个测试的一个代表性持续时间：Duration filterability is the measurement gate, not another label-cleaning filter. Duration-based rewards need within-problem timing spread: if all correct solutions run in nearly the same time on all tests, a reward can only amplify noise or constant-factor artifacts. For each test category of each problem, we aggregate runtimes over verified correct human solutions to obtain one representative duration per test:d¯ j是测试的平均运行时间is the average runtime of testj跨越这些解决方案。然后我们定义鲁棒变异系数across those solutions. We then define the robust coefficient of variation

使用 IQR 而不是标准差可以使指标对少量异常值测试不敏感，而除以中位数则可以使其在具有不同绝对运行时间的问题之间具有可比性。当该值至少为 0.3 时，我们称问题可持续时间过滤，这意味着四分位间距至少为中位持续时间的 30%。当这种情况成立时，按持续时间过滤或排名可以揭示快速和慢速解决方案之间的有意义的差异。如果没有，可用的测试大多会执行相同的工作负载，并且基于持续时间的奖励会崩溃到时间噪音。Using IQR rather than standard deviation keeps the metric insensitive to a small number of outlier tests, while dividing by the median makes it comparable across problems with different absolute runtimes. We call a problem duration-filterable when this value is at least 0.3, meaning the interquartile spread is at least 30% of the median duration. When this condition holds, filtering or ranking by duration can expose meaningful differences between fast and slow solutions. When it does not, the available tests mostly exercise the same workload, and duration-based rewards collapse toward timing noise.

我们故意不根据固定测试中正确的人类解决方案的运行时间分布来定义可过滤性。这种替代方案将使问题选择偏向于可用人类解决方案池的偶然组成：低扩散可能仅仅意味着保留的解决方案是同质的，而高扩散可能是由一些常数因子异常值或病态但仍然正确的实现驱动的。即使所有测试都保持亚秒级并因此暴露于附加时序噪声下，它也可以使问题看起来信息丰富。相反，我们使用跨测试的变化，这更直接地是测试套件的属性。We deliberately do not define filterability from the spread of runtimes across correct human solutions on a fixed test. That alternative would bias problem selection toward the incidental composition of the available human-solution pool: low spread may simply mean that the retained solutions are homogeneous, while high spread may be driven by a few constant-factor outliers or pathological but still correct implementations. It can also make a problem look informative even when all tests remain sub-second and therefore exposed to additive timing noise. We instead use variation across tests, which is more directly a property of the test suite.

一些环境变体在执行之前使用测试长度，因此我们还通过测试长度和持续时间之间的皮尔逊相关性来跟踪长度过滤性，Some environment variants use test length before execution, so we also track length filterability through the Pearson correlation between test length and duration, withr≥r ≥ 0 .9 作为代理阈值。此属性9 as the proxy threshold. This property

52

回答了一个不同的问题：不是计时是否提供信息，而是序列化长度是否可以在不执行测试的情况下代替计时。我们仍然需要最终优化数据集的持续时间扩展标准，因为即使运行时间更多地依赖于输入值或结构而不是字符计数，某些问题也具有明显的持续时间扩展。answers a different question: not whether timing is informative, but whether serialized length can stand in for timing without executing the tests. We still require the duration-spread criterion for the final optimization dataset because some problems have clear duration spread even when runtime depends more on input values or structure than on character count.

表 20 显示优化测试是唯一能够大规模提供信息的类别。在预重新分割训练分区上，原始的公共、私有和生成的测试几乎永远无法进行持续时间过滤。正确性测试会有所帮助，但主要是通过添加孤立的硬案例来实现。优化测试的行为有所不同：1,169 个问题 (48.2%) 是可过滤持续时间的，863 个问题 (35.6%) 满足持续时间和长度可过滤性。在持续时间可过滤的优化问题中，863 / 1,169 (73.8%) 也是长度可过滤的，因此要求这两个标准将丢弃 306 个问题，即使长度只是一个弱代理，其运行时间已经提供了信息。更改原始竞争测试的超时阈值可以改变奖励严格性，但它不能创建测试本身所缺少的工作负载多样性。这使得互补的不可持续时间过滤的池成为自然的阴性对照。如果持续时间过滤性选择的问题是时间奖励实际上可以区分更快和较慢的正确解决方案，那么用不可过滤的问题替换持续时间可过滤的问题应该会损害基于时间的训练。对于以正确性为导向的训练来说，它应该不那么重要，因为奖励不依赖于问题内的持续时间分布。Table 20 shows that optimization tests are the only category that remains informative at scale. On the preresplit training partition, the original public, private, and generated tests are almost never duration-filterable. Correctness tests help, but mostly by adding isolated hard cases. Optimization tests behave differently: 1,169 problems (48.2%) are duration-filterable, and 863 (35.6%) satisfy both duration and length filterability. Among duration-filterable optimization problems, 863 / 1,169 (73.8%) are also length-filterable, so requiring both criteria would discard 306 problems whose runtimes are already informative even when length is only a weak proxy. Changing timeout thresholds on raw competition tests can change reward strictness, but it cannot create workload diversity that is absent from the tests themselves. This makes the complementary non-duration-filterable pool a natural negative control. If duration filterability is selecting problems where timing rewards can actually distinguish faster from slower correct solutions, then replacing duration-filterable problems with non-filterable ones should mostly hurt timing-based training. It should matter much less for correctness-oriented training, where the reward does not rely on within-problem duration spread.

表20给出了计数级结果；接下来的两张图显示了那些被选中和被拒绝的配置文件的样子。图 21 对保留和拒绝的示例使用相同的持续时间范围。保留的优化套件分布在运行时中，而被拒绝的套件则聚集在基本运行时附近。图 22 放大了一对保留/拒绝的情况，并区分了三种视觉情况：平坦、拐角和坡道。扁平型材无计时信号；角型材依靠小慢尾；类似斜坡的配置文件在整个套件中分配工作负载变化。这就是稳健 CV 阈值要捕获的模式。Table 20 gives the count-level result; the next two figures show what those selected and rejected profiles look like. Figure 21 uses the same duration scale for retained and rejected examples. Retained optimization suites spread across runtimes, while rejected suites stay clustered near the base runtime. Figure 22 zooms in on one retained/rejected pair and separates three visual cases: flat, corner, and ramp. Flat profiles have no timing signal; corner profiles rely on a small slow tail; ramp-like profiles distribute workload variation across the suite. This is the pattern that the robust-CV threshold is meant to capture.

图 21 共享 x 轴尺度下的持续时间可过滤与不可持续时间过滤的优化配置文件。Figure 21 Duration-filterable versus non-duration-filterable optimization profiles under a shared x-axis scale. 面板aPanel a显示保留的持续时间可过滤集群。对于问题A，原始基础测试仍然集中在0附近shows a retained duration-filterable cluster. For Problem A, the original base tests remain concentrated near 0.065 s，而生成的正确性测试拓宽了轮廓，生成的优化测试创建了更广泛的多模态分布。问题 B 和 C 显示了阈值同一保留侧的仅优化示例；它们的优化稳健 CV 分别为 0.47 和 1.13，而问题 A 为 0.74。065 s, while generated correctness tests widen the profile and generated optimization tests create a broader multi-modal distribution. Problems B and C show optimization-only examples on the same retained side of the threshold; their optimization robust CVs are 0.47 and 1.13, respectively, compared with 0.74 for Problem A.面板bPanel b在相同的 x 轴尺度上显示匹配的拒绝簇。所有三个配置文件仍然集中在原始的亚秒运行时间范围内，问题 A、B 和 C 的优化鲁棒 CV 分别为 0.01、0.01 和 0.29。鲁棒 CV 的计算方式为代表性每次测试持续时间的四分位数范围除以中值。对于保留的问题 A，值为 0.74 意味着优化测试的中间一半跨越中位持续时间的 74%；对于被拒绝的问题 A，值为 0.01 意味着中间一半几乎在中位数附近退化。使用四分位数范围而不是最大值使得标准依赖于整个套件的持续分布而不是一个孤立的慢速测试，而除以中位数使得阈值在具有不同绝对运行时间的问题之间具有可比性。该比较说明了为什么持续时间可过滤性与问题相关：有用的优化测试绝对不需要很慢，但它们必须创建足够的问题内分布，以便按速度对正确的解决方案进行排名。shows the matched rejected cluster on the same x-axis scale. All three profiles remain concentrated around the original sub-second runtime regime, with optimization robust CVs 0.01, 0.01, and 0.29 for Problems A, B, and C. The robust CV is computed as the interquartile range of the representative per-test durations divided by their median. For the retained Problem A, a value of 0.74 means that the middle half of the optimization tests spans 74% of the median duration; for the rejected Problem A, a value of 0.01 means that the middle half is almost degenerate around the median. Using the interquartile range rather than the maximum makes the criterion depend on a persistent spread across the suite rather than on one isolated slow test, while dividing by the median makes the threshold comparable across problems with different absolute runtimes. The comparison illustrates why duration filterability is problem-relative: useful optimization tests need not be slow in absolute terms, but they must create enough within-problem spread to rank correct solutions by speed.

图 22 保留与拒绝的优化测试配置文件。Figure 22 Retained versus rejected optimization-test profiles.该图通过相同的持续时间剖面形状读取两行。在顶行中，测试按代表性持续时间排序。平坦的曲线意味着整个套件有近一个运行时间，因此没有时序信号。角意味着大多数测试保持快，只有小尾部慢；这会产生持续时间分布，但将有用的工作负载变化集中在一个小子集中。类似对角线的斜坡是所需的形状：套件大部分的运行时间都会增加，从而提供广泛的工作负载来比较正确的解决方案。被拒绝的正确问题是扁平情况，基础测试、正确性测试和优化测试都压缩在 0.065 秒左右。保留的左问题显示了两个生成的分支之间的差异。生成的正确性测试增加了较慢的情况，但曲线仍然更接近拐角：许多测试保持在原始运行时间附近，并且增加集中在慢端附近。生成的优化测试更接近斜坡，具有稳健的 CV 0.74 和中值持续时间 0.099 秒，表明工作负载范围更广泛。底行通过最慢的测试所带来的累积运行时份额显示了相同的区别。如果所有测试都具有相同的持续时间，则该曲线是对角线：最慢的 30% 测试占用 30% 的运行时间，如被拒绝的问题所示。如果 90% 的测试非常快，10% 的测试非常慢，则曲线会形成一个尖角：最慢的 10% 占用了大部分运行时间，而添加接下来的 40% 则几乎没有增加。有用的状态位于这两个极端之间，对角线上方有一条弓形曲线，但不会塌陷成角。在保留左问题中，优化测试具有以下模式：最慢的 10%、30% 和 50% 分别占总运行时间的 15%、44% 和 65%。The figure reads both rows through the same duration-profile shapes. In the top row, tests are sorted by representative duration. A flat curve means that the whole suite has nearly one runtime, so there is no timing signal. A corner means that most tests remain fast and only a small tail is slow; this creates duration spread, but concentrates the useful workload variation in a small subset. A diagonal-like ramp is the desired shape: runtimes increase across much of the suite, giving the reward a broad range of workloads on which to compare correct solutions. The rejected right problem is the flat case, with base, correctness, and optimization tests all compressed near 0.065 s. The retained left problem shows the difference between the two generated branches. Generated correctness tests add slower cases, but the curve is still closer to a corner: many tests stay near the original runtime and the increase is concentrated near the slow end. Generated optimization tests are closer to a ramp, with robust CV 0.74 and median duration 0.099 s, indicating a broader workload range. The bottom row shows the same distinction through cumulative runtime share carried by the slowest tests. If all tests have the same duration, this curve is diagonal: the slowest 30% of tests carry 30% of runtime, as in the rejected problem. If 90% of tests are very fast and 10% are very slow, the curve makes a sharp corner: the slowest 10% carry most of the runtime, and adding the next 40% adds little. The useful regime lies between these extremes, with a bowed curve above the diagonal but not collapsed into a corner. In the retained left problem, the optimization tests have this pattern: the slowest 10%, 30%, and 50% account for 15%, 44%, and 65% of total runtime.

B.8 最终的持续时间可过滤 RL 池B.8 The final duration-filterable RL pool

The share of tests above 1 s likewise rises from 0.42% on the original suite to 1.54% on correctness tests and 6.87% on optimization tests. The 1 s cutoff is a common reference point rather than a requirement that every problem cross it: some valid problem domains cannot produce second-scale tests, so the main signal is the relative enrichment, about 16×原始测试率。the original-test rate.

关于保留的测试。这些持续时间锚定了稍后使用的与问题相关的奖励和时间敏感的评估指标：在某些优化强化学习环境中，新的模型部署可以与同一问题和测试套件存储的人类时间分布进行比较，而不是与一个全局运行时阈值进行比较。因为这些参考持续时间和新的推出持续时间可以在不同的执行服务状态下收集，所以C部分描述了在将存储的持续时间用于奖励或评估之前将存储的持续时间映射到当前时间尺度的校准步骤。on the retained tests. These durations anchor the problem-relative rewards and timing-sensitive evaluation metrics used later: in some optimization-RL environments, a fresh model rollout can be compared with the stored human timing distribution for the same problem and test suite, rather than with one global runtime threshold. Because those reference durations and the fresh rollout durations may be collected under different execution-service states, Section C describes the calibration step that maps stored durations into the current timing scale before they are used for reward or evaluation.

创建 DMC-Optim 时的目标不是让大多数测试超出固定的绝对阈值。对于许多问题，有效的输入域和内在的算法结构不支持这样的机制；强制它要么会违反约束，要么会将套件折叠到最困难的角落。目标是尽可能多地覆盖每个问题的可行持续时间范围，以便足够的测试在该有效范围内变慢，以便计时保持信息丰富。图 23 在聚合级别上对此进行了可视化，图 24 将相同的模式分解为合并尾部、每个问题分位数和代表性问题示例。The goal when creating DMC-Optim was not to push most tests beyond a fixed absolute threshold. For many problems, the valid input domain and intrinsic algorithmic structure do not support such a regime; forcing it would either violate constraints or collapse the suite onto only the hardest corner. The goal is to cover as much of each problem’s feasible duration range as possible, so that enough tests become slow within that valid range for timing to remain informative. Figure 23 visualizes this at the aggregate level, and Figure 24 breaks the same pattern into pooled tails, per-problem quantiles, and representative problem examples.

这些持续时间尾部测量建立了保留池的测量属性，但它们本身并不能证明持续时间可过滤选择对于学习很重要，也不能证明单独的数据构造足以优化强化学习。因此，我们进行了两次训练消融：首先改变训练池，同时保持奖励、评估集和训练预算固定，然后询问朴素的原始持续时间奖励是否可以取代主要实验中使用的与问题相关的奖励。These duration-tail measurements establish the measurement property of the retained pool, but they do not by themselves prove that duration-filterable selection matters for learning, nor that data construction alone is sufficient for optimization RL. We therefore run two training ablations: first changing the training pool while keeping the reward, evaluation set, and training budget fixed, and then asking whether a naive raw-duration reward can replace the problem-relative reward used in the main experiments.

B.9 训练消融：DMC-Optim 有帮助吗？它足以解决优化 RL 问题吗？B.9 Training ablations: does DMC-Optim help, and is it enough to solve optimization RL?

前面的诊断是仅数据集的测量。因此，我们训练模型来询问这些测量背后的数据选择是否会改变学习行为。第一个消融隔离了问题选择：我们在相同的奖励和环境下比较了 1,000 个问题的三个同等大小的训练池。这些池是主运行中使用的可持续时间过滤的池；包含 478 个持续时间可过滤问题和 522 个不可持续时间过滤问题的混合比例池，与全语料库比率 1 相匹配The preceding diagnostics are dataset-only measurements. We therefore train models to ask whether the data choices behind those measurements change learning behavior. The first ablation isolates problem selection: we compare three equal-sized training pools of 1,000 problems under the same reward and environment. The pools are the duration-filterable pool used in the main runs; a mixed-proportion pool with 478 duration-filterable and 522 non-duration-filterable problems, matching the full-corpus ratio 1, 302 / 2 ,第723章和从 1 中采样的不可过滤池723; and a non-filterable pool sampled from the 1,421-问题补充，其优化测试未达到持续时间过滤性标准。不可过滤的同伴是在持续时间可过滤选择之前从运行时合并的清理语料库构建的，而不是通过编辑已经过滤的训练分割来构建：它保留相同的转换后的训练模式，删除不正确的解决方案，并保留聚合的持续时间元数据和持续时间分布。奖励、强化学习环境、添加测试系列、CES 执行后端、评估集、评估节奏和训练预算在每行中保持固定。我们在评估百分位报告 pass@1421-problem complement whose optimization tests fail the duration-filterability criterion. The non-filterable companion is constructed from the runtime-merged cleaned corpus before duration-filterable selection, not by editing the already filtered training split: it preserves the same transformed training schema, removes incorrect solutions, and keeps aggregated duration metadata and duration distributions. The reward, RL environment, added-test family, CES execution backend, evaluation set, evaluation cadence, and training budget are kept fixed within each row. We report pass@1 at evaluation percentilep30 作为绝对分数，括号中是其相对于持续时间可过滤基线的相对变化，30 as an absolute score and, in parentheses, its relative change from the duration-filterable baseline,

所以负值是相对下降。so negative values are relative drops.

This is why the filterable-pool charlen score, 9.5%, is below the filterable-pool Filter 2s score of 10.4% and well below the rankedp30分16.1%。随着池被混合问题和不可过滤问题所取代，相同的大字符测试与有用的持续时间配置文件的一致性较差：根据定义，这些问题没有足够的问题内持续时间分布供优化测试来支持与问题相关的计时奖励。放弃非常大的测试成本会更低：无论如何，这些测试并没有创建有用的时序阶梯。在其余较小的测试中，具有 10% 超时容差的更严格的 0.5 秒优化门可能是比 MC+MO 基线使用的 10 秒优化超时更好的选择，因为它应用的优化压力更适合保留的测试规模。这解释了为什么 charlen 在混合和不可过滤池上保持稳定的性能，以及为什么它可以在相同的不可过滤池中击败 MC+MO（9.4% vs 8.3%）30 score of 16.1%. As the pool is replaced by mixed and then non-filterable problems, the same large-character tests are less aligned with a useful duration profile: by definition, these problems do not have enough within-problem duration spread for the optimization tests to support a problem-relative timing reward. Dropping very large tests is then less costly: those tests were not creating a useful timing ladder anyway. On the remaining smaller tests, the stricter 0.5 s optimization gate with 10% timeout tolerance can be a better choice than the 10 s optimization timeout used by the MC+MO baseline, because it applies optimization pressure better matched to the retained test sizes. This explains why charlen stays at stable performance on the mixed and non-filterable pools, and why it can beat MC+MO at the same non-filterable pool (9.4% versus 8.3% atp30）。相反，在持续时间可过滤池上，删除的大型测试是区分正确解决方案之间重要优化差异的尾部的一部分。在丢弃定时信号的有意义部分后，Charlen 必须依赖于较小的保留测试。相比之下，在不可过滤的池中，保留的小测试提供了30). On the duration-filterable pool, on the contrary, the removed large tests are part of the tail that distinguishes important optimization differences between correct solutions. Charlen then has to rely on the smaller retained tests after discarding a meaningful part of the timing signal. On the non-filterable pool, by contrast, the retained small tests provide

更弱和更平坦的计时压力，但至少被放弃的大型测试没有带来有用的优化压力的单独分支。这并不意味着不可过滤池与优化强化学习数据集一样好：其在不可过滤数据上的最佳 charlen 分数仍然低于排名weaker and flatter timing pressure, but at least the discarded large tests were not carrying a separate branch of useful optimization pressure. 因此决定性的比较是排名The decisive comparison is therefore rankedp30. 该奖励使用人类参考时间分布来按速度对正确的解决方案进行排名，因此它需要一个真实的问题内持续时间概况。当持续时间可过滤的问题被混合或纯粹不可过滤的池取代时，排名30. This reward uses the human-reference timing distribution to rank correct solutions by speed, so it needs a real within-problem duration profile. When duration-filterable problems are replaced by mixed or purely non-filterable pools, rankedp30 从 16.1% 下降到 13.3%，然后下降到 9.1%，在相同的不可过滤池中仅比 MC+MO 高出 0.8 个点。对于排名奖励来说，这是一个不好的机制：如果优化测试持续时间曲线几乎平坦，则奖励仍然会强制执行速度排序，但测试对这种排序的支持很弱，并且大多会增加奖励的噪音。结论并不是不可过滤的问题毫无用处，而是持续时间的可过滤性预测了与问题相关的计时奖励在哪里有可用的训练信号。30 falls from 16.1% to 13.3% and then 9.1%, ending only 0.8 points above MC+MO on the same non-filterable pool. This is a bad regime for a ranking reward: if the optimization-test duration profile is nearly flat, the reward still forces a speed ordering, but that ordering is weakly supported by the tests and mostly adds noise to the reward. The conclusion is not that non-filterable problems are useless, but that duration filterability predicts where problem-relative timing rewards have a usable training signal.

良好的优化测试不足以实现良好的优化强化学习。Good optimization tests are not enough for good optimization RL.构建 DMC-Optim 后，一个自然的问题是数据集工作本身是否足够：一旦测试更强并且包含更大的优化工作负载，我们是否可以简单地奖励较短的原始执行时间，或者下游 RL 环境和奖励是否仍然添加大量信号？我们通过在两个分支上运行原始持续时间奖励来测试这一点。基础测试变体测量原始正确性测试的绝对秒数；优化测试变体测量生成的优化测试的绝对秒数。每个都通过从秒到奖励的线性和对数图进行测试。 [0203] 表24报告相关After constructing DMC-Optim, a natural question is whether the dataset work is enough by itself: once the tests are stronger and include larger optimization workloads, can we simply reward shorter raw execution time, or do the downstream RL environment and reward still add substantial signal? We test this by running raw-duration rewards on two branches. The base-test variant measures absolute seconds on the original correctness-style tests; the optimization-test variant measures absolute seconds on the generated optimization tests. Each is tested with a linear and a logarithmic map from seconds to reward. The log-map rows are a useful diagnostic. Given the very short base-test durations in Table 21, one might expect a logarithmic map to help by expanding reward differences among small runtimes. This is not what we observe, but the log map also does not provide the missing signal: on base tests, it is indistinguishable from the linear map (-10.8% versus -10.8% relative to MC+MO), and on optimization tests it only slightly improves the linear variant (+19.4% versus +17.2%). This suggests that extra resolution at very small runtimes mostly turns timing noise into reward noise, while the larger duration gaps that carry more useful optimization signal still need a problem-relative reward. 这些对照与表 23 一起得出了两个方面的结论。改进数据集然后进行原始优化 RL 是不够的：即使在生成的优化测试中，最佳原始持续时间控制仍然远远低于更细粒度的排名Together with Table 23, these controls give a two-sided conclusion. Improving the dataset and then doing raw optimization RL is not enough: even on generated optimization tests, the best raw-duration control remains far below the more fine-grained rankedp30环境。相反，对错误数据使用明确设计的时间奖励也是不够的：30 environment. Conversely, using an explicitly designed timing reward on the wrong data is also not enough: rankedp30 从持续时间可过滤池的 16.1% 下降到不可过滤池的 9.1%。因此，DMC-Optim 仅与下游奖励和环境设计一起有用：数据集必须创建可测量的问题内持续时间结构，并且 RL 配方必须将该结构转变为稳定的训练信号。30 falls from 16.1% on the duration-filterable pool to 9.1% on the non-filterable pool. DMC-Optim is therefore useful only together with downstream reward and environment design: the dataset must create measurable within-problem duration structure, and the RL recipe must turn that structure into a stable training signal.

C 使运行时测量值得信赖：代码执行后端C Making runtime measurements trustworthy: code execution backend

本附录扩展了第 3 节的后半部分。B 节首先说明了为什么 DMC-Optim 需要具有有用的持续时间分布的优化测试：没有这种分布，时间奖励几乎没有信号。这还不够。如果后端无法可靠地测量这些持续时间，则无论测试生成得多么仔细，表观分布都将无法用于训练或评估。在仅正确性 RLVR 中，沙箱主要决定解决方案在超时之前是否通过；小的运行时扰动是次要的。在优化强化学习中，运行时本身会进入奖励，因此工作负载、重试、回退行为和后端漂移都可以改变学习信号。在这里，我们记录了我们最初的本地沙箱设置是如何被丢弃并被更受控制的远程代码执行服务（CES）取代的，在构建、训练和评估期间如何使用 CES，如何处理基础设施故障，以及如何根据新的执行来校准存储的人类参考持续时间。This appendix expands the second half of Section 3. Section B first showed why DMC-Optim needs optimization tests with useful duration spread: without such spread, a timing reward has little signal. That is still not enough. If the backend cannot measure these durations reliably, the apparent spread will not be usable for training or evaluation, no matter how carefully the tests were generated. In correctness-only RLVR, the sandbox mostly decides whether a solution passes before the timeout; small runtime perturbations are secondary. In optimization RL, the runtime itself enters the reward, so worker load, retries, fallback behavior, and backend drift can change the learning signal. Here we record how our initial local-sandbox setup was discarded and replaced by a more controlled remote code execution service (CES), how CES is used during construction, training, and evaluation, how infrastructure failures are handled, and how stored human-reference durations are calibrated against fresh executions.

读地图。Reading map.C.1 节说明了代码执行后端必须为稳定的优化 RL 提供什么。然后，C.2 节从初始的本地沙箱设置开始，这是我们标准 RLVR 的基本设置，并展示了为什么它无法优化 RL：它改变了严格超时的排名，创建了不稳定的问题内排序，增加了持续时间过滤性，使校准或校正尝试失败，并在 RL 循环内崩溃。 C.3 至 C.6 节将 CES 描述为更受控制的替代方案：执行路径、后备策略、经验故障率以及我们使用或考虑的替代后备设计。 C.7 至 C.10 节分析了 CES 设置的剩余可变性，将实时执行期间的短期噪声与存储的人类参考持续时间和新的 CES 测量之间的长期漂移分开，然后测试仿射校准在评估时和 RL 训练期间改变校准时对于本文中使用的指标是否足够稳定。 C.11 节总结了我们从这些基于时间的培训和评估检查中获得的经验教训，C.12 节列出了仍然悬而未决的测量问题。Section C.1 states what a code execution backend must provide for stable optimization RL. Section C.2 then starts from the initial local-sandbox setup, which is our base setup for standard RLVR, and shows why it fails for optimization RL: it changes tight-timeout rankings, creates unstable within-problem orderings, inflates duration filterability, fails calibration or correction attempts, and breaks down inside the RL loop. Sections C.3 to C.6 describe CES as the more controlled replacement: the execution path, fallback policy, empirical failure rates, and alternative fallback designs we use or considered. Sections C.7 to C.10 analyze the remaining variability of that CES setup, separating short-run noise during live execution from long-term drift between stored human-reference durations and fresh CES measurements, then testing whether affine calibration is stable enough for the metrics used in the paper both at evaluation time and when the calibration is changed during RL training. Section C.11 summarizes the lessons we take from these checks for timing-based training and evaluation, and Section C.12 lists the measurement questions that remain open.

C.1 代码执行后端必须建立什么C.1 What a code execution backend has to establish

对于仅正确性的 RL，偶尔的超时噪音可能仍然是次要的，因为奖励主要由通过/失败结果决定。对于优化 RL，测量的持续时间本身可以确定推出是否获得信用。这在 DMC-Optim 创建的机制中非常重要：即使在生成大输入测试之后，池化优化测试For correctness-only RL, occasional timeout noise can remain secondary because the reward is dominated by pass/fail outcomes. For optimization RL, the measured duration itself can determine whether a rollout receives credit. This matters in exactly the regime created by DMC-Optim: even after large-input test generation, the pooled optimization-testp95 是 195 is 1.296年代，296 s, thep99 是 399 is 3.710 s，只有 6.87% 的优化测试超过 1 s（表 21）。因此，亚秒级扰动可以改变排名、阈值和奖励。测量后端必须将代码时序与工作线程争用隔离开来，显式地暴露故障，并在服务状态发生变化时支持校准。710 s, and only 6.87% of optimization tests exceed 1 s (Table 21). Sub-second perturbations can therefore change rankings, thresholds, and rewards. The measurement backend must isolate code timing from worker contention, expose failures explicitly, and support calibration when service state changes.

我们考虑了两个实用的后端。第一个是本地沙箱，我们可以将其视为基本 RLVR 设置，它在托管模型推理、部署编排以及来自 RL 作业的其他 CPU 和内存负载的相同训练工作线程上运行。第二个是专用的远程沙盒执行服务，在下面表示为 CES，它在固定资源范围内运行每个代码测试对，具有 1 GB 内存和 10 秒硬限制，并返回状态和持续时间。 CES 每次调用的开销更大，但它将计时与工作端争用分开，让我们可以控制重试和并发性，并且可以独立于 GPU 作业进行配置。接下来的证据引导我们将校准的 CES 设置用于本文中的所有优化 RL 运行。We considered two practical backends. The first was a local sandbox, which we can consider our base RLVR setup, running on the same training workers that host model inference, rollout orchestration, and other CPU and memory load from the RL job. The second is a dedicated remote sandboxed execution service, denoted CES below, that runs each code-test pair under a fixed resource envelope, with 1 GB memory and a 10 s hard limit, and returns both status and duration. CES has more overhead per call, but it separates timing from worker-side contention, lets us control retries and concurrency, and can be provisioned independently of the GPU job. The evidence that follows led us to use the calibrated CES setup for all optimization-RL runs in the paper.

稳定的计时不仅需要速度奖励训练，而且还需要比较消融。控制执行噪声使得运行之间的差异可以解释为数据集、奖励或训练配方的变化，而不是后端的不受控制的变化。Stable timing is needed not only to train with a speed reward, but also to compare ablations. Controlling execution noise makes differences between runs interpretable as changes in the dataset, reward, or training recipe, rather than uncontrolled changes in the backend.

C.2 本地沙箱以及为什么它不利于计时测量C.2 Local sandbox and why it is bad for timing measurements

我们的基本 RLVR 执行路径是本地 Python I/O 对沙箱。训练人员将候选程序和一批输入/输出对发送到沙盒 Python 运行器。运行程序编译程序一次，通过重定向标准输入和输出来顺序重放输入，将生成的输出与预期输出进行比较，并返回每个测试用例的一个状态和一个经过的持续时间。运行程序通过一个默认后端为 Bubblewrap (Bubblewrap) 的分叉服务器启动：提交的代码在 Linux 命名空间沙箱中运行，该沙箱具有已删除的功能、新的临时文件系统、资源限制、Our base RLVR execution path is a local Python I/O-pair sandbox. A training worker sends the candidate program and a batch of input/output pairs to a sandboxed Python runner. The runner compiles the program once, replays the inputs sequentially by redirecting standard input and output, compares the produced output with the expected output, and returns one status and one elapsed duration per test case. The runner is launched through a fork server whose default backend is Bubblewrap (Bubblewrap): the submitted code runs in a Linux namespace sandbox with dropped capabilities, a fresh temporary filesystem, resource limits,

61

和过程清理。对于仅正确性 RLVR 来说，这是一个合理的设置，因为它可以安全地将不受信任的程序转变为通过/失败结果。and process cleanup. This is a reasonable setup for correctness-only RLVR because it safely turns untrusted programs into pass/fail outcomes.

对于时序测量来说，三个细节很重要。首先，本地计时器在编译后包装每个输入/输出对的执行，因此它排除了沙箱启动和一次性编译步骤，但它仍然测量本地工作线程挂钟时间。其次，输入/输出对在一个运行程序进程中按顺序重放；因此，全局状态、导入、缓存或输入/输出重定向状态可以跨情况进行。第三，超时是由等待运行器响应的父进程检测到的，因此超时行为还取决于本地工作人员按计划观察和清理沙盒进程的能力。这些细节对于通过/失败 RLVR 来说是可以接受的，但它们使本地路径成为一个弱计时工具，因为进程调度、共享工作负载和批处理执行状态可以输入测量的持续时间。Three details matter for timing measurements. First, the local timer wraps the execution of each input/output pair after compilation, so it excludes sandbox startup and the one-time compilation step, but it still measures local worker wall-clock time. Second, the input/output pairs are replayed sequentially in one runner process; global state, imports, caches, or input/output redirection state can therefore carry across cases. Third, timeouts are detected by the parent process waiting for runner responses, so timeout behavior also depends on the local worker’s ability to observe and clean up the sandboxed process on schedule. These details are acceptable for pass/fail RLVR, but they make the local path a weak timing instrument because process scheduling, shared worker load, and batch execution state can enter the measured duration.

BigO(Bench) 进一步研究了如何使本地沙箱计时更加稳定，包括改进用于计时程序的时钟以及尝试控制其复杂性标记沙箱中的 CPU 争用（Chambon 等人，2025）。BigO(Bench) further investigates how to make local sandbox timing more stable, including by refining the clock used to time programs and by trying to control CPU contention in its complexity-labeling sandbox (Chambon et al., 2025).2这里的主要区别是沙箱周围的工作负载。 BigO（Bench）作为离线标记作业运行执行，其中执行可以作为主要工作负载隔离。在优化强化学习中，执行与模型推理、部署编排、日志记录和其他工作端负载同时运行。即使沙箱本身稳定，周围的强化学习系统仍然会扰乱用作奖励的持续时间。The main difference here is the workload around the sandbox. BigO(Bench) runs execution as an offline labeling job where execution can be isolated as the main workload. In optimization RL, execution runs concurrently with model inference, rollout orchestration, logging, and other worker-side load. Even if the sandbox itself is stabilized, the surrounding RL system can still perturb the durations used as rewards.

本地计时故障分为三层：本地线束以更快的速度运行，返回的可用计时单元更少，并且对相同代码和测试给出不稳定的重复测量。然后，我们直接比较本地和 CES 输出：不匹配不仅仅是额外的噪声或简单的恶化，因为两个后端改变了哪些问题看起来可以持续过滤，而事后拟合无法恢复 CES 排序。最后，我们测试 RL 循环内本地执行的实际效果，首先使用合成并发负载，然后使用真正的本地执行 RL 运行。The local-timing failure has three layers: the local harness runs in a much faster regime, returns fewer usable timing cells, and gives unstable repeated measurements of the same code and tests. We then compare local and CES outputs directly: the mismatch is not just extra noise or a simple deterioration, because the two backends change which problems look duration-filterable and post-hoc fits cannot recover the CES ordering. Finally, we test the practical effect of local execution inside the RL loop, first with synthetic concurrent load and then with real local-execution RL runs.

本地沙箱速度将评估推入嘈杂的低于 0.1 秒的范围。Local sandbox speed pushes evaluation into the noisy sub-0.1 s regime.作为事后诊断，我们可以从第 5 节的结果研究中采取四个检查点：32B 基线、32B 优化 RL 模型、7B 基线和 7B 优化 RL 模型。我们使用两种工具评估了生成的相同解决方案，一种由 CES 支持，另一种由本地沙箱支持。本地线束要快得多。在 10 秒超时时，两个环境仍然接近，因为几乎所有正确的解决方案都有足够的时间来完成，但随着超时进入亚秒范围，差异会迅速增大。在 0.1 秒时，本地执行将 pass@1 膨胀了 21.3As a post-hoc diagnostic, we can take four checkpoints from the result study in Section 5: a 32B baseline, a 32B optimization-RL model, a 7B baseline, and a 7B optimization-RL model. We evaluated the same generated solutions with two harnesses, one backed by CES and one backed by the local sandbox. The local harness is much faster. At a 10 s timeout the two environments are still close because almost all correct solutions have enough time to finish, but the difference grows rapidly as the timeout enters the sub-second range. At 0.1 s, local execution inflates pass@1 by 21.3×在 32B 基线上，32.8on the 32B baseline, 32.8×在 32B RL 型号上，50.3on the 32B RL model, 50.3×在 7B 基线上，以及 20.0on the 7B baseline, and 20.0×7B RL 模型（表 25）。这种额外的速度并不是一个有用的测量信号：它使许多执行进入了微小的时钟扰动占主导地位的状态。该表已经显示了由此产生的不稳定性。在 0.1 秒的本地执行下，7B 基线得分高于 7B 优化 RL 模型，尽管校准的 DMC-Optim 结果显示优化模型在严格的持续时间阈值下更强。因此，本地执行引入了第一个问题：它将评估置于更快的计时机制中，接下来研究的不稳定性只会变得更加重要。on the 7B RL model (Table 25). This extra speed is not a useful measurement signal: it moves many executions into the regime where tiny wall-clock perturbations dominate. The table already shows the resulting instability. Under local execution at 0.1 s, the 7B baseline scores higher than the 7B optimization-RL model, even though the calibrated DMC-Optim results show the optimization model is stronger at strict duration thresholds. Local execution therefore introduces a first problem: it places the evaluation in a much faster timing regime, where the instabilities studied next can only become more important.

本地沙箱会扭曲执行结果并降低覆盖率。Local sandbox distorts execution results and lowers coverage.抛开计时值本身，我们看看线束是否返回可用的计时矩阵。我们在 CES 上和本地沙箱上运行了一次相同的优化测试系列。在原始输出的 50 个问题切片上，本地执行Putting aside the timing values themselves, we look at whether the harness returns a usable timing matrix at all. We ran the same optimization-test family once on CES and once on the local sandbox. On a 50-problem slice of the raw outputs, local execution

2https://github.com/facebookresearch/BigOBench/tree/main/src/complexity/sandbox

62

产生的可用时序测量较少：成功率从 99.62% 下降到 97.41%，不成功率从 0.38% 上升到 2.59%，每个解决方案每个测试的可用时序覆盖率从 99.2% 下降到 93.3%。有些问题恶化得更加严重，定时矩阵覆盖率下降了高达 50%。这已经是一个可信度问题，与剩余持续时间的分配方式无关。丢失的单元格并不是无害的空白：本地内存不足事件可能更难与主机工作程序隔离，小的运行时扰动可以将接近极限的执行推过超时边界，并且工作程序端的不稳定可以改变执行状态而不仅仅是测量的持续时间。produced fewer usable timing measurements: success rate fell from 99.62% to 97.41%, non-success rate rose from 0.38% to 2.59%, and usable per-solution per-test timing coverage fell from 99.2% to 93.3%. Some problems deteriorated much more sharply, with timing-matrix coverage falling by up to 50%. This is already a trustworthiness issue, independent of how the remaining durations are distributed. Missing cells are not harmless blanks: local out-of-memory events can be harder to isolate from the host worker, small runtime perturbations can push near-limit executions across a timeout boundary, and worker-side instability can change the execution status rather than only the measured duration.

询问哪种代码最快，每次都会得到不同的答案。Ask which code is fastest, get a different answer each time.接下来我们测试本地沙箱是否至少可以产生相对于其自身稳定的定时信号。在专门的本地测量活动中，我们针对相同问题反复重新评估未更改的候选解决方案，仅询问一次重新运行是否恢复了与另一次重新运行相同的问题内时序排名。对于每个优化测试，候选持续时间根据同一测试的人类参考持续时间进行排名，0% 表示最快位置，100% 表示最慢位置。平均百分位数是优化测试中这些每次测试百分位数的平均值。平均百分位数为 20% 意味着该解决方案看起来比大多数参考文献更快；平均百分位数为 70% 意味着它看起来慢得多。诊断询问在相同的测试中重新运行相同的代码是否会保持排行榜位置固定。We next tested whether the local sandbox can at least produce a stable timing signal relative to itself. In a dedicated local measurement campaign, we repeatedly re-evaluated unchanged candidate solutions on the same problems, asking only whether one rerun recovers the same within-problem timing ranking as another. For each optimization test, the candidate duration is ranked against the human-reference durations for that same test, with 0% denoting the fastest position and 100% the slowest. The mean percentile is the average of these per-test percentiles across the optimization tests. A mean percentile of 20% means the solution looks faster than most references; a mean percentile of 70% means it looks much slower. The diagnostic asks whether rerunning the same code on the same tests keeps that leaderboard position fixed.

在 23 个问题和 425 次重新运行中，重复的本地重新执行使平均百分位数分数峰峰值平均上升了 41.2 个百分点：对于每个问题，我们采用重新运行中获得的最佳和最差平均百分位数分数之间的差异，然后对各个问题的范围进行平均。同样，平均百分位的问题内标准差平均为 10.961 个百分点，问题内范围平均为 41.173 个百分点。在排名奖励中，这并不是表面上的波动：11 点的标准差足以使解决方案跨越前 30% 的门，而 41 点的范围是看起来明显快速的解决方案和明显平庸的解决方案之间的区别。用排行榜术语来说，解决方案可以在本地重新运行中从前 30% 左右移动到后 30% 左右，而无需更改任何代码。观察到的最大问题内范围为 73.743 个百分点，足以在不更改代码的情况下将候选人移动到排行榜的几乎整个有用部分。Across 23 problems and 425 reruns, repeated local re-execution moved the mean-percentile score by 41.2 percentage points peak-to-peak on average: for each problem, we take the difference between the best and worst mean-percentile score obtained across reruns, then average that range across problems. Equivalently, the within-problem standard deviation of mean percentile averaged 10.961 percentage points and the withinproblem range averaged 41.173 percentage points. On a ranked reward, this is not a cosmetic fluctuation: an 11-point standard deviation is large enough to move a solution across a top-30% gate, and a 41-point range is the difference between looking like a clearly fast solution and a clearly mediocre one. In leaderboard terms, a solution can move from around the top 30% to around the bottom 30% across local reruns without any code change. The largest observed within-problem range, 73.743 percentage points, would be enough to move a candidate across almost the whole useful part of the leaderboard without changing the code.

我们还检查本地重新运行是否保留测试本身的顺序。对于每次重新运行，我们都会在重新执行的人类参考解决方案中获取每个优化测试的中位持续时间，并从该测试的存储中位持续时间到新测量的本地中位持续时间拟合一条线。稳定的计时后端应该在重新运行时给出正的且相似的斜率：如果存储的数据表明测试 A 的工作负载比测试 B 更重，则本地重新运行不应使 A 看起来比 B 更容易。符号翻转是最明显的失败情况，因为拟合线改变了方向；一次重新运行表明较大的存储工作负载仍然较大，而另一次重新运行则表明它们变得更小。 22 个问题中有 18 个问题发生了足够的测量。我们还计算了拟合斜率范围，即同一问题的局部重运行中的最大拟合斜率减去最小拟合斜率。该范围的平均值为 67.213，在第 90 个百分位时达到 208.401，在最坏的情况下达到 651.462。这些是按测试中位数聚合后从存储秒数到本地秒数的线性映射中的斜率，因此它们的绝对规模不如不稳定性重要：相同的问题可以从几乎平坦的拟合转变为跨重新运行的非常陡峭的拟合。最严重的问题We also check whether local reruns preserve the ordering of the tests themselves. For each rerun, we take each optimization test’s median duration across re-executed human reference solutions, and fit a line from the stored median duration of that test to the newly measured local median duration. A stable timing backend should give a positive and similar slope across reruns: if the stored data says test A is a heavier workload than test B, rerunning locally should not make A look easier than B. A sign flip is the clearest failure case, because the fitted line changes direction; one rerun says larger stored workloads remain larger, whereas another says they become smaller. This happened on 18 of 22 problems with enough measurements. We also compute the fitted-slope range, the maximum fitted slope minus the minimum fitted slope across local reruns for the same problem. This range averaged 67.213, reached 208.401 at the 90th percentile, and reached 651.462 in the worst case. These are slopes in a linear map from stored seconds to local seconds after aggregating by test median, so their absolute scale is less important than the instability: the same problem can move from an almost flat fit to a very steep fit across reruns. The worst-problemR2中位数接近于零，这意味着存储的工作负载排序几乎无法解释这些问题的本地时序变化。因此，与上面的平均百分位数移动一起，相同的代码和测试套件在本地重新运行时看起来可能会明显更强或更弱。medians were near zero, meaning the stored workload ordering explains almost none of the local timing variation on those problems. Together with the mean-percentile movement above, the same code and test suite can therefore look substantially stronger or weaker across local reruns.

本地测量和 CES 测量不兼容。Local and CES measurements are incompatible.设置新的远程执行后端（我们称之为 CES）后，我们可以在相同的优化测试中比较这两个后端。 CES 并不是绝对的事实，但如果两个后端混合在同一奖励路径中，则需要相称，例如在 CES 过载时使用本地执行作为后备。该比较还检查我们的默认本地后端和远程后端是否仅在程度上有所不同，其中一个噪声更大但平均而言仍然一致，或者它们是否完全导致不同的时序顺序。在后一种情况下，这可能意味着至少一个后端无法信任定时测量。它们不相称：本地测量和 CES 测量的差异不仅仅是围绕同一信号的小扰动。在完整数据集上，相同的优化测试看起来在本地可进行持续时间过滤：81.1% 的问题在本地沙箱下可进行持续时间过滤，而在 CES 上这一比例为 48.2%。因为过滤性标准是基于After setting up a new remote execution backend that we call CES, we could compare the two backends on the same optimization tests. CES is not an absolute ground truth, but the two backends would need to be commensurate if they were ever mixed in the same reward path, for example by using local execution as a fallback when CES is overloaded. The comparison also checks whether our default local backend and the remote backend differ only by degree, with one being noisier but still agreeing on average, or whether they induce different timing orderings altogether. In the latter case, it probably means at least one backend cannot be trusted for timing measurements. They are not commensurate: local and CES measurements disagree by more than a small perturbation around the same signal. On the full dataset, the same optimization tests look much more duration-filterable locally: 81.1% of problems are duration-filterable under the local sandbox versus 48.2% on CES. Because the filterability criterion is based

63

在稳健的变异系数下，这种差距很难用简单的全局加速来解释。统一的重新缩放将主要保持稳健的 CV。更合理的解释是，本地执行注入了额外的时序扩展，使得一些原本不可过滤的问题看起来对时序有用。 50 个问题的比较指向同一方向：本地执行减少了可用的持续时间单元覆盖范围，同时使更多问题显得可持续时间过滤。最后，如果本地计时仅受到比例因子的偏差，我们可以拟合从本地持续时间到 CES 持续时间的映射。我们在 36,660 个加入的本地/CES 持续时间对上进行了尝试。乘法、幂律、仿射和多项式映射在留一问题评估下均失败，交叉验证为负on a robust coefficient of variation, this gap is difficult to explain by a simple global speedup. A uniform rescaling would mostly preserve robust CV. The more plausible explanation is that local execution injects extra timing spread, making some otherwise non-filterable problems appear useful for timing. The 50-problem comparison points in the same direction: local execution reduces usable duration-cell coverage while making more problems appear duration-filterable. Finally, if local timing were only biased by a scale factor, we could fit a mapping from local durations to CES durations. We tried this on 36,660 joined local/CES duration pairs. Multiplicative, power-law, affine, and polynomial mappings all failed under leave-one-problem-out evaluation, with negative cross-validatedR2（表 26）。拟合的地图崩溃到几乎持平的预测，而不是恢复 CES 持续时间，并且排名质量仍然严重失调（图 25 和 26）。因此，本地时间不是我们可以调整或校准的量。(Table 26). The fitted maps collapse toward nearly flat predictions rather than recovering CES durations, and ranking quality remains badly misaligned (Figures 25 and 26). Local timing is therefore not a quantity we can adjust or calibrate.

本地沙箱因 CPU 争用而崩溃。Local sandboxes collapse under CPU contention.之前的诊断已经识别出三个故障点，这些故障点使得本地时序难以用于优化 RL。剩下的操作问题是，当工作线程也面临 CPU 压力时，本地执行的行为如何，就像在 rollout 收集期间一样。为了测量对争用的敏感度，我们重新运行相同的 724 个本地计时作业，同时在同一节点上以节点 CPU 容量的 50%、100% 和 200% 添加后台 CPU 负载。即使没有增加负载，724 个作业中的 294 个也没有完成。 50% 负载时升至 485/724，100% 时升至 531/724，200% 时升至 613/724。我们观察到执行速度非常慢、本地内存不足故障以及工作端不稳定。这些事件无法通过服务端重试来隔离，因为工作端争用是失败机制。The previous diagnostics already identify three failure points that make local timing hard to use for optimization RL. The remaining operational question is how local execution behaves when the worker is also under CPU pressure, as it would be during rollout collection. To measure sensitivity to contention, we reran the same 724 local timing jobs while adding background CPU load on the same node at 50%, 100%, and 200% of node CPU capacity. Even with no added load, 294 of 724 jobs did not complete. This rose to 485/724 at 50% load, 531/724 at 100%, and 613/724 at 200%. We observed very slow executions, local out-of-memory failures, and worker-side instability. These events cannot be isolated by a service-side retry because worker-side contention is the failure mechanism.

64

图 25 尝试将本地运行时调整回 CES 运行时失败。Figure 25 Attempting to fit local runtimes back to CES runtimes fails.该图聚合了 36,660 个加入的本地/CES 持续时间对。恒等线仅作为参考显示：其上方的细对角线带是良性情况，其中本地执行比 CES 更快，但仍大致排序。较大的图案则不同。许多具有非常小的 CES 持续时间的点分布在很宽的局部持续时间范围内，形成一条长的水平羽流而不是一条紧密的线。这就是失败模式：本地执行有时会增加工作端延迟，而代码测试对本身无法解释这种延迟，因此相同的 CES-fast 执行可能在本地看起来很快、中度慢或极慢。因此，拟合的映射向几乎平坦的预测崩溃，并且所有四个映射都具有负留一问题The plot aggregates 36,660 joined local/CES duration pairs. The identity line is shown only as a reference: the thin diagonal band above it is the benign case where local execution is faster than CES but still roughly ordered with it. The larger pattern is different. Many points with very small CES durations spread over a wide range of local durations, forming a long horizontal plume rather than a tight line. This is the failure mode: local execution sometimes adds worker-side delay that is not explained by the code-test pair itself, so the same CES-fast execution can look locally fast, moderately slow, or extremely slow. The fitted mappings therefore collapse toward nearly flat predictions, and all four have negative leave-one-problem-outR2。本地计时不仅仅缺少一个比例因子；它包含无法通过简单的事后校准消除的额外变化。. Local timing is not missing only a scale factor; it contains extra variation that cannot be removed by a simple post-hoc calibration.

图 26 即使在跨环境校正后，本地排名仍然不一致。Figure 26 Local rankings remain misaligned even after cross-environment correction.该图针对相同问题和测试的人类参考解决方案的分布对模型生成的解决方案进行了排名。对于一个问题和一项优化测试，我们采用 x 轴上命名的协议下的模型解决方案的持续时间，将其插入到同一测试的人类解决方案的持续时间分布中，并记录其百分位数。百分位 0% 表示模型解决方案比该测试中的所有人类参考更快； 50%表示接近人类分布的中间； 100% 意味着它比所有人类参考都慢。 y 轴是问题优化测试中这些每次测试百分位数的平均值，因此较低的值意味着模型解决方案相对于人类参考集排名更快。每个框总结了配对的本地/CES 诊断中可用的三个问题级别平均百分位数分数，红色菱形标记了它们的平均值。 50% 处的虚线是人类参考分布的中间。 x 轴标签是比较协议：The figure ranks model-generated solutions against distributions of human-reference solutions on the same problems and tests.
