This is the failure one would expect from comparing quantities produced by different timing backends. This is why local timing cannot be used as a drop-in replacement for CES timing: even after correction, it does not recover the ranking used by the reward and evaluation.

66

本地沙箱优化强化学习完全失败。Local sandbox optimization RL fully fails.上面的独立诊断是在完整的 RL 循环之外获得的，这可能只会让事情变得更糟。因此，我们尝试了直接消融：现有基于 CES 的 Qwen 2.5 7B 配置的八个本地执行变体，每个变体都针对 5,000 个 RL 步骤并使用直接本地结果。这些变体在训练期间跳过了 CES，而评估部分仍然使用 CES，因此消融隔离了训练时执行，而不是同时更改报告的评估后端。仅正确性基线未运行优化测试，已成功完成。七个优化测试变体却没有：每个变体都耗尽了所有六次调度程序尝试（最初的启动加上五次重新启动），累积了 12-32 小时的挂钟时间，并且仅达到了 160-1,620 个训练步骤。在独立测量中，每次测试的本地执行速度似乎比 CES 更快；在 RL 循环内，完整运行要慢得多。对于经过优化测试的配置，本地执行为 4-8The standalone diagnostics above were obtained outside the full RL loop, which can probably only make things worse. We therefore tried the direct ablation: eight local-execution variants of existing CES-based Qwen 2.5 7B configurations, each targeting 5,000 RL steps and using direct local outcomes. These variants skipped CES during training, while the evaluation section still used CES, so the ablation isolates training-time execution rather than changing the reported evaluation backend as well. The correctness-only baseline, which runs no optimization tests, completed successfully. The seven optimization-test variants did not: each exhausted all six scheduler attempts (the original launch plus five restarts), accumulated 12–32 hours of wall-clock time, and reached only 160–1,620 training steps. In standalone measurements, local execution appeared faster per test than CES; inside the RL loop, the full runs were much slower. For configurations with optimization tests, local execution was 4–8×比 CES 端到端慢。这些故障是训练节点上的内存不足导致的，然后是跨等级的分布式通信故障，因为本地执行消耗了已经保存模型和优化器状态的同一台机器上的内存。这并不能证明任何本地执行训练配置都无法运行：降低部署并发性、减少每步执行的测试数量或为训练工作人员分配更多 CPU 和内存可能会避免一些崩溃。但这些变化会直接降低吞吐量，而且当前的本地运行在取得进展时已经很慢了：在最坏的情况下，数据加载时间达到每步 355 秒，而相应的 CES 运行中的数据加载时间仅为个位数秒。这种端到端的消融将时序争论转变为可操作的争论：对于此模型规模和吞吐量目标的优化测试强化学习，CES 不仅是一个更清晰的时序源，而且还使训练运行变得可行。slower than CES end to end. The failures were out-of-memory kills on the training nodes, followed by distributed-communication failures across ranks, because local execution consumes memory on the same machines already holding the model and optimizer states. This is not a proof that no local-execution training configuration can ever run: lowering rollout concurrency, reducing the number of executed tests per step, or allocating more CPU and memory to the training workers would likely avoid some crashes. But those changes would directly reduce throughput, and the current local runs were already slow when they made progress: data-loading time reached 355 s per step in the worst case, compared with single-digit seconds in the corresponding CES runs. This end-to-end ablation turns the timing argument into an operational one: for optimization-test RL at this model scale and throughput target, CES is not only a cleaner timing source but also what makes the training run feasible.

本地执行仅对于非定时回退有用。当远程请求失败时，它仍然可以帮助恢复判决。它不能作为优化奖励或评估的时序源，也不能与CES时序混合，就好像两者是相当的一样。两个后端的计时完全不一致：本地持续时间更快、不太完整、重新运行时不稳定、未校准回 CES，并且与训练工作负载本身耦合。因此，混合本地和 CES 持续时间会扰乱任何试图学习严格的基于时间的奖励的模型。Local execution remains useful only for non-timing fallback. It can still help recover a verdict when a remote request fails. It cannot be used as the timing source for optimization rewards or evaluation, and it cannot be mixed with CES timings as if the two were commensurate. The two backends are fully misaligned for timing: local durations are faster, less complete, unstable across reruns, not calibrated back to CES, and coupled to the training workload itself. Mixing local and CES durations would therefore perturb any model trying to learn a serious timing-based reward.

C.3 远程执行服务C.3 Remote execution service

CES 是一个预先存在的远程执行后端，我们用于时间敏感的数据集构建、训练和评估；构建服务本身并不是本文的贡献。培训或评估人员将候选代码和测试发送到服务； CES 安排服务管理的虚拟机容量上的执行，在远程沙箱中运行它们，并返回执行状态和持续时间。我们依赖的服务属性是隔离：每个测试都在 1 GB 内存限制和 10 秒硬限制下运行，并且配置了执行能力，因此 CPU 核心被隔离并分配给沙盒作业，而不是与推出进程隐式共享。亚秒级奖励阈值在 CES 返回持续时间后应用，而不是通过更改服务限制来应用。大规模构建这样的服务本身就是一个基础设施问题，超出了本文的范围。CES is a pre-existing remote execution backend that we use for timing-sensitive dataset construction, training, and evaluation; building the service itself is not a contribution of this paper. A training or evaluation worker sends candidate code and tests to the service; CES schedules the executions on service-managed virtual-machine capacity, runs them in remote sandboxes, and returns execution statuses and durations. The service property we rely on is isolation: each test runs under a 1 GB memory limit and a 10 s hard limit, and the execution capacity is provisioned so CPU cores are isolated and assigned to sandboxed jobs rather than implicitly shared with rollout processes. Sub-second reward thresholds are applied after CES returns durations rather than by changing the service limit. Building such a service at scale is an infrastructure problem in its own right and is outside the scope of this paper.

我们使用的主要架构属性是与部署工作人员分离。候选代码不再在运行模型推理、部署编排、数据移动以及模型或优化器状态的同一台计算机上执行。相反，CES 将执行放在单独配置的队列后面，在该队列中可以控制并发性、重试可见，并且一个提交的程序不太可能通过共享 CPU 或内存压力溢出到另一个程序中。这就是为什么即使一个独立的代码测试调用比本地沙箱有更多的开销，远程服务也可能更好。The main architectural property we use is separation from the rollout workers. Candidate code is no longer executed on the same machines that run model inference, rollout orchestration, data movement, and model or optimizer state. CES instead places execution behind a separately provisioned queue where concurrency can be controlled, retries are visible, and one submitted program is less likely to spill over into another through shared CPU or memory pressure. This is why a remote service can be preferable even if one isolated code-test call has more overhead than a local sandbox.

CES 并不被认为是无噪音的：它存在服务开销、基础设施故障和时序漂移。其作用是将计时置于一个受监控的远程后端下，该后端的故障可以重试，并且其存储的持续时间可以根据新的执行进行校准。 C.4 节定义了后备规则，C.5 节量化了基础设施故障，C.7 节解释了存储持续时间校准。CES is not assumed to be noiseless: it has service overhead, infrastructure failures, and timing drift. Its role is to put timing under one monitored remote backend whose failures can be retried and whose stored durations can be calibrated against fresh executions. Section C.4 defines the fallback rule, Section C.5 quantifies infrastructure failures, and Section C.7 explains the stored-duration calibration.

C.4 远程执行失败后的回退C.4 Fallback after remote execution failures

后备路径。Fallback path.当 CES 返回不确定结果时，系统首先在 CES 上重试。如果代码测试对始终无法返回明确的远程结果，则可以使用本地回退来恢复正确性判决。最终 CES 结果保持原样。When CES returns an inconclusive result, the system first retries on CES. If a code-test pair keeps failing to return a definitive remote result, local fallback may be used to recover a correctness verdict. Definitive CES results are kept as-is.

67

提前停止与后备相互作用。当 CES 在任何测试中返回明确的硬故障时，系统可以跳过剩余不确定测试的本地重新运行，因为无论如何，轨迹都会收到负奖励。如果唯一明确的非成功状态是超时，则本地重新运行仍可能继续进行不确定的测试，因为未解决的测试可能会揭示错误的输出或运行时错误，这可能比将超时视为单独结果的奖励变体中的超时受到更多惩罚。Early stopping interacts with fallback. When CES returns a definitive hard failure on any test, the system can skip local reruns for remaining inconclusive tests because the trajectory will receive a negative reward regardless. If the only definitive non-success status is timeout, local reruns may still proceed for inconclusive tests because an unresolved test could reveal a wrong output or runtime error, which can be more penalized than a timeout in reward variants that treat timeouts as a separate outcome.

回退执行的计时测量。Timing measurements for fallback executions.训练节点上的本地执行速度可能比 CES 更快。由于服务开销、不同的 CPU 分配或内存行为，在本地完成的解决方案可能无法在远程沙箱下完成。因此，当地的成功不能被视为 CES 会在同一时间限制内报告成功的证据。对于本地持续时间本身来说更是如此，它没有理由接近同一代码测试对返回的 CES 持续时间。例如，考虑一个大型边缘情况优化测试的问题。在强化学习早期，模型推出可能会解决测试问题，但会分配不必要的大型数据结构； CES 返回的持续时间很慢，时间奖励可能会对其进行惩罚。策略更新后，稍后针对同一问题的部署可能会在该边缘情况下恶化并分配更多内存。如果 CES 仍然返回明确的结果，那么缓慢的持续时间或失败仍然是正确的训练信号。相反，如果请求跨越远程执行范围并在没有可用 CES 持续时间的情况下结束，则本地回退可以在不同的 CPU 或内存压力下重新运行相同的代码测试对，并报告成功的较短运行时间。导入本地运行时将反转信号：对于在远程执行后端下更差的一代，模型将收到更好的时序证据。Local execution on a training node can be faster than CES. A solution that completes locally may not complete under the remote sandbox because of service overhead, different CPU allocation, or memory behavior. A local success therefore cannot be treated as evidence that CES would have reported success within the same time limit. This is even more true for the local duration itself, which has no reason to be close to the CES duration that would have been returned for the same code-test pair. For example, consider one problem with a large edge-case optimization test. Early in RL, a model rollout may solve the test but allocate an unnecessarily large data structure; CES returns a slow duration, and the timing reward can penalize it. After policy updates, a later rollout on the same problem may deteriorate on that edge case and allocate even more memory. If CES still returns a definitive result, that slow duration or failure remains the correct training signal. If instead the request crosses the remote execution envelope and ends without a usable CES duration, local fallback can rerun the same code-test pair under different CPU or memory pressure and report a successful shorter runtime. Importing that local runtime would reverse the signal: the model would receive better timing evidence for a generation that was worse under the remote execution backend.

为了避免信任更快的本地计时，系统对计时敏感的优化测试应用保守转换：在回退期间本地成功的测试被重新分类为超时，持续时间设置为时间限制。正确性测试仅在 CES 仍不确定时使用本地回退来恢复通过/失败判定；本地运行时不会作为计时证据导入。To avoid trusting faster local timing, the system applies a conservative conversion on timing-sensitive optimization tests: a test that succeeds locally during fallback is reclassified as a timeout with duration set to the time limit. Correctness tests use local fallback only to recover pass/fail verdicts when CES remains inconclusive; the local runtime is not imported as timing evidence.

这两个测试类别的处理方式不同，因为超时在每个测试类别中都会产生不同的后果。在正确性测试中，将本地恢复的成功转换为超时将使原本正确的部署无法通过所有测试必须通过的大门并获得奖励The two test categories are treated differently because a timeout has different consequences in each one. On correctness tests, converting a locally recovered success into a timeout would make an otherwise correct rollout fail the all-tests-must-pass gate and receive reward−1. 因此，我们在那里使用本地回退只是为了恢复通过/失败判决，而不是创建计时值。在优化测试中，超时是计时信号的一部分。在主要实验中，超时容忍度为1.0，因此二元门可以容忍优化超时。转换后的本地成功会为排名计算贡献 10 秒的持续时间，并可能降低质量分数，但它本身并不会使解决方案变得不正确。这是预期的权衡：优化测试的保守时机，不会在正确性测试中产生错误的正确性失败。1. We therefore use local fallback there only to recover the pass/fail verdict, not to create a timing value. On optimization tests, timeouts are part of the timing signal. In the main experiments, the timeout tolerance is 1.0, so optimization timeouts are tolerated by the binary gate. A converted local success then contributes a 10 s duration to the ranking computation and can lower the quality score, but it does not by itself make the solution incorrect. This is the intended tradeoff: conservative timing for optimization tests, without creating false correctness failures on correctness tests.

C.5 基础设施故障的定量影响C.5 Quantitative impact of infrastructure failures

Fallback仅在CES未返回明确结果时才进入奖励。因此，我们首先要问的是，这些缺失的远程结果在训练运行的规模上有多重要。Fallback only enters the reward when CES does not return a definitive result. We therefore first ask how often those missing remote results can matter at the scale of a training run.

罕见的失败会随着训练的增加而增加。Rare failures add up over training.让Letf是每次测试的基础设施故障率，be the per-test infrastructure-failure rate andn正确性测试的数量。考虑一个只要 CES 返回明确答案就会通过的正确解决方案。如果失败独立地影响测试，则至少一个正确性测试达到回退的概率为the number of correctness tests. Consider a correct solution that would pass whenever CES returns a definitive answer. If failures hit tests independently, the probability that at least one correctness test reaches fallback is

独立性假设是一种简化，但它足以说明为什么统一的后备规则在正确性测试中可能是危险的。每次测试的失败率看起来可以忽略不计，而在部署过程中至少出现一次回退的可能性却不容忽视。表 27 显示了灵敏度。如果每次测试失败率为 2%，进行 30 次正确性测试，则 45.5% 的正确部署将至少达到回退一次。即使在The independence assumption is a simplification, but it is enough to show why a uniform fallback rule can be dangerous on correctness tests. A per-test failure rate can look negligible while the probability of seeing at least one fallback in a rollout is not negligible. Table 27 shows the sensitivity. At a 2% per-test failure rate with 30 correctness tests, 45.5% of correct rollouts would reach fallback at least once. Even atf = 0 .1%，30 次测试的风险为 3.0%，100 次测试的风险为 9.5%，150 次测试的风险为 13.9%。1%, the risk is 3.0% with 30 tests, 9.5% with 100 tests, and 13.9% with 150 tests.

一旦我们乘以优化器步骤，规模就会变得更大。 Qwen 2.5 7B 运行具有 10,000 个优化器步骤、16 个优化器数据并行副本以及每个副本每个步骤最多 32,768 个打包令牌。在整个运行过程中，优化器可以处理多达 5.24B 的打包训练令牌。如果每个消耗的代码片段向训练器贡献大约 11k 个令牌，则填充这些批次需要大约 4The scale becomes even larger once we multiply by the optimizer steps. A Qwen 2.5 7B run has 10,000 optimizer steps, 16 optimizer data-parallel replicas, and up to 32,768 packed tokens per replica per step. Across the run, the optimizer can process up to 5.24B packed training tokens. If each consumed code snippet contributes about 11k tokens to the trainer, filling those batches requires about 4. 8 × 105代码片段。因为训练器会打包整个片段并且不会分割较长的片段，所以要进行更严格的统一计数code snippets. Because the trainer packs whole snippets and does not split a long one, a stricter count with uniformly

CES 可靠性研究。CES reliability study.接下来我们检查这些速率在实际 RL 运行中的表现。该案例研究涵盖 13 次训练运行：6 次 CWM 32B 运行和 7 次 Qwen 2.5 7B 运行，所有训练均针对 10,000 个优化器步骤进行训练，并使用 CES 进行时序敏感的执行。每 10 个优化器步骤都会记录一次指标。在每个模型和奖励系列中，运行使用相同的数据集、环境、模型初始化、种子、学习率、GRPO 设置和持续时间校准；主要的不受控制的变量是启动时间，它会改变在推出收集期间看到的 CES 服务状态。分布式异步训练系统还在推出顺序和批次组成中引入了不确定性，因此相同的配置预计不会产生相同的轨迹。We next check how these rates look in actual RL runs. The case study covers 13 training runs: six CWM 32B runs and seven Qwen 2.5 7B runs, all trained for 10,000 optimizer steps with CES used for timing-sensitive execution. Metrics are logged every 10 optimizer steps. Within each model and reward family, the runs use the same dataset, environment, model initialization, seed, learning rate, GRPO settings, and duration calibration; the main uncontrolled variable is launch time, which changes the CES service state seen during rollout collection. The distributed asynchronous training system also introduces nondeterminism in rollout ordering and batch composition, so identical configurations are not expected to produce identical trajectories.

表 28 报告了所有 13 次运行的预热后统计数据，在步骤 1,000 之后计算。记录的 CES-only 速率是一个推出级别的数量：仅当模型输出解析、代码测试执行正确发送到 CES、并且 CES 解决它而不启动本地沙箱回退时，它才很高。本地回退是补充恢复路径：它跟踪 CES 未提供明确的可用结果后必须启动本地沙箱的情况。因此，CES-only 可能会因两种不同的原因而下降：CES 之前解析失败，或者可解析程序发送到 CES 后远程执行失败。在第 1,000 步之后，这些运行中的解析率高于 97%，因此剩余的下降主要提供有关 CES 和回退行为的信息。在该预热后状态下，Qwen 2.5 7B 平均仅 CES 执行率为 99.3%，而 CWM 32B 为 98.1%。这种差异是预料之中的，因为 CWM 32B 会生成更正确的程序，因此更多的部署将继续进行优化测试执行并将并发负载置于 CES 上。 Qwen 2.5 7B 的本地回落平均值为 0.13%，CWM 32B 的本地回落平均值为 0.21%；基础设施故障和未知故障平均分别为 0.10% 和 0.05%。这里，未知结果意味着 CES 没有返回可以解析为正常程序结果的状态。由于解析失败、错误答案、异常和超时是单独记录的，因此在下面的分析中，这些未知结果将被视为基础设施端不确定的结果。Table 28 reports post-warmup statistics from all 13 runs, computed after step 1,000. The logged CES-only rate is a rollout-level quantity: it is high only when the model output parses, the code-test execution is correctly sent to CES, and CES resolves it without launching a local sandbox fallback. Local fallback is the complementary recovery path: it tracks cases where a local sandbox had to be launched after CES did not provide a definitive usable result. CES-only can therefore drop for two different reasons: parse failures before CES, or remote-execution failures after a parseable program is sent to CES. After step 1,000, parsing is above 97% in these runs, so the remaining drops are mostly informative about CES and fallback behavior. In that post-warmup regime, Qwen 2.5 7B has 99.3% CES-only execution on average, while CWM 32B has 98.1%. The difference is expected because CWM 32B produces more correct programs, so more rollouts proceed to optimization-test execution and place concurrent load on CES. Local fallback averages 0.13% for Qwen 2.5 7B and 0.21% for CWM 32B; infrastructure and unknown failures average 0.10% and 0.05%, respectively. Here, an unknown result means that CES did not return a status that could be parsed into a normal program outcome. Since parse failures, wrong answers, exceptions, and timeouts are logged separately, these unknown results are treated as infrastructure-side inconclusive results in the analysis below.

解析热身。Parsing warmups.图 27 还显示了前大约 500 个优化器步骤中的普遍早期训练下降，并通过步骤 1,000 恢复。这不是 CES 基础设施故障。记录的指标显示，这种早期下降跟踪解析率：无法生成可解析代码的首次部署永远不会到达 CES，而在下降期间本地重新运行和基础设施故障率仍然接近于零。对于 CWM 32B，主要的早期问题是 CWM 报告（FAIR CodeGen 团队等人，2025）中记录的 SFT 和 RL 之间的输出格式切换：在第 10 步左右，大约 26% 的部署无法解析，而模型重新适应所需的 RL 格式。 Qwen 2.5 7B 的下降要小得多，因为它的 SFT 检查点已经为该环境生成了格式良好的代码。一旦解析稳定在 97% 以上，较低的 CES-only 值就不再是格式学习工件，并且可以解释为远程执行或回退事件。这就是下表报告预热后统计数据的原因。Figure 27 also shows a universal early-training dip during the first roughly 500 optimizer steps, with recovery by step 1,000. This is not a CES infrastructure failure. The logged metrics show that this early dip tracks parse rate: rollouts that fail to produce parseable code never reach CES, while local rerun and infrastructure-failure rates remain near zero during the dip. For CWM 32B, the main early issue is the output-format switch between SFT and RL documented in the CWM report (FAIR CodeGen team et al., 2025): around step 10, roughly 26% of rollouts fail to parse while the model re-adapts to the required RL format. Qwen 2.5 7B has a much smaller dip because its SFT checkpoint already produces well-formatted code for this environment. Once parsing stabilizes above 97%, lower CES-only values are no longer a format-learning artifact and can be interpreted as remote-execution or fallback events. This is why the tables below report post-warmup statistics.

基础设施错误的随机突发。Random bursts of infra errors.解析预热后，主要的可见故障是短突发。表 29 列出了仅 CES 低于 90% 的每个预热后间隔。有四项赛事：CWM 32B p30 run 2 中有两项，Qwen 2.5 7B p30 run 2 中有一项，Qwen 2.5 7B p30 run 3 中有一项。最差的 CWM 32B 赛事下降After parsing warmup, the main visible failures are short bursts. Table 29 lists every post-warmup interval where CES-only falls below 90%. There are four events: two in CWM 32B p30 run 2, one in Qwen 2.5 7B p30 run 2, and one in Qwen 2.5 7B p30 run 3. 仅 CES 至 54.3%，将本地重新运行率提高至 41.6%，并将超时率提高至 14.1%。 Qwen 2.5 7B 爆发较为温和，仅在 CES 上爆发的最低值约为 80%，本地重播峰值约为 19%。在同一模型和奖励系列中，具有相同配置的其他运行不会显示这些突发，因此最好将这些事件视为服务状态事件而不是奖励的属性。CES-only to 54.3%, pushes local rerun to 41.6%, and raises timeout rate to 14.1%. The Qwen 2.5 7B bursts are milder, with CES-only minima around 80% and local rerun peaks around 19%. Within the same model and reward family, other runs with the same configuration do not show these bursts, so these events are best treated as service-state events rather than properties of the reward.

突发机制并不是硬基础设施错误的大幅增加。在最差的单个记录步骤中，CWM 32B p30 在步骤 9,260 处运行 2，硬件基础设施错误为 0.00%，未知或不确定的 CES 结果为 6.3%，未知触发的本地重新运行达到 44.5%，超时达到 14.1%。这些未知的结果不是模型解析失败或普通的测试失败；这些状态是单独记录的。它们是 CES 结果，未解析为正常结论，因此与基础设施方面的不确定结果归为一组。仅跟踪硬件基础设施错误的监视器将错过这种降级。该服务仍在返回响应，但其中太多是不确定的，这会通过回退推动执行并更改奖励所看到的优化测试持续时间。The burst mechanism is not a large spike in hard infrastructure errors. In the worst single logged step, CWM 32B p30 run 2 at step 9,260, hard infrastructure errors are 0.00%, unknown or inconclusive CES results are 6.3%, unknown-triggered local reruns reach 44.5%, and timeouts reach 14.1%. These unknown results are not model parse failures or ordinary test failures; those statuses are logged separately. They are CES outcomes that were not parsed into a normal verdict and are therefore grouped with infrastructure-side inconclusive results. A monitor that only tracks hard infrastructure errors would miss this degradation. The service is still returning responses, but too many of them are inconclusive, which pushes execution through fallback and changes the optimization-test durations seen by the reward.

除了基础设施错误之外，还有无声的服务降级。Beyond infra errors, silent service degradations.图 30 比较了具有相同配置的两次 CWM 32B p30 运行。运行 1 几乎没有明显的回退或基础设施事件：98.53% 平均仅 CES，95.09% 最低仅 CES，0.0003% 本地回退，0.0001% 基础设施或未知故障。运行 2 有明显的后期爆发：97.18% 平均仅 CES，54.33% 最低仅 CES，0.77% 本地回退，0.16% 基础设施或未知故障。然而，运行 1 在第 1,000 步和第 9,000 步之间的训练返回噪声要大得多：平均返回Figure 30 compares two CWM 32B p30 runs with the same configuration. Run 1 has almost no visible fallback or infrastructure events: 98.53% mean CES-only, 95.09% minimum CES-only, 0.0003% local fallback, and 0.0001% infrastructure or unknown failures. Run 2 has visible late bursts: 97.18% mean CES-only, 54.33% minimum CES-only, 0.77% local fallback, and 0.16% infrastructure or unknown failures. Yet run 1 has much noisier training returns between steps 1,000 and 9,000: mean return− 0 .14，标准差0.34，范围1.43，与+0相比14, standard deviation 0.34, and range 1.43, compared with +0.对于运行 2，分别为 34、0.15 和 0.85。此比较并不能证明噪声较大的运行是单独由 CES 状态引起的；异步推出顺序和早期探索差异也可能改变轨迹，并且通过率手段与早期训练不同。它确实显示了我们必须监控的失败模式：CES 可以不断返回判决，因此通过/失败结果和回退率看起来很健康，而较慢或嘈杂的服务状态会改变排名奖励使用的记录持续时间。34, 0.15, and 0.85 for run 2. This comparison does not prove that the noisier run is caused by CES state alone; asynchronous rollout ordering and early exploration differences can also change the trajectory, and the pass-rate means differ from early training onward. It does show the failure mode we have to monitor: CES can keep returning verdicts, so pass/fail outcomes and fallback rates look healthy, while slower or noisier service state shifts the recorded durations used by the ranking reward.

图 27 仅 CES 执行超过 10,000 个训练步骤。Figure 27 CES-only execution over 10,000 training steps.顶部： 6 次 CWM 32B 跑步，按排名划分Top: six CWM 32B runs, split between rankedp30和QP奖励。底部：七次 Qwen 2.5 7B 运行，按排名划分30 and QP rewards. Bottom: seven Qwen 2.5 7B runs, split between rankedp30和QP奖励。所有运行都显示在步骤 1,000 之前出现早期下降，然后恢复到预热后的运行状态。这种初始下降反映了解析预热而不是 CES 健康状况：无法生成可解析代码的首次展示永远不会发送到 CES，因此不算作仅限 CES。 CWM 32B 的影响最大，因为 CWM 配方改变了 SFT 和 RL 之间的输出格式（FAIR CodeGen 团队等人，2025）；在强化学习开始时，模型会暂时重新适应所需的强化学习格式。 Qwen 2.5 7B 开始时更接近最终解析率。 CWM 32B 在预热后以略低的仅 CES 速率运行，因为它生成更正确的代码并执行更多测试。解析稳定后，CWM 32B p30 run 2 和 Qwen 2.5 7B p30 run 2-3 中的急剧下降是短远程执行突发事件，而不是格式学习效应。30 and QP rewards. All runs show an early dip before step 1,000, then recover to the post-warmup operating regime. This initial dip reflects parsing warmup rather than CES health: rollouts that fail to produce parseable code are never sent to CES and therefore do not count as CES-only. The effect is largest for CWM 32B because the CWM recipe changes output format between SFT and RL (FAIR CodeGen team et al., 2025); at the start of RL, the model temporarily re-adapts to the required RL format. Qwen 2.5 7B starts much closer to the final parse rate. CWM 32B operates at slightly lower post-warmup CES-only rate because it produces more correct code and executes more tests. After parsing has stabilized, the sharp drops in CWM 32B p30 run 2 and Qwen 2.5 7B p30 runs 2–3 are short remote-execution burst events rather than format-learning effects.

表 30 两个 CWM 32B p30 运行具有不同的 CES 症状。Table 30 Two CWM 32B p30 runs with different CES symptoms.两次运行都使用相同的模型、数据、奖励和超参数。运行 1 几乎没有明显的回退或基础设施事件，但回报不稳定；运行 2 具有较晚的可见突发，但在突发之前返回更平滑。这种比较促使我们监控奖励流和持续时间分布，而不仅仅是硬故障率。Both runs use the same model, data, reward, and hyperparameters. Run 1 has almost no visible fallback or infrastructure events but volatile returns; run 2 has late visible bursts but smoother returns before the bursts. The comparison motivates monitoring the reward stream and duration distributions, not only hard failure rates.

返回范围，步长 1,000–9,000Return range, steps 1,000–9,000 1.43 0.85
第 1,000 步后的平均通过率Pass-rate mean after step 1,000 0.45 0.68

16 个优化器数据并行副本，每个副本每个步骤大约有两个消耗的代码片段，这对应于大约 1,200 个低于 90% CES 的消耗代码片段和 2,300 个低于 95% 的代码片段。这些只是 10,000 步运行的一小部分，在记录的步数下分别为 0.4% 和 0.8%，但每步的影响可能很严重：在最严重的突发情况下，高达 41.6% 的执行由本地回退处理。回退在运行范围内的平均值中可能很少见，集中到仅影响优化器步骤的一小部分，并且在突发期间仍然足够大以改变这些步骤的奖励流。16 optimizer data-parallel replicas and about two consumed snippets per replica per step, this corresponds to roughly 1,200 consumed code snippets below 90% CES-only and 2,300 below 95%. These are small fractions of the 10,000-step run, 0.4% and 0.8% under the logged-step count, but the per-step effect can be severe: during the worst burst, up to 41.6% of executions are handled by local fallback. Fallback can be rare in the run-wide average, concentrated enough to affect only a small fraction of optimizer steps, and still large enough during a burst to change the reward stream for those steps.

图 28 CES 突发事件剖析。Figure 28 Anatomy of CES burst events.上图：两台 CWM 32B p30 运行 2 次突发。底部：两颗 Qwen 2.5 7B p30 连发。每个面板显示仅 CES 执行、本地重新运行和超时率以及降级间隔附近的轨迹返回。该机制在各个模型中是一致的：仅 CES 下降、不确定结果重运行上升、超时率上升以及突发期间返回下降。对于 CWM 32B p30 运行 2，仅 CES 在两个记录记录内从大约 98% 下降到 54%，并在第一次突发的八个记录记录内恢复。Top: the two CWM 32B p30 run 2 bursts. Bottom: the two Qwen 2.5 7B p30 bursts. Each panel shows CES-only execution, local rerun and timeout rates, and trajectory return around the degraded interval. The mechanism is consistent across models: CES-only drops, inconclusive-result reruns rise, timeout rate rises, and the return falls during the burst. For CWM 32B p30 run 2, CES-only drops from about 98% to 54% within two logged records and recovers within eight logged records in the first burst.

图 30 CES 静默退化会影响计时奖励。Figure 30 Silent CES degradation can affect timing rewards.该图比较了 CWM 32B p30 运行 1 和运行 2。运行 1 将 CES-only 保持在 95% 以上，并且几乎没有局部回退，但其轨迹返回在整个训练过程中不稳定。运行 2 有两个后期可见的 CES 突发，但在步骤 1,000 和步骤 9,000 之间，其返回更平滑且更高。这并不能确定运行 1 的单一原因：训练不确定性和早期探索也可以改变回报。它说明了为什么即使没有硬基础设施错误，服务状态退化也可能很重要：如果 CES 仍然返回判决，但记录发生了变化或噪音较大，则通过/失败结果可能看起来很正常，而时间奖励发生了变化。The figure compares CWM 32B p30 run 1 and run 2. Run 1 keeps CES-only above 95% and has almost no local fallback, but its trajectory returns are volatile throughout training. Run 2 has two late visible CES bursts, but between step 1,000 and step 9,000 its returns are smoother and higher. This does not identify a single cause for run 1: training nondeterminism and early exploration can also change returns. It shows why a service-state degradation can matter even without hard infrastructure errors: if CES still returns verdicts but records shifted or noisier durations, pass/fail outcomes may look normal while the timing reward changes.

74

C.6 替代后备设计C.6 Alternative fallback designs

按类别转换。Per-categoryconversion.在训练中，我们以不同的方式处理正确性和优化测试。对于优化测试，当持续时间进入奖励时，本地恢复的成功将转换为超时。对于正确性测试，目标只是恢复通过/失败判定，我们保留本地判定，因为 10 秒的限制使得亚秒级本地成功可能仍然是 CES 成功。这消除了正确性假阴性模式，同时保持保守的优化时机。如果 CES 可靠性下降，剩下的改进将是调整特定于类别的回退阈值，并决定何时应丢弃而不是恢复轨迹。In training, we handle correctness and optimization tests differently. For optimization tests, where the duration enters the reward, a locally recovered success is converted to a timeout. For correctness tests, where the goal is only to recover a pass/fail verdict, we keep the local verdict because the 10 s limit makes a sub-second local success likely to remain a CES success. This removes the correctness false-negative mode while keeping conservative timing for optimization. If CES reliability degrades, the remaining refinement would be to tune category-specific fallback thresholds and decide when a trajectory should be discarded instead of recovered.

立即 CES 重试。Immediate CES retry.在使用本地回退之前，在最终确定推出奖励之前，会立即重试不确定的 CES 结果。这对于瞬时请求失败很有用：仍然可以从远程执行计算奖励，而无需混合计时后端。它不能解决持续爆发的问题。如果同一批次不断返回基础设施或未知结果，重试只会延迟该步骤，系统仍然需要上面的按类别转换或丢弃规则。在异步强化学习中，重试预算也必须保持较短。如果重试的时间过长，它最终会被策略已经转移的优化器消耗。同样的问题也出现在提示组中：如果立即测量某个问题的某些部署，而在很晚之后重新执行不确定的问题，则 CES 速度的瞬态变化可能会使优势计算所比较的持续时间变得不具有可比性。Before local fallback is used, inconclusive CES results are retried immediately, before the rollout reward is finalized. This is useful for transient request failures: the reward can still be computed from a remote execution without mixing timing backends. It does not solve persistent bursts. If the same batch keeps returning infrastructure or unknown results, retrying only delays the step and the system still needs the per-category conversion above or a discard rule. The retry budget also has to stay short in asynchronous RL. If a rollout is retried for too long, it is eventually consumed by an optimizer whose policy has already moved on. The same issue appears inside a prompt group: if some rollouts for a problem are measured immediately while the inconclusive ones are re-executed much later, transient changes in CES speed can make the durations compared by the advantage computation less comparable.

丢弃或跳过受影响的数据。Discarding or skipping affected data.一种替代方法是放弃至少一个测试回退到本地执行的任何轨迹。这消除了回退中的误报和漏报，但会丢失太多数据。与One alternative is to discard any trajectory where at least one test falls back to local execution. This removes both false positives and false negatives from fallback, but it loses too much data. Withn总测试总数，丢弃率为1total total tests, the discard rate is 1− (1 - f− f )_n_总计_n_total。如果每次测试失败率为 2%，总共进行 80 次测试，则大约 80% 的轨迹将被丢弃。即使是0.5%，报废率也达到33%。丢弃概率还与每个问题的测试数量相关，从而使训练偏向于较小的套件。另一种选择是跳过失败的测试并评估剩余的测试。这可以避免转换超时造成的误报，但它可能会产生误报：如果跳过的测试是唯一捕获错误的测试，则错误的解决方案会获得积极的奖励。对于正确性测试，一个错过的错误可能会翻转奖励标志，这种权衡对于数百万条轨迹来说是不利的。. At a 2% per-test failure rate with 80 total tests, about 80% of trajectories would be discarded. Even at 0.5%, the discard rate reaches 33%. The discard probability also correlates with the number of tests per problem, biasing training toward smaller suites. Another option is to skip failed tests and evaluate on the remaining tests. This avoids false negatives from converted timeouts, but it can create false positives: if the skipped test is the only one that catches a bug, a wrong solution receives positive reward. For correctness tests, where one missed bug can flip the reward sign, this trade-off is unfavorable over millions of trajectories.

隔离降级的窗口。Quarantining degraded windows.更强大的操作控制是在服务进入不良状态时暂停推出收集、删除受影响的批次或回滚到较早的检查点。如果退化信号足够可靠，这对于可见的突发和无声的减速都是合理的。它的成本也很高：在诊断退化之前，策略可能已经消耗了噪音奖励，丢弃批次会浪费 GPU 时间，回滚分布式 RL 运行会增加操作复杂性的另一个来源。因此，我们不会在本文的 RL 实验中实现这样的例程。A stronger operational control would be to pause rollout collection, drop affected batches, or roll back to an earlier checkpoint when the service enters a bad state. This is plausible for both visible bursts and silent slowdowns if the degradation signal is reliable enough. It is also expensive: the policy may have already consumed noisy rewards before the degradation is diagnosed, discarding batches wastes GPU time, and rolling back a distributed RL run adds another source of operational complexity. We therefore do not implement such routine for the RL experiments of this paper.

实时校准探头。Live calibration probes.另一个自然的想法是在训练期间发送一组具有已知参考持续时间的固定代码片段，使用它们测量的减速来检测无声的 CES 退化，并重新调整当前的推出持续时间。仅当探针组准确估计当前服务状态时，这才有效。太少的探测器会产生噪音，特别是在几十毫秒很重要的亚秒级范围内。太多的探针与 CES 容量的训练工作量竞争；监控流量可能会减慢服务和培训进度，从而成为问题的一部分。这使得实时探测更适合作为健康诊断而不是作为廉价的每步校正机制。Another natural idea is to send a fixed set of code snippets with known reference durations during training, use their measured slowdown to detect silent CES degradation, and rescale current rollout durations. This can work only if the probe set estimates the current service state accurately. Too few probes are noisy, especially in the sub-second regime where tens of milliseconds matter. Too many probes compete with the training workload for CES capacity; the monitoring traffic can slow the service and training progress enough to become part of the problem. This makes live probing more suitable as a health diagnostic than as a cheap per-step correction mechanism.

奖励选择。Reward choices.奖励设计可以降低但不能消除对服务漂移的敏感性。相同提示分组和 GRPO 的优点是比较紧密收集的部署，这在服务转移在批次中很常见时很有帮助。纯粹的批次内时间比较将进一步推动这个想法，但我们没有将其用作主要奖励，因为我们的排名奖励还将新模型执行与存储的人类参考持续时间进行比较。这些存储的参考仍然需要接下来研究仿射校准，F 部分中的奖励消融表明数据质量、回退行为、校准和奖励设计必须一起处理。Reward design can reduce, but not remove, sensitivity to service drift. Same-prompt grouping and GRPO advantages compare rollouts collected close together, which helps when the service shift is common across a batch. A purely within-batch timing comparison would push this idea further, but we did not use it as the main reward because our ranked rewards also compare fresh model executions against stored human-reference durations. Those stored references still need the affine calibration studied next, and the reward ablations in Section F show that data quality, fallback behavior, calibration, and reward design have to be handled together.

75

C.7 CES 噪声、漂移和校准C.7 CES noise, drift, and calibration

切换到 CES 并不能消除噪音。它将噪音集中在后端，后端的行为可以被监控、重试和重新校准。 CES 时序影响管道的四个阶段：数据集构建，存储参考持续时间； RL 训练，每次部署都会进行评估；评估，再次衡量对时间敏感的结果；以及交叉运行比较，其中当前结果与排名奖励变体使用的存储的人类参考持续时间进行比较，或者与在早期 CES 状态下运行的实验的评估结果进行比较。前三个阶段取决于实时测量噪声。第四个引入了服务状态漂移：存储的持续时间或存储的评估结果可能是在与当前执行不同的 CES 队列、集群、负载条件或沙箱版本上收集的。Switching to CES does not remove noise. It concentrates noise in a backend whose behavior can be monitored, retried, and recalibrated. CES timings affect four stages of the pipeline: dataset construction, where reference durations are stored; RL training, where each rollout is evaluated; evaluation, where timing-sensitive outcomes are measured again; and cross-run comparisons, where current results are compared either with stored human-reference durations used by ranked reward variants or with evaluation results from experiments run under an earlier CES state. The first three stages depend on live measurement noise. The fourth introduces service-state drift: stored durations or stored evaluation results may have been collected on a different CES fleet, cluster, load condition, or sandbox version than the current execution.

因此，我们将 CES 时序分为两层。现场 CES 噪音询问当前服务状态下的重复执行是否足够稳定以进行排名。长期 CES 漂移询问历史测量结果（无论是人类参考持续时间还是早期运行的评估输出）是否仍与当前模型执行具有可比性。对于存储的参考持续时间，我们应用下面的仿射校正；对于交叉运行评估比较，同样的问题会促使重新执行或校准敏感性检查。We therefore treat CES timing in two layers. Live CES noise asks whether repeated executions under the current service state are stable enough for ranking. Long-term CES drift asks whether historical measurements, either human-reference durations or evaluation outputs from earlier runs, remain comparable to current model executions. For stored reference durations, we apply the affine correction below; for cross-run evaluation comparisons, the same issue motivates re-execution or calibration-sensitivity checks.

同时分组可以减弱实时漂移。Same-time grouping attenuates live drift.在强化学习期间，同一提示的转出会同时执行和分组，并且训练器不会对这些配置使用重播缓冲区。这很有帮助，因为提示组内的共模后端转换是部分共享的，并且不会与来自更旧的服务体系的轨迹混合。它不会消除奖励噪声：排名奖励仍然将模型执行与校准的历史人类参考持续时间进行比较，因此校准和健康监控仍然是必要的。During RL, rollouts for the same prompt are executed and grouped contemporaneously, and the trainer does not use a replay buffer for these configurations. This helps because common-mode backend shifts within a prompt group are partially shared and are not mixed with trajectories from much older service regimes. It does not remove reward noise: ranked rewards still compare model executions against calibrated historical human-reference durations, so calibration and health monitoring remain necessary.

校准活动设置。Calibration campaign setup.第一个校准活动适合对人类参考持续时间对的校正。每次运行还记录一个用于排名诊断的模型解决方案，但拟合地图本身是根据人类参考估计的，因为目标是在将新模型解决方案插入到同一排行榜之前将存储的参考持续时间映射到当前的 CES 时间尺度。我们从过滤后的 DMC-Optim 池中抽取了 33 个问题。在这些问题中，人类参考池包含 10,841 个解决方案，每个问题有 14-3,185 个解决方案，中位数为 158。优化测试套件总共包含 4,035 个测试，每个问题有 30-150 个测试，中位数为 135。每个问题通过 CES 独立重新执行约 20 次； 27 个问题有全部 20 次运行，6 个问题有 19 次运行，因为此校准设置不允许局部回退，因此在没有回退的情况下仍然太不稳定的运行将从拟合中删除。这给出了 654 个问题运行。The first calibration campaign fits the correction on human-reference duration pairs. Each run also records one model solution for ranking diagnostics, but the fitted map itself is estimated from human references because the goal is to map stored reference durations into the current CES timing scale before a fresh model solution is inserted into the same leaderboard. We sample 33 problems from the filtered DMC-Optim pool. Across these problems, the human-reference pool contains 10,841 solutions, with 14–3,185 solutions per problem and median 158. The optimization-test suites contain 4,035 tests in total, with 30–150 tests per problem and median 135. Each problem is re-executed independently about 20 times through CES; 27 problems have all 20 runs and 6 problems have 19 runs because this calibration setup does not allow local fallback, so runs that remain too unstable without fallback are removed from the fit. This gives 654 problem-runs.

原始规模大于问题计数所显示的规模。单个问题运行执行优化测试中的所有人类参考，因此该活动产生 26,675,153 个原始对（The raw scale is larger than the problem count suggests. A single problem-run executes all human references on the optimization tests, so the campaign yields 26,675,153 raw pairs (d已存储stored, d新鲜）。在拟合之前，我们通过唯一的三元组（问题fresh). Before fitting, we pool repeated measurements by the unique triple (problem,测试test,解决方案）：存储的持续时间由数据集固定，而新的 CES 持续时间是 19-20 次运行的平均值。这给出了 1,369,381 个汇总观察值。拟合仅使用优化测试，因为这些测试的持续时间进入排名奖励；正确性测试对于通过/失败恢复很有用，但它们不是时序校准的目标分布。solution): the stored duration is fixed by the dataset, while the fresh CES duration is averaged across the 19–20 runs. This gives 1,369,381 pooled observations. The fit uses only optimization tests, because these are the tests whose duration enters the ranking reward; correctness tests can be useful for pass/fail recovery, but they are not the target distribution for timing calibration.

短期 CES 变化。Short-run CES variability.重复的 CES 执行表明存在短期噪声，但噪声规模远低于 C.2 节中的本地沙箱不稳定性。在原始问题-测试-解决方案水平上，重复 CES 测量的变异系数平均值为 9.1%，中位数为 8.2%，p5-p95 范围为 3.5%-18.0%。从绝对值来看，重复持续时间测量的标准差平均值为 12.4 ms，中位数为 9.9 ms，p5-p95 范围为 6.2-26.7 ms。这些数字不为零：它们足够大，对于最快的测试很重要，其中几十毫秒就可以使解决方案跨越严格的百分位数边界。它们也比奖励试图利用的跨解决方案时序信号小得多。Repeated CES executions show that short-run noise is present, but the noise scale is far below the local-sandbox instability in Section C.2. At the raw problem-test-solution level, the coefficient of variation across repeated CES measurements has mean 9.1%, median 8.2%, and a p5–p95 range of 3.5%–18.0%. In absolute terms, the standard deviation of a repeated duration measurement has mean 12.4 ms and median 9.9 ms, with p5–p95 range 6.2–26.7 ms. These numbers are not zero: they are large enough to matter for the fastest tests, where a few tens of milliseconds can move a solution across a tight percentile boundary. They are also much smaller than the cross-solution timing signal the reward tries to exploit.

将持续时间转换为排名奖励所使用的排行榜统计数据后，同样的结论成立。对于每个优化测试，候选持续时间根据同一测试的人类参考持续时间进行排名，0% 表示最快，100% 表示最慢。平均百分位数是优化测试中这些每次测试百分位数的平均值。在 20 次重复的 CES 运行中，平均百分位数的标准差平均为 2.1 个百分点，中位数为 2.0，范围为 1.1-4.5 个百分点。一个两点The same conclusion holds after converting durations into the leaderboard statistic used by the ranked rewards. For each optimization test, the candidate duration is ranked against human-reference durations for that same test, with 0% denoting fastest and 100% slowest. The mean percentile is the average of these per-test percentiles across optimization tests. Across the 20 repeated CES runs, the standard deviation of mean percentile averages 2.1 percentage points, with median 2.0 and range 1.1–4.5 percentage points. A two-point

在严格的指标中，百分位噪声尺度仍然可见，但它与上面报告的 10.961 点局部标准差和 41.173 点局部范围相距甚远。percentile noise scale is still visible in strict metrics, but it is far from the 10.961-point local standard deviation and 41.173-point local range reported above.

方差分解。Variance decomposition.原始持续时间分解是有利的。三重组间方差为 0.02906，而重复 CES 测量的三重组内方差为 0.00027，而总方差为 0.02994。同样，大约 97.1% 的变异是由问题-测试-解决方案三元组之间的实际差异来解释的，而重复测量部分大约占总规模的 0.9%。这就是对运行期间的新测量值进行平均可以得到稳定拟合的原因：校准拟合的是存储持续时间和新持续时间之间的系统关系，而不是主要的 CES 抖动。对于持续时间排名的奖励，剩余的 2.1 点平均百分位噪声对应于 0 量级的奖励扰动The raw-duration decomposition is favorable. The between-triple variance is 0.02906, while the within-triple variance from repeated CES measurement is 0.00027, against total variance 0.02994. Equivalently, about 97.1% of the variation is explained by real differences between problem-test-solution triples, while the repeated-measurement component is about 0.9% of the total scale. This is the reason averaging fresh measurements across runs gives a stable fit: the calibration is fitting the systematic relationship between stored and fresh durations, not mostly CES jitter. For duration-ranked rewards, the remaining 2.1-point mean-percentile noise corresponds to a reward perturbation on the order of 0. 01–0 .每集 02，相对于正确性波动较小02 per episode, small relative to the correctness swing from−1 获得正奖励，并在最终训练分组中对每个问题的大约 120 次优化测试进行部分平均。1 to a positive reward and partly averaged out across the roughly 120 optimization tests per problem in the final training split.

长期 CES 漂移。Long-term CES drift.更大的问题是将当前的 CES 测量值与之前存储的持续时间进行比较，这些持续时间要么来自人类参考，要么来自在早期 CES 状态下评估的模型生成。一项早期的 20 个问题试点显示，新鲜与存储的持续时间比率范围为 0.96 到 1.34，平均值为 1.15，对于一个全局乘数来说过于异质。完整的 33 个问题活动使漂移的形状更加清晰。采用一组固定的先前存储的人类参考持续时间，新校准活动中的新 CES 测量值通常低于较长测试中的存储值，因此需要低于 1 的斜率。然而，对于最快的测试，会出现固定开销：接近零的存储持续时间不会映射到接近零的新持续时间。这正是严格计时百分位数所使用的制度，因此即使在中期和长期测试中乘法拟合看起来合理，忽略截距也会扭曲排名。在更大规模的校准研究中，未校正的存储排名与新的 CES 排名只有 0.54 Spearman 相关性，33 个问题中有 29 个在同一方向上存在平均 19.6 个百分点的偏差。即使现场 CES 通话没有噪音，冻结的存储持续时间也会扭曲参考排名，除非重新校准。The larger issue is comparing current CES measurements against previously stored durations, either from human references or from model generations evaluated under an earlier CES state. An early 20-problem pilot showed fresh-to-stored duration ratios ranging from 0.96 to 1.34, with mean 1.15, too heterogeneous for one global multiplier. The full 33-problem campaign makes the shape of the drift clearer. Taking a fixed set of previously stored human-reference durations, fresh CES measurements from the new calibration campaign are often below the stored values on longer tests, so a slope below one is needed. For the fastest tests, however, a fixed overhead appears: a stored duration near zero does not map to a fresh duration near zero. This is exactly the regime used by strict timing percentiles, so ignoring the intercept can distort the ranking even if a multiplicative fit looks plausible on medium and long tests. In the larger calibration study, uncorrected stored rankings have only 0.54 Spearman correlation with fresh CES rankings, and 29 of 33 problems are biased in the same direction by an average of 19.6 percentage points. Even if live CES calls were noiseless, frozen stored durations would distort reference rankings unless recalibrated.

仿射校正。Affine correction.我们通过拟合从存储的 CES 持续时间到当前 CES 持续时间的仿射图来纠正长期漂移：We correct long-term drift by fitting an affine map from stored to current CES durations:

拟合是通过留一问题交叉验证来执行的：每次折叠保留一个完整的问题，拟合其他 32 个问题，并预测保留问题的新持续时间。这比随机保留单个持续时间对更严格，因为保留问题的所有测试和解决方案在拟合期间都是看不见的。表 32 报告了所考虑的四个系列。需要拦截。纯粹的乘法修正仅达到The fit is performed with leave-one-problem-out cross-validation: each fold holds out one complete problem, fits on the other 32, and predicts fresh durations on the held-out problem. This is stricter than randomly holding out individual duration pairs, because all tests and solutions from the held-out problem are unseen during fitting. 图 33 持续时间修正对解决方案排名的影响。Figure 33 Impact of duration correction on solution rankings.每个框总结了 654 个问题运行：33 个选定的问题，每个问题重新执行约 20 次。对于每个选定的问题，诊断都会使用已保存的部署中模型生成的一个候选解决方案，并将其与同一问题的人类参考解决方案进行比较。在每次优化测试中，候选人的新 CES 持续时间都会根据该测试的人类参考持续时间进行排名；绘制的值是优化套件中这些每次测试百分位数的平均值。越低越好，因为候选者比更多的人类参考更快。这些框的不同之处仅在于人类参考持续时间的表示方式：绿色框使用新测量的 CES 人类参考持续时间并被视为目标分布，而其他框使用未校正的存储持续时间或由一个校正系列转换的存储持续时间。未校正的存储持续时间将平均百分位数从 48.3 移动到 67.9，偏差为 19.6 个点。乘法校正更差，平均值为 86.4，因为它删除了比例标度而不校正快速测试偏移。仿射校正将平均值移至 39.4，将多项式移至 42.0；与未校正的存储持续时间相比，两者都更接近新鲜的 CES 参考，并且仿射族被保留，因为其残差和交叉验证行为几乎与少一个参数的多项式相同。Each box summarizes 654 problem-runs: 33 selected problems, each re-executed about 20 times. For each selected problem, the diagnostic uses one model-generated candidate solution from a saved rollout and compares it to the human-reference solutions for that same problem. On each optimization test, the candidate’s fresh CES duration is ranked against the human-reference durations for that test; the plotted value is the mean of these per-test percentiles across the optimization suite. Lower is better because the candidate is faster than more human references. The boxes differ only in how the human-reference durations are represented: the green box uses freshly measured CES human-reference durations and is treated as the target distribution, while the other boxes use uncorrected stored durations or stored durations transformed by one correction family. Uncorrected stored durations shift the mean percentile from 48.3 to 67.9, a 19.6-point bias. The multiplicative correction is worse, with mean 86.4, because it removes proportional scale without correcting the fast-test offset. The affine correction moves the mean to 39.4 and the polynomial to 42.0; both are much closer to the fresh-CES reference than uncorrected stored durations, and the affine family is kept because its residual and cross-validation behavior are nearly identical to the polynomial with one fewer parameter.

C.8 校准的时间稳定性C.8 Temporal stability of calibration

前一小节使用第一个校准活动将固定的存储数据集持续时间映射到更新的 CES 时序尺度。这是高转移情况，特别是在执行服务基础设施发生变化（例如迁移到新的 CPU 组）之后。还有一个更常规的问题：当服务基础设施名义上固定时，校准在日常重新运行中会移动多少？在我们的实验过程中，我们使用相同的重复执行设计对相同的 33 个问题重复校准多次。在本小节中，我们将在上面报告的结果之上报告一次此类重新运行。每个活动都会产生大约 2700 万个原始持续时间对，汇集到大约 137 万个独特的观察结果。两个活动都使用留一问题交叉验证来适应相同的校正系列。The preceding subsection used a first calibration campaign to map fixed stored dataset durations into a newer CES timing scale. That is the high-shift case, especially relevant after execution-service infrastructure changes such as moving to a new CPU fleet. There is also a more routine question: when the service infrastructure is nominally fixed, how much does the calibration move across day-to-day reruns? During the course of our experiments, we repeated the calibration several times on the same 33 problems with the same repeated-execution design. In this subsection, we report one such rerun on top of the results already reported above. Each campaign produces approximately 27M raw duration pairs, pooled to about 1.37M unique observations. Both campaigns fit the same correction families using leave-one-problem-out cross-validation.

表 33 比较了仿射参数。斜率仅变化 1.0% (0Table 33 compares the affine parameters. The slope changes by only 1.0% (0. 6306 → 0 .6243），而截距从 53 毫秒变为 38 毫秒。对于 1 秒的存储持续时间，这会使预测执行时间改变大约 3%；持续 5 秒，大约下降 1.5%。交叉验证6243), while the intercept shifts from 53 ms to 38 ms. For a 1 s stored duration, this changes the predicted execution time by roughly 3%; for a 5 s duration, by roughly 1.5%. Cross-validatedR2两次活动中该值均保持在 0.98 以上。remains above 0.98 in both campaigns.

跨问题的模型性能概况在活动之间也是稳定的。对于这 33 个问题中的每一个问题，我们都会根据该问题的人类参考来计算候选模型的新 CES 平均百分位数：较低意味着该候选模型相对于该问题的更多人类解决方案更快。然后，我们询问在两次校准活动中，对于模型来说，相同的问题是否仍然容易或困难。斯皮尔曼相关系数为The model-performance profile across problems is also stable between campaigns. For each of the 33 problems, we compute the model candidate’s fresh-CES mean percentile against the human references for that problem: lower means that the candidate is faster relative to more human solutions on that problem. We then ask whether the same problems remain easy or hard for the model across the two calibration campaigns. The Spearman correlation isρ = 0 .99，这意味着候选模型在第一个活动中看起来相对较强的问题与在重新运行活动中看起来相对较强的问题大多是相同的，对于弱问题也是如此。这支持使用相同的仿射系列进行日常重新校准，同时仍然允许数值（99, meaning that problems where the model candidate looks relatively strong in the first campaign are mostly the same problems where it looks relatively strong in the rerun campaign, and likewise for weak problems. This supports using the same affine family for day-to-day recalibration, while still allowing the numerical (α、βα, β) 值随服务状态而变化。) values to move with the service state.

C.9 仿射校准的评估时间灵敏度C.9 Evaluation-time sensitivity to affine calibration

计时校准使新记录的模型生成的解决方案持续时间与排名指标使用的固定存储的人类解决方案持续时间相当。我们现在问校准本身是否会影响评估结果：不同的合理校准是否会导致不同的优化分数，或者它们是否主要移动绝对分数尺度，同时保留模型之间的比较？因此，我们扫描当前的仿射拟合，保持模型执行固定并仅更改模型使用的修正的人类参考持续时间。Timing calibration keeps freshly recorded model-generated solution durations comparable to the fixed stored human-solution durations used by the ranking metric. We now ask whether the calibration itself affects the evaluation results: do different plausible calibrations lead to different optimization scores, or do they mainly move the absolute score scale while preserving comparisons between models? We therefore sweep a current affine fit, holding model executions fixed and changing only the corrected human-reference durations used by thep50 和50 andp10 个排名指标。这是评估时间敏感性检查：减少10 ranking metrics. This is an evaluation-time sensitivity check: decreasingα or β使校正的参考更短，因此相同的模型执行是根据更严格的速度阈值来判断的。图 34 显示了 CWM 32B 的结果。相对而言，扫描移动makes corrected references shorter, so the same model executions are judged against a stricter speed threshold. Figure 34 shows the result for CWM 32B. 底部面板给出了更重要的训练结论。使校准更加严格会降低所有绝对值The bottom panels give the more important training conclusion. Making the calibration stricter lowers all absolutep10 分，但它比优化训练模型更快地降低了正确性基线。在宽松的 beta 端点，过滤器 2、QP（质量百分位数）和 QP 训练10 scores, but it lowers the correctness-only baseline faster than the optimization-trained models. At the lenient beta endpoint, Filter 2s, QP (quality percentile), and QP trainp30 得分为 56.3%、55.8% 和 57.8%，而基线为 38.8%；他们的相对收益分别为 45%、44% 和 49%。在更严格的贝塔点，30 score 56.3%, 55.8%, and 57.8%, compared with 38.8% for the baseline; their relative gains are 45%, 44%, and 49%. At a more stringent beta point,β≃β ≃ 0 .042 中，基线下降至 9.2%，而相同的三个优化训练模型仍保持在 22.2%、21.8% 和 22.2%；相对收益增长至 141%、137% 和 141%。在最严格的 beta 点，基线降至 1.2%，而相同的三个优化训练模型仍保持在 4.0%、3.9% 和 4.2%；相对收益增长至 233%、225% 和 250%。这个端点不太有趣，因为基线已经很低，任何非零优化训练分数相对而言看起来要大得多。 Alpha 扫描显示相同的模式：042, the baseline falls to 9.2%, while the same three optimization-trained models remain at 22.2%, 21.8%, and 22.2%; the relative gains grow to 141%, 137%, and 141%. At the most stringent beta point, the baseline falls to 1.2%, while the same three optimization-trained models remain at 4.0%, 3.9%, and 4.2%; the relative gains grow to 233%, 225%, and 250%. This endpoint is less interesting because the baseline is already so low that any nonzero optimization-trained score looks much larger in relative terms. The alpha sweep shows the same pattern: atα = 0 .6321 的基线为 6.5%，而三个优化训练模型分别达到 17.5%、17.0% 和 17.8%，增益分别为 169%、162% 和 174%。 MC+MO 也保持在基线之上，但增益较小：在宽松的 beta 端点处为 14%，在严格的 beta 点处为 58%。这意味着更严格的校准不会消除优化强化学习效应；相反，它使差距更加明显，因为基线失去了其前十分位数解决方案的更大份额。因此，这里使用的优化强化学习设置在更苛刻的评估设置下显得特别有益。6321, the baseline is 6.5%, while the three optimization-trained models reach 17.5%, 17.0%, and 17.8%, for gains of 169%, 162%, and 174%. MC+MO also stays above the baseline, but with smaller gains: 14% at the lenient beta endpoint and 58% at the stringent beta point. This means that stricter calibration does not erase the optimization-RL effect; on the contrary, it makes the gap more visible, because the baseline loses a larger share of its top-decile solutions. The optimization-RL setups used here therefore appear particularly beneficial under more demanding evaluation settings.

C.10 联合训练时间和评估时间校准扫描C.10 Joint training-time and evaluation-time calibration sweeps

C.9 节固定训练模型，仅更改用于训练后评分的仿射校正。下一个问题是学习时校准是否也很重要。这是依赖于奖励的：仅基于通过/失败结果或原始超时的奖励不需要校准的人类参考分布，但执行后排名的奖励需要，因为它们将每次推出与存储的人类参考持续时间的固定分布进行比较。如果这样的奖励对确切的（Section C.9 held the trained models fixed and changed only the affine correction used for post-training scoring. The next question is whether calibration also matters while learning. This is reward-dependent: rewards based only on pass/fail outcomes or raw timeouts do not need a calibrated human-reference distribution, but the post-execution ranked rewards do, because they compare each rollout to a fixed distribution of stored human-reference durations. If such rewards were very sensitive to the exact (α、βα, β）在训练时使用，那么即使最终评分排名稳定，小的校准不匹配也可能会改变学习的策略。因此，我们对 Qwen 2.5 7B 进行了二维敏感性研究：一个轴改变 RL 训练期间使用的仿射校正，另一个轴改变训练后重新模拟期间使用的仿射校正。) used while training, then a small calibration mismatch could change the learned policy even if final scoring rankings were stable. We therefore run a two-dimensional sensitivity study on Qwen 2.5 7B: one axis changes the affine correction used during RL training, and the other axis changes the affine correction used during post-training resimulation.

训练时校准扫描。Training-time calibration sweep.我们在两个执行后排名环境中训练 16 个 Qwen 2.5 7B 模型，这两个环境均在 D.2 节中定义。第一名已排名We train 16 Qwen 2.5 7B models across two post-execution ranking environments, both defined in Section D.2. The first is rankedp30：执行后，候选者会被插入到校准的人类参考排行榜中，并且仅当其落在参考质量分布的前 30% 内时才会收到二进制优化门。第二个是 QP（质量百分位数）：使用相同的排行榜结构，但每个正确的候选者都会根据其在整个参考池中的质量百分位数获得分级奖励。我们在每种环境下训练八个模型。因此，这两个系列使用相同的底层校准持续时间分布，但以不同的方式将其暴露给 RL：30: after execution, the candidate is inserted into the calibrated human-reference leaderboard and receives the binary optimization gate only if it falls inside the top 30% of the reference-quality distribution. The second is QP (quality percentile): the same leaderboard construction is used, but each correct candidate receives a graded reward according to its quality percentile over the full reference pool. We train eight models with each environment. Thus the two families use the same underlying calibrated duration distribution but expose it to RL differently:p30 将排名变成更清晰的通过/失败优化门，而 QP 为所有正确的候选者提供更平滑的分级信号。30 turns the ranking into a sharper pass/fail optimization gate, while QP gives a smoother graded signal to all correct candidates.

在每个系列中，唯一预期的训练更改是在计算持续时间过滤器和参考排名之前对存储的参考持续时间应用仿射校正。我们将八个奖励校准点索引为 ab1-ab8。它们线性地扫过Within each family, the only intended training change is the affine correction applied to stored reference durations before duration filters and reference rankings are computed. We index the eight reward-calibration points as ab1–ab8. They linearly sweep fromα = 0 . 594939 , β = 0 .054482 至054482 toα = 0 . 719699 , β = 0 .048993，从重复 CES 重新校准活动中观察到的校准变化中选择的范围。 ab3点，048993, a range chosen from the calibration variation observed across repeated CES recalibration campaigns. The ab3 point,α = 0 . 630585 , β = 0 .052914，与 C.7 节中的原始仿射拟合相匹配直至舍入。消融运行在同一 CES 设置下作为一次并发扫描启动，因此实时执行后端保持固定，而奖励使用的存储参考校正发生变化。包括两个在旧 CES 状态下使用 ab3 系数训练的复制参考，以检查当奖励校准固定但服务状态不同时有多少变化。052914, matches the original affine fit from Section C.7 up to rounding. The ablation runs are launched as one concurrent sweep under the same CES setup, so the live execution backend is held fixed while the stored-reference correction used by the reward changes. Two replication references trained with the ab3 coefficients under older CES states are included to check how much changes when the reward calibration is fixed but the service state differs.

评估时间校准扫描。Evaluation-time calibration sweep.所有扫描训练模型和两个复制参考在 CES 上同时重新执行，以获得相同服务状态下的新候选时序。然后，我们在 10 个评分校准下重新模拟每个模型，表示为 s0–s9，从 ( 线性插值All swept training models and the two replication references are re-executed concurrently on CES to obtain fresh candidate timings under the same service state. We then resimulate each model under 10 scoring calibrations, denoted s0–s9, linearly interpolating from (α = 0 . 701876 , β = 0 .049777) 至 (049777) to (α = 0 . 606033 , β = 0 .038306）。这些端点是来自重复重新校准运行的两个似是而非的仿射校正，此处用于测试模型输出固定后校正的选择是否重要。降低038306). These endpoints are two plausible affine corrections from repeated recalibration runs and are used here to test whether the choice of correction matters once the model outputs are fixed. Loweringα和andβ使校正后的人类参考更短，因此相同的模型执行是根据更严格的速度目标来判断的。我们评估makes the corrected human references shorter, so the same model execution is judged against a stricter speed target. 奖励校准影响较小。Reward calibration has small effect.在任何固定评分校准中，与评分校准之间的绝对分数变动相比，八个奖励校准之间的差异很小。对于At any fixed scoring calibration, the spread across the eight reward calibrations is small compared with the absolute score movement across scoring calibrations. Forp100，不依赖于 alpha/beta 评分，排名差距为 1.9 个百分点100, which does not depend on scoring alpha/beta, the spread is 1.9 percentage points for rankedpQP分别为30分和2.5分。这是最纯粹的训练时间读数：更改奖励中使用的校准不会实质性改变正确性。严格的30 and 2.5 points for QP. This is the purest training-time readout: changing the calibration used inside the reward does not materially change correctness. The strictp10 读数类似。在s0时，排名10 readout is similar. At s0, rankedp30 跨越 ab1-ab8 的 39.3-41.9%，2.6 个点的范围； QP 范围为 39.1-40.3%，范围为 1.2 个百分点。在 s5 时，点差为 2.0 和 2.9 点。这些范围与重新运行稳健性分析中观察到的 1-2.5 点运行变化相当，因此我们不应将相邻奖励校准点之间的微小差异解释为有意义的模型质量变化。30 spans 39.3–41.9% across ab1–ab8, a 2.6 point range; QP spans 39.1–40.3%, a 1.2 point range. At s5, the spreads are 2.0 and 2.9 points. These ranges are comparable to the 1–2.5 point run-to-run variation observed in the rerun-robustness analysis, so we should not interpret small differences between adjacent reward-calibration points as meaningful model-quality changes.

图 38 和图 39 测试奖励和评分校准是否需要匹配。如果这样做，图 38 将显示一条对角线脊：在较低奖励校准点下训练的模型在较低评分校准下表现最好，在较高奖励校准点下训练的模型在较高评分校准下表现最好。我们没有看到这种模式。等高线几乎是垂直的，图 39 显示了紧密的行内簇。实际结论是，测试的校准范围改变了奖励尺度，但没有选择足够强的不同类型的解决方案来主导训练噪声。这在操作上很有用，因为优化 RL 运行可以持续两天或更长时间：在此处测试的范围内，只要服务状态变化与我们研究的范围相当，训练运行就不需要持续的奖励侧重新校准。一个可能的原因是 GRPO 计算策略内批次内的优势，因此绝对奖励值的常见变化在参数更新之前被部分吸收。训练后评分方面有所不同。运行的排名保持稳定，但绝对严格的百分位数分数可能会随着评分校准而大幅变化，并且更严格的评分条件强调经过优化训练的模型，以保留更多前十分位数的解决方案。Figure 38 and Figure 39 test whether reward and scoring calibrations need to be matched. If they did, Figure 38 would show a diagonal ridge: models trained under lower reward-calibration points would do best under lower scoring calibrations, and models trained under higher reward-calibration points would do best under higher scoring calibrations. We do not see that pattern. The contours are nearly vertical, and Figure 39 shows tight within-row clusters. The practical conclusion is that the tested calibration range changes the reward scale but does not select a different kind of solution strongly enough to dominate training noise. This is useful operationally because optimization-RL runs can span two days or more: within the range tested here, the training run does not need continuous reward-side recalibration as long as the service-state variation stays comparable to the range we study. A likely reason is that GRPO computes advantages within the on-policy batch, so common shifts in absolute reward values are partly absorbed before the parameter update. The posttraining scoring side is different. The ranking of runs remains stable, but absolute strict-percentile scores can move substantially with the scoring calibration, and stricter scoring conditions emphasize optimization-trained models that preserve more top-decile solutions.

评分校准主导着绝对严格的指标。Scoring calibration dominates absolute strict metrics.评分轴的影响要大得多。对于排名The scoring axis has much larger effect. For the rankedp30 复制参考，30 replication reference,p10 pass@1 从 s0 时的 41.1% 下降到 s9 时的 5.8%，下降了 35.3 个百分点。 QP 则从 40.1% 下降至 4.6%，下降了 35.5 个百分点。这比固定得分点的奖励校准差大一个数量级以上。同样的模式适用于10 pass@1 falls from 41.1% at s0 to 5.8% at s9, a 35.3 point drop. For QP, it falls from 40.1% to 4.6%, a 35.5 point drop. This is more than an order of magnitude larger than the reward-calibration spread at a fixed scoring point. The same pattern holds atp20，虽然下降不那么严重：排名20, though the drop is less severe: rankedp30 从 42.1% 升至 12.0%，QP 从 41.7% 升至 9.9%。因此，评分校准控制绝对严格的百分位数。这并不意味着模型会发生变化；而是意味着模型会发生变化。相同的共享的重新执行的模型输出正在根据较短的校正参考进行重新评分。30 moves from 42.1% to 12.0% and QP from 41.7% to 9.9%. Scoring calibration therefore controls the absolute strict-percentile scale. This does not mean the models change; the same shared re-executed model outputs are being rescored against shorter corrected references.

百分位数依赖性很有用。The percentile dependence is useful.p100 是不变的，因为它测量时间敏感的重新执行下的正确性，而不是速度百分位数的成员资格。100 is invariant because it measures correctness under the timing-sensitive reexecution, not membership in a speed percentile.p20 保留了 s0 排名的 28.5%-20 retains 28.5% of the s0 ranked-ps9 得分为 30，QP 得分为 23.7%。严格30 score at s9 and 23.7% of the QP score. Strictp10 分别仅保留 14.1% 和 11.5%。10 retains only 14.1% and 11.5%, respectively.p相对而言，10 pass@10 比 pass@1 更脆弱，排名保留 28.9%10 pass@10 is less brittle than pass@1 in relative terms, retaining 28.9% for rankedpQP 分别为 30% 和 23.1%，但其绝对下降幅度更大，因为起始 pass@10 分数更高。这是严格的百分位数度量的预期行为：随着参考文献的缩短，位于前十分位数的边界移动得比正确或仅位于前五分之一的边界移动得更快。30 and 23.1% for QP, but its absolute drop is even larger because the starting pass@10 scores are higher. This is the expected behavior of a strict percentile metric: as references are shortened, the boundary for being in the top decile moves faster than the boundary for being correct or merely within the top fifth.

两个奖励系列的反应类似，但是The two reward families respond similarly, but p 30 在严格优化方面始终更强。is consistently stronger on strict optimization.排名中的The rankedp30和QP系列不是两种校准方法；它们是将相同的校准人类参考排行榜转化为奖励的两种​​方法。 QP 在整个排行榜上提供分级奖励，同时排名30 and QP families are not two calibration methods; they are two ways of turning the same calibrated human-reference leaderboard into reward. QP supplies a graded reward across the full leaderboard, while rankedp30 使前 30% 的事件成为二元优化门。在本次横扫中，排名30 makes the top-30% event a binary optimization gate. Across this sweep, rankedp30 在严格方面始终领先30 is consistently ahead on strictp10 次通过@1。平均 ab1-ab8，排名-10 pass@1. Averaging over ab1–ab8, the ranked-p30相对于QP的优势是s0时1.2分，s5时2.3分，s9时0.8分。与评分校准下降相比，差距不大，但在整个校准轴上仍然存在。这支持了 D.2 节的奖励设计结果：对于 Qwen 7B 设置，越锐利30 advantage over QP is 1.2 points at s0, 2.3 points at s5, and 0.8 points at s9. The gap is modest compared with the scoring-calibration drop, but it persists across the calibration axis. This supports the reward-design result from Section D.2: for this Qwen 7B setting, the sharperp30 门比更平滑的 QP 奖励产生更强的严格优化信号，30 gate produces a stronger strict-optimization signal than the smoother QP reward,

QP系列有时略高The QP family sometimes has slightly higherp100，例如，QP ab6 与排名的相比为 48.4%100, for example 48.4% for QP ab6 versus a ranked-p30最高为46.9%。这与严格的优化结果并不矛盾。这表明更平滑的 QP 奖励可以保持或提高广泛的正确性，同时对最高十分位速度的选择性较低。反之，排名30 maximum of 46.9%. That does not contradict the strict optimization result. It suggests that the smoother QP reward can preserve or improve broad correctness while being less selective for top-decile speed. Conversely, rankedp30 更符合严格要求30 is more aligned with the strictp10个目标，但并不主导每个正确性方面的数字。10 target, but does not dominate every correctness-side number.

跨服务状态复制。Cross-service-state replication.对于两个奖励系列，我们还包括使用与 ab3 相同的仿射系数训练但在不同 CES 服务状态下收集的参考运行。共享重新执行后，可以将这些参考与 ab3 进行比较，以询问在保持奖励校准固定的同时，在训练期间改变服务状态是否会明显改变学习模型。答案大多是否定的。对于排名For the two reward families, we also include reference runs trained with the same affine coefficients as ab3 but collected under a different CES service state. After shared re-execution, these references can be compared with ab3 to ask whether changing the service state during training, while keeping the reward calibration fixed, visibly changes the learned model. The answer is mostly no. For rankedp30，参考行和 ab3 行相同或在严格的 0.4 点以内30, the reference and ab3 rows are identical or within 0.4 points across the strictp10 个评分网格。对于 QP 来说，它们也是紧密结合的。这与我们在保持 CES 执行固定并改变校准参考持续时间时尝试模拟的效果相同：它改变了模型时序和人类参考时序的相对位置。复制检查确认，在此范围内，在固定参考校准下更改 CES 条件的行为与在固定 CES 执行下更改参考校准类似。10 scoring grid. For QP, they are also closely aligned. This is the same effect we tried to simulate when we keep CES executions fixed and vary the calibrated reference durations: it changes the relative position of model timings and human-reference timings. The replication check confirms that, within this range, changing CES conditions under fixed reference calibration behaves similarly to changing reference calibration under fixed CES executions.

本小节中的绝对分数具有诊断意义，仅应在此共享的重新执行活动中进行比较。在主结果表中，我们在评估时重新运行校准活动，并使用结果拟合来尽可能接近我们在理想的无偏移、无噪声计时系统中获得的分数：在同一测试上运行相同的片段将始终返回相同的持续时间，因此存储的人类参考持续时间和新执行的模型持续时间将已经存在于相同的计时范围内。该校正将存储的人类参考持续时间移向评估所使用的 CES 状态的时间尺度。例如，排名The absolute scores in this subsection are diagnostic and should be compared only within this shared reexecution campaign. In the main result tables, we rerun a calibration campaign at evaluation time and use the resulting fit to approximate, as closely as possible, the score we would obtain in an ideal no-shift, no-noise timing system: running the same snippet on the same test would always return the same duration, so stored human-reference durations and freshly executed model durations would already live on the same timing scale. That correction moves stored human-reference durations toward the timing scale of the CES state used by the evaluation. For example, the rankedp30 个复制参考分数30 replication reference scoresp 10 = 41 .这里在 s0 下为 1%，而相应的主结果表报告为 6.0%。这种差异并不是模型的改进；而是模型的改进。它来自于使用不同的 CES 状态和校准。因此，本小节的重点是奖励和评分校准之间的活动内部比较，而不是标题分数的更新。1% here under s0, while the corresponding main-result table reports 6.0%. This difference is not a model improvement; it comes from using a different CES state and calibration. The point of this subsection is therefore the within-campaign comparison across reward and scoring calibrations, not an update of the headline scores.

外卖。Takeaway.二维扫描分离了三个容易混淆的事实。首先，测试范围内的奖励时间仿射校准对学习的 Qwen 7B 模型影响较弱。其次，训练后评分校准对绝对严格百分位分数有很大影响，尤其是The two-dimensional sweep separates three facts that are easy to conflate. First, reward-time affine calibration over the tested range has weak effect on the learned Qwen 7B models. Second, post-training scoring calibration has large effect on absolute strict-percentile scores, especiallyp10，因为它改变了固定模型执行排名的校正参考持续时间，尽管它保留了本次扫描中运行的相对顺序，这支持了比较的稳健性。第三，两个奖励系列之间的相对比较足够稳定，可以解释：排名10, because it changes the corrected reference durations against which fixed model executions are ranked, though it preserves the relative ordering of the runs in this sweep, which supports the robustness of the comparisons. Third, the relative comparison between the two reward families is stable enough to interpret: rankedp30 仍然是更强的严格优化训练信号，而 QP 可以保留更多的正确性。30 remains the stronger strict-optimization training signal, while QP can preserve a bit more correctness.

This visualizes the main quantitative conclusion: reward-side alpha/beta choices move the final score by only a few points, while post-training scoring calibration determines the absolute strict-p10 级。10 scale.

C.11 基于时序的强化学习的经验教训C.11 Lessons learned for timing-based RL

从上面的分析来看，我们保留这样的观点：From the analyses above, we retain that:

  1. 时间敏感的数据集构建、训练和评估应使用受控的、受限的服务来测量生成的代码片段的运行时间，而不是与 RL 运行同时运行的本地沙箱：问题不仅在于额外的时间噪声，还在于时间噪声。它会完全破坏定时信号。Timing-sensitive dataset construction, training, and evaluation should use a controlled, confined service to measure runtimes of generated code snippets, rather than local sandboxes running concurrently with the RL run: the issue is not only an extra degree of timing noise; it can completely corrupt the timing signal.

  2. 只要超时足够大且不受限制，强化学习工作线程上的本地沙箱执行就可以恢复不确定的正确性判决，但其持续时间不应进入计时奖励或计时敏感指标。Local sandbox execution on the RL workers can recover inconclusive correctness verdicts, as long as timeouts are generous and not constraining, but its durations should not enter timing rewards or timing-sensitive metrics.

  3. 聚合服务运行状况可能隐藏短暂的有害窗口：罕见的基础设施突发可能会影响几个优化器步骤中的大部分部署，而无声的服务减速可能会改变测量的持续时间，即使通过/失败计数器看起来正常。Aggregate service health can hide short harmful windows: rare infrastructure bursts can affect a large fraction of rollouts in a few optimizer steps, and silent service slowdowns can shift measured durations even when pass/fail counters look normal.

  4. 模型比较应在同一受控设置中使用共享和并发重新执行，而不是混合在不同服务状态下收集的计时。Model comparisons should use shared and concurrent re-execution in the same controlled setting rather than mixing timings collected under different service states.

  5. 应重新运行校准活动以进行时间敏感的评估，并且在迁移到新的执行服务队列时、沙箱升级后或持续的服务状态更改后，应重新调整仿射存储持续时间校正。A calibration campaign should be rerun for timing-sensitive evaluation, and the affine stored-duration correction should be refit when moving to a new execution-service fleet, after a sandbox upgrade, or after a sustained service-state change.

  6. GRPO 批次内优势计算可以部分吸收训练期间常见的实时服务变化；尽管相对模型排名稳定并且经过优化训练的模型在更严格的评分设置下更突出，但训练后评分对绝对严格分数中的校准更敏感。GRPO within-batch advantage computation can partially absorb common live-service shifts during training; post-training scoring is more sensitive to calibration in absolute strict scores, although the relative model ranking is stable and optimization-trained models stand out more under stricter scoring settings.

C.12 我们没有时间进行的改进和实验C.12 Improvements and experiments we did not have the time to conduct

受控定时噪声注入。Controlled timing-noise injection.上述研究使用了实际运行中发生的噪声：重复的 CES 重新运行、突发、减速和校准更改。下一项研究可能会在沙箱返回结果后添加已知的噪音。例如，我们可以为每个持续时间添加 5-50 毫秒，重新计算每个测试现在是否应计为超时，并更新存储的持续时间。我们还可以增加持续时间，在短测试中添加更大的噪声，或者模拟持续几个优化器步骤的突发。因为注入的噪声是已知的，所以我们可以直接比较奖励环境，看看哪些环境首先崩溃，哪些保持更稳定。The studies above use noise that happened in real runs: repeated CES reruns, bursts, slowdowns, and calibration changes. A next study could add known noise after the sandbox returns a result. For example, we could add 5–50 ms to each duration, recompute whether each test should now count as a timeout, and update the stored duration. We could also multiply durations, add larger noise on short tests, or simulate bursts that last for several optimizer steps. Because the injected noise is known, we could compare reward environments directly and see which ones break first, and which ones stay more stable.

在固定部署上重放服务状态。Replaying service states on fixed rollouts.RL 运行分析很难隔离，因为策略、抽样问题、抽样部署和 CES 状态都会同时发生变化。更清晰的研究将使推出日志保持固定。考虑到已保存的代码、测试、状态和持续时间，我们可以在不同的服务跟踪下重新计算奖励：健康执行、真实突发窗口、综合减速、不同的后备策略、丢弃或隔离。这可以帮助衡量 RL 环境在沙箱执行后是否能够正确平滑部分执行噪声。它还可以估计有多少奖励差异来自服务状态。然后，GRPO 可以使用此估计来标准化优势、降低噪声批次的权重或触发额外的重新执行。在受控服务状态下重放固定的推出批次还可以测量策略更新在每个服务状态下的漂移情况。The RL-run analysis is hard to isolate because the policy, sampled problems, sampled rollouts, and CES state all change at the same time. A cleaner study would keep the rollout logs fixed. Given saved rollouts with code, tests, statuses, and durations, we could recompute rewards under different service traces: healthy execution, real burst windows, synthetic slowdowns, different fallback policies, discard, or quarantine. This could help measure whether the RL environment can correctly smooth part of the execution noise after sandbox execution. It could also estimate how much reward variance comes from the service state. GRPO could then use this estimate to normalize advantages, downweight noisy batches, or trigger extra re-execution. Replaying fixed rollout batches under controlled service states could also measure how the policy update would drift under each service state.

存储标准差估计值。Storing standard deviation estimates.一些环境使用人类参考持续时间。重复的 CES 活动不仅可以让我们存储一个校正的持续时间，还可以存储其标准偏差的估计。这可以针对每个问题测试解决方案持续时间进行存储，或者更粗略地针对每个测试级别参考分布进行存储。这些估计可以在多个地方使用。过滤可以避免持续时间分布主要是测量噪声的测试。超时选择可以包括噪声测试的安全裕度。当生成的片段和参考边界太接近而无法可靠区分时，排名奖励可能会软化比较。相同的估计还可以触发选择性重新执行，或者在计算或标准化优势时为 GRPO 提供奖励标准差估计。Several environments use human-reference durations. The repeatedCES campaign could let us store not only one corrected duration, but also an estimate of its standard deviation. This could be stored for each problem-test-solution duration, or more coarsely for each test-level reference distribution. These estimates could then be used in several places. Filtering could avoid tests whose duration spread is mostly measurement noise. Timeout choices could include a safety margin for noisy tests. Ranking rewards could soften comparisons when the generated snippet and the reference boundary are too close to distinguish reliably. The same estimate could also trigger selective re-execution, or give GRPO a reward-standard-deviation estimate when it computes or normalizes advantages.

91

自适应监控和重新校准。Adaptive monitoring and recalibration.实时校准探针可以帮助检测无声服务漂移，但它们也会使用执行能力。探头太少会产生噪音；太多会减慢推出执行速度。哨兵套件可以在训练期间不时运行，并用于检测坡度、偏移或尾部行为的变化。当偏移足够大时，系统可以重新调整仿射校正、隔离最近的批次或暂停推出收集。Live calibration probes could help detect silent service drift, but they also use execution capacity. Too few probes are noisy; too many slow down rollout execution. A sentinel suite could be run from time to time during training and used to detect changes in slope, offset, or tail behavior. When the shift is large enough, the system could refit the affine correction, quarantine recent batches, or pause rollout collection.

92

D 优化强化学习的环境和奖励设计D Designing Environments and Rewards for Optimization RL

优化测试本身并不能定义 RL 奖励。他们生成执行记录，奖励设计决定这些记录的哪些部分应该影响学习。本附录给出了第 4 节中压缩的参数的详细版本：时序约束可以在执行之前、执行期间或执行之后进入环境；所有设置都简化为一个常见的面向奖励的界面，该界面将正确性、硬优化门和持续时间质量分成三个数字信号，然后可以在这些信号上建立奖励。Optimization tests by themselves do not define an RL reward. They produce execution records, and the reward design decides which parts of those records should affect learning. This appendix gives the detailed version of the argument compressed in Section 4: timing constraints can enter the environment before execution, during execution, or after execution; all settings are reduced to a common reward-facing interface that separates correctness, hard optimization gate, and duration quality into three numerical signals that the reward can then be built upon.

D.1 问题分类D.1 Problem taxonomy

在本文中，强化学习环境For this paper, an RL environmentE是围绕模型准备部署的完整例程，优化器随后将采取步骤。给定一个编程问题is the full routine around the model to prepare a rollout that the optimizer will then take a step upon. Given a programming problemx，环境准备向模型显示的提示，接收生成的答案, the environment prepares the prompt shown to the model, receives a generated answery，决定运行哪些测试以及在哪些约束下运行，将结果状态和持续时间聚合为结果，并返回奖励, decides which tests to run and under which constraints, aggregates the resulting statuses and durations into outcomes, and returns a rewardRE ( x、yx, y）。我们定义了分类法，以便每个 RL 环境都公开一组通用信号，不同的奖励函数将推出映射到奖励范围 [). We define the taxonomy so that each RL environment exposes a common set of signals, from which different reward functions map the rollout to the reward range [− 1 , 1].

我们将附加的测试分开We split the tests attached tox进入正确性测试into correctness testsTC ( x）和一个原始优化测试池) and a raw optimization-test poolTO ( x）。正确性测试针对给定环境是固定的，并产生基本正确性信号；然后执行的优化测试可以通过拒绝失败来完善该信号。第 4 节中研究的设计选择作用于优化测试池、时序约束或持续时间记录。在这个级别，优化端例程可以写成三个参数化运算符：). The correctness tests are fixed for a given environment and produce the base correctness signal; executed optimization tests can then refine this signal by rejecting failures. The design choices studied in Section 4 act on the optimization-test pool, the timing constraints, or the duration record. At this level, the optimization-side routine can be written as three parameterized operators:

哪里whereZO ( x、yx, y) 是优化端执行记录，包括执行的测试、状态、持续时间和预期限制。这些运算符是环境的元类。预执行环境由用于实例化的例程定义) is the optimization-side execution record, including the executed tests, statuses, durations, and intended limits. These operators are meta-classes of environments. A pre-execution environment is defined by the routine used to instantiateFpre，例如绝对持续时间过滤器、相对持续时间过滤器或字符长度过滤器。内部执行环境由以下约束定义pre, such as an absolute-duration filter, a relative-duration filter, or a character-length filter. An intra-execution environment is defined by the constraints inE内部，例如绝对超时、相对超时或排名最差的时间限制。执行后环境由是否定义intra, such as absolute timeouts, relative timeouts, or ranked-worst time limits. A post-execution environment is defined by whetherQpost 将观察到的持续时间转换为排名门、分级排名标量或两者。这些例程运行后，面向奖励的界面包含正确性信号post turns the observed durations into a ranked gate, a graded ranked scalar, or both. After these routines run, the reward-facing interface contains a correctness signalccor，其严格的优化意识 〜 细化cor, its strict optimization-aware ˜ refinementccor，硬优化门cor, a hard optimization gateg，以及分级持续时间质量信号, and a graded duration quality signalq .

D.2 优化 RL 环境的类别D.2 Classes of Optimization RL Environments

B 节和 C 节中的构造和计时堆栈为我们提供了优化测试、校准的人类参考持续时间以及可以记录每次测试计时的沙箱，但这些成分还不是一个良好的优化 RL 环境。一个好的环境应该会产生一个有用的学习曲线：早期的推出仍然应该得到一个The construction and timing stack in Sections B and C give us optimization tests, calibrated human-reference durations, and a sandbox that can record per-test timing, but these ingredients are not yet a good optimization RL environment. A good environment should induce a useful learning curve: early rollouts should still receive a

正确性信号，正确但缓慢的代码片段不应与错误代码无法区分，并且逐渐更快的正确代码片段应该会看到逐渐更好的优化反馈。如果不加修饰，原始优化测试可能会使部署变得更加困难的正确性检查，使奖励过于稀疏，因为只有接近顶级的解决方案才能获得效率信用，或者使标量过于关注速度并给予退化快速但错误的程序信用。稀疏机制也很重要：仅向孤立候选片段的前 1% 提供效率信用的约束可能永远不会奖励早期 GRPO 的推出，而宽松的约束可以在几乎没有优化压力的情况下产生密集的奖励。 D.5 节中的原始持续时间控制和第 5 节中的基线讨论从经验上显示了这种局限性：即使在推出中包含优化测试，直接将持续时间添加到标量奖励中也只能获得相对于参考标准化环境/奖励选择而言较小的严格阈值增益。correctness signal, correct but slow snippets should not be indistinguishable from wrong code, and progressively faster correct snippets should see progressively better optimization feedback. Left unshaped, raw optimization tests can make the rollout mostly a harder correctness check, make the reward too sparse because only near-top solutions ever receive efficiency credit, or make the scalar too focused on speed and give credit to degenerate fast-but-wrong programs. The sparsity regime also matters: a constraint that gives efficiency credit only to the top 1% of isolated candidate snippets may never reward early GRPO rollouts, while a loose constraint can produce dense rewards with little optimization pressure. The raw-duration controls in Section D.5 and the baseline discussion in Section 5 show this limitation empirically: even when optimization tests are included in the rollout, directly adding duration to the scalar reward gives only small strict-threshold gains relative to reference-normalized environment/reward choices.

我们将优化强化学习作为一种从头开始的一次性生成问题来研究。该模型根据问题陈述生成一个程序，而不是围绕已知正确解决方案的迭代细化轨迹，并且环境分配We study optimization RL as a one-shot generation-from-scratch problem. The model produces one program from the problem statement, not an iterative refinement trajectory around a known correct solution, and the environment assignsRE ( x、yx, y) 到那个生成的程序) to that one generated programy为了一个问题for one problemx。校准的人类参考可能会设置过滤器、时间限制或排名，但是当 GRPO 将采样批次的标量奖励转化为优势时，模型片段之间的任何相对比较都会稍后出现；它不是环境分数本身的一部分。因此，分类法的目标是详尽地介绍此推出管道：可以在沙箱执行之前、沙箱执行期间或执行之后输入时序约束。在每个阶段中，环境族修复约束类型，具体环境实例修复其参数化。. Calibrated human references may set filters, time limits, or ranks, but any relative comparison among model snippets appears later, when GRPO turns the scalar rewards of a sampled batch into advantages; it is not part of the environment score itself. The goal of the taxonomy is therefore to be exhaustive with respect to this rollout pipeline: a timing constraint can enter before sandbox execution, during sandbox execution, or after execution. Within each stage, an environment family fixes the type of constraint, and a concrete environment instance fixes its parameterization.

预执行过滤是在生成的程序运行之前起作用的环境的一部分。无操作过滤器保留整个优化池，Pre-execution filtering is the part of the environment that acts before the generated program is run. The no-op filter keeps the entire optimization pool,

这些过滤器本身并不奖励速度，而是奖励速度。他们选择允许哪些工作负载对稍后的超时或排名信号做出贡献。绝对持续时间过滤使工作负载规模变得明确。与These filters do not by themselves reward speed; they choose which workloads are allowed to contribute to the later timeout or ranking signal. Absolute-duration filtering makes the workload scale explicit. Witha= 2 s，例如，保留的优化测试是那些校准的总人类运行时间低于两秒的优化测试，因此选择可比较的优化上限具有可解释的范围。然而，相同的全局阈值对于不同的问题可能意味着不同的事情：一些问题已经完全存在于两秒以下并且实际上未被过滤，而其他问题则具有阈值分成两个非常不同的子集的持续时间概况。字符长度过滤与第 3 节和 B.7 节中的持续时间/长度过滤性分析相关。它很便宜，具有确定性，甚至可以在没有存储参考持续时间的情况下应用，但它假设序列化输入输出长度是计算工作量的可用代理；当运行时由输入结构、值或控制流而不是字符计数驱动时，这种假设可能会失败。相对持续时间过滤通过删除缓慢校准测试的每个问题部分来避免全局截止，这看起来可以更好地匹配异构任务。它的风险在于，被移除的尾部可能正是优化压力所在的地方：即使是很小的移除部分也可以删除某些问题上的困难情况，并且由于排序来自人类参考池，因此它可能会通过丢弃测试来过度拟合参考，这些测试在人类解决方案中平均速度较慢，但​​可能会在强化学习期间的模型部署中产生广泛的持续时间分布。调音= 2 s, for example, the retained optimization tests are those whose calibrated aggregate human runtime is below two seconds, so choosing a comparable optimization cap has an interpretable scale. The same global threshold can nevertheless mean different things across problems: some problems already live entirely below two seconds and are effectively unfiltered, while others have a duration profile that the threshold cuts into two very different subsets. Character-length filtering connects to the duration/length filterability analysis in Section 3 and Section B.7. It is cheap, deterministic, and can be applied even without stored reference durations, but it assumes serialized input-output length is a usable proxy for computational workload; this assumption can fail when runtime is driven by input structure, values, or control flow rather than character count. Relative-duration filtering avoids a global cutoff by removing a per-problem fraction of slow calibrated tests, which can look better matched to heterogeneous tasks. Its risk is that the removed tail may be exactly where the optimization pressure lives: even a small removal fraction can delete hard cases on some problems, and because the ordering comes from the human reference pool, it may overfit to references by discarding tests that are slow on average across human solutions but could have produced a broad duration spread across model rollouts during RL. Tuninga , L, 和, andr因此，这是定义学习曲线的一部分，在奖励稀疏性和严格的优化压力下交换广泛的覆盖范围。is therefore part of defining the learning curve, trading broad coverage against reward sparsity and strict optimization pressure.
