  1. 排名参考超时环境Ranked-reference timeout environments首先选择一个顶级参考集first select a top reference setHp ( x)，然后根据选定的参考持续时间设置每个测试限制：), then set each test limit from the selected reference durations:

Whenever the CES path is given an optimization time-limit override, execution runs under the 10s service cap and then applies the intended limit by post-filtering: a successful test is changed to timeout wheneverδt ( y ) ≥ λt .

内部执行环境通过沙箱预算本身引入优化压力。这与预执行过滤不同：预执行过滤器选择构成工作负载的测试，然后让执行盲目展开，而执行内环境则保持保留的工作负载固定，并更改分配给候选者完成它的时间。因此，这些选择并不等同。删除测试会从硬门和分级信号中删除其证据，同时收紧Intra-execution environments introduce optimization pressure through the sandbox budget itself. This is different from pre-execution filtering: a pre-execution filter chooses which tests form the workload and then lets execution unfold blindly, whereas an intra-execution environment keeps the retained workload fixed and changes how much time the candidate is allocated to complete it. These choices are therefore not equivalent. Removing a test removes its evidence from both the hard gate and the graded signal, while tighteningλt将测试留在推出中，但更改运行是否超时。绝对超时是这个想法的最简单版本，使截止日期易于阅读，但是单个leaves the test in the rollout but changes whether the run becomes a timeout. Absolute timeouts are the simplest version of this idea and make the deadline easy to read, but a singleℓ当保留的测试跨越非常不同的工作负载时，可能会过于粗略。相对超时通过从中提取分位数来适应测试can be too coarse when the retained tests span very different workloads. Relative timeouts adapt test by test by extracting a quantile fromDt，但是当不同的测试奖励不同的算法权衡时，它们可能会组合出不切实际的同时约束：每个测试的 20% 最快持续时间不一定来自同一类型的解决方案。排名参考超时尝试通过首先选择强参考解决方案的连贯池，然后从其跟踪中得出最后期限来避免每次测试的混合。他们可能会在相反的方向上失败：如果每个选定的参考人在不同的测试子集上都很弱，那么由此产生的截止日期可能会让候选人在每次测试中匹配最差的选定参考人，而不匹配任何参考人的优势。这两种参考衍生的变体也继承了人类参考池的偏差，因此它们的参数化不仅控制严格性，还控制环境是否可能与观察到的参考池过度拟合。, but they can assemble an unrealistic simultaneous constraint when different tests reward different algorithmic trade-offs: the 20% fastest duration on each test need not come from the same kind of solution. Ranked-reference timeouts try to avoid that per-test mixture by first selecting a coherent pool of strong reference solutions and then deriving deadlines from their traces. They can fail in the opposite direction: if each selected reference is weak on a different subset of tests, the resulting deadlines may allow a candidate to match the worst selected reference on every test without matching any reference’s strengths. Both reference-derived variants also inherit the biases of the human reference pool, so their parameterization controls not only strictness but also whether the environment potentially overfits the observed reference pool.

与withϕ当没有使用优化测试时= 0。我们使用与以下百分位数相同的“越低越好”约定：= 0 when no optimization tests are used. A timeout toleranceρ定义状态端优化硬门，相同的超时分数可以作为分级优化标量公开：defines the status-side optimization hard gate, and the same timeout fraction can be exposed as a graded optimization scalar:

设定The settingρ= 1 使超时门成为无操作，而较小的值将超时分数变成硬优化约束。这种容差参数化不是它自己的一个类，而是不同类优化 RL 环境的附加参数，因为它直接控制超时状态如何转移到下游奖励影响。设置为= 1 makes the timeout gate a no-op, while smaller values turn the timeout fraction into a hard optimization constraint. This tolerance parameterization is not a class of its own but an attached parameter of the different classes of optimization RL environments, because it directly controls how timeout statuses are transferred into downstream reward impact. Set toρ= 1，它通过硬优化门关闭由预执行过滤器选择并受内部执行时间限制约束的工作负载的任何优化压力。= 1, it shuts down any optimization pressure through the hard optimization gate for the workload selected by pre-execution filters and constrained by intra-execution time limits.

执行后排名通过使候选者在执行后与参考排行榜竞争来施加优化压力，而不是在执行前选择工作负载截止或在执行期间更改沙箱预算。相对于执行前和执行内系列，这将严格选择移出工作负载构建：环境不需要调整Post-execution ranking applies optimization pressure by making the candidate compete against a reference leaderboard after execution, instead of choosing a workload cutoff before execution or changing the sandbox budget during execution. Relative to pre- and intra-execution families, this moves the strictness choice out of workload construction: the environment does not need to tunea , L , r，或每次测试的截止日期, or a per-test deadlineλt使速度可见，尽管有下游阈值to make speed visible, although a downstream thresholdp仍然可以将排名标量变成硬门。在实践中，模型会因同一问题的参考排序向上移动而获得奖励，这接近于评估作为排名速度比较的代码优化基准的数量。代价是参考分布成为环境的一部分。如果can still turn the ranked scalar into a hard gate. In practice, the model is rewarded for moving upward in the reference ordering on the same problem, which is close to how many code-optimization benchmarks are evaluated as ranked speed comparisons. The price is that the reference distribution becomes part of the environment. IfH ( x) 较弱、太窄或偏向于一个实现系列，则靠近参考包的生成解决方案和更快的解决方案可能无法充分分离，或者模型可能会过度拟合观察到的参考权衡。请注意，这并不会使这种类型的环境变得不那么通用，或者陷入这个潜在的陷阱：可以针对当前模型生成、学习、刷新参考分布，或者通过过去的推出位置进行增强。为了简单起见，我们将其固定在这里，正如本小节开头我们的研究限制中所述。) is weak, too narrow, or biased toward one implementation family, then a generated solution near the reference pack and a much faster solution may not be separated enough, or the model may overfit to the observed reference trade-offs. Note that this does not make this type of environment less generalizable, or stuck in this potential pitfall: the reference distribution could be generated, learned, refreshed adversarially against the current model, or augmented with past rollout positions. We keep it fixed here for the sake of simplicity, as stated in the limits of our study at the beginning of this subsection.

一旦选择了执行后排名，QAR/QP 分割就是主要的量化选择。 QAR 保留每次测试的平均百分位The QAR/QP split is the main scalarization choice once post-execution ranking is selected. QAR keeps the average per-test percentilea ( y）本身，因此它奖励保留测试之间的广泛移动，并使每个测试到问题的聚合选择明确；显示的定义使用平均值，而 D.3 节将该族与中值、修剪和过滤变体进行比较。 QP 采用相同的聚合) itself, so it rewards broad movement across the retained tests and makes the per-test-to-problem aggregation choice explicit; the displayed definition uses a mean, while Section D.3 compares this family to median, trimmed, and filtered variants. QP takes the same aggregatea ( y），对参考文献中的候选人进行排序，并将该位置转换为问题级排行榜百分位数。权衡是动态范围。如果不同的参考解决方案在不同的测试中速度快而在其他测试中速度慢，则原始平均值), sorts the candidate among the references, and converts that position into a problem-level leaderboard percentile. The tradeoff is dynamic range. If different reference solutions are fast on different tests and slow on others, the raw averagea ( y) 可以将许多候选者压缩到一个狭窄的区间内； QP 将这个总数重新分配到排行榜位置，使奖励可以看到更多的相对位置。成本在极端情况下是饱和的：一旦候选人在现有阶梯上表现最好，QP 仍处于最佳位置，而如果候选人在测试中不断改进超出任何预先存在的参考，则 QAR 仍然会下降。因此，D.3 节研究了在奖励构成小节决定如何消费之前，哪个排名标量具有低重复 CES 噪声、足够的问题内辨别力以及校准后稳定的排序) can compress many candidates into a narrow interval; QP spreads this aggregate back into leaderboard positions, making more relative positions visible to the reward. The cost is saturation at the extreme: once a candidate is best on the existing ladder, QP remains at the best position, while QAR can still decrease if the candidate keeps improving across tests beyond any pre-existing reference. Section D.3 therefore studies which ranked scalar has low repeated-CES noise, enough within-problem discrimination, and stable ordering after calibration, before the reward-composition subsection decides how to consumeg和andq .

D.3 如何根据记录的持续时间创建良好的排名分数D.3 How to create a good ranking score based on recorded durations

在 D.2 节中，执行后环境实例化In Section D.2, post-execution environments instantiateQ发布：沙箱运行候选程序后，他们将记录的持续时间转换为排名优化信号。排名分数可以是慷慨的，也可以是严厉的，这取决于它是否经常将候选人置于良好的等级或最差的等级；它可以使用较宽的有效范围或将大多数候选者压缩为一个较小的区间；它对定时测量或多或少敏感，因此在重新运行时或多或少稳定。上一小节中的公式使用标准化 [0post: after the sandbox has run the candidate, they turn the recorded durations into a ranked optimization signal. A ranking score can be generous or harsh, depending on whether it frequently places candidates in good tiers or in the worst tiers; it can use a wide effective range or collapse most candidates into a small interval; and it can be more or less sensitive to timing measurements, and therefore more or less stable across reruns. The formulas in the previous subsection use normalized [0,1] 我们在这里将数量映射到 0-100 百分位区间，值越低越好。1] quantities that we here rather map to the 0–100 percentile interval, with lower values better.

修复问题Fix a problemx, 候选程序, a candidate programy，以及优化测试, and the optimization testsT其上on whichy具有可用持续时间且至少一个参考持续时间是可用的。对于每个has a usable duration and at least one reference duration is usable. For eacht ∈ Tt ∈T, 让, letDt = {dt,h : h ∈ Ht}是该测试的可用参考持续时间，让be the usable reference durations on that test, and letnt = |DT||Dt|+ 1 为插入后排名的参与者数量+ 1 be the number of ranked participants after insertingy。基本对象是每次测试的百分位数. The basic object is the per-test percentile

哪里wherer ¯ t ( y) 是单索引排名) is the one-indexed rank ofδt ( y) 排序后) after sortingDt ∪{δt ( y ) }从最快到最慢。绑定持续时间被分配在此指标扫描中的平均绑定排名。因此from fastest to slowest. Tied durations are assigned the average tied rank in this metric sweep. Thuspt ( y) = 0 表示测试最快) = 0 means fastest on testt , pt ( y) = 100 表示最慢，并且指标在聚合或转换向量的方式上可能有所不同) = 100 means slowest, and metrics can differ in how they aggregate or transform the vector{点{pt ( y ) : t ∈ T }t ∈T }。我们写 Π(. 最直接的聚合保留每次测试的百分位数并对其求平均值：The most direct aggregation keeps the per-test percentile and averages it:

而中位数百分位数则用中位数代替平均值。修剪平均百分位数对每个测试的百分位数进行排序，在求平均值之前删除最低和最高的 10%，并且仅在修剪后剩余足够的测试时才定义；这滴while median percentile replaces the average by the median. Trimmed mean percentile sorts the per-test percentiles, removes the lowest and highest 10% before averaging, and is only defined when enough tests remain after trimming; this dropsm= 最大值= max{ 1 , ⌊ 0 . 1 |T |⌋}各端的值当values at each end when|T | ≥5. 动机是每次测试的最低和最高百分位数可能来自时序测量异常值，因此删除它们可以使排名函数对时序噪声不太敏感。对数空间百分位数在计算之前首先用其对数替换每个正持续时间5. The motivation is that the lowest and highest per-test percentiles may come from timing-measurement outliers, so removing them can make the ranking function less sensitive to timing noise. Log-space percentile first replaces each positive duration by its logarithm before computingpt，然后对测试进行平均；这主要是为了对集中在最慢百分位数而不是最快百分位数附近的异常值更加稳健。而且，在最慢的百分位数附近，小的变化可能意义不大，可以通过对数来展平。, then averages across tests; this mainly tries to be more robust towards outliers that concentrate near the slowest percentiles rather than the fastest percentiles. And also, near the slowest percentiles, small variations are probably less meaningful and can be flattened by the log.

另一种方法是避免直接输入标量百分位数，因为它们可能有噪声，而是对它们进行阈值处理以滤除部分时序噪声。对于Another approach is to avoid feeding the scalar percentiles directly, since they can be noisy, and instead threshold them to filter out part of the timing noise. Fork ∈ {k ∈{ 25 , 50 }，顶部-, the top-k份额是share is

这是一个越高越好的候选排名分数：This is a higher-is-better candidate ranking score:S25 = 60 意味着考生在 60% 的保留测试中进入最快的四分位。25 = 60 means that the candidate lands in the fastest quartile on 60% of the retained tests.

另一种可能性是在排名之前汇总各个测试的原始持续时间。总持续时间百分位数根据参考总持续时间对候选总持续时间进行排名，Another possibility is to aggregate raw durations across tests before ranking. Total-duration percentile ranks the candidate total duration against reference totals,

哪里whereHT表示在所比较的测试中至少有一个可用持续时间的参考。这个想法是，跨测试聚合可以提供更多测量结果，因此可以抵消各个时间异常值。还可以让少量的长测试主导问题级别的价值；如果这些测试包含真正的优化信号，这可能很有用，但如果其他测试也对根据参考池对候选是否良好进行评分很重要，则这是有害的。denotes references with at least one usable duration on the tests being compared. The idea is that aggregating across tests gives more measurements, so individual timing outliers can cancel out. It can also let a small number of long tests dominate the problem-level value; this can be useful if these tests are the ones that contain the real optimization signal, but harmful if the other tests also matter for scoring whether a candidate is good according to the reference pool.

我们还测试了将候选运行时间曲线的形状与参考池进行比较的指标。在较高层面上，这些指标询问随着测试变得更加困难，候选人的速度是否比参考人慢得多或慢。实际上，它是该测试中存储的人类参考持续时间的中位数，We also tested metrics that compare the shape of the candidate runtime curve to the reference pool. At a high level, these metrics ask whether the candidate slows down more or less than the references as tests become harder. In practice, it is the median stored human-reference duration on that test,

如果人类参考通常在测试中花费更长的时间，则该测试将被视为更难。截距斜率指标询问候选运行时间随着人为难度的增加是缓慢还是快速增长，并且适合If human references usually take longer on a test, that test is treated as harder. The slope-with-intercept metric asks whether candidate runtime grows slowly or quickly as this human-derived difficulty increases, and fits

然后排名then ranksβy对照相应的参考斜率。零截距变体拟合against the corresponding reference slopes. The zero-intercept variant fits

然后排名then ranksβy0. 较低的坡度对应于在测试中持续时间增长较少的候选人0.Lowerslopescorrespondtocandidateswhosedurationgrowslessquicklyonteststhatare参考池的速度较慢。因此，这些分数在排名本身之上添加了第二个建模选择：标量不再仅取决于候选者在每次测试中的位置，还取决于单个线性缩放模型总结其持续时间曲线的程度。slower for the reference pool. These scores therefore add a second modeling choice on top of the ranking itself: the scalar no longer depends only on where the candidate stands on each test, but also on how well a single linear scaling model summarizes its duration curve.

最后，一些指标会丢弃大部分幅度信息。平均胜率和中位胜率将每个测试转化为与参考摘要的二元比较，Finally, some metrics discard most of the magnitude information. Mean win rate and median win rate turn each test into a binary comparison against a reference summary,

哪里whereµt和andmt是平均值和中位数are the mean and median ofDt。这些都是越高越好。过滤后的百分位数变体按参考难度对测试进行排序，使用该测试的平均参考持续时间作为正在评估的持续时间源，然后在删除最简单的 10%、最难的 25% 或最难的 50% 的测试后重新计算平均值或中位数百分位数。这六个变体询问在聚合之前是否应该忽略最简单或最难的测试。与修剪一样，定义过滤分数时至少会删除一项测试，因此我们仅在剩余足够的测试时才对这些变体进行评分。. These are higher-is-better. The filtered percentile variants sort tests by reference difficulty, using the mean reference duration on that test for the duration source being evaluated, then recompute either the mean or median percentile after dropping the easiest 10% of tests, the hardest 25%, or the hardest 50%. These six variants ask whether the easiest or hardest tests should be ignored before aggregation. As with trimming, at least one test is removed when the filtered score is defined, so we only score these variants when enough tests remain.

因此，我们总共有 17 个指标需要筛选：四个直接百分位数聚合（均值、中位数、修剪均值、对数空间均值）、两个顶-So in total we have 17 metrics to be screened: four direct percentile aggregations (mean, median, trimmed mean, log-space mean), two top-k份额、一个总持续时间百分位、两个斜率百分位、两个获胜率和六个过滤百分位变体。我们沿着三个轴筛选它们。首先，分数不应该太容易或太苛刻：如果几乎每个候选人都落在最差的一层，就会产生稀疏的奖励，而给许多候选人良好价值的分数可以消除优化压力。其次，它应该在每个问题中使用有意义的动态范围，否则速度不同的正确解决方案仍然看起来与奖励相似。第三，在重复的 CES 测量和持续时间校准后它应该是稳定的，因为shares, one total-duration percentile, two slope percentiles, two win rates, and six filtered percentile variants. We screened them along three axes. First, the score should not be too easy or too harsh: if almost every candidate lands in the worst tier, it creates a sparse reward, while a score that gives many candidates good values can remove optimization pressure. Second, it should use a meaningful dynamic range within each problem, otherwise correct solutions that differ in speed still look similar to the reward. Third, it should be stable under repeated CES measurements and after duration calibration, because

101

校准应该提高与新时间的一致性，而不是引入排名尺度的变形。calibration should improve agreement with fresh timings rather than introduce deformations of the ranking scale.

图 40 中的整页网格直观地显示了此筛选。行是候选指标，列是持续时间源或拟合的校准变体，在留一问题交叉验证下进行评估。在一个面板中，一个点就是一个问题：它The full-page grid in Figure 40 visualizes this screening. Rows are candidate metrics and columns are duration sources or fitted calibration variants, evaluated under leave-one-problem-out cross-validation. In one panel, a dot is one problem: itsx-坐标是根据新的 CES 持续时间计算出的问题级指标在重复运行中的平均值，及其-coordinate is the mean, over repeated runs, of the problem-level metric computed from fresh CES durations, and itsy- 坐标是根据列的存储或校准持续时间计算的相同度量。点周围的椭圆显示了重复运行的一个标准偏差-coordinate is the same metric computed from the stored or calibrated durations for the column. The ellipse around the dot shows one standard deviation across repeated runs on thex- 和- andy-轴。因此，一个好的面板具有靠近对角线的点、小椭圆以及使用轴范围的有意义部分的扩展；这意味着该指标与新的计时一致，在重播过程中保持稳定，并且不会崩溃到狭窄的分数范围。不良面板会偏离对角线弯曲，出现大椭圆，或者将大多数点折叠成窄带或极端角落。网格使这些故障模式立即可见：一些指标在重新运行过程中不稳定，而其他指标则稳定但只占据分数范围的一小部分。-axes. A good panel therefore has dots close to the diagonal, small ellipses, and a spread that uses a meaningful part of the axis range; this means the metric agrees with fresh timings, is stable across reruns, and does not collapse to a narrow score band. A bad panel bends away from the diagonal, has large ellipses, or collapses most dots into a narrow band or an extreme corner. The grid makes these failure modes visible at once: some metrics are unstable across reruns, while others are stable but occupy only a narrow part of the score range.

通过此规则阅读图 40，候选指标因不同原因而失败。胜率指标在我们保留的仿射校准下占据了有用的值范围，但有些问题具有很宽的椭圆，因此重复的 CES 运行可以大幅移动分数。中值百分位数和中值过滤变体显示出更多的重新运行噪音，并且相对于新的时间，校准分数往往会过度奖励候选人。顶-Reading Figure 40 with this rule, the candidate metrics fail for different reasons. Win-rate metrics occupy a useful range of values under the affine calibration that we retain, but some problems have wide ellipses, so repeated CES runs can move the score substantially. Median percentile and median-filtered variants show even more rerun noise, and the calibrated score tends to over-reward candidates relative to fresh timings. Top-k股票也有很大的噪音，但往往会低估经过校准的候选人。带截距的斜率度量具有非常大的重新运行变异性，使得拟合的斜率对单独的计时测量过于敏感。尽管其校准值比斜率分数更能与新鲜时长值保持一致，但总时长百分位数在重播过程中也有很大差异。这些模式指向基于平均值的百分位家族，它保持有用的动态范围，同时减少重新运行的变异性并避免其他家族明显的过度或不足奖励模式。我们还检查未保留的校准变量，因为所选的仿射校正可能不是确切的基础重新校准模型；当一个指标在这些替代的持续时间修正场景下表现相似时，它就更有吸引力。shares also have substantial noise, but tend to under-reward candidates under calibration. The slopewith-intercept metric has very large rerun variability, making the fitted slope too sensitive to individual timing measurements. Total-duration percentile also varies strongly across reruns, although its calibrated values are better aligned with fresh-duration values than the slope scores. These patterns point to the mean-based percentile family, which keeps a useful dynamic range while reducing rerun variability and avoiding the visible over- or under-rewarding patterns of other families. We also inspect the calibration variants that are not retained, because the selected affine correction may not be the exact underlying recalibration model; a metric is more attractive when it behaves similarly under these alternative duration-correction scenarios.

表 39 详细介绍了基于平均值的排名指标。它将简单的平均百分位数与经过修剪和过滤的变体进行比较，这些变体测试放弃极端或困难的测试是否足以提高稳定性以证明额外的设计选择是合理的。在对其他问题进行持续时间修正后，交叉验证列测量与新 CES 时间的一致性；问题内列测量同一问题的重新运行噪声。Table 39 zooms into the mean-based ranking metrics. It compares plain mean percentile to trimmed and filtered variants, which test whether dropping extreme or difficult tests improves stability enough to justify an extra design choice. The cross-validation columns measure alignment to fresh CES timings after fitting the duration correction on other problems; the within-problem column measures rerun noise for the same problem.

保留平均百分位数是因为它具有最低的交叉验证离散度、与过滤后的变体相当的重新运行噪声以及强解和弱解之间的 47 点分布，而无需添加修剪超参数。然后，我们保留两种方法将该聚合转换为 RL 消融所使用的执行后信号。 QAR 排名质量使用每次测试的平均百分位数本身，因此Mean percentile is retained because it has the lowest cross-validation dispersion, comparable rerun noise to filtered variants, and a 47-point spread between strong and weak solutions without adding trimming hyperparameters. We then keep two ways of turning this aggregate into the post-execution signal used by the RL ablations. QAR ranked quality uses the mean per-test percentile itself, soqQAR 保留了候选人在测试中的平均位置。 QP 将质量首先按照相同的平均百分位数统计数据对参考文献和候选人进行排序，然后报告候选人的问题级别排行榜百分位数。图 41 显示这主要是相同排名信息的轮换：我们保留两者，因为其中一个可能具有太大或太小的动态范围，或者在极端情况下过早饱和，而在线 RL 扫描可以保留效果最好的标量化。QAR keeps the average location of the candidate across tests. QP ranked quality first sorts references and the candidate by that same mean-percentile statistic, then reports the candidate’s problem-level leaderboard percentile. Figure 41 shows this is mostly a rotation of the same ranking information: we keep both because one may have too much or too little dynamic range, or saturate too early at the extreme, and the online RL sweep can retain the scalarization that works best.

D.4 组合奖励以最好地平衡正确性和优化目标D.4 Composing rewards that best balance correctness and optimization objectives

一旦环境减少到（Once an environment is reduced to (c〜cor˜cor, g, q），奖励消融是一个组合规则。设计问题是如何平衡正确性目标和优化目标。严格的外部正确性门使得效率信用以有效程序为条件，而较软的混合或多任务混合可以暴露一些优化信号，即使尚未满足严格的正确性。如果模型学习速度快但程序错误，这可能是有害的，或者如果它在模型达到高通过率之前提供早期优化反馈，这可能是有用的。我们使用标准化接口), the reward ablation is a composition rule. The design question is how to balance the correctness objective and the optimization objective. A strict outer correctness gate makes efficiency credit conditional on a valid program, while softer blends or multitask mixtures can expose some optimization signal even when strict correctness is not yet satisfied. This can be harmful if the model learns fast but wrong programs, or useful if it gives early optimization feedback before the model reaches high pass rates. We use the normalized interfaceq ∈ [0 ,1]，其中1], whereq= 0 最好。信号= 0 is best. The signalccor 是正确性测试通过指标，而cor is the correctness-test pass indicator, whilec～cor 是 D.2 节中的严格先决条件：正确性测试必须通过，并且执行的优化测试可能成功或超时，但不会失败。二进制优化信号是˜cor is the strict prerequisite from Section D.2: correctness tests must pass, and executed optimization tests may be successes or timeouts but not failures. The binary optimization signal isg ∈ {g ∈{ 0 , 1 }，超时容忍门或排名阈值。对于使用的奖励, either a timeout-tolerance gate or a ranked threshold. The idea behind bucketing is that discretizing nearby quality values may smooth out part of the timing noise before it reaches the reward. 〜 根据奖励形式，标量消耗的正确性门可以是˜ Depending on the reward form, the correctness gate consumed by the scalar can be eitherc或cor orc科。折叠和两门奖励（包括质量百分位数）需要通过正确性测试并执行优化测试，以避免在给予任何效率信用之前失败。只能通过环境的显式超时容忍规则来容忍超时，而失败则保留在cor. Collapsed and two-gate rewards, including quality percentile, require correctness tests to pass and executed optimization tests to avoid failures before giving any efficiency credit. Timeouts may be tolerated only through the environment’s explicit timeout-tolerance rule, while failures stay at−1. 仅优化、混合和多任务变体以不同方式放松这种耦合，在严格正确性之前测试一些优化侧反馈是否会改善或损害在线学习。1. Optimization-only, blend, and multitask variants relax this coupling in different ways, testing whether some optimization-side feedback before strict correctness improves or hurts online learning.

所有分级家庭将全球奖励范围保持在[All graded families keep the global reward range in [− 1 ,1]经过相应的组合规则使用的有符号转换后。线性映射将传递解发送到 11] after the signed conversion used by the corresponding composition rule. The linear map sends passing solutions to 1− q，分桶映射将相同的间隔离散成十个桶。范围压缩映射, and the bucketed map discretizes the same interval into ten bins. The range-compressed mapsm [1 / 3 ,1]和1] andm [1 / 2 ,1]保持相同的排序，但提高分配给最差通过解的奖励，使得失败与通过的差距大于通过解之间的优化范围。在奖励已经定义了正确性和优化信号如何相互作用之后，这些映射消除了固定组合规则的正确性和优化的相对规模。1] keep the same ordering but raise the reward assigned to the worst passing solution, so that the failure-to-passing gap is larger than the optimization range among passing solutions. These mappings ablate the relative scale of correctness and optimization for a fixed composition rule, after the reward has already defined how the correctness and optimization signals interact.

D.5 原始持续时间（又名幼稚）奖励基线D.5 Raw-durations (aka naive) reward baselines

天真的持续时间基线测试了我们可以认为是代码优化奖励的最直接方式：将原始秒数直接添加到正确性奖励中。让The naive-duration baselines test the most direct way we could think of as a reward for code optimization: add raw seconds directly to the correctness reward. Letd¯基数是原始公共、私人和生成的正确性测试的平均持续时间，不包括附加的正确性测试，并让base be the mean duration on the original public, private, and generated correctness tests, excluding the additional correctness tests, and letd¯选择优化测试的平均持续时间。超时、缺失持续时间和非正持续时间在平均之前被该测试源的有效时间限制所取代，大多数时间为 10 秒；如果源没有经过测试，则使用相同的有效限值。四个基线选择opt be the mean duration on optimization tests. Timeouts, missing durations, and non-positive durations are replaced by the effective time limit for that test source before averaging, most of the time 10s; if a source has no tests, the same effective limit is used. The four baselines chooses ∈ {s ∈{基地base,选择opt}以及两张地图中的一张，and one of two maps,

然后设置then set

因此，正确的 10 秒平均解接收 0，正确的 1 秒线性解接收 0Thus a correct 10s average solution receives 0, a correct 1s linear solution receives 0.9，一个不正确但非常快的解决方案可以获得接近 0 的奖励，而不是9, and an incorrect but very fast solution can receive a reward close to 0 rather than−1. 这是参考标准化家庭要避免的行为。尝试对数图背后的想法是，对于许多问题，可能的平均持续时间的动态范围可能集中在 [0 s 的左侧附近1. This is the behavior the reference-normalized families avoid. The idea behind trying a log map is that, for many problems, the dynamic range of possible average durations may be concentrated near the left of the [0 s,10秒]范围。10 s] range.

表 42 初始持续时间基线。Table 42 Naive-duration baselines.这些变体的区别仅在于哪些测试提供原始平均持续时间以及该持续时间如何映射到 [0The variants differ only in which tests provide the raw average duration and how that duration is mapped to [0,1]。没有人将候选者与每个问题的人类参考进行比较。1]. None compares the candidate to per-problem human references.

变体Variant 持续时间来源Duration source 持续时间图Duration map
基线Base-linear ́_d_碱基¯_d_base _m_lin
基对数Base-log ́_d_碱基¯_d_base _m_日志_m_log
优化线性Optimization-linear ́_d_opt¯_d_opt _m_lin
优化日志Optimization-log ́_d_opt¯_d_opt _m_日志_m_log

这些基线显然受到以下事实的影响：无论潜在问题是什么，它们都赋予绝对时间奖励。无论问题的参考解决方案在 0.05 秒还是 5 秒内运行，0.1 秒的程序都会获得几乎相同的原始持续时间积分，因此奖励不会根据问题难度或可达到的速度分布进行标准化。一个很大的优点仍然是，这种奖励不需要任何参考分布或额外的数据来校准：它可以直接从执行的输入输出测试中计算出来。相应的训练结果总结在第 5 节中。These baselines obviously suffer from the fact that they attribute absolute timing rewards whatever the underlying problem is. A 0.1s program receives nearly the same raw-duration credit whether the problem’s reference solutions run in 0.05s or 5s, so the reward is not normalized by problem difficulty or by the attainable speed distribution. A big advantage remains that such a reward does not need any reference distribution or extra data to be calibrated: it can be computed directly from the executed input-output tests. The corresponding training results are summarized in Section 5.

D.6 先前发表的论文中优化/效率定义的分类D.6 Taxonomy of optimization/efficiency definitions in previously published papers

上面构建的分类法试图涵盖围绕代码优化已经发布的不同作品：它们的不同之处在于如何定义优化问题，以及是否对其进行训练、评估或两者兼而有之。基准测试可以在执行前构建硬输入，在执行期间强制执行时间限制，或在执行后对候选进行排名，尽管这不一定使其成为在线 RL 环境。类似地，推理时间搜索系统可以使用执行反馈作为适应度分数，而不更新模型策略。因此，表 43 和 44 回顾了之前发表的与我们工作密切相关的主题的研究，并根据上面定义的环境分类和奖励系列描述了它们主要做什么。比较仍然处于高水平。有些系统使用内存、指令计数、复杂性类别或特定于任务的评估器分数，而不是挂钟时间，并且小的实现选择可能很重要。The taxonomy built above tries to cover the different works already published around code optimization: they differ by how they define the optimization problem, and whether they train for it, evaluate on it, or both. A benchmark can construct hard inputs before execution, enforce time limits during execution, or rank a candidate after execution, though this does not necessarily make it an online RL environment. Similarly, an inference-time search system may use execution feedback as a fitness score without updating the model policy. Tables 43 and 44 therefore go over previously published studies on topics close to our work, and describe what they mainly do in light of the environment taxonomy and reward families defined above. The comparison remains high-level. Some systems use memory, instruction counts, complexity classes, or task-specific evaluator scores rather than wall-clock time, and small implementation choices can matter a lot.

我们注意到两件事：首先，基准论文通常解决测量问题，但在在线奖励问题之前停止：它们可以使用压力测试、硬件计数器、右审查或针对特定测试集精心细化的曲线拟合，包括进行大量计算以使分数尽可能可靠，但随后可能变得过于昂贵或过于间接，无法在每次 GRPO 推出时进行评估，也无法提供可用的反馈以按照正确的方向更新策略。其次，使用执行反馈的训练和搜索论文通常比一次性生成更容易，可以通过编辑现有程序或在推理时在候选程序中进行选择。我们的环境定义涵盖了计时可以进入此类系统的主要位置，并且我们保留的训练设置试图避免任何形式的简化：一次性We notice two things: first, benchmark papers often solve measurement but stop before the online reward question: they can use stress tests, hardware counters, right-censoring, or curve fitting that are carefully refined for a specific test set, including with heavy computations to make scores as reliable as possible, but can then become too expensive or too indirect to be evaluated on every GRPO rollout or provide usable feedback to update the policy in the right direction. Second, training and search papers that do use execution feedback often work in an easier regime than one-shot generation, either by editing an existing program or selecting among candidates at inference time. Our environment definitions cover the main places where timing can enter such systems, and our retained training setting tries to avoid any form of simplification: one-shot

108

从头开始生成，仅基于问题描述，没有执行反馈，使用实时执行沙箱进行评估，根据执行结果给予奖励。generation from scratch, based on the problem description only, with no execution feedback, evaluated with a live execution sandbox that gives reward based on execution results.

E 进行离线 RL 模拟，以节省在线 RL 运行的计算量E Doing offline RL simulations to spare compute of online RL runs

第 4 节中的环境空间太大，无法仅通过在线 GRPO 运行进行搜索。本附录解释了用于在训练前修剪该空间的离线模拟器。该模拟器保持与在线强化学习相同的环境侧过滤、超时、排名和结果计算，但用采样的人类解决方案和存储的校准持续时间取代了模型生成和实时执行。为了方便起见，我们使用“模拟器”这个术语，但它仍然是对参考样本的 RL 环境结果的轻量级计算，而不是在线学习本身的模型。The environment space in Section 4 is too large to search only with online GRPO runs. This appendix explains the offline simulator used to prune that space before training. The simulator keeps the same environment-side filtering, timeout, ranking, and outcome computation as online RL, but replaces model generation and live execution with sampled human solutions and stored calibrated durations. We use the term “simulator” for convenience, but it remains a lightweight computation of RL-environment outcomes on reference samples, not a model of online learning itself.

E.1 相同的环境，更便宜的执行E.1 Same environment, cheaper executions

图 43 显示了等效关系。在线训练在工作端有两个昂贵的组件，第一次筛选不需要这些组件：当前模型必须生成代码，沙箱必须执行每个生成的程序（在训练器端，完整的 RL 运行还包括梯度计算；在训练器和工作器之间，它还包括通信成本，包括权重广播）。模拟器替换了这两个组件，同时保持环境/结果路径固定。在模拟器中，从质量带中对人类解决方案进行采样（使用该解决方案预先记录的持续时间构建，这会为其分配一个质量分数，以衡量其超出正确性的优化程度），存储的持续时间取代沙箱调用，并且相同的环境/结果路径产生通过率和质量观察。将质量带从较弱的参考解决方案移至较强的参考解决方案会为每个环境配置生成一条模拟通过率曲线，就像在线强化学习训练的策略会生成越来越强的样本一样。这仅模拟了强化学习运行的一方面：环境如何对更强大的一代做出反应和奖励，而不是政策如何对那些分配的奖励做出反应。Figure 43 shows the equivalence. Online training has two expensive components on the worker side that are not needed for a first screening pass: the current model must generate code, and the sandbox must execute every generated program (on the trainer side, the full RL run also includes gradient computations; across trainers and workers, it also includes communication costs, including weight broadcasting). The simulator replaces those two components while leaving the environment/outcome path fixed. In the simulator, a human solution is sampled from a quality band (built using the pre-recorded durations of that solution, which assigns it a quality score for how optimized it is beyond correctness), stored durations replace sandbox calls, and the same environment/outcome path produces pass-rate and quality observations. Moving the quality band from weaker to stronger reference solutions yields one simulated pass-rate curve per environment configuration, in the same manner that an online-RL-trained policy would generate stronger and stronger samples. This simulates only one side of the RL run: how the environment reacts to and rewards stronger generations, not how the policy reacts to those assigned rewards.

图 43 工作端的在线 RL 训练循环与离线模拟器。Figure 43 Online RL training loop vs. offline simulator, on the worker side.该模拟器通过用人工解决方案采样和预先计算的持续时间查找替换 LLM 推理和沙箱执行来评估候选环境配置。环境端计算保持不变：测试被过滤，时间限制被强制执行，并且在排名环境中，解决方案根据人类参考进行相同的排名。然后，在线培训将结果映射到 GRPO 更新的奖励；而模拟器会记录这些结果的诊断结果，然后根据预先计算的解决方案分数，为下一轮抽取更好的候选者。The simulator evaluates candidate environment configurations by replacing LLM inference and sandbox execution with human solution sampling and precomputed duration lookups. The environment-side computation stays the same: tests are filtered, time limits are enforced, and, in ranked environments, solutions are ranked against human references identically. Online training then maps outcomes to rewards for GRPO updates; whereas the simulator records diagnostics from those outcomes and then samples better candidates for the next round, according to precomputed solution scores.

E.2 量化什么是良好的强化学习环境E.2 Quantifying what a good RL environment is

有用的环境不是一个通过所有事情或失败所有事情的环境，也不是一个奖励采样解决方案的中间部分而不与更好生成的样本相关的环境。让A useful environment is not one that passes everything or fails everything, nor one that rewards an intermediate fraction of sampled solutions without correlating with better generated samples. Letci ∈ [0 ,1] 是标准化步骤的模拟通过率1] be the simulated pass rate at normalized stepxi（所以在 0 和 1 之间），并让(so between 0 and 1), and letc ˜ i是移动平均平滑版本be a moving-average smoothed version of theci曲线。然后，我们在特定 RL 环境配置的完整模拟运行中测量以下数量：curve. We then measure the following quantities on a full simulated run of a certain RL environment configuration:

112

1. 曲线下面积。AUC.

AUC 衡量模拟运行中的通过结果密度。它标记了几乎奖励一切的环境，或者太稀疏而无法生成有用的在线更新的政权。它量化了环境中的激活量，但不告诉它如何激活或激活的形式。AUC measures pass-outcome density across the simulated run. It flags environments that reward almost everything, or regimes that are too sparse to generate useful online updates. It quantifies the amount of activation that goes through the environment, without telling how it activates or the shape of that activation.

  1. 陡度。Steepness.

陡度是激活曲线的剪裁线性拟合的有界斜率。它测量环境是否从大部分失败的采样解决方案顺利过渡到大部分通过的采样解决方案，或者信号是否在窄质量带内突然打开。 AUC 衡量的是激活的总量，而陡度衡量的是激活的方式，无论是突然的还是平滑的。Steepness is the bounded slope of a clipped linear fit to the activation curve. It measures whether the environment moves smoothly from mostly failing sampled solutions to mostly passing sampled solutions, or whether the signal turns on abruptly in a narrow quality band. Whereas AUC measures the total amount of activation that goes through, steepness measures how it activates, whether abruptly or smoothly.

  1. 平滑曲线周围的方差。Variance around the smoothed curve.

方差衡量移动平均趋势的局部波动。该指标并不衡量环境的行为和奖励样本的情况，而是衡量该奖励的稳定性或噪音程度。Variance measures local wobble around the moving-average trend. This metric does not measure how the environment behaves and rewards samples, but how stable or noisy this reward is.

  1. 偏离对角线。Deviation from the diagonal.

与对角线的偏差衡量激活曲线与整个质量范围内逐渐进展的距离。该诊断混合了多种效果：它与 AUC 重叠，反映了激活的数量以及激活突然变化的陡度，而且还捕获了质量扫描中环境激活的位置。在采样质量范围中间附近平滑激活的曲线保持靠近对角线；具有相同平滑形状的曲线仅在样本变得更强时才激活，具有更大的偏差。Deviation from the diagonal measures how far the activation curve is from a gradual progression over the full quality range. This diagnostic mixes several effects: it overlaps with AUC on how much activation goes through and with steepness on how abruptly activation changes, but also captures where the environment activates along the quality sweep. A curve that smoothly activates near the middle of the sampled-quality range stays close to the diagonal; a curve with the same smooth shape that activates only later, when samples become stronger, has larger deviation.

  1. 质量相关性。Quality-correlation.

质量相关性衡量通过结果是否会随着标准化样本质量而增加。这捕获了具有合理平均密度但实际上并未以有用的方式排序采样解决方案的环境。平滑版本尝试消除单个样本噪声，以检查总体环境是否具有有用的方向。Quality-correlation measures whether pass outcomes tend to increase with normalized sample quality. This catches environments that have a reasonable average density but do not actually order sampled solutions in a useful way. The smoothed version tries to remove individual-sample noise to check whether the environment in general has a useful direction.

E.3 这些指标与下游在线强化学习性能相关吗？E.3 Do these metrics correlate with downstream online RL performance?

仅当模拟器的离线诊断说明了我们必须启动的在线运行时，模拟器才有用。我们将其与 Qwen 2.5 7B 启动的 20 个 RL 优化环境进行比较，涵盖执行前、执行中和执行后系列。每次运行均在 DMC-Optim 测试中进行评估The simulator is useful only if its offline diagnostics say something about the online runs we would otherwise have to launch. We compare it to 20 RL-optimization environments launched with Qwen 2.5 7B, across pre-execution, intra-execution, and post-execution families. Each run is evaluated on DMC-Optim test atp 100, p 80, p50，和50, andp30. 主要统计数据是模拟器诊断与该运行达到的在线 RL pass@1 之间的 Spearman 相关性。30. The main statistic is Spearman correlation between a simulator diagnostic and the online RL pass@1 reached by that run.

根据表 45，上面计算的指标不能预测正确性性能：According to Table 45, the metrics computed above do not predict correctness performance: atp100，所有相关性都很弱，而且没有一个是显着的。随着评估门槛变得更加严格，有些变得具有预测性。由100, all correlations are weak and none is significant. As the evaluation threshold becomes stricter, some become predictive. Byp30、最强的信号是偏离30, the strongest signals are deviation fromy = x ( rs = − 0 . 832, p = 2 × 10−_5），原始质量相关性（_rs), raw quality-correlation ( _rs = 0 . 787, p = 10−_4）和陡度（_rs), and steepness ( _rs = 0 . 723, p = 7 × 10−_4），而曲线噪声仍然没有提供信息（_rs), while curve noise remains uninformative ( _rs = − 0 . 002, p = 0 . 994).

当将这些指标与 RL 运行中的早期检查点评估相关时，相关性较弱，与第 5 节末尾观察到的运行变异性一致。然而，到 RL 运行结束时，一些指标显示出更强的信号。When correlating these metrics with earlier checkpoint evaluations in the RL runs, the correlations are weaker, consistent with the run variability observed at the end of Section 5. By the end of the RL runs, however, some metrics show stronger signal.

有用的后续工作是在更细的粒度上研究模拟器，无论是在 RL 优化环境系列中还是在每个系列中，随着其参数的变化。我们没有足够的下游强化学习运行来进行该分析。在当前的研究中，模拟器仍然是最有用的修剪工具：它将高度退化的环境与可以支持在线学习的配置分开，但仍不清楚离线诊断是否可以可靠地对剩余的可行配置进行排名。表 45 中的诊断表明，某些指标在严格的优化约束下携带有关训练结束测试 pass@1 的信号，但它们并不能证明模拟器可以预测在线训练曲线的形状。图 44 将固定质量采样计划得出的离线仿真曲线与表 4 中报告的 Qwen 2.5 7B 配置的在线 RL 训练曲线进行了比较；其中大多数一开始都是成功的候选者，而在更广泛的匹配曲线比较中，主要的失败是 RWA useful follow-up would study the simulator at finer granularity, both across RL-optimization environment families and within each family as its parameters vary. We do not have enough downstream RL runs to conduct that analysis. In the current study, the simulator remains most useful as a pruning tool: it separates highly degenerate environments from configurations that can support online learning, but it is still unclear whether offline diagnostics can reliably rank the remaining viable configurations. The diagnostics in Table 45 suggest that some metrics carry signal about end-of-training test pass@1 under a strict optimization constraint, but they do not establish that the simulator predicts the shape of the online training curve. Figure 44 compares the offline simulation curve induced by a fixed quality-sampling schedule with the online RL training curve for the Qwen 2.5 7B configurations reported in Table 4; most of these were successful candidates in the first place, and the main failures in the broader matched-curve comparison are RWp20 和 RW20 and RWp50. 相同的面板显示了用于计算陡度的夹紧线性拟合。50. The same panels show the clamped-linear fit used to compute steepness.

E.4 使用离线模拟器选择每个环境最有希望的参数化E.4 Using the offline simulator to select the most promising parametrization of each environment

在实践中，我们使用离线模拟器主要是为了扫描每个环境族内的大参数化空间，而不是一次性消除整个族。例如，预执行环境可以具有三个连续值控制：过滤器阈值、优化时间限制和超时容差。图 45 显示了这些扫描生成的热图类型。绝对过滤预执行扫描表明，许多过滤器/时间限制对具有高 AUC，这意味着它们可能奖励太多样本，因此对优化生成几乎没有压力；它们的低质量相关性指向同一方向。排名最差的内部执行家族表现出更广泛的行为，包括过于困难的退化设置，例如In practice, we use the offline simulator mainly to sweep the large parameterization space within each environment family, rather than to eliminate whole families at once. For example, pre-execution environments can have three continuous-valued controls: the filter threshold, the optimization time limit, and the timeout tolerance. Figure 45 shows the type of heatmaps produced by these sweeps. The absolute-filtering preexecution sweep shows that many filter/time-limit pairs have high AUC, meaning they likely reward too many samples and therefore provide little pressure toward optimized generations; their low quality-correlation points in the same direction. The ranked-worst intra-execution family shows a broader range of behaviors, from degenerate settings that are simply too hard, such asp10 具有较低的超时容差，处于较高百分位数的中间设置，超时容差约为 010 with low timeout tolerance, to middle-ground settings at higher percentiles and timeout tolerance around 0.1. 最后，执行后排行榜排名是低维的：一旦排名分数聚合固定，如 D.3 节中所研究的，该环境主要取决于排行榜百分位阈值。扫描表明1. Finally, post-execution leaderboard ranking is lower-dimensional: once the ranking-score aggregation is fixed, as studied in Section D.3, this environment mainly depends on the leaderboard percentile threshold. The sweep suggests thatp10 太严格了10 is too strict whilep100 和100 andp90后太宽容了。尽管表 45 显示趋势方差与下游在线 RL 性能没有太大相关性，但在执行后系列中它仍然保持较低水平。90 are too permissive. Although Table 45 shows that variance around trend does not correlate much with downstream online RL performance, it stays low across this post-execution family.

G.1 用于成对分析的判断提示G.1 Judge prompt used for the pairwise analysis

对于每个盲对，以下用户消息被发送到GPT-OSS 120B。The following user message is sent to GPT-OSS 120B for each blinded pair.

这是一个竞争性编程问题和两个解决方案。仔细分析它们。Here is a competitive programming problem and two solutions. Analyze them carefully.

问题Problem

{problem_description} ## 解决方案 1{problem_description} ## Solution 1

  • '''python {solution_1_code} '''‘‘‘python {solution_1_code} ‘‘‘

解决方案2Solution 2

'''蟒蛇‘‘‘python

  • {solution_2_code} ‘‘‘

使用说明Instructions

  1. 详细分析这两种解决方案。比较他们的算法、数据结构和实现选择。参考具体代码行。Analyze both solutions in detail. Compare their algorithms, data structures, and implementation choices. Reference specific lines of code.

  2. 然后输出具有以下字段的单个 JSON 对象：Then output a single JSON object with the following fields:

{

  • "predicted_faster": "解决方案 1" 或 "解决方案 2" 或 "相同","predicted_faster": "Solution 1" or "Solution 2" or "Same",

  • "algorithm_analysis": "作为单个字符串的详细分析。解释每个解决方案的作用、它们有何不同以及为什么一个解决方案更快。参考特定的代码模式。","algorithm_analysis": "Your detailed analysis as a single string. Explain what each solution does, how they differ, and why one is faster. Reference specific code patterns.",

  • “复杂性比较”：{"complexity_comparison": {

  • “solution_2”：{“最佳”：“O（...）”，“平均”：“O（...）”，“最差”：“O（...）”}"solution_2": {"best": "O(...)", "average": "O(...)", "worst": "O(...)"}

  • },

  • "faster_solution_complexity_improves": { "best": true 或 false 或 null,"faster_solution_complexity_improves": { "best": true or false or null,

  • “最差”： true 或 false 或 null"worst": true or false or null

  • “CONSTANT_FACTOR”，“IMPLMENTATION_STYLE”，“MATHEMATICAL_SHORTCUT”]，"CONSTANT_FACTOR", "IMPLEMENTATION_STYLE", "MATHEMATICAL_SHORTCUT"],

  • “次要类别”：[...]，"secondary_categories": [...],

  • “分类”：“算法”或“表面”，"classification": "ALGORITHMIC" or "SUPERFICIAL",

  • "classification_reason": "一行总结原因","classification_reason": "One-line summary of why",

  • "key_differences": ["特定差异 1", "特定差异 2", ...]"key_differences": ["specific difference 1", "specific difference 2", ...]

  • }

类别定义：Category definitions:

  • ALGORITHM_CHANGE：不同的算法范式（暴力 -> DP、BFS -> Dijkstra、递归 -> 迭代）ALGORITHM_CHANGE: Different algorithmic paradigm (brute force -> DP, BFS -> Dijkstra, recursion -> iterative)

  • DATA_STRUCTURE_CHANGE：不同的核心数据结构（列表 -> 堆、字典 -> 排序集、数组 -> 线段树）DATA_STRUCTURE_CHANGE: Different core data structure (list -> heap, dict -> sorted set, array -> segment tree)

    • COMPLEXITY_REDUCTION：相同的范例，但更严格的复杂性（删除嵌套循环，二分搜索而不是线性扫描）COMPLEXITY_REDUCTION: Same paradigm but tighter complexity (removing nested loop, binary search instead of linear scan)

  • IO_OPTIMIZATION：更快的输入/输出（sys.stdin 与 input()、printf 与 cout）IO_OPTIMIZATION: Faster input/output (sys.stdin vs input(), printf vs cout)

  • CACHING_MEMOIZATION：添加lru_cache、预计算表、记忆化CACHING_MEMOIZATION: Adding lru_cache, precomputation tables, memoization

  • CONSTANT_FACTOR：相同的算法，相同的复杂度，每次迭代的操作更少（修剪，稀疏表示，提前终止）CONSTANT_FACTOR: Same algorithm, same complexity, fewer operations per iteration (pruning, sparse representation, early termination)

  • IMPLMENTATION_STYLE：代码风格差异，对性能影响可以忽略不计IMPLEMENTATION_STYLE: Code style differences with negligible performance impact

  • MATHEMATICAL_SHORTCUT：封闭式公式、模算术技巧、组合恒等式MATHEMATICAL_SHORTCUT: Closed-form formula, modular arithmetic trick, combinatorial identity

分类定义：Classification definitions:

  • 算法：更快的解决方案使用根本不同的算法、范式或方法来解决问题（例如，强力与 DP、BFS 与 Dijkstra、完全不同的数学公式）。这两种解决方案体现了不同的问题解决策略。ALGORITHMIC: The faster solution uses a fundamentally different algorithm, paradigm, or approach to solve the problem (e.g., brute force vs DP, BFS vs Dijkstra, completely different mathematical formulation). The two solutions reflect different problem-solving strategies.

  • 肤浅的：更快的解决方案使用相同的核心算法/方法，但具有实现级别的改进——更紧密的循环、修剪、同一框架内更好的数据结构、I/O 技巧或常数因子优化。这包括同一范式内的优化恰好提高了渐近复杂度的情况（例如，添加二分搜索以跳过其他相同算法中的线性扫描）。SUPERFICIAL: The faster solution uses the same core algorithm/approach but with implementation-level improvements -- tighter loops, pruning, better data structures within the same framework, I/O tricks, or constant-factor optimizations. This includes cases where optimizations within the same paradigm happen to improve asymptotic complexity (e.g., adding a binary search to skip a linear scan in an otherwise identical algorithm).

118

对于“faster_solution_complexity_improves”：将预测的更快解决方案的复杂度与其他解决方案进行比较。 true = 更快的解决方案在该级别上具有严格更好的复杂性， false = 相同或更差， null = 无法确定。For "faster_solution_complexity_improves": compare the predicted-faster solution’s complexity against the other. true = faster solution has strictly better complexity at that level, false = same or worse, null = cannot determine.

G.2 优化 RL 优于标准 RLVR 的情况G.2 Cases where optimization-RL beats standard RLVR

在这两个问题中，CWM 32B 提交的内容都是正确的，但优化 RL 恢复了已知的预期解决方案，而标准 RLVR 生成了正确但更通用的程序。In these two problems, both CWM 32B submissions are correct, but optimization-RL recovers the known intended solution while standard RLVR produces a correct but more generic program.

Codeforces 1466F，“欧几里得的噩梦”。问题。Codeforces 1466F, “Euclid’s Nightmare”. Problem.给定GivennZ 上的向量vectors over Zm 2,每个最多有两个非零,eachwithatmosttwonon-zero坐标，计算其跨度的大小模 10coordinates, compute the size of their span modulo 109+7 并输出跨越相同空间的字典顺序最小的最小子集。这里+7 and output the lexicographically smallest minimum subset that spans the same space. Heren、m≤n, m ≤ 5 · 105 . 测量：Measured: RLVR 46 . 4 s →优化-RL 28optimization-RL 28. 5 s ( × 1 .63）；判断标签：算法改变/算法。63); judge label: algorithm change / algorithmic.

RLVR解决方案。RLVR solution.

def main(): import sys input = sys.stdin.read().split() ptr = 0 n = int(input[ptr]) ptr += 1 m = int(input[ptr]) ptr += 1 个向量 = [] for i in range(n): k = int(input[ptr]) ptr += 1 coords = list(map(int, input[ptr:ptr + k])) ptr += k coords.sort() if k == 1: vec = (coords[0],) else: vec = (coords[0], coords[1]) Vectors.append((vec, i + 1)) basic = {} selected_indices = []def main(): import sys input = sys.stdin.read().split() ptr = 0 n = int(input[ptr]) ptr += 1 m = int(input[ptr]) ptr += 1 vectors = [] for i in range(n): k = int(input[ptr]) ptr += 1 coords = list(map(int, input[ptr:ptr + k])) ptr += k coords.sort() if k == 1: vec = (coords[0],) else: vec = (coords[0], coords[1]) vectors.append((vec, i + 1)) basis = {} selected_indices = []

def merge(a, b): if a == b: return if size[a] < size[b]: a, b = b, aparent[b] = a size[a] += size[b] has_single[a] = has_single[a] 或 has_single[b]def merge(a, b): if a == b: return if size[a] < size[b]: a, b = b, a parent[b] = a size[a] += size[b] has_single[a] = has_single[a] or has_single[b]

for idx in range(1, n + 1): parts = list(map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())) k = parts[0] if k == 1: x = parts[1] rx = find(x) if not has_single[rx]: basic.append(idx) has_single[rx] = True else: x, y = parts[1], parts[2] rx = find(x) ry = find(y) 如果 rx == ry: 继续 s_rx = has_single[rx] s_ry = has_single[ry] 如果 s_rx 和 s_ry: merge(rx, ry) elif s_rx 或 s_ry: basic.append(idx) merge(rx, ry) else: basic.append(idx) merge(rx, ry)for idx in range(1, n + 1): parts = list(map(int, sys.stdin.readline().split())) k = parts[0] if k == 1: x = parts[1] rx = find(x) if not has_single[rx]: basis.append(idx) has_single[rx] = True else: x, y = parts[1], parts[2] rx = find(x) ry = find(y) if rx == ry: continue s_rx = has_single[rx] s_ry = has_single[ry] if s_rx and s_ry: merge(rx, ry) elif s_rx or s_ry: basis.append(idx) merge(rx, ry) else: basis.append(idx) merge(rx, ry)

发生了什么变化。What changed.两个程序都计算给定 GF(2) 向量的基并报告跨度大小 2Both programs compute a basis of the given GF(2) vectors and report the span size 2等级rank连同最小生成子集。 RLVR 构建一个通用线性基，以每个向量的最小非零坐标为键控，并通过对称差分坐标集重复减少输入向量。这是 GF(2) 上有效的高斯消去法，但它忽略了输入的特殊结构。 Optimization-RL 使用预期的图形重构：一个两坐标向量 (together with a minimum generating subset. RLVR builds a generic linear basis, keyed by the smallest non-zero coordinate of each vector, and repeatedly reduces incoming vectors by symmetric-differencing coordinate sets. This is valid Gaussian elimination over GF(2), but it ignores the special structure of the input. Optimization-RL uses the intended graph reformulation: a two-coordinate vector (x、yx, y) 是坐标之间的边，单坐标向量是组件级的单个向量，并且可以使用每个组件携带一个 has_single 标志的不相交集并集来维护等级。通用消除被近线性联合查找取代。) is an edge between coordinates, a one-coordinate vector is a component-level single, and the rank can be maintained with a disjoint-set union carrying one has_single flag per component. Generic elimination is replaced by near-linear union–find.

G.3 优化强化学习击败人类最快速度的案例G.3 Cases where optimization-RL beats the fastest available human

这里的“最佳人类”是指源数据集中针对该问题提供的最快、正确的人类解决方案，不一定是全球最佳的 Codeforces 或 AtCoder 提交。下面的例子仍然是真正的竞争性编程解决方案；重点是检查优化强化学习何时编写更干净或更优化的变体。Here “best human” means the fastest correct human solution available in the source dataset for the problem, not necessarily the globally best Codeforces or AtCoder submission. The examples below are still real competitive-programming solutions; the point is to inspect when optimization-RL writes a cleaner or more optimized variant.

def count_invalid(s1, s2, s3, l): d = s1 + s2 - s3 if d > 0: if l < d: return 0 m = (l - d) // 2 C = l - d + 1 sum1 = (m + 1) * (m + 2) // 2 sum2 = m * (m + 1) * (m + 2) // 3 return C * sum1 - 2 * sum2 否则: k = -d m1 = min(k, l) part1 = 0 如果 m1 >= 0: sum1_part1 = (m1 + 1) * (m1 + 2) // 2 sum2_part1 = m1 * (m1 + 1) * (m1 + 2) // 3 part1 = (l + 1) * sum1_part1 - sum2_part1 s_start = k + 1 s_end = (l + k) // 2 部分 2 = 0 如果 s_start <= s_end: C = l + k + 1 a = s_start b = s_end sum1_part2 = ((b + 1) * (b + 2) // 2) - (a * (a + 1) // 2) sum2_part2 = (b * (b + 1) * (b + 2) - (a - 1) * a * (a + 1)) // 3 部分 2 = C * sum1_part2 - 2 * sum2_part2 返回 部分 1 + 部分 2def count_invalid(s1, s2, s3, l): d = s1 + s2 - s3 if d > 0: if l < d: return 0 m = (l - d) // 2 C = l - d + 1 sum1 = (m + 1) * (m + 2) // 2 sum2 = m * (m + 1) * (m + 2) // 3 return C * sum1 - 2 * sum2 else: k = -d m1 = min(k, l) part1 = 0 if m1 >= 0: sum1_part1 = (m1 + 1) * (m1 + 2) // 2 sum2_part1 = m1 * (m1 + 1) * (m1 + 2) // 3 part1 = (l + 1) * sum1_part1 - sum2_part1 s_start = k + 1 s_end = (l + k) // 2 part2 = 0 if s_start <= s_end: C = l + k + 1 a = s_start b = s_end sum1_part2 = ((b + 1) * (b + 2) // 2) - (a * (a + 1) // 2) sum2_part2 = (b * (b + 1) * (b + 2) - (a - 1) * a * (a + 1)) // 3 part2 = C * sum1_part2 - 2 * sum2_part2 return part1 + part2

发生了什么变化。What changed.如果按照进入顺序扫描汽车，其出口指数低于迄今为止看到的最大出口指数，则汽车将被罚款。人类的解决方案是使用一组已经被罚款的汽车进行正确的两指针实现，但它是问题的复杂版本。 Optimization-RL 使用一组退出位置编写规范的单遍运行最大解决方案。这不是渐近意义上的算法飞跃——两者都是A car is fined iff, scanning cars in entry order, its exit index is below the maximum exit index seen so far. The human solution is a correct two-pointer implementation with a set of already-fined cars, but it is the fiddly version of the problem. Optimization-RL writes the canonical one-pass running-maximum solution with an array of exit positions. This is not an algorithmic leap in the asymptotic sense—both areO ( n）——但它是更干净的实现，具有更少的散列和更少的指针簿记。)—but it is the cleaner implementation with less hashing and less pointer bookkeeping.

发生了什么变化。What changed.时At timei，速度的上限都是从一开始就逐渐上升，, the speed is upper-bounded both by ramping up from the start,v 1 +( 我 -i − 1) d，并且仍然能够逐渐下降到最后，, and by still being able to ramp down to the end,v 2 + ( t−it − i ) d。将这两个界限的最小值相加即可直接得出最佳距离. Summing the minimum of those two bounds gives the optimal distance directly inO ( t）。人类的解决方案是正确的贪婪模拟，对可行的速度变化进行每步扫描，因此它是). Optimization-RL writes the intended two-ramp observation directly; this is a cleaner solution more than a surprising one.
